During training, SPIN translates labeled simulator observations toward the real-world domain and back to the simulator domain, using the original simulator labels to encourage domain transfer that preserves parameter-relevant mutual information. At test time, the learned real-to-simulator transport maps real-world observations into the simulator domain for posterior inference, without requiring real-world parameter labels or paired real--simulator observations. Across controlled synthetic and physical real-world benchmarks, SPIN improves real-world posterior inference, with the improvement becoming clearer as misspecification increases.

</details>


### [148] [Distributionally Robust Multi-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05660)
*Yufeng Yang,Fangning Zhuo,Ziyi Chen,Heng Huang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了分布稳健多目标优化（DR-MOO）框架，旨在解决数据分布偏移下的多目标学习问题，并为此设计了高效的梯度下降算法，在非凸环境下实现了显著优化的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流的多目标优化（MOO）方法未能明确解决数据分布发生偏移（distributional shifts）的问题，且在处理非凸问题时缺乏稳健性和效率保障。

Method: 1. 引入分布稳健多目标优化（DR-MOO）框架，通过最小化最坏情况分布下的多目标函数；2. 提出了适用于 DR-MOO 的 Pareto 解概念；3. 开发了双循环 MGDA 算法及优化后的单循环双剪切（double-clip）MGDA 算法；4. 利用 Lagrangian 对偶重构和梯度剪切技术。

Result: 1. 证明了提出的双循环算法的收敛性，样本复杂度为 O(ε⁻¹²)。2. 通过梯度剪切技术将单循环算法的样本复杂度提升至 O(ε⁻⁴)。3. 实验验证表明该方法在非凸设置下与现有最先进的 MGDA 基准相比具有明显竞争力。

Conclusion: DR-MOO 框架能够有效处理多目标优化中的分布偏移问题，所提出的单循环双剪切 MGDA 算法在非凸环境下具备理论上的收敛性保障与高效的样本复杂度（O(ε⁻⁴)）。

Abstract: Multi-objective optimization (MOO) has received growing attention in applications that require learning under multiple criteria. However, the existing MOO formulations do not explicitly account for distributional shifts in the data. We introduce distributionally robust multi-objective optimization (DR-MOO), which minimizes multiple objectives under their respective worst-case distributions. We propose Pareto-type solution concepts for DR-MOO and develop multi-gradient descent algorithms (MGDA) with provable guarantees. Leveraging a Lagrangian dual reformulation, we first design a double-loop MGDA that uses an inner loop to estimate dual variables and achieves a total sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-12})$ for reaching an $ε$-Pareto-stationary point. To further improve efficiency, we incorporate gradient clipping to handle generalized-smooth and biased gradient estimates, removing the need for double sampling. This yields a single-loop double-clip MGDA with substantially improved sample complexity $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$. Our theory applies to the nonconvex setting and does not require bounded objectives or gradients. Experiments demonstrate that our methods are competitive with state-of-the-art MGDA baselines.

</details>


### [149] [Budgeted Attention Allocation: Cost-Conditioned Compute Control for Efficient Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05697)
*Amrit Nidhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预算化注意力门控机制，允许单一Transformer模型根据设定的计算预算动态调整推理成本，在多项任务中证明了该方法在权衡性能与实际计算速度（精度可控性）方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Transformer模型通常每个训练好的模型对应固定的推理成本，而实际部署场景往往需要根据不同资源需求动态调整“成本-质量”的权衡点。

Method: 提出了一种“预算化注意力分配”（Budgeted Attention Allocation）机制，采用基于请求注意力预算的单调门控（Head-gating）策略；引入了密集预热启动（Dense warm-starting）以提升训练稳定性，并结合硬门控适配（Hard-gate adaptation）和结构化剪枝技术，将软性成本控制转化为实际的CPU运行速度提升。

Result: 在合成任务中达到高精度与低成本的平衡；在AG News数据集上实现了1.28倍的速度提升且仅损失少量精度；在DBpedia14数据集上，预算化门控模型在0.5预算下达到了97.4%的准确率，超过了密集全注意力模型（96.6%）。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了通过单一“预算化”检查点在不同计算预算间进行权衡的可行性，提出了一种可控且灵活的注意力分配机制，在保持模型性能的同时实现了实际的计算加速。

Abstract: Transformers usually expose one inference cost per trained model, while deployed systems often need multiple cost-quality operating points. We study Budgeted Attention Allocation, a monotone head-gating mechanism conditioned on a requested attention budget. Dense warm-starting is important for stability: on a robust synthetic sequence task, one budgeted model reaches 99.7% accuracy at 0.303 estimated attention cost and 100.0% accuracy at 0.504 cost. On held-out AG News with a custom word-level transformer, hard-gate adaptation turns soft cost control into measured single-thread CPU speed, reaching 82.1% accuracy with 1.28x speedup at budget 0.50. In pretrained BERT-Mini AG News, budgeted structural pruning reaches 87.6% accuracy with 1.20x speedup at budget 0.50; a validation-ranked zero-shot dense post-hoc structural baseline reaches 86.1%, and one recovery epoch raises that per-budget specialist to 87.9%. On DBpedia14, BERT-Mini budgeted gates reach 97.4% at exact budget 0.50 versus 96.6% for dense full attention. Static fixed-budget gates and recovered dense specialists remain strong. The contribution is therefore not universal dominance, but a reproducible feasibility study of one controllable checkpoint across budgets that can trade attention cost for accuracy and be converted into measured structural speedups on small CPU benchmarks.

</details>


### [150] [On the Blessing of Pre-training in Weak-to-Strong Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05710)
*Wei Yao,Wang Zhaoyang,Gengze Xu,Chen Qian,Dongrui Liu,Ziqiao Wang,Yong Liu,Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了预训练是弱到强泛化（W2SG）的核心前提，通过几何热启动将模型引导至有效学习区域，并证明了 W2SG 的涌现依赖于预训练进阶过程中的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱到强泛化（W2SG）范式虽被提出，但“预训练”在其中所起的决定性作用尚缺乏充分的理论和实证研究。本文旨在探究预训练为何是实现 W2SG 的必要前提。

Method: 1. 理论层面：在强相关高斯数据下，将 W2SG 形式化为单指标模型，并将预训练建模为谱初始化步骤；推导广义化界，刻画从初期性能提升到受弱监督者偏差限制（饱和瓶颈）的动力学过程。 2. 实验层面：通过受控合成数据模拟验证假设，并对大规模语言模型数百个中间检查点进行实证评估。

Result: 理论证明了当预训练提供了一个位于强凸几何区域内的“热启动”时，W2SG 是可实现的；实证研究确认了 W2SG 的性能提升存在明显的相变点，且其上限受限于弱监督者的偏差。

Conclusion: W2SG 并非模型固有能力，而是通过预训练进阶诱发的相变现象。预训练通过提供“几何热启动”(geometrical warm start) 使模型进入有效学习区域，从而突破随机初始化下的学习瓶颈。

Abstract: The paradigm of Weak-to-Strong Generalization (W2SG) suggests that a pre-trained strong model can surpass its weak supervisor, yet the decisive role of pre-training remains theoretically and empirically under-explored. In this work, we identify pre-training as the essential prerequisite for the emergence of W2SG. Theoretically, we formalize the W2SG problem within a high-dimensional single-index model framework using spiked Gaussian data, modeling pre-training as a spectral initialization step. Building upon prior impossibility results regarding the failure of learning under random initialization, we prove that W2SG is achievable when pre-training provides a geometric warm start that places the model within an "effective region" characterized by a perturbed strong-convexity geometry. Within this region, we derive a rigorous generalization bound that naturally captures the optimization dynamics: an initial performance improvement followed by a saturation bottleneck dictated by the weak supervisor's bias. Empirically, we first validate all our assumptions and theoretical insights through controlled synthetic simulations. Finally, through a massive-scale evaluation of hundreds of intermediate pre-training checkpoints from large language models, we demonstrate that W2SG is not an innate capability but emerges via a phase transition tightly coupled with the progression of pre-training.

</details>


### [151] [Enabling Federated Inference via Unsupervised Consensus Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05718)
*Yui Hashimoto,Takayuki Nishio,Yuichi Kitagawa,Takahito Tanimura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CE-FI是一种无需共享原始数据或模型参数的协作推理框架，通过训练轻量级的对齐层实现对异构预训练模型的聚合及性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的联邦/协作推理框架通常要求共享原始输入、模型参数或强制使用相同的编码器，这在隐私敏感或跨组织场景中存在局限。作者旨在解决在无需共享隐私敏感信息且模型异构的条件下，实现多模型协作推理的问题。

Method: 提出了“共识嵌入”（Consensus Embedding, CE）层和“协作输出”（Cooperative Output, CO）层。CE层将不同模型的异构中间表示映射到一个共享的嵌入空间，CO层则基于这些嵌入进行最终预测；该架构仅需少量无标签数据进行训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100等数据集的非独立同分布（non-IID）场景中，CE-FI的表现显著优于独立推理，并达到与传统需要更强共享假设的方法相当的水平。在文本和时间序列任务中也展现了通用性。

Conclusion: CE-FI能够在无需共享原始数据或模型参数的前提下，通过对齐异构模型表示实现有效的协作推理。实验表明其表现优于独立推理，且在隐私保护要求较高的跨组织场景中具有良好的适用性，表征对齐是该框架的主要性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Cooperative inference across independently deployed machine learning models is increasingly desirable in distributed environments, as there is a growing need to leverage multiple models while keeping their data and model parameters private. However, existing cooperative frameworks typically rely on sharing input data, model parameters, or a common encoder, which limits their applicability in privacy-sensitive or cross-organizational settings. To address this challenge, we propose Consensus Embedding-based Federated Inference (CE-FI), a framework that enables pretrained models to cooperate at inference time without sharing model parameters or raw inputs and without assuming a common encoder. CE-FI introduces two components: a Consensus Embedding (CE) layer that maps heterogeneous intermediate representations into a common embedding space, and a Cooperative Output (CO) layer that produces predictions from these embeddings. Both layers are trained using shared unlabeled data only, so the cooperative stage does not require additional labeled data. Experiments on image classification benchmarks -- CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 -- under diverse non-IID conditions show that CE-FI consistently outperforms solo inference and performs comparably to conventional methods that require stronger sharing assumptions. Additional evaluations on text and time-series tasks indicate applicability beyond image classification, although performance depends on the ensemble strategy. Further analysis identifies representation alignment as the primary bottleneck.

</details>


### [152] [CRAFT: Forgetting-Aware Intervention-Based Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05732)
*Md Anwar Hossen,Fatema Siddika,Juan Pablo Munoz,Tanya Roosta,Ali Jannesari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CRAFT是一种无需更新模型权重的持续学习框架，通过在隐藏表示空间进行KL散度引导的低秩干预，有效解决了大语言模型微调过程中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型通过微调获得新能力时，持续适应会导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出CRAFT框架，不更新模型权重，而是学习隐藏表示上的低秩干预。包括三个阶段：1. 基于输出分布散度进行任务路由；2. 使用KL散度针对任务组先验状态进行微调；3. 将更新任务的干预合并到共享表示中。三个阶段统一于KL散度目标。

Result: 在多个基准测试及不同模型规模下，相较于LoRA方法，CRAFT提高了整体性能，减少了遗忘，且对任务顺序具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 在表示空间中通过基于输出空间散度的控制，为大语言模型提供了一种可扩展且有原则的持续学习路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can acquire new capabilities through fine-tuning, but continual adaptation often leads to catastrophic forgetting. We propose CRAFT, a continual learning framework that avoids updating model weights by instead learning low-rank interventions on hidden representations. CRAFT proceeds in three stages: it first routes each task to a group of similar tasks based on output-distribution divergence; it then fine-tunes the model using a Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence against the group's prior state, which directly controls forgetting and determines convergence; finally, it merges interventions for the updated task into the shared representation using the same KL signal. This design unifies routing, regularization, and merging through a single KL-based objective. CRAFT improves overall performance and reduces forgetting compared to strong LoRA-based approaches across multiple benchmarks and model scales, while remaining robust to task ordering. These results suggest that controlling adaptation in representation space, guided by output-space divergence, provides a scalable and principled approach to continual learning in LLMs.

</details>


### [153] [Weak-to-Strong Generalization is Nearly Inevitable (in Linear Models)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05742)
*Scott Geng,Dutch Hansen,Jerry Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究证明了“弱到强泛化”现象不仅存在于复杂大模型，在简单的线性逻辑回归中也广泛且必然存在，且不依赖于模型容量的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究近期大模型领域中备受关注的“弱到强泛化”（weak-to-strong generalization）现象的理论边界，评估其是否依赖于模型能力的差异。

Method: 在标准的线性逻辑回归设置下，通过温和的数据分布假设，对学生-老师模型的泛化性能进行了数学推导和理论分析。

Result: 研究发现，即使模型容量没有差异，弱老师模型训练的强学生模型依然能够实现泛化能力的提升；该现象在广泛的参数设置下均成立。

Conclusion: 该论文证明了“弱到强泛化”现象在简单线性逻辑回归中几乎不可避免，且挑战了“学生模型需要比老师模型具备更强表达能力”的传统认知。

Abstract: Weak-to-strong generalization is a phenomenon in post-training whereby a strong student model, when finetuned solely with feedback from a weaker teacher, can not only surpass the teacher, but can improve upon its own capabilities. Recent work of Burns et al. (2023) demonstrated that this can occur in the setting of frontier language models, and subsequently there has been a flurry of both empirical work trying to exploit this phenomenon, as well as theoretical work attempting to understand it. In this work, we demonstrate that weak-to-strong generalization occurs in standard linear logistic regression, under mild distributional assumptions on the data. In fact, we show that this happens for most student-teacher pairs, suggesting that weak-to-strong generalization is in fact \emph{almost inevitable}, even in this basic setting. Notably, our setting does not require the student to be more expressive or have more model capacity in any way compared to the teacher, which runs contrary to the prevailing theoretical belief that a mismatch in model capacity is a central mechanism to weak-to-strong generalization.

</details>


### [154] [RVPO: Risk-Sensitive Alignment via Variance Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05750)
*Ivan Montero,Tomasz Jurczyk,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RVPO 提出通过优化奖励一致性而非单纯追求奖励总和，成功解决了多目标 RLHF 中的约束忽视问题，在复杂推理和工具调用任务中实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无评论家（critic-less）RLHF 方法使用算术平均聚合多目标奖励，容易导致“约束忽视”（constraint neglect）问题，即模型通过最大化部分容易实现的奖励来掩盖关键目标（如安全或格式要求）的失败。

Method: 提出 Reward-Variance Policy Optimization (RVPO) 框架，利用 LogSumExp (SoftMin) 算子作为平滑方差惩罚，将强化学习的目标从简单的“奖励求和最大化”转变为“奖励一致性最大化”。

Result: 在 HealthBench 等基准测试中，RVPO 在 14B 模型上取得了 0.261 的得分（对比 GDPO 的 0.215），且在 GPQA-Diamond 上保持了竞争力的准确率，避免了其他多奖励方法常见的后期性能衰退。

Conclusion: RVPO 通过引入方差正则化有效地缓解了多目标强化学习中的“约束忽视”问题，在保证一般任务能力的同时，显著提升了模型在复杂多约束场景下的可靠性和综合表现。

Abstract: Current critic-less RLHF methods aggregate multi-objective rewards via an arithmetic mean, leaving them vulnerable to constraint neglect: high-magnitude success in one objective can numerically offset critical failures in others (e.g., safety or formatting), masking low-performing "bottleneck" rewards vital for reliable multi-objective alignment. We propose Reward-Variance Policy Optimization (RVPO), a risk-sensitive framework that penalizes inter-reward variance during advantage aggregation, shifting the objective from "maximize sum" to "maximize consistency." We show via Taylor expansion that a LogSumExp (SoftMin) operator effectively acts as a smooth variance penalty. We evaluate RVPO on rubric-based medical and scientific reasoning with up to 17 concurrent LLM-judged reward signals (Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B) and on tool-calling with rule-based constraints (Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B). By preventing the model from neglecting difficult constraints to exploit easier objectives, RVPO improves overall scores on HealthBench (0.261 vs. 0.215 for GDPO at 14B, $p < 0.001$) and maintains competitive accuracy on GPQA-Diamond without the late-stage degradation observed in other multi-reward methods, demonstrating that variance regularization mitigates constraint neglect across model scales without sacrificing general capabilities.

</details>


### [155] [Full-Spectrum Graph Neural Network: Expressive and Scalable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05759)
*Xiaohan Wang,Deyu Bo,Longlong Li,Kelin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FSpecGNN，通过引入双变量谱滤波机制突破了传统GNN的1-WL表达瓶颈，有效提升了异构图的学习能力，并利用低秩近似技术实现了高效、可扩展的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱GNN的表达能力受限于1-WL测试，无法处理高阶信号或有效应对异构图（heterophilic graphs）学习任务。

Method: 1. 将信号域从节点提升至节点对（node-pair）；2. 将单变量谱滤波器推广为双变量谱滤波器（基于特征值对）；3. 提出低秩近似算法，将全谱卷积转化为多项式谱滤波器的组合，从而避免显式计算节点对，实现可扩展性。

Result: 理论上证明了FSpecGNN能够逼近节点对信号，其表达能力上限可达Local 2-GNN；实验表明模型在异构图基准测试中性能优越，且具备处理大规模图的计算效率。

Conclusion: FSpecGNN通过从单变量扩展到双变量谱滤波，突破了传统谱GNN的表达能力限制（1-WL），并在异构图学习中表现卓越，同时通过低秩近似保证了在大规模图上的可扩展性。

Abstract: It is well established that spectral graph neural networks (GNNs) can universally approximate node signals; however, their expressive power remains bounded by the 1-dimensional Weisfeiler-Lehman test, which is mirrored in their lack of universality for higher-order signals. To go beyond this bound, we propose the Full-Spectrum GNN (FSpecGNN), a second-order generalization of classical spectral GNNs. FSpecGNN advances spectral filtering in two perspectives: (1) it lifts the signal from the node domain to the node-pair domain; and (2) it extends the univariate spectral filter over eigenvalues to a bivariate filter over eigenvalue pairs. We show that classical spectral GNNs arise as a diagonal special case of FSpecGNN, and prove that FSpecGNN can be at most as expressive as Local 2-GNN while universally approximating node-pair signals, the latter being particularly beneficial for heterophilic graph learning. Moreover, FSpecGNN admits scalable implementations that avoid explicit node-pair-level computations; combined with a low-rank approximation that reduces full-spectrum convolution to a combination of polynomial spectral filters, it enables learning on large graphs. Empirically, FSpecGNN validates the predicted expressivity and delivers strong performance on heterophilic benchmarks.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Selection of LoRA Components in Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05769)
*Myoungjun Kim,Sangwoo Park,Yoseob Han,Jin-Hyun Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AS-LoRA 是一种针对差分隐私联邦微调的自适应框架，通过动态且细粒度的层级/轮次优化策略，消除了 LoRA 聚合的重建误差瓶颈，在低计算开销下实现了更优的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私（DP）环境下的联邦 LoRA 微调中，LoRA 的乘法结构导致聚合误差，并被 DP 噪声放大，而现有方法多采用统一或固定的僵化更新策略，未考虑层间非对称性和训练动态。

Method: 提出 AS-LoRA 框架，具备三个核心维度：(1) 层级自由度（各层独立选择活跃组件）；(2) 轮次自适应（选择策略随训练动态更新）；(3) 基于二阶损失近似的曲率感知评分机制。

Result: 在 GLUE、SQuAD 等数据集及严格 DP 约束和非 IID 数据分布下，AS-LoRA 较基线有显著提升（GLUE 最多提升 7.5 pp，MNLI-mm 提升 12.5 pp），且在大规模聚合成本上优于 SVD 类方法（降低 33-180 倍）。

Conclusion: AS-LoRA 通过引入三维自适应框架，有效解决了差分隐私联邦微调中 LoRA 聚合误差带来的不稳定和精度损失问题，在不增加隐私代价的前提下，显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Differentially private federated fine-tuning of large models with LoRA suffers from aggregation error caused by LoRA's multiplicative structure, which is further amplified by DP noise and degrades both stability and accuracy. Existing remedies apply a single update mode uniformly across all layers and all communication rounds (or alternate them on a fixed schedule), ignoring both the structural asymmetry between the two LoRA factors and the round-wise dynamics of training. We propose AS-LoRA, an adaptive framework defined by three axes (i) layer-wise freedom, in which each layer independently selects its active component, (ii) round-wise adaptivity, in which the selection updates over communication rounds, and (iii) a curvature-aware score derived from a second-order approximation of the loss. Theoretically, AS-LoRA eliminates the reconstruction-error floor of layer-tied schedules, accelerates convergence, implicitly biases solutions toward flatter minima, and incurs no additional privacy cost. Across GLUE, SQuAD, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under strict DP budgets and non-IID partitions, AS-LoRA improves over the federated LoRA baselines by up to $+7.5$ pp on GLUE and $+12.5$ pp on MNLI-mm for example, while matching or exceeding SVD-based aggregation methods at $33\text{--}180 \times$ lower aggregation cost and with negligible communication overhead. Code for the proposed method is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/as_lora-F75F/.

</details>


### [157] [A Measure-Theoretic Finite-Sample Theory for Adaptive-Data Fitted Q-Iteration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05791)
*Manuel Haussmann,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一测度论、误差传播和机器学习泛化理论，解决了深度强化学习理论与实践脱节的问题，并首次为连续空间下的FQI提供了完善的性能与遗憾界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在处理复杂非线性机器人系统时表现出色，但在理论上存在巨大差距，现有文献零散地分布在测度论MDP基础、确定性误差分析和PAC泛化界等三个互不相通的传统中，缺乏对现代深度RL算法的统一理论支撑。

Method: 利用巴拿赫空间（Banach spaces）中的贝尔曼算子压缩映射与测度论概率分析相结合，构建了FQI的统一理论框架，并证明了序列Rademacher复杂度（sequential Rademacher complexity）能够控制策略依赖数据收集下的贝尔曼回归泛化误差。

Result: 推导出了一套关于FQI的有限样本、自适应数据性能界；证明了序列Rademacher复杂度的作用；给出了连续空间下FQI的第一个累积、路径依赖的在线遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文将测度论MDP基础、确定性误差传播分析和PAC泛化界这三个孤立的领域统一起来，为一般Borel空间上的FQI算法提供了有限样本、自适应数据的性能界，并首次给出了连续空间下FQI的累积路径在线遗憾保证，奠定了深度强化学习算法形式化分析的基础。

Abstract: While reinforcement learning (RL) promises to revolutionize the control of complex nonlinear robotic systems, a profound gap persists between the heuristic success of model-free off-policy deep RL and the underlying theory, which remains largely confined to tabular or linearizable settings. We identify the cause of this gap as an emergent isolation of three traditions: (i) measure-theoretic MDP foundations on general spaces limit their analysis to exact dynamic programming and ignore all error sources of a learning process; (ii) deterministic error propagation analysis addresses the approximation error via concentrability coefficients without a finite-sample analysis of the estimation error; and (iii) PAC generalization bounds characterize the estimation errors of simplified topologies. We bridge these traditions with a unified theoretical framework for fitted Q-iteration (FQI) on general measurable Borel spaces. Our main result provides a finite-sample, adaptive-data performance bound by chaining measure-theoretic probability with Bellman-operator contraction in Banach spaces. We prove that sequential Rademacher complexity controls Bellman-regression generalization under policy-dependent data collection. We further extend this analysis to provide the first cumulative, pathwise online regret guarantee for FQI in continuous spaces. These results lay the necessary foundations for the formal analysis of many modern deep RL algorithms.

</details>


### [158] [Revealing Modular Gradient Noise Imbalance in LLMs: Calibrating Adam via Signal-to-Noise Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05794)
*Ziqing Wen,Zhouyang Liu,Jiahuan Wang,Ping Luo,Li Shen,Dongsheng Li,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoLS 是一种基于 SNR 的自动化模块级学习率缩放技术，解决了大模型训练中模块间梯度异质性带来的优化难题，提升了训练效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Adam(W) 优化器缺乏对大模型中模块级梯度异质性的显式建模，导致收敛缓慢或训练不稳定；而人工调整模块学习率的方法既耗时又难以泛化。

Method: 提出了 Module-wise Learning Rate Scaling via SNR (MoLS) 方法。该方法通过估计模块级的信噪比 (SNR) 来动态缩放 Adam 的更新步长，从而实现自动化的模块级学习率分配。

Result: 在多个大模型训练基准测试中，MoLS 方法在无需人工调参的前提下，实现了与精心调整的学习率相当甚至更优的性能，同时保持了与内存高效训练算法的兼容性。

Conclusion: MoLS 提供了一种无需手动调参的模块化学习率缩放方法，不仅显著提升了 LLM 在训练中的收敛速度和泛化能力，且具有良好的兼容性和通用性。

Abstract: The impressive performance of large language models (LLMs) arises from their massive scale and heterogeneous module composition. However, this structural heterogeneity introduces additional optimization challenges. While adaptive optimizers such as Adam(W) provide per-parameter adaptivity, they do not explicitly account for module-level gradient heterogeneity, resulting in slower convergence, suboptimal performance, or training instability. Existing approaches typically rely on manually tuned module-specific learning rates or specific optimization strategies, which are computationally costly and difficult to generalize across tasks or models. To establish a more principled approach, we first analyze the noise-damping behavior of Adam in high-noise modules and introduce \textbf{Module-wise Learning Rate Scaling via SNR (MoLS)}. MoLS estimates module-level SNRs to scale Adam updates, allowing automated module-wise learning rate allocation without manual tuning. Empirical results through multiple LLM training benchmarks demonstrate that MoLS improves convergence speed and generalization, achieving performance comparable to carefully tuned module-specific learning rates, while remaining compatible with memory-efficient training algorithms.

</details>


### [159] [Retrieval from Within: An Intrinsic Capability of Attention-Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05806)
*Elad Hoffer,Yochai Blau,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INTRA 是一种将检索功能内化到编解码器注意力机制中的架构，消除了独立检索模块带来的不匹配问题，并在问答任务中表现卓越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RAG 系统将检索和生成视为独立模块，存在“检索器-生成器不匹配”问题；探究编解码器模型是否能直接从自身内部表示中进行检索。

Method: 提出 INTRA (INTrinsic Retrieval via Attention) 框架，将检索与生成统一在单一的编解码器架构中；利用解码器注意力查询直接对预编码的证据块进行打分，并将其作为生成上下文。

Result: 在问答基准测试中，INTRA 在证据召回率和端到端答案质量上均优于强力的工程化 RAG 流水线，同时实现了上下文编码的摊销（复用预计算的编码状态）。

Conclusion: 注意力机制本身即具备检索能力，无需依赖外部检索模块，通过利用解码器注意力查询内部编码状态即可实现高效的检索增强生成。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically treats retrieval and generation as separate systems. We ask whether an attention-based encoder-decoder can instead retrieve directly from its own internal representations. We introduce INTRA (INTrinsic Retrieval via Attention), a framework where decoder attention queries score pre-encoded evidence chunks that are then directly reused as context for generation. By construction, INTRA unifies retrieval and generation, eliminating the retriever-generator mismatch typical of RAG pipelines. This design also amortizes context encoding by reusing precomputed encoder states across queries. On question-answering benchmarks, INTRA outperforms strong engineered retrieval pipelines on both evidence recall and end-to-end answer quality. Our results demonstrate that attention-based models already possess a retrieval mechanism that can be elicited, rather than added as an external module.

</details>


### [160] [A Testable Certificate for Constant Collapse in Teacher-Guided VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05813)
*Zegu Zhang,Jianhua Peng,Jian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为VAE的后验崩溃问题提供了一个精确的定量判据。通过计算与数据集平均教师分布的对齐损失，作者定义了一个阈值，超过该阈值即可从理论上证明编码器未陷入与输入无关的常量解，解决了传统诊断指标模糊不清的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验崩溃诊断方法（如KL项过小、解码器过强等）大多是基于症状的经验性判断，缺乏严谨的理论边界定义。作者旨在构建一个可测量的、理论上合理的崩溃判据，用以区分模型是真的在利用 latent space，还是仅仅陷入了与输入无关的常量解。

Method: 1. 定义了输入无关的常量崩溃（Input-independent constant collapse）作为核心研究对象；\n2. 推导出了基于教师分布（Teacher distribution）的互信息 $I_T(X;T)$ 作为分类的临界阈值；\n3. 通过构建“潜变量敏感的原始见证（raw witness）”并比较其对齐损失与该阈值的大小，确立了检测崩溃的定量指标；\n4. 在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet-200数据集上，通过预防、破坏、恢复的实验范式验证了该指标的一致性。

Result: 1. 成功建立了一个可量化的崩溃判据，当对齐损失低于特定阈值时，模型必然处于非常量状态；\n2. 实验证明该方法在防止、检测以及从崩溃中恢复VAE模型方面具有高度的有效性；\n3. 证明了传统的VAE基准方法仍无法通过这一严格的定量测试，揭示了这类模型在潜在表示学习上的潜在局限。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一个用于检测VAE变量后验崩溃（Posterior Collapse）的严格定量判据。该方法通过将变分推断中的“输入无关常量崩溃”转化为一个可测量的阈值问题，为评估编码器是否真正利用了潜变量提供了基于信息的理论保证，而不止于观察经验性的关联指标。

Abstract: Posterior collapse in variational autoencoders is often diagnosed by its symptoms: a small KL term, a strong decoder, or weak use of the latent code. These signals are useful, but they do not define a collapse boundary. We study a concrete failure mode, input-independent constant collapse, and show that this case admits an exact threshold. For any fixed nonconstant teacher distribution \(T(\cdot\mid x)\), the best constant student is the dataset-average teacher distribution, and its alignment cost is the teacher mutual information \(I_T(X;T)\). Therefore, if a strictly latent-only raw witness achieves alignment loss below this value, with a safety margin, the witness cannot be constant in the input.
  This identity turns a qualitative failure mode into a measurable one. In CIFAR-100 experiments with per-seed teacher search, full training stays on the certified side of the boundary, removing alignment drives the raw witness into the constant-student regime, and restarting from a collapsed checkpoint with alignment enabled restores the certificate. Tiny-ImageNet-200 fixed-target runs show the same prevention--collapse--rescue pattern across three independently searched teachers. Standard VAE-style baselines, including methods that preserve reconstruction quality or post-hoc predictability, remain negative under the raw certificate. The guarantee is intentionally narrow: it certifies that the matched nonconstant teacher-relative variation passes through the latent pathway, rather than claiming that all forms of posterior collapse have been ruled out.

</details>


### [161] [HCInfer: An Efficient Inference System via Error Compensation for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05819)
*Shen Xu,Xiangwen Zhuge,Zhe Xu,Yingkun Hu,Zheng Yang,Yunhao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型部署痛点，HCInfer 通过将残差补偿分支卸载至 CPU 并进行异步流水线优化，在保障推理速度的同时显著提升了压缩模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型参数量过大导致在消费级硬件上部署困难；现有的模型压缩和卸载方案在精度保持或推理时效性上存在明显瓶颈。

Method: 提出 HCInfer 系统，将 LoRA 风格的残差补偿分支卸载至 CPU，骨干模型在 GPU 上运行；引入异步流水线处理补偿计算，并采用感知模型敏感度的动态秩分配策略来优化精度恢复。

Result: 实验表明，HCInfer 在下游任务上相比单纯模型压缩带来了高达 5.2% 的精度提升，且相比全精度模型实现了最高 10.4 倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: HCInfer 通过异构推理设计，有效解决了大模型在消费级硬件部署中精度与推理速度难以兼顾的矛盾，为部署大规模模型提供了更优的性价比方案。

Abstract: LLMs often struggle with memory-constrained deployment on consumer-grade hardware due to their massive parameter sizes. While existing solutions such as model compression and offloading improve deployment feasibility, they often suffer from substantial accuracy degradation or severe throughput bottlenecks. Recent error compensation methods recover accuracy through auxiliary LoRA-style branches, and we observe that these branches are inherently amenable to offloading: they require substantial parameter storage but access only a small subset of compensation parameters during each inference step. Motivated by this opportunity, we propose HCInfer, a heterogeneous inference system that offloads residual compensation to the CPU while executing the compressed backbone on the GPU, and further introduces an asynchronous compensation pipeline and sensitivity-aware dynamic rank allocation to hide compensation overhead and maximize accuracy recovery. Experimental results show that HCInfer achieves a maximum accuracy improvement of 5.2% on downstream tasks compared to compression model and sustaining a maximum speedup of 10.4x compared to full-precision model.

</details>


### [162] [MDN: Parallelizing Stepwise Momentum for Delta Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05838)
*Yulong Huang,Xiang Liu,Hongxiang Huang,Xiaopeng Lin,Zunchang Liu,Xiaowen Chu,Zeke Xie,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对线性注意力模型中信息衰减与收敛性问题，论文提出了 Momentum DeltaNet (MDN)，通过引入动量规则及系统的稳定性分析，实现了在长序列建模中性能与训练效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的线性注意力模型（如 Mamba2、GDN）将循环归结为在线 SGD，但存在信息快速衰减和收敛性次优的问题。传统的动量优化器虽能改善效果，但在大规模并行训练中难以同时保证效率与性能。

Method: 1. 提出一种基于步进动量准则的分块并行算法（通过几何重排序更新系数）。2. 从动力系统视角分析动量循环，构建二阶系统并推导稳定的门控（gating）约束。3. 利用 Triton 内核优化实现高效训练。

Result: 在 400M 和 1.3B 参数规模下，MDN 在多个基准测试中优于 Transformer、Mamba2 和 GDN 等强基线模型，且保持了极高的训练吞吐量。

Conclusion: Momentum DeltaNet (MDN) 将动量机制引入线性注意力模型，通过几何重排序和二阶动力系统分析解决了信息衰减与训练效率平衡的问题，是高性能高效长序列建模的有效架构。

Abstract: Linear Attention (LA) offers a promising paradigm for scaling large language models (LLMs) to long sequences by avoiding the quadratic complexity of self-attention. Recent LA models such as Mamba2 and GDN interpret linear recurrences as closed-form online stochastic gradient descent (SGD), but naive SGD updates suffer from rapid information decay and suboptimal convergence in optimization. While momentum-based optimizers provide a natural remedy, they pose challenges in simultaneously achieving training efficiency and effectiveness. To address this, we develop a chunkwise parallel algorithm for LA with a stepwise momentum rule by geometrically reordering the update coefficients. Further, from a dynamical systems perspective, we analyze the momentum-based recurrence as a second-order system that introduces complex conjugate eigenvalues. This analysis guides the design of stable gating constraints. The resulting model, Momentum DeltaNet (MDN), leverages Triton kernels to achieve comparable training throughput with competitive linear models such as Mamba2 and KDA. Extensive experiments on the 400M and 1.3B parameter models demonstrate consistent performance improvements over strong baselines, including Transformers, Mamba2 and GDN, across diverse downstream evaluation benchmarks. Code: https://github.com/HuuYuLong/MomentumDeltaNet .

</details>


### [163] [Hypothesis generation and updating in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05851)
*Hua-Dong Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明 LLM 的推理并非真正的贝叶斯最优，虽然在受控环境下表现出类贝叶斯行为，但在处理超出既有数据范围的问题时表现出严重的泛化能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大模型在处理碎片化信息时如何进行假设生成与动态更新，以及其推理模式与最优贝叶斯模型或人类思维的差异，从而评估其作为通用问题解决者的能力上限。

Method: 使用“数字游戏”（Number Game）作为受控实验场景，通过后验预测、假设评估和假设生成三个维度进行探测，并将 LLM 的表现与最优贝叶斯模型及人类行为进行对比。

Result: 1. LLM 表现出明显的强采样偏差（隐式奥卡姆剃刀），偏好窄假设；2. 存在“评估-生成差距”，即评估时更准确，生成时偏好简单规则；3. 贝叶斯偏差模式无法外推至未观察到的数据领域。

Conclusion: LLMs 表现出“带有偏差的贝叶斯推理”模式，但这种模式无法外推。它们在未覆盖的数据领域泛化能力较差，作为通用问题解决者（特别是科学推理任务）存在局限性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly help people solve problems, from debugging code to repairing machinery. This process requires generating plausible hypotheses from partial descriptions, then updating them as more information arrives. Yet how LLMs perform this form of inference, and how close it is to optimal, remains unclear. We study this question in the number game, a controlled setting in which a learner infers the hypothesis supported by a few positive integers, such as $\{16, 8, 2, 64\}$: a rule like powers of 2 or an interval like numbers near 20. We measure the posterior over hypotheses using three complementary probes: posterior prediction, hypothesis evaluation, and hypothesis generation. We then compare LLM behavior with an optimal Bayesian model and human behavior, and test whether the same posterior is expressed across probes. LLMs are often well described by a two-parameter Bayesian fit, but with systematic offsets: by default they show a strong-sampling assumption that creates an implicit Occam's razor, favoring narrower hypotheses, while thinking mode shifts them toward greater prior reliance. We also find a robust evaluation--generation gap: LLMs select more correct hypotheses during hypothesis evaluation but generate simpler, more rule-like hypotheses. Finally, this Bayesian-with-bias pattern does not extrapolate. Models can behave as if they hold rule-like hypotheses over observed examples, yet generalize poorly to parts of the hypothesis domain not covered by those examples. Our results highlight a limitation of LLMs as general problem solvers, especially for scientific inference, where hypotheses must go beyond the data.

</details>


### [164] [Measuring Learning Progress via Gradient-Momentum Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05856)
*Samuel Blad,Martin Längkvist,Amy Loutfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的探索信号（GMC），利用梯度与动量的耦合关系过滤噪声，帮助强化学习智能体更有效地识别可学习的探索目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的好奇心驱动探索方法（如预测误差）难以区分“可学习的模式”与“随机噪声”，导致在复杂环境或高噪声环境下探索效率低下。

Method: 提出了梯度-动量耦合（GMC）指标，通过计算当前样本梯度与历史动量的按参数归一化绝对乘积，衡量样本对持续学习的贡献度。GMC利用动量作为低通滤波器，过滤掉随机噪声和震荡。

Result: 控制实验表明GMC具有良好的抗噪性，并能引发涌现性的课程学习（按学习速率而非难度排序任务）；在MiniGrid环境下的实验证明，将其集成于现有架构可显著提升对观测噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GMC通过量化样本梯度与动量的耦合关系，有效过滤了环境噪声，能够引导智能体进行更高效、目标导向的探索，是比传统预测误差更鲁棒的内在驱动探索信号。

Abstract: Measuring learning progress is essential for curiosity-driven exploration in reinforcement learning, but widely used signals such as prediction error often fail to distinguish meaningful, learnable patterns from random noise. This paper proposes Gradient-Momentum Coupling (GMC), a signal derived from optimization dynamics that quantifies how useful each sample's gradient is for ongoing learning by measuring its per-parameter normalized absolute product with the momentum from previous gradients. By leveraging momentum's natural filtering of noise and oscillations, GMC identifies samples that contribute to ongoing parameter updates. Controlled experiments demonstrate noise robustness and emergent curriculum learning, with the signal prioritizing tasks by learning speed rather than difficulty. Experiments on MiniGrid suggest that replacing prediction error with GMC within existing curiosity-driven architectures can improve robustness to observation noise.

</details>


### [165] [Offline Reinforcement Learning for Rotation Profile Control in Tokamaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05857)
*Rohit Sonker,Hiro Josep Farre Kaga,Jiayu Chen,Andrew Rothstein,Ian Char,Ricardo Shousha,Egemen Kolemen,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过利用DIII-D托卡马克历史实验数据，运用离线强化学习与概率模型仿真，成功实现了等离子体旋转剖面的有效控制，并在真实装置上完成了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托卡马克等离子体旋转剖面的精准控制对稳定性和能量约束至关重要，但由于其高维度、复杂的多执行器响应及动力学建模困难，传统方法难以应对。现有强化学习需要大量模拟数据，但高质量的模拟器匮乏。

Method: 结合离线强化学习（Offline RL）与离线模型辅助强化学习（Offline Model-based RL）。利用历史数据训练概率性动力学模型，进而生成训练序列（rollouts）以支持强化学习策略的学习。

Result: 在DIII-D托卡马克装置上成功部署了训练好的控制策略，并在实际运行中观察到了良好的控制效果。

Conclusion: 离线强化学习是从有限历史数据中学习复杂托卡马克控制策略的可行方案，能够有效应对高维、多输入多输出控制挑战，为实现真实核聚变装置的自动化控制提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Tokamaks remain leading candidates for achieving practical fusion energy, yet many important control problems inside these devices are still difficult or unsolved. One such challenge is controlling the plasma rotation profile, which strongly influences stability, confinement, and transport. While the average rotation can be controlled, controlling the full profile is challenging due to high dimensionality, response to multiple actuators and dependence on plasma condition. Learning-based control methods, such as reinforcement learning (RL), provide a potential solution to this challenging problem with ability to model complex interactions leading to effective multi-input multi-output control. However, learning such policies is challenging due to the lack of accurate simulators that can model the rotation profile dynamics. In this work, we investigate the use of offline RL and offline model-based RL algorithms for rotation profile control, training them solely on historical data from the DIII-D tokamak. Our final method uses probabilistic models of plasma dynamics to generate rollouts for RL training. We deploy this policy on the DIII-D Tokamak and observe promising real-world results. We conclude by highlighting key challenges and insights from training and deploying an RL policy on a complex physical device while using only limited past data.

</details>


### [166] [SOPE: Stabilizing Off-Policy Evaluation for Online RL with Prior Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05863)
*Carlo Romeo,Girolamo Macaluso,Alessandro Sestini,Andrew D. Bagdanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SOPE 是一种通过 OPE 信号自动动态调整在线强化学习中离线训练阶段长度的算法，解决了静态调度需人工调节的痛点，显著提升了效率与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将先验数据纳入在线强化学习的方法在计算成本与训练流程复杂性之间存在权衡，而人工调节的静态更新调度容易导致先验知识浪费或过拟合。

Method: 提出 SOPE 算法。利用策略对齐的离线策略评估（OPE）作为自动提前停止机制，在离线训练阶段动态控制梯度更新，通过在保留验证集上评估当前策略下的指标，在超出分布收益饱和时精确停止更新。

Result: 在 25 个连续控制任务（Minari 基准）中，相比基准方法，性能提升最高达 45.6%，同时降低了最高 22 倍的计算量（TFLOPs）。

Conclusion: 自适应、基于评估的策略更新调度在在线强化学习中优于静态、穷举的调度，能有效平衡样本效率与计算成本。

Abstract: Incorporating prior data into online reinforcement learning accelerates training but typically forces a difficult trade-off between high computational costs and long, multi-stage training pipelines. While fixed-length stabilization phases are significantly more computationally efficient than static update schedules, they require task-dependent manual tuning, risking either the waste of prior knowledge or severe overfitting. To address this, we propose SOPE, an algorithm that uses an actor-aligned Off-Policy Policy Evaluation (OPE) signal as an automated early-stopping mechanism to dynamically control the length of offline training phases. By evaluating the critic on a held-out validation split under the current policy's action distribution, SOPE halts gradient updates exactly when out-of-distribution benefits saturate, eliminating the need for manual schedule tuning. Evaluated on 25 continuous control tasks from the Minari benchmark suite, SOPE improves baseline performance by up to 45.6% while reducing the required TFLOPs by up to 22x, thus balancing the tradeoff between sample and computational efficiency. These findings demonstrate that adaptive, evaluation-driven update schedules are more effective than relying on static, exhaustive update schedules.

</details>


### [167] [QuadraSHAP: Stable and Scalable Shapley Values for Product Games via Gauss-Legendre Quadrature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05870)
*Majid Mohammadi,Grigory Reznikov,Pavel Sinitcyn,Krikamol Muandet,Siu Lun Chau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对乘积博弈的高效Shapley值计算框架QuadraSHAP，通过将指数级求和转化为一维多项式积分，利用高斯-勒让德求积法实现了极高精度的计算，并提供了一种在保持数值稳定性前提下的并行算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Shapley值计算方法在处理具有乘积结构（如核方法、树模型）的机器学习模型时计算复杂度过高，难以应对特征数量众多的场景。

Method: 1. 将Shapley值的指数级求和转化为一维多项式积分；2. 采用高斯-勒让德（Gauss-Legendre）求积法进行离散化处理；3. 开发了对数空间稳定计算方案及基于关联扫描（associative scan）的并行化实现。

Result: 该方法（QuadraSHAP）在特征数量达数千时，仅使用数百个节点即可实现极高精度的近似；在并行环境下具备$O(d\,m_q)$工作量和$O(\log d)$的时间复杂度，性能优于现有所有同类方法。

Conclusion: 针对乘积博弈（product games），利用积分表示和高斯-勒让德求积法实现了Shapley值的高效、精准计算，大幅提升了相关模型解释的可扩展性。

Abstract: We study the efficient computation of Shapley values for \emph{product games} -- cooperative games in which the coalition value factorizes as a product of per-player terms. Such games arise in machine learning explainability whenever the value function inherits a multiplicative structure from the underlying model, as in kernel methods with product kernels and tree-based models. Our key result is that the Shapley value of each player in a product game admits an exact one-dimensional integral representation: the weighted sum over exponentially many feature coalitions collapses to the integral of a degree-$(d-1)$ polynomial over $[0,1]$, where $d$ is the total number of features. This yields a Gauss--Legendre quadrature scheme that is \emph{provably exact} whenever the number of nodes satisfies $m_q \geq \lceil d/2 \rceil$, and otherwise provides a \emph{near-exact} approximation with error provably decaying geometrically in $m_q$. In practice, a few hundred nodes can achieve highly precise estimates even with thousands of features. Building on this formulation, we derive a numerically stable implementation via log-space evaluation, together with an efficient parallel implementation based on associative scan primitives that achieves $O(d\,m_q)$ total work and $O(\log d)$ parallel time. Experiments show that \textsc{QuadraSHAP} is the fastest numerically stable method across all tested configurations.

</details>


### [168] [Retain-Neutral Surrogates for Min-Max Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05871)
*Junhao Cai,Dohun Kim,Dowon Kim,Sung Il Choi,Chengjun Jin,Juhyun Park,Changhee Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对机器遗忘中遗忘操作损害模型保留性能的问题，提出ROSU方法，通过强制保持梯度正交，在消除遗忘数据影响的同时最小化对保留数据的性能损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的近似机器遗忘方法在“遗忘”梯度与“保留”梯度高度对齐时，往往会因为试图最大化遗忘效果而无意中导致保留数据的损失（即增加模型在保留集上的损失）。

Method: 提出了Retain-Orthogonal Surrogate Unlearning (ROSU)方法，通过在固定扰动预算下，最大化“遗忘（forget）”增益，同时强制满足“保留（retain）”的一阶变化为零，从而导出一个闭式解的保留-正交扰动。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、Tiny-ImageNet、TOFU和WMDP等视觉与语言基准测试中，ROSU在梯度高度耦合的场景下显著优于传统方法，在其他场景下也保持了极强的竞争力，并验证了理论上的二阶损失界限。

Conclusion: ROSU在处理“遗忘”和“保留”梯度高度耦合的情况下表现优异，通过正交化处理有效降低了遗忘过程对模型性能的副作用，证明了在保留数据上进行约束优化的有效性。

Abstract: Machine unlearning seeks to remove the influence of designated training data while preserving performance on the remaining data. Approximate unlearning can be viewed as a local editing problem; in min-max unlearning, the key local object is the surrogate point at which the retain objective is evaluated. When forget and retain gradients are strongly aligned, an unconstrained forget-maximizing perturbation can move to a surrogate point that increases retain loss. We propose Retain-Orthogonal Surrogate Unlearning (ROSU), which constrains the inner surrogate construction by maximizing first-order forget gain subject to zero first-order retain change under a fixed perturbation budget. This yields a closed-form retain-orthogonal perturbation, a lightweight transported outer update, and amplification along the retain-neutral direction. Our analysis establishes (i) a curvature-controlled second-order bound on retain damage, (ii) a positive-alignment regime in which ROSU strictly reduces surrogate retain loss relative to standard min-max perturbations, and (iii) near-equivalence when the two gradients are nearly orthogonal. Across vision and language benchmarks (CIFAR-10/100, Tiny-ImageNet, TOFU, WMDP), the empirical pattern follows this geometry: ROSU gives its clearest gains in high-coupling regimes while remaining competitive elsewhere.

</details>


### [169] [RepFlow: Representation Enhanced Flow Matching for Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05890)
*Yifei Xie,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RepFlow 是一种结合了表示学习与条件流匹配（CFM）的新型因果效应估计框架，通过均衡表示学习和分布建模，有效解决了选择偏差并实现了对潜在结果分布的精确捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果推理方法多局限于点估计，且难以对潜在结果的分布进行建模；此外，观测数据中普遍存在的缺失反事实和选择偏差问题限制了估计的准确性。

Method: 提出 RepFlow 框架，通过最小化受处理组与对照组表示之间的熵正则化 Wasserstein 距离来减轻选择偏差，并引入 L2 正则化提升数值稳定性；利用条件流匹配（CFM）对潜在结果的分布进行建模。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，RepFlow 在点估计和分布估计任务中均表现出优于现有基准方法的性能。

Conclusion: RepFlow 通过将表示学习与条件流匹配（CFM）集成，在因果效应的点估计和分布估计任务中均优于现有方法，证明了其处理复杂因果数据的有效性。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data has become increasingly critical in diverse fields including healthcare, economics, and social policy. The fundamental challenge in causal inference arises from the missing counterfactuals and the selection bias. Existing methods are largely limited to point estimates and lack the capacity for distribution modeling. In this work, we propose RepFlow, a novel framework that formulates causal effect estimation as a joint optimization problem integrating representation learning with Conditional Flow Matching (CFM).
  RepFlow mitigates selection bias by minimizing the entropically regularized Wasserstein distance between treated and control representations.
  To enhance numerical stability, we further introduce an $L_2$ normalization constraint on latent representations.
  This balanced representation enables the flow model to accurately capture the distribution of potential outcomes. Extensive experiments across a wide range of benchmarks demonstrate that RepFlow consistently outperforms existing methods in both point and distributional causal effect estimation.

</details>


### [170] [Near-Policy: Accelerating On-Policy Distillation via Asynchronous Generation and Selective Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05940)
*Miao Rang,Zhenni Bi,Hang Zhou,Kai Han,Xuechun Wang,An Xiao,Xinghao Chen,Yunhe Wang,Hanting Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对自回归模型蒸馏中分布偏移与计算开销的矛盾，论文提出NPD方法，通过异步训练与样本过滤机制，在实现高效监督微调的同时保持了接近策略的一致性，显著提升了模型性能与训练速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准知识蒸馏存在分布不匹配问题，而现有在线策略（on-policy）方法虽然能缓解该问题，但依赖昂贵的强化学习框架，计算成本高。需在保持分布一致性的前提下提升训练效率。

Method: 提出近策略蒸馏（Near-Policy Distillation, NPD），采用异步更新机制解耦生成与训练，支持序列打包（Sequence Packing），并引入稀疏学生更新和$\Delta$-IFD过滤机制来稳定优化轨迹、过滤分布外（OOD）样本。

Result: NPD相较于在线策略基线实现了8.1倍的训练速度提升，性能优于传统SFT（提高8.09%），并在openPangu-Embedded-1B上达到68.73%的最优得分，超越了更大的Qwen3-1.7B。

Conclusion: NPD提供了一种高效且高性能的知识蒸馏范式。通过异步机制和$\Delta$-IFD过滤，实现了与在线策略训练相当的效果，同时大幅提升了训练效率，并为后续的强化学习提供了更优的探索起点。

Abstract: Standard knowledge distillation for autoregressive models often suffers from distribution mismatch. While on-policy methods mitigate this by leveraging student-generated outputs, they rely on computationally expensive Reinforcement Learning (RL) frameworks. To improve efficiency, we propose Near-Policy Distillation (NPD), an asynchronous approach that decouples student generation from training. This reformulation enables Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with sequence packing. However, asynchronous updates inevitably introduce policy lag and sample noise, which can cause the behavior to drift from near-policy toward off-policy. To counteract this without sacrificing efficiency, NPD integrates sparse student updates and the $Δ$-IFD filtering mechanism, a heuristic sample selection mechanism that empirically stabilizes the optimization trajectory. By filtering extreme out-of-distribution samples, $Δ$-IFD prevents noise from dominating the gradients, ensuring updates remain within a safe proximal learning zone. Empirically, the NPD framework achieves a 8.1x speedup over on-policy baselines and outperforms SFT by 8.09%. Crucially, by effectively narrowing the exploration space for subsequent RL, our method enables openPangu-Embedded-1B to reach a state-of-the-art score of 68.73%, outperforming the substantially larger Qwen3-1.7B. Codes will be released soon.

</details>


### [171] [VisMMOE: Exploiting Visual-Expert Affinity for Efficient Visual-Language MoE Offloading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05899)
*Cheng Xu,Xiaofeng Hou,Jiacheng Liu,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VisMMoE 是一种专为视觉语言混合专家模型设计的卸载系统，通过视觉标记修剪减少专家访问的随机性，极大提升了模型在内存受限设备上的推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模视觉语言专家混合模型（VL-MoE）在内存受限平台上部署困难，且针对文本优化的现有方案在视觉密集型任务中因标记数量巨大、访问模式不可预测而失效。

Method: 提出了一种结合亲和感知视觉标记压缩（Affinity-aware Token Compression）、专家预测先验（Lookahead Expert Prediction）以及缓存与流水线编排（Cache/Pipeline Orchestration）的系统方案，旨在通过修剪冗余标记来优化专家访问模式，从而提高专家局部性和预取效率。

Result: VisMMoE 在多种架构和基准测试中表现优异，相比当前强基准线，端到端推理性能最高提升了 2.68 倍和 1.61 倍。

Conclusion: VisMMoE 通过利用“视觉-专家亲和性”现象，有效解决了视觉语言混合专家模型（VL-MoE）在内存受限设备上的部署难题，在保持竞争性准确率的同时，显著提升了推理性能。

Abstract: Large-scale vision-language mixture-of-experts (VL-MoE) models provide strong multimodal capability, but efficient deployment on memory-constrained platforms remains difficult. Existing MoE offloading systems are largely designed for text-centric workloads and become much less effective for visual-heavy inputs, where large numbers of visual tokens induce broader and less predictable expert accesses. We present VisMMoE, a VL-MoE offloading system built on a single systems insight: pruning redundant visual tokens can improve offloading not only by reducing computation, but also by reshaping expert demand. We refer to this effect as \textit{visual-expert affinity}: token pruning makes expert accesses more concentrated within layers and more stable across layers, producing a smaller and more predictable expert working set. Guided by this insight, VisMMoE combines affinity-aware token compression, lookahead expert prediction, and cache/pipeline orchestration to improve expert locality and prefetch effectiveness under tight memory budgets. We implement VisMMoE on multiple frameworks and evaluate it on representative VL-MoE models and benchmarks. VisMMoE improves end-to-end inference performance by up to 2.68x and 1.61x, respectively, over strong baselines for today's VL-MoE deployments while maintaining competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [172] [Quadratic Objective Perturbation: Curvature-Based Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05905)
*Daniel Cortild,Coralia Cartis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统差分隐私训练方法依赖梯度有界假设的局限，本文提出一种基于二次型扰动（QOP）的新机制，通过调整曲率实现隐私保护，从而放宽了模型假设，并验证了其在理论与计算层面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性目标扰动（LOP）方法依赖于损失函数梯度有界的强假设，这限制了其在现代机器学习模型中的应用。需要一种更灵活、假设更弱的差分隐私训练机制。

Method: 提出“二次目标扰动”（QOP）方法：通过添加随机二次型项来扰动目标函数。利用该扰动项的谱属性来增强目标函数的强凸性与稳定性，从而控制隐私敏感度。同时研究了近似求解下的隐私保持性，并利用现代分裂方法实现高效算法。

Result: 1. 在插值机制下实现了$(\varepsilon, \delta)$-差分隐私；2. 证明了隐私保证在近似求解情境下依然成立；3. 推导了实证超额风险的效用界；4. 理论和数值实验表明QOP优于LOP。

Conclusion: QOP利用曲率而非梯度界来控制敏感度，从而在插值机制下实现了更弱的假设条件及更优的性能。此外，QOP在非精确解情况下仍能维持隐私，且可高效求解，是优于线性目标扰动（LOP）的方案。

Abstract: Objective perturbation is a standard mechanism in differentially private empirical risk minimization. In particular, Linear Objective Perturbation (LOP) enforces privacy by adding a random linear term, while strong convexity and stability are ensured by an additional deterministic quadratic term. However, this approach requires the strong assumption of bounded gradients of the loss function, which excludes many modern machine learning models. In this work, we introduce Quadratic Objective Perturbation (QOP), which perturbs the objective with a random quadratic form. This perturbation induces strong convexity and enforces stability of the problem through curvature, thereby enabling privacy and allowing sensitivity to be controlled through spectral properties of the perturbation rather than assumptions on the gradients. As a result, we obtain $(\varepsilon, δ)$-differential privacy under weaker assumptions, in the interpolation regime. Furthermore, we extend the analysis to account for approximate solutions, showing that privacy guarantees are preserved under inexact solves. Additionally, we derive utility guarantees in terms of empirical excess risk, and provide a theoretical and numerical comparison to LOP, highlighting the advantages of curvature-based perturbations. Finally, we discuss algorithmic aspects and show that the resulting problems can be solved efficiently using modern splitting schemes.

</details>


### [173] [From Drops to Grid: Noise-Aware Spatio-Temporal Neural Process for Rainfall Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05912)
*Rafael Pablos Sarabia,Joachim Nyborg,Morten Birk,Ira Assent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DropsToGrid 是一种利用神经过程融合雷达与稀疏观测数据的高分辨率降雨空间插值模型，不仅提升了预测精度，还能实现不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统降雨观测分辨率低、偏差大，以及现有深度学习降雨密度增强方法难以处理降雨空间偏态分布、噪声及 spatio-temporal 融合受限等挑战。

Method: 提出 DropsToGrid 框架，采用基于神经过程的架构；核心技术包括多尺度特征提取、时间注意力机制（temporal attention）以及多模态融合，实现对稀疏、噪声数据与空间上下文的整合。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，DropsToGrid 在预测精度上超越了现有的业务方法和深度学习基线，并能提供校准良好的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: DropsToGrid 是一种基于神经过程（Neural Process）的有效方法，能够通过融合多源异构数据（私人站点与雷达）生成高质量、带有不确定性量化的降雨精细化地图，且在数据稀缺和跨区域场景下具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: High-resolution rainfall observations are crucial for weather forecasting, water management, and hazard mitigation. Traditional operational measurements are often biased and low-resolution, limiting their ability to capture local rainfall. Accurate high-resolution rainfall maps require integrating sparse surface observations, yet existing deep learning densification methods are hindered by rainfall's skewed, localized nature, noise, and limited spatio-temporal fusion. We present DropsToGrid, a Neural Process-based method that generates dense rainfall fields by fusing temporal sequences from noisy, irregularly distributed private weather stations with spatial context from radar. Leveraging multi-scale feature extraction, temporal attention, and multi-modal fusion, the model produces stochastic, continuous rainfall estimates and explicitly quantifies uncertainty. Evaluations on real-world datasets demonstrate that DropsToGrid outperforms both operational and deep learning baselines, generating accurate high-resolution rainfall maps with well-calibrated uncertainty, even when only few stations are available and in cross-regional scenarios.

</details>


### [174] [Knowing but Not Correcting: Routine Task Requests Suppress Factual Correction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05957)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Lin,Zizhe Chen,Yizhou Tian,Garry Yang,Depeng Wang,Ya Guo,Huijia Zhu,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型常因盲目顺从任务指令而忽视事实错误（纠错抑制）。本文通过机制分析发现其本质是响应选择阶段的偏重问题，并提出CDS和DPA两种无训练干预手段，成功提升了模型在上下文干扰下的事实严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在孤立呈现虚假声明时能正确纠正，但在任务导向的上下文中往往会为了“顺从”任务目标而忽略或配合这些虚假声明，作者将这种失效模式称为“纠错抑制”（correction suppression）。

Method: 1. 构建了包含300个错误前提的基准测试集；2. 进行了机制分析，揭示纠错抑制发生于响应选择阶段（而非知识编码）；3. 提出两种训练无关（training-free）干预方法：纠错方向引导（CDS）和动态负载放大（DPA）。

Result: 1. 发现纠错抑制在主流模型中普遍存在（抑制率19%-90%）；2. CDS在Qwen3.5-9B上将纠错率从0%显著提升至58.2%；3. DPA在纠错的同时，能有效保持甚至提升模型的推理能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出了“事实严谨性”（factual strictness）作为评估模型可靠性的新维度，并证明通过干预中间层激活或注意力机制，可以在不损失推理能力的前提下有效缓解纠错抑制现象。

Abstract: LLMs reliably correct false claims when presented in isolation, yet when the same claims are embedded in task-oriented requests, they often comply rather than correct. We term this failure mode \emph{correction suppression} and construct a benchmark of 300 false premises to systematically evaluate it across eight models. Suppression rates range from 19\% to 90\%, with four models exceeding 80\%, establishing correction suppression as a prevalent and severe phenomenon. Mechanistic analysis reveals that suppression is not a knowledge failure: the model registers the error internally but task context diverts early-layer attention from the false claim as output intent crystallizes toward compliance at middle layers. We characterize this as \emph{knowing but not correcting} -- suppression occurs at response selection rather than knowledge encoding. Guided by this mechanism, we propose two training-free interventions. Correction Direction Steering (CDS) estimates a correction-compliance direction from matched pairs and injects it at middle layers before output intent crystallizes. Dynamic Payload Amplification (DPA) localizes payload tokens via attention divergence between early and late layers and amplifies their representation at the final layer, requiring no calibration data. Experiments on Qwen3.5-9B and LLaMA3.1-8B show both methods substantially improve factual strictness. CDS achieves the highest correction rate on Qwen3.5-9B (0\%$\to$58.2\%). DPA is the only method that preserves or improves reasoning capability on both models. These findings introduce \emph{factual strictness} -- the willingness to uphold accuracy against contextual pressures -- as a new dimension of model reliability.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Uniform Credit Assignment: Selective Eligibility Traces for RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05965)
*Chaoli Mou,Zhan Zhuang,Xinning Chen,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决无评价器 LLM 强化学习中信用分配粗糙的问题，本研究提出了 S-trace。通过引入选择性资格迹机制，精细化分配推理步骤的重要性，从而在各种规模的模型上显著提升了推理性能与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型强化学习方法（如 GRPO）缺乏评价器（Critic），过度依赖“统一信用分配”假设，即对整条推理轨迹赋予相同的优势，这导致模型无法区分关键的推理步骤，严重影响了学习效率。

Method: 提出了一种基于部分信任区域保留的资格迹方法：首先构建 P-trace（样本高效的无评价器资格迹），在此基础上进一步开发了 S-trace。S-trace 通过利用低熵 Token 的过滤机制（选择性掩蔽）来实现稀疏的资格迹，从而实现细粒度的信用分配。此外，研究从理论上将 GSPO 统一到 eligibility traces 框架下并作为其特例。

Result: S-trace 在 Qwen3 系列模型（1.7B, 4B, 8B）上的平均 pass@16 指标均优于 GRPO（提升幅度分别为 0.49%、3.16%、2.98%），并且在训练过程中达到了更高的采样效率和 Token 利用效率。

Conclusion: S-trace 是针对 GRPO 等无评价器（critic-free）强化学习算法的一种有效改进。它通过引入选择性资格迹实现了更细粒度的信用分配，在提升大语言模型推理能力的同时，显著提高了采样和 Token 使用效率，并能更好适应不同规模的模型。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become a key approach for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. However, widely used critic-free algorithms such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) necessitate a ``uniform credit assignment'' assumption that indiscriminately broadcast trajectory-level advantages, hindering learning efficiency by failing to distinguish critical reasoning steps. To address this limitation, we propose Selective Eligibility Traces (S-trace). Grounded in the intuition of partial trust region preservation, we initially introduce P-trace as a sample-efficient, critic-free eligibility traces method, upon which we build S-trace, implementing a sparse eligibility traces mechanism to further mitigate variance and achieve fine-grained credit assignment by selectively masking low-entropy tokens. Theoretically, we contextualize the recent Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO) method within the critic-free eligibility traces framework, identifying it as a special instance of the eligibility traces method operating under uniform credit assignment. Experiments demonstrate that S-trace not only outperforms GRPO, showing gains of 0.49\% on Qwen3-1.7B and 3.16\% on Qwen3-4B, and maintaining a robust 2.98\% improvement when scaled further to Qwen3-8B in average pass@16, but notably achieves this with simultaneously higher sample and token efficiency.

</details>


### [176] [Training Transformers for KV Cache Compressibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05971)
*Yoav Gelberg,Yam Eitan,Michael Bronstein,Yarin Gal,Haggai Maron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长上下文模型中 KV 缓存的瓶颈，提出 KV-CAT 训练方法。通过在预训练过程中强制模型进行 KV 稀疏化，引导模型主动学习更易于后续压缩的表征，从而突破了传统后验压缩方法的性能限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 KV 缓存压缩方法受限于预训练模型既有的内部表征，存在“上限”。作者旨在从表征学习的角度出发，解决长上下文语言模型中 KV 缓存带来的内存和计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出了 KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT)。这是一种持续预训练程序，引入了训练时的 KV 稀疏化策略（在训练中掩盖 KV 插槽），强制模型在较少的 KV 插槽下表现良好，从而引导模型学习到更易于进行后验压缩的表征。

Result: 实验表明，KV-CAT 在检索、长上下文问答以及困惑度评估等任务中，显著改善了下游压缩方法的性能与计算预算之间的权衡（quality-budget tradeoff）。

Conclusion: KV-CAT 证明了通过在训练期间主动诱导模型学习可压缩的表征，可以显著提升后续 KV 缓存压缩技术的效果。本研究表明，压缩性并非上下文固有的属性，而是可以通过训练范式进行优化。

Abstract: Long-context language modeling is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value (KV) cache, whose memory and decode-time access costs scale linearly with the prefix length. This bottleneck has motivated a range of context-compression methods, from token-level summarization to recent optimization-based KV compression methods. These post-hoc methods operate on the KV cache of a fixed pretrained model, so their effectiveness is fundamentally limited by how well the model's internal representations can be compressed. In this work, we formalize the notion of KV compressibility and show that it is a property of the learned representations, rather than of the context alone. We prove that almost any sequence-to-vector function admits both highly compressible and inherently non-compressible transformer implementations, highlighting the need to guide transformers toward compressible representations during training. Motivated by this, we propose KV-Compression Aware Training (KV-CAT), a continued pretraining procedure that incentivizes the emergence of compressible representations. We introduce a train-time KV sparsification policy that masks KV slots during training. This forces the model to use fewer KV slots and encourages it to learn representations amenable to post-hoc compression. Empirically, we show that KV-CAT improves the quality-budget tradeoff of downstream compression methods across retrieval, long-context question answering, and perplexity-based evaluation of compressed-prefix continuation.

</details>


### [177] [Is Escalation Worth It? A Decision-Theoretic Characterization of LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06350)
*Dylan Bouchard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于约束优化理论分析了LLM级联的成本-质量权衡，发现级联的性能瓶颈在于“先支付廉价模型成本”的结构性缺陷，而非级联阶段的数量，且成对级联的包络线往往已是此类策略的最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型级联方法通常将延迟阈值视为经验超参数，缺乏对模型池在成本与质量权衡中所构成的几何形态（成本-质量前沿）的深入理论指导。

Method: 提出了一种基于约束优化和对偶理论的决策论框架。在两模型级联中证明了成本-质量前沿的分段凹性，并建立了影子价格与预算/质量约束间的联系。通过表征 $k$ 个模型下的成对级联最优包络，推导出多阶段级联的均衡边界条件。在五个基准测试和八个模型上验证了该框架。

Result: 在确定性阈值级联中，全链条结构表现不如成对级联的包络线；优化后的子序列级联在留一数据集上无显著增益；轻量级路由在4/5的数据集上超过了最佳级联策略。

Conclusion: 模型级联的性能主要受限于其“结构性成本”（即必须先支付廉价模型的生成成本），而非中间阶段的匮乏。两模型级联的成对组合包络线已接近该类策略的性能上限，进一步增加阶段数带来的边际收益有限。轻量级路由方案表现更好是因为其避免了不必要的初始阶段成本。

Abstract: Model cascades, in which a cheap LLM defers to an expensive one on low-confidence queries, are widely used to navigate the cost-quality tradeoff at deployment. Existing approaches largely treat the deferral threshold as an empirical hyperparameter, with limited guidance on the geometry of the resulting cost-quality frontier over a model pool. We develop a decision-theoretic framework grounded in constrained optimization and duality. For a two-model cascade, we establish piecewise concavity of the cost-quality frontier on decreasing-benefit regions of the confidence support, with reciprocal shadow prices linking the budget- and quality-constrained formulations. Given a pool of $k$ models, we characterize the frontier achievable by deterministic two-model threshold cascades as the pointwise envelope over $\binom{k}{2}$ pairwise cascades, with switching points where the optimal pair changes. For $k$-model cascades, we derive first-order conditions in which a single shadow price equalizes marginal quality-per-cost across stage boundaries. We validate the framework on five benchmarks (MATH, MMLU, TriviaQA, SimpleQA, LiveCodeBench) across eight models from five providers. Within the deterministic threshold-cascade class, full fixed chains underperform the pairwise envelope, and optimized subsequence cascades do not deliver practically meaningful held-out gains over it. A lightweight pre-generation router exceeds the best cascade policy on four of five datasets, mainly because it avoids the cheap model's generation cost on queries sent directly to a larger model rather than because of a stronger routing signal. These results suggest that cascade performance is limited primarily by structural cost, since cascades pay the cheap model before any escalation decision, rather than by a shortage of intermediate stages.

</details>


### [178] [Physical Fidelity Reconstruction via Improved Consistency-Distilled Flow Matching for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05975)
*Sicheng Ma,Tianyue Yang,Xiuzhe Wu,Xiao Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将高保真流匹配模型通过蒸馏技术压缩为一步推断的一致性模型的方法，实现了科学流场重建的极速推理（12倍加速），且显著优于直接训练的基准，为高效、低成本的科学生成模型部署提供了新方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散和流匹配模型依赖迭代采样，在科学机器学习的实时仿真、集成预报及工程应用中计算成本过高，无法满足时延敏感型工作流的需求。

Method: 提出了一种将最优传输流匹配（Optimal-Transport Flow-Matching）教师模型蒸馏为一步（one-step）一致性模型的方法；通过从沿传输路径的加噪观测值初始化生成轨迹，实现无监督教师模型的条件重建，无需重新训练即可处理低保真观测信息。

Result: 在三个流体动力学基准测试中，蒸馏模型相比流匹配教师模型，参数量减少约一半，推理速度提升12倍，且在频谱指标上表现接近；相比直接从头训练的一步一致性模型，SSIM指标提升了23.1%。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过蒸馏将高保真流匹配模型压缩为一步一致性模型是实现高效科学流重建的有效路径，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了推理速度和训练效率。

Abstract: Reconstructing high-fidelity flow fields from low-fidelity observations is a central problem in scientific machine learning, yet recent diffusion and flow-matching models typically rely on iterative sampling, making them costly for latency-sensitive workflows such as ensemble forecasting, real-time visualization, and simulation-in-the-loop inference. We study whether a high-fidelity flow-matching generative model can be compressed into a compact one-step model for fast scientific flow reconstruction. Our approach distills an optimal-transport flow-matching teacher into a one-step consistency model. Low-fidelity observations are incorporated at inference by initializing the generative trajectory from a noised observation along the transport path, allowing an unconditional high-fidelity flow model to perform conditional reconstruction without retraining the teacher. We evaluate this distillation strategy on three fluid benchmarks, Smoke Buoyancy, Turbulent Channel Flow, and Kolmogorov Flow, using coarse-to-fine reconstruction as a controlled testbed at field sizes up to $256 \times 256$. Across these settings, the distilled student retains similar performance of the teacher's model on spectrum metrics, while using roughly half as many parameters and achieving a $12\times$ inference speedup over the flow-matching teacher. Under the same training budget, the distilled student also outperforms a one-step consistency model trained directly from scratch by $23.1\%$ in SSIM, showing that teacher distillation improves training efficiency rather than merely accelerating sampling. These results suggest a promising route for turning future high-capacity scientific generative models into compact reconstruction models that are faster to train, cheaper to run, and easier to deploy.

</details>


### [179] [E = T*H/(O+B): A Dimensionless Control Parameter for Mixture-of-Experts Ecology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06415)
*Qingjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究引入了一个无量纲参数 E，用于预测 MoE 模型中专家生态的健康状况，仅靠 E >= 0.5 即可消除专家塌陷，取代了繁琐的负载均衡辅助损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型在训练中容易出现“专家塌陷”（Dead Experts）问题，且现有的负载均衡辅助损失函数往往需要手动精心调节，缺乏理论指导。研究旨在寻找一种能预测并确保专家生态健康的通用诊断指标。

Method: 通过 12 个受控实验（8 个视觉，4 个语言任务，共 11,000 多个训练 epoch），在 CIFAR-10/100、TinyImageNet-200、WikiText-2/103 等数据集上进行跨模态验证，构建了无量纲控制参数 E = T*H/(O+B)。

Result: 1. 验证了 E >= 0.5 即可保证没有死专家；2. 揭示了死专家可被唤醒、正交毒性与数据集相关、任务复杂度改变临界阈值、过拟合与生态健康解耦等 6 项附加发现。

Conclusion: 提出了参数 E 作为 MoE 模型专家生态健康的统一诊断指标，类比流体力学中的雷诺数。实验证实 E >= 0.5 是避免“专家死亡”的充分条件，无需额外设计负载均衡损失函数。

Abstract: We introduce E = T*H/(O+B), a dimensionless control parameter that predicts whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models will develop a healthy expert ecology or collapse into dead experts. E combines four hyperparameters -- routing temperature T, routing entropy weight H, oracle weight O, and balance weight B -- into a single quantity. Through 12 controlled experiments (8 vision, 4 language) totaling over 11,000 training epochs, we establish that E >= 0.5 alone is sufficient to guarantee zero dead experts, removing the necessity for handcrafted load-balancing auxiliary losses. We validate this cross-modally on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet-200, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. Six additional findings emerge: (1) dead experts can resuscitate -- triggered by balance loss driving router re-exploration; (2) ortho toxicity is dataset-dependent, not universal; (3) task complexity shifts the critical E threshold; (4) model overfitting is decoupled from expert ecological health; (5) three-tier MoE spontaneously collapses into a two-tier functional structure; (6) ecological structure is temperature-invariant across a 50x range. We propose that E serves as a unified diagnostic for MoE training, analogous to the Reynolds number in fluid dynamics.

</details>


### [180] [Towards Steering without Sacrifice: Principled Training of Steering Vectors for Prompt-only Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05983)
*Yuntai Bao,Qinfeng Li,Xinyan Yu,Xuhong Zhang,Ge Su,Wenqi Zhang,Liu Yan,Haiqin Weng,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为提升大模型转向向量（SV）的易用性与效果，本文提出了联合训练方向与因子的方案，并引入仅干预 Prompt 的 PrOSV 方法，成功解决了参数依赖与过度干预问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微调转向向量（SV）的方法存在两大痛点：1. 需要针对每个 SV 手工调整转向因子以平衡效果与质量；2. 全序列干预（FSSV）会对模型生成产生过度干扰，折损生成质量。

Method: 1. 提出联合训练方案：将转向方向与转向因子共同训练，免除推理时的后验因子选择；利用神经缩放理论确定初始化与学习率。2. 提出 Prompt-only SV (PrOSV)：仅在 Prompt 阶段施加干扰，避免对生成全过程的过度干预。

Result: 在 AxBench 基准测试中，PrOSV 在联合训练方案下的表现优于传统的 FSSV，并且在保持模型通用性能与提升对抗稳健性方面取得了更佳的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出的联合训练方案与仅在提示词阶段进行干预的 PrOSV 方法，有效解决了传统转向向量（SV）在推理时需要手工调整超参数以及过度干预模型生成过程的问题，在提升模型对抗稳健性的同时，更好地平衡了模型效用。

Abstract: Recently, steering vectors (SVs) have emerged as an effective and lightweight approach to steer behaviors of large language models (LLMs), among which fine-tuned SVs are more effective than optimization-free ones. However, current approaches to fine-tuned SVs suffer from two limitations. First, they require careful selection of steering factors on a per-SV basis to balance steering effectiveness and generation quality at inference time. Second, they operate as full-sequence SVs (FSSVs), which can sacrifice generation quality regardless of factor selection due to excessive intervention on the model generation process. To address the first limitation, we propose joint training of steering factors and directions, such that post-hoc factor selection is no longer required. Using neural network scaling theory, we find that moderately large initialization sizes and learning rates for steering factors are essential for stability and efficiency of joint training. To tackle the second limitation, we draw inspiration from representation fine-tuning and introduce Prompt-only SV (PrOSV), an SV that intervenes only on a few prompt tokens. Our empirical results show that PrOSV outperforms traditional FSSVs on AxBench when using our joint training scheme. We also find that PrOSV achieves a better tradeoff between general model utility and adversarial robustness than FSSV.

</details>


### [181] [Invariant Features in Language Models: Geometric Characterization and Model Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06458)
*Agnibh Dasgupta,Abdullah Tanvir,Xin Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个几何框架来解释语言模型如何通过潜在空间中的“不变子空间”实现对不同释义的语义理解，并开发了对比子空间发现方法，成功应用于模型归因任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型对于释义（paraphrasing）表现出极强的鲁棒性，暗示其内部存在某种稳定的语义表示机制，但这种不变性（invariance）的具体结构和成因尚不明确。

Method: 1. 提出局部几何框架，将语义一致的输入定义为潜在空间中的结构化区域；2. 引入对比子空间发现方法（Contrastive Subspace Discovery），用于区分语义改变与语义保持的特征；3. 通过表示层面的干预实验验证不变组件的因果作用；4. 利用不变表示中的几何模式进行零样本模型归因。

Result: 1. 发现不变结构通常出现在特定的模型深度区域；2. 语义偏移主要位于“干扰子空间”（nuisance subspace）之外；3. 实验表明不变组件对模型输出具有因果影响；4. 不变表示捕获了模型特定的几何模式，能够实现高精度的零样本模型归因。

Conclusion: 语言模型中的语义不变性可以理解为潜在空间中的一种局部几何属性。该研究为理解模型如何组织语义提供了原则性的框架，并证明了语义不变表示在模型归因任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: Language models exhibit strong robustness to paraphrasing, suggesting that semantic information may be encoded through stable internal representations, yet the structure and origin of such invariance remain unclear. We propose a local geometric framework in which semantically equivalent inputs occupy structured regions in latent space, with paraphrastic variation along nuisance directions and semantic identity preserved in invariant subspaces. Building on this view, we make three contributions: (1) a geometric characterization of invariant latent features, (2) a contrastive subspace discovery method that separates semantic-changing from semantic-preserving variation, and (3) an application of invariant representations to zero-shot model attribution. Across models and layers, empirical results support these contributions. Invariant structure emerges in specific depth regions, semantic displacement lies largely outside the nuisance subspace, and representation-level interventions indicate a causal role of invariant components in model outputs. Invariant representations also capture model-specific geometric patterns, enabling accurate attribution. These findings suggest that semantic invariance can be viewed as a local geometric property of latent representations, offering a principled perspective on how language models organize meaning.

</details>


### [182] [A Fine-Grained Understanding of Uniform Convergence for Halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06004)
*Aryeh Kontorovich,Kasper Green Larsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究精细化分析了半空间的一致收敛行为，发现非齐次半空间受限于 VC 界，而二维齐次半空间表现出显著不同的、更优的收敛特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越最坏情况的 VC 维分析，旨在探究半空间在不同维度和结构条件下细粒度的一致收敛行为，揭示标准 VC 界在某些场景下的紧致性，以及在特定几何结构下的改进潜力。

Method: 理论分析与收敛界证明。利用关键楔形局部化（critical-wedge localization）技术分析不可知情形下的风险带；通过构造下界证明 $\ln\ln n$ 开销的不可避免性。

Result: 1. 非齐次半空间（$d \ge 2$）：VC 界本质上是紧的，可实现情形误差为 $\Theta(d\ln(n/d)/n)$，不可知情形偏差为 $\sqrt{\tau\ln(1/\tau)}$。2. 二维齐次半空间：可实现情形误差为 $O(1/n)$；不可知情形通过关键楔形局部化证明了对数无损界，联合边界导致了 $\ln\ln n$ 的不可避免开销。

Conclusion: 半空间一致收敛性表现出明显的维度和结构阈值。非齐次半空间受限于一阶 VC 界，而二维齐次半空间在可实现情形下表现出 $O(1/n)$ 的极快收敛速度，在不可知情形下表现出对数无损的强收敛特性。

Abstract: We study the fine-grained uniform convergence behavior of halfspaces beyond worst-case VC bounds. For inhomogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^d$ with $d\ge 2$, we show that standard first-order VC bounds are essentially tight: even consistent hypotheses can incur population error $Θ(d\ln(n/d)/n)$, and in the agnostic setting the deviation scales as $\sqrt{τ\ln(1/τ)}$ at true error $τ$. In contrast, homogeneous halfspaces in $\mathbb{R}^2$ exhibit a markedly different behavior. In the realizable case, every hypothesis consistent with the sample has error $O(1/n)$. In the agnostic case, we prove a bandwise, log-free deviation bound on each dyadic risk band via a critical-wedge localization argument. Unioning over bands incurs only a $\ln\ln n$ overhead, and we establish a matching lower bound showing this overhead is unavoidable. Together, these results give a fine-grained and nearly complete picture of uniform convergence for halfspaces, revealing sharp dimensional and structural thresholds.

</details>


### [183] [Cubit: Token Mixer with Kernel Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06501)
*Chuanyang Zheng,Jiankai Sun,Yihang Gao,Yuehao Wang,Liangchen Tan,Mac Schwager,Anderson Schneider,Yuriy Nevmyvaka,Xiaodong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核岭回归（KRR）的新型架构 Cubit，它通过优化注意力机制的数学逻辑并引入训练稳定性技术，显著增强了模型在长序列任务中的建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Transformer 架构在不断演进，但其核心的 token 混合机制（注意力机制）在数学本质上仍是 Nadaraya-Watson 回归，作者旨在通过引入更先进的统计学方法（核岭回归）来改进现有架构的数学建模能力。

Method: 1. 将 Transformer 的注意力机制重新解释为 Nadaraya-Watson 回归。2. 提出 Cubit 架构：用核岭回归（KRR）替代标准的注意力计算，利用核相似度进行价值聚合，并通过核矩阵的逆进行归一化。3. 引入“有限范围重缩放”（Limited-Range Rescale, LRR）技术，以保证训练的稳定性。

Result: 实验表明，Cubit 在长序列建模任务中展现出比 Transformer 更强的性能，且模型优势会随着训练序列长度的增加而进一步扩大。

Conclusion: Cubit 架构通过引入核岭回归 (KRR) 提供了一个比 Transformer 的 Nadaraya-Watson 回归更坚实的数学基础。实验证明，该模型在长序列任务中表现出色，且随着序列长度增加，性能提升愈发显著，是具有潜力的下一代架构。

Abstract: Since its introduction in 2017, the Transformer has become one of the most widely adopted architectures in modern deep learning. Despite extensive efforts to improve positional encoding, attention mechanisms, and feed-forward networks, the core token-mixing mechanism in Transformers remains attention. In this work, we show that the attention module in Transformers can be interpreted as performing Nadaraya-Watson regression, where it computes similarities between tokens and aggregates the corresponding values accordingly. Motivated by this perspective, we propose Cubit, a potential next-generation architecture that leverages Kernel Ridge Regression (KRR), while the vanilla Transformer relies on Nadaraya-Watson regression. Specifically, Cubit modifies the classical attention computation by incorporating the closed-form solution of KRR, combining value aggregation through kernel similarities with normalization via the inverse of the kernel matrix. To improve the training stability, we further propose the Limited-Range Rescale (LRR), which rescales the value layer within a controlled range. We argue that Cubit, as a KRR-based architecture, provides a stronger mathematical foundation than the vanilla Transformer, whose attention mechanism corresponds to Nadaraya-Watson regression. We validate this claim through comprehensive experiments. The experimental results suggest that Cubit may exhibit stronger long-sequence modeling capability. In particular, its performance gain over the Transformer appears to increase as the training sequence length grows.

</details>


### [184] [PairAlign: A Framework for Sequence Tokenization via Self-Alignment with Applications to Audio Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06582)
*Adhiraj Banerjee,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PairAlign 是一种新型音频标记化框架，通过序列级自对齐替代传统的局部量化，实现了更紧凑、语境一致性更好且更适合推理任务的符号化音频表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频标记化方法主要依赖局部的量化或聚类，难以直接优化序列一致性、紧凑度、长度控制及编辑相似度，导致生成的离散结构在处理推理和检索任务时存在局限。

Method: PairAlign 将音频标记化建模为条件序列生成任务；采用自回归解码器学习标记序列，利用双视图（Content-preserving views）进行交叉对齐训练；集成了EMA教师目标、交叉对齐教师强制（Cross-paired teacher forcing）、前缀破坏（Prefix corruption）、似然对比（Likelihood contrast）和长度控制等技术。

Result: 在3秒语音任务中，PairAlign 生成了紧凑且非退化的序列；在TIMIT检索任务中，在保持编辑距离搜索精度的同时，将归档标记数量减少了55%；对比传统地理几何标记器，具备更强的长度控制和稳定的编辑轨迹。

Conclusion: PairAlign 是一种序列符号预测学习框架，它通过序列级自对齐在音频表征中实现了高效的离散化，在保持内容一致性的同时优化了符号序列的结构特性，为离散化音频任务提供了优于传统量化方法的替代方案。

Abstract: Many operations on sensory data -- comparison, memory, retrieval, and reasoning -- are naturally expressed over discrete symbolic structures. In language this interface is given by tokens; in audio, it must be learned. Existing audio tokenizers rely on quantization, clustering, or codec reconstruction, assigning tokens locally, so sequence consistency, compactness, length control, termination, and edit similarity are rarely optimized directly.
  We introduce PairAlign, a framework for compact audio tokenization through sequence-level self-alignment. PairAlign treats tokenization as conditional sequence generation: an encoder maps speech to a continuous condition, and an autoregressive decoder generates tokens from BOS, learning token identity, order, length, and EOS placement. Given two content-preserving views, each view's sequence is trained to be likely under the other's representation, while unrelated examples provide competing sequences. This gives a scalable surrogate for edit-distance preservation while discouraging many-to-one collapse.
  PairAlign starts from VQ-style tokenization and refines it with EMA-teacher targets, cross-paired teacher forcing, prefix corruption, likelihood contrast, and length control.
  On 3-second speech, PairAlign learns compact, non-degenerate sequences with broad vocabulary usage and strong cross-view consistency.
