On TIMIT retrieval, it preserves edit-distance search while reducing archive token count by 55%. A continuous-sweep probe shows lower local overlap than a dense geometric tokenizer, but stronger length control and bounded edit trajectories under 100 ms shifts. PairAlign is a sequence-symbolic predictive learner: like JEPA-style objectives, it predicts an abstract target from another view as a learned variable-length symbolic sequence, not a continuous latent.

</details>


### [185] [Matrix-Decoupled Concentration for Autoregressive Sequences: Dimension-Free Guarantees for Sparse Long-Context Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06017)
*Pei-Sen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种 MDC 框架，解决了 autoregressive LLM 评估中因依赖结构导致的方差膨胀问题，通过严谨的因果数学分析实现了最优化的集中不等式界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型（LLM）序列级评估存在两个瓶颈：一是经典不等式导致的“标量坍缩”，使得稀疏奖励的方差估计过于保守；二是现有空间分析法缺乏因果过滤（causal filtration），无法直接应用于自回归生成过程。

Method: 提出了一种矩阵解耦集中（Matrix-Decoupled Concentration, MDC）框架。该方法通过因果依赖解算子（causal dependency resolvent）与目标敏感度向量的矩阵-向量乘积，推导出针对依赖序列的尖锐 McDiarmid 型不等式，并利用有向 d-分离技术处理因果树结构。

Result: MDC 框架实现了维度无关（dimension-free）的 $\mathcal{O}(1)$ 方差估计，同时对马尔可夫链实现了最优常数界，对因果树实现了阶最优（order-optimal）界，显著提升了对自回归过程的评估准确性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的 MDC 框架通过精确捕捉因果依赖结构，成功避免了自回归模型评估中的“标量坍缩”问题，为长上下文推理的稳定性提供了严谨的数学证明。

Abstract: Sequence-level evaluations in autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs) rely on highly dependent token generation. Establishing tight concentration bounds for these processes remains a challenge due to two fundamental bottlenecks in existing frameworks: (i) classical inequalities typically separate dependency structures from target sensitivities, leading to a scalar collapse that inflates the variance proxy to a suboptimal $\mathcal{O}(N)$ for sparse terminal rewards; (ii) conversely, while certain spatial methods achieve tighter bounds, they lack the strictly causal filtration required by sequential generation, rendering them inapplicable to the autoregressive setting. To resolve both bottlenecks, we establish a sharp McDiarmid-type inequality for dependent sequences, governed strictly by the exact matrix-vector multiplication of the causal dependency resolvent and the target sensitivity vector. This Matrix-Decoupled Concentration (MDC) framework natively recovers optimal constants for Markov chains and exploits directed $d$-separation to yield order-optimal bounds for causal trees. Crucially, by exactly preserving the coordinate-wise sparsity of rewards within a strictly causal framework, MDC mathematically prevents scalar collapse, guaranteeing a dimension-free $\mathcal{O}(1)$ variance proxy and providing a rigorous mathematical justification for the stability of long-context reasoning.

</details>


### [186] [Multi-agent decision making: A Blackwell's informativeness approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06028)
*Zheng Zhang,Cuong C. Nguyen,Kevin Wells,Gustavo Carneiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Blackwell信息论框架分析多LLM决策，指出投票和辩论的信息效率上限，并提出了一种基于贝叶斯后验估计的协作决策方法，在问答任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多大模型（LLM）决策方法（如投票、辩论）缺乏正式的理论保障，且缺乏对决策信息量的量化分析。

Method: 1. 利用Blackwell信息论框架分析多智能体决策。2. 提出一种基于“后验乘积估计器”（product-of-posteriors estimator）的实用决策方法，用于近似贝叶斯池化后验。

Result: 在六个问答基准测试上，该方法在性能上优于当前最先进的多LLM辩论和投票方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入基于贝叶斯池化后验估计的决策规则，该方法在理论上达到了Blackwell信息论框架下的最优上限，并在实验中证明了其在多LLM协作问答任务中优于现有的投票和辩论方法。

Abstract: The rapid development of large language models (LLMs) has motivated research on decision-making in multi-agent systems, where multiple agents collaborate to achieve shared objectives. Existing aggregation approaches, such as voting and debate, are largely ad-hoc and lack formal guarantees regarding the informativeness of the resulting decisions. In this paper, we provide a principled approach to analyse decisions made in the multi-LLM setting using Blackwell's informativeness framework. Within the Blackwell information-structure abstraction, we show that voting and debate induce information structures that are no more informative than the pooled private information of all agents. This result identifies Bayesian pooled posterior maximisation as an information-theoretic upper-bound decision rule under the Blackwell ordering. Motivated by this theoretical analysis, we introduce a practical method for LLM-based question-answering (QA) tasks that estimates each agent's posterior and approximates the pooled posterior using a product-of-posteriors estimator. Extensive experiments on six QA benchmarks demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art multi-LLM debate and voting methods.

</details>


### [187] [When and Why SignSGD Outperforms SGD: A Theoretical Study Based on $\ell_1$-norm Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06615)
*Hongyi Tao,Dingzhi Yu,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了一套新的理论框架（基于 $\ell_1$-范数平稳性），成功从理论上解释了 SignSGD 和 Muon 等符号优化算法在特定条件下为何优于传统的 SGD，并证明其在稀疏噪声环境下能显著降低 $d$ 倍的问题复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SignSGD 和 Muon 等基于符号的优化算法在训练大规模模型时表现出色，但在理论上，在标准平滑度和有限方差条件下，SGD 已被证明是 $\ell_2$-范数平稳性下的极小极大最优解。因此，现有的理论框架无法解释为何符号优化算法在实践中优于 Vanilla SGD，本文旨在建立新的理论框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 1. 放弃传统的 $\ell_2$-范数平稳性框架，改用 $\ell_1$-范数平稳性、$\ell_\infty$-平滑度和可分离噪声模型来重新分析优化问题；2. 推导 SignSGD 的上下界，并与 SGD 的下界进行对比；3. 将该分析框架推广至矩阵域，分析 Muon 优化器；4. 通过训练 124M 参数的 GPT-2 模型进行实证分析。

Result: 1. 证明了在特定问题类（稀疏噪声）下，SignSGD 的复杂度比 SGD 低一个维度因子 $d$；2. 获得了 Muon 优化器在矩阵域的等价最优下界，证明了符号算子推广到矩阵后仍能保持最优的维数缩放性能；3. 实验结果表明，该理论预测与 GPT-2 模型预训练中的收敛速度一致。

Conclusion: 本文通过 $\ell_1$-范数平稳性、$\ell_\infty$-平滑度以及可分离噪声模型，证明了在特定问题几何结构下，基于符号（Sign-based）的优化算法能够比 SGD 实现 $d$ 倍的任务复杂度提升。此理论框架不仅适用于 SignSGD，也成功推广至矩阵域的 Muon 算法，并得到了 GPT-2 模型训练实验的验证。

Abstract: Sign-based optimization algorithms, such as SignSGD and Muon, have garnered significant attention for their remarkable performance in training large foundation models. Despite this empirical success, we still lack a theoretical understanding of when and why these sign-based methods outperform vanilla SGD. The core obstacle is that under standard smoothness and finite variance conditions, SGD is known to be minimax optimal for finding stationary points measured by $\ell_2$-norms, thereby fundamentally precluding any complexity gains for sign-based methods in standard settings. To overcome this barrier, we analyze sign-based optimizers leveraging $\ell_1$-norm stationarity, $\ell_\infty$-smoothness, and a separable noise model, which can better capture the coordinate-wise nature of signed updates. Under this distinct problem geometry, we derive matched upper and lower bounds for SignSGD and explicitly characterize the problem class in which SignSGD provably dominates SGD. Specifically, we compare the \emph{upper bound of SignSGD} with the \emph{lower bound of SGD}, illustrating that SignSGD effectively reduces the complexity by a factor of $d$ under \emph{sparse noise}, where $d$ is the problem dimension. Furthermore, we elevate this framework to the matrix domain, providing an equivalent optimal lower bound for the Muon optimizer, proving that extending the sign operator to matrices preserves this optimal scaling with dimensionality. Finally, we bridge our theoretical bounds to practice, demonstrating that the theoretical superiority of SignSGD accurately predicts its faster convergence during the pretraining of a 124M parameter GPT-2 model.

</details>


### [188] [Does Synthetic Data Help? Empirical Evidence from Deep Learning Time Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06032)
*Hugo Cazaux,Eyjólfur Ingi Ásgeirsson,Hlynur Stefánsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对时间序列预测中的合成数据增强进行了大规模实证研究，发现其效果取决于模型架构：通道混合架构通常获益，通道独立架构则往往受损，并提出了基于架构和退火策略的工业级使用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然合成数据已在语言模型领域取得巨大成功，但其在时间序列预测中的有效性及其潜在的机制尚不明确，缺乏系统性的实证评估。

Method: 进行了一项大规模实证研究，涵盖9个实验组、4218次系统性运行，测试了5种架构、4种合成信号和7个数据集。对比了不同模型架构与合成数据增强的兼容性及训练策略。

Result: 合成数据增强在67%的试验中表现为负面效果，但通道混合模型（如TimesNet, iTransformer）在多数情况下获益；在低资源数据设置下（如仅用10%数据），增强策略可显著超越全量数据基准。硬课程学习（Hard curriculum switching）导致MSE性能下降24%。

Conclusion: 合成数据在时间序列预测中的有效性高度依赖于模型架构；通道混合（Channel-mixing）模型通常获益，而通道独立（Channel-independent）模型表现下降。此外，使用Seasonal-Trend生成器和渐进式退火调度（Gradual annealing）是获得性能提升的关键策略。

Abstract: Synthetic data has transformed language model training, yet its role in time series forecasting remains poorly understood. We present a large-scale empirical study: nine experiment groups, 4,218 runs systematically evaluating synthetic time series augmentation across five architectures, four synthetic signals and seven datasets. The effect is sharply architecture-conditional: channel-mixing models (TimesNet, iTransformer) benefit in the majority of trials, while channel-independent models (DLinear, PatchTST) are consistently degraded. In selected low-resource settings the gains are striking: TimesNet trained on only 10\% of Weather data with synthetic augmentation surpasses the full-data baseline (4 of 16 sparsity-dataset combinations). Averaged across all architectures, augmentation hurts in 67\% of trials. We further find that only the Seasonal-Trend generator reliably helps across the tested benchmarks, and that hard curriculum switching is actively harmful (+24\% MSE degradation). These results provide concrete, actionable guidelines on how to use synthetic data: use synthetic augmentation with channel-mixing architectures, use gradual annealing schedules, and treat low-resource augmentation as architecture- and dataset-dependent. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/hugoiscracked/synthetic-ts/tree/main}

</details>


### [189] [Optimal Transport for LLM Reward Modeling from Noisy Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06036)
*Licheng Pan,Haochen Yang,Haoxuan Li,Yunsheng Lu,Yongqi Tong,Yinuo Wang,Shijian Wang,Zhixuan Chu,Lei Shen,Yuan Lu,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 RLHF 偏好数据中的噪声问题，提出了一种基于最优传输的 SelectiveRM 框架，通过质量松弛机制自动剔除噪声样本，实现了更鲁棒的奖励模型训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 RLHF 中 reward model 容易对噪声偏好数据过拟合，且现有去噪方法往往依赖于简单的同质噪声假设，无法处理语言偏好复杂性的问题。

Method: 提出了基于最优传输（Optimal Transport）的 SelectiveRM 框架，包含“联合一致性差异”（Joint Consistency Discrepancy）用于数据对齐，以及“质量松弛”（Mass Relaxation）机制用于自动剔除不符合语义一致性的噪声偏好样本。

Result: 通过理论推导证明了该方法能优化未观测干净风险的更紧上界，实验结果表明其在多个基准测试中显著超越当前顶尖方法。

Conclusion: SelectiveRM 有效解决了语言偏好数据中的噪声问题，通过最优传输理论优化了干净风险的上界，在鲁棒性和泛化性能上优于现有基准方法。

Abstract: Reward models are fundamental to Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), yet real-world datasets are inevitably corrupted by noisy preference. Conventional training objectives tend to overfit these errors, while existing denoising approaches often rely on homogeneous noise assumptions that fail to capture the complexity of linguistic preferences. To handle these challenges, we propose SelectiveRM, a framework grounded in optimal transport. We first devise a Joint Consistency Discrepancy to align the distribution of model predictions with preference data. Furthermore, to address the limitation of strict mass conservation which compels the model to fit outliers, we incorporate a Mass Relaxation mechanism via partial transport. This enables the autonomous exclusion of samples with noisy preference that contradict semantic consistency. Theoretically, we demonstrate that SelectiveRM optimizes a tighter upper bound on the unobserved clean risk. Extensive experiments validate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across diverse benchmarks.

</details>


### [190] [Requests of a Feather Must Flock Together: Batch Size vs. Prefix Homogeneity in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06046)
*Saksham Rathi,Preeti,Mythili Vutukuru*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 推理因 KV Cache 引起的性能瓶颈，Feather 调度器通过强化学习优化批处理同质性，并利用轻量级 Chunked Hash Tree 消除前缀检测的 CPU 开销，大幅提升推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理是内存密集型任务，KV Cache 的频繁访问是性能瓶颈。现有调度器虽利用前缀共享节省显存，但未针对吞吐量优化批处理的同质性，且现有前缀检测机制 CPU 开销过大。

Method: 1. 提出 Feather：一种使用强化学习（RL）来动态决策最优批处理大小与前缀同质性权衡的调度器；2. 引入 Chunked Hash Tree (CHT)：一种轻量级数据结构，用于快速前缀检测及请求调度，替代了高开销的 Radix 树遍历。

Result: 在 vLLM 和 SGLang 中集成后，Feather 实现了 2-10 倍的端到端吞吐量提升；在缺乏前缀共享的工作负载下，其性能亦不劣于主流调度器。

Conclusion: Feather 通过平衡批处理大小与前缀同质性，并引入 CHT 数据结构减少开销，显著提升了 LLM 自回归推理的吞吐量，优于现有的前缀感知调度器。

Abstract: Auto-regressive token generation in large language models is memory-bound because it requires "attending to" key and value tensors (KV cache) of all previous tokens. Prior work aims to improve the efficiency of this decode process by batching multiple requests together, and maximizing batch size subject to GPU memory constraints. The key observation of our work is that with prefix-sharing workloads, smaller, prefix-homogeneous batches -- where all requests share a common prefix -- can achieve higher decode throughput than larger, heterogeneous batches, due to better spatial and temporal locality during KV cache accesses. However, prefix-aware schedulers in state-of-the-art inference engines maximize prefix reuse within a batch only to reduce KV cache memory footprint, but do not stop batch formation at smaller homogeneous batches that could have performed better. Further, we show that shared prefix detection in existing schedulers relies on radix-tree traversals, incurring substantial CPU overhead that is often comparable to GPU execution time. This paper presents Feather, a prefix-aware scheduler that uses reinforcement learning (RL) to learn the optimal tradeoff between batch size and prefix homogeneity. We also introduce Chunked Hash Tree (CHT), a lightweight data structure that enables fast prefix detection and efficient request selection for the RL scheduler, avoiding expensive tree traversals. We integrate Feather into vLLM and SGLang, and our evaluation shows that Feather achieves 2--10$\times$ higher end-to-end throughput as compared to existing schedulers, while doing no worse than the status quo when the workload does not have enough prefix sharing. Feather achieves these gains by reducing the total number of KV cache accesses, surpassing the performance of prefix-aware attention kernels that have the same goal.

</details>


### [191] [Recursive Agent Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06639)
*Apurva Gandhi,Satyaki Chakraborty,Xiangjun Wang,Aviral Kumar,Graham Neubig*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了递归智能体优化（RAO），通过引入一种让智能体能够递归生成子任务并委派的机制和训练方法，实现了性能的有效扩展，使其能处理超出训练难度及上下文限制的复杂任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有大模型在处理超出上下文窗口的复杂任务时受限的问题，利用递归式任务拆解与委派来提升模型的推理扩展能力和泛化性。

Method: 提出 Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO) 强化学习方法，通过训练模型学会自主拆分任务、生成子智能体并进行任务委派（递归推断），以实现任务的“分而治之”。

Result: 实验表明，采用 RAO 训练的递归智能体在训练效率、长序列上下文扩展、复杂任务泛化性以及推理延迟方面均优于传统的单智能体系统。

Conclusion: Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO) 是一种有效的递归智能体训练范式，能够显著提升模型在处理复杂任务、长上下文及泛化能力方面的表现，是提升智能体自主解决复杂问题的关键路径。

Abstract: We introduce Recursive Agent Optimization (RAO), a reinforcement learning approach for training recursive agents: agents that can spawn and delegate sub-tasks to new instantiations of themselves recursively. Recursive agents implement an inference-time scaling algorithm that naturally allows agents to scale to longer contexts and generalize to more difficult problems via divide-and-conquer. RAO provides a method to train models to best take advantage of such recursive inference, teaching agents when and how to delegate and communicate. We find that recursive agents trained in this way enjoy better training efficiency, can scale to tasks that go beyond the model's context window, generalize to tasks much harder than the ones the agent was trained on, and can enjoy reduced wall-clock time compared to single-agent systems.

</details>


### [192] [TFM-Retouche: A Lightweight Input-Space Adapter for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06047)
*Duong Nguyen,Mohammed Jawhar,Nicolas Chesneau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了 TFM-Retouche，这是一种架构无关的输入空间适配方法，通过在输入层引入轻量级残差修正，无需微调模型参数即可显著提升各类表格基础模型的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型（TFM）高度依赖零样本学习，对于特定任务的微调（如全参数微调或 LoRA）既昂贵又受限于特定架构。此外，现有研究对权重空间微调能否有效提升准确率和校准度存在争议。

Method: 提出 TFM-Retouche：一种输入空间残差适配器，它不修改模型内部参数，而是通过学习一个输入空间的微小残留修正来对齐数据与预训练模型的归纳偏置。通过冻结原始 TFM 实现端到端训练，并设计了“身份保护机制”（identity guard）以确保在验证集无效时自动回退到原始模型。

Result: 在 TabArena-Lite 基准测试（55 个数据集）中，TabICLv2-Retouche 排名第一，聚合 Elo 分数较原始 TabICLv2 提升了 56 分，并在预测质量与资源开销之间处于帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: TFM-Retouche 通过一种轻量级、模型架构无关的输入空间残差适配方法，有效提升了表格基础模型（TFM）的预测性能。它不仅超过了冻结的基准模型，而且在预测质量与计算效率（训练和推理时间）之间取得了最优的平衡。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs), such as TabPFN-2.6, TabICLv2, ConTextTab, Mitra, LimiX, and TabDPT, achieve strong zero-shot performance through in-context learning, but their inductive biases remain fixed at inference time. Adapting a pretrained TFM to a specific dataset or task typically requires either full fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive, or parameter-efficient tuning methods (PEFT) such as LoRA, which must be tailored to the internal architecture of each TFM. Furthermore, the evidence on whether weight-space fine-tuning improves accuracy or calibration is mixed \citep{tanna_exploring_2026,rubachev_finetuning_2025}. We introduce TFM-Retouche, a lightweight input-space residual adapter that is architecture-agnostic by design with respect to the frozen TFM backbone. TFM-Retouche learns a small residual correction in the input space to align the input data with the inductive biases of the pretrained model. The adapter is trained end-to-end through the frozen TFM, with a post-training identity guard that falls back to the unmodified TFM whenever adaptation does not help on held-out validation. On TabArena-Lite (51 datasets spanning binary classification, multiclass classification, and regression), TabICLv2-Retouche -- the framework instantiated on TabICLv2 -- is the top-ranked method on the leaderboard with light per-task tuning and ensembling, lifting aggregate Elo by +56 over the frozen TabICLv2 base and sitting on the Pareto front of predictive quality versus both training and inference time.

</details>


### [193] [When No Benchmark Exists: Validating Comparative LLM Safety Scoring Without Ground-Truth Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06652)
*Sushant Gautam,Finn Schwall,Annika Willoch Olstad,Fernando Vallecillos Ruiz,Birk Torpmann-Hagen,Sunniva Maria Stordal Bjørklund,Leon Moonen,Klas Pettersen,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种“基准无对比安全评分”框架，通过仪器效度链验证模型安全性，并证明了安全评估高度依赖于具体情境，不应简单化为单一排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多模型部署场景缺乏针对特定语言、行业或法规的标注基准，需要一种在没有基准的情况下对比候选模型安全性的方法。

Method: 提出了“基准无对比安全评分”（benchmarkless comparative safety scoring）框架。核心方法是采用“仪器效度链”（instrumental-validity chain）替代地面真值，具体包括：1. 针对安全型与擦除型（abliterated）模型的响应对比度；2. 目标驱动方差对审计/评判伪影的支配性；3. 多次运行的稳定性。并实现了 SimpleAudit 仪器进行验证。

Result: 在挪威语安全包测试中，SimpleAudit 能有效区分“安全”与“擦除”模型（AUROC 0.89-1.00）；目标身份是方差的主要来源（η² ≈ 0.52）；严重程度评估在 10 次运行后趋于稳定。案例研究显示，安全优劣取决于具体的场景和风险度量。

Conclusion: 在缺乏标注基准的场景下，基于“仪器效度链”的基准无对比安全评分是可行的。评分结果具有情境依赖性，不能简化为单一排名，必须连同评分配置（审计员、评判、场景集等）共同报告以确保透明度和有效性。

Abstract: Many deployments must compare candidate language models for safety before a labeled benchmark exists for the relevant language, sector, or regulatory regime. We formalize this setting as benchmarkless comparative safety scoring and specify the contract under which a scenario-based audit can be interpreted as deployment evidence. Scores are valid only under a fixed scenario pack, rubric, auditor, judge, sampling configuration, and rerun budget. Because no labels are available, we replace ground-truth agreement with an instrumental-validity chain: responsiveness to a controlled safe-versus-abliterated contrast, dominance of target-driven variance over auditor and judge artifacts, and stability across reruns.
  We instantiate the chain in SimpleAudit, a local-first scoring instrument, and validate it on a Norwegian safety pack. Safe and abliterated targets separate with AUROC values between 0.89 and 1.00, target identity is the dominant variance component ($η^2 \approx 0.52$), and severity profiles stabilize by ten reruns. Applying the same chain to Petri shows that it admits both tools. The substantial differences arise upstream of the chain, in claim-contract enforcement and deployment fit. A Norwegian public-sector procurement case comparing Borealis and Gemma 3 demonstrates the resulting evidence in practice: the safer model depends on scenario category and risk measure. Consequently, scores, matched deltas, critical rates, uncertainty, and the auditor and judge used must be reported together rather than collapsed into a single ranking.

</details>


### [194] [Verifier-Backed Hard Problem Generation for Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06660)
*Yuhang Lai,Jiazhan Feng,Yee Whye Teh,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型在生成高质量、创新性科学/数学难题时易产生无效问题的问题，提出 VHG 三方自我博弈框架，通过引入独立验证器约束出题者，成功提升了难题生成的有效性与挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型问题生成方法要么依赖昂贵的人工标注，要么通过简单的自博弈产生大量无效问题或陷入奖励劫持（reward hacking），缺乏生成既有效又具挑战性和创新性问题的能力。

Method: 提出了 VHG (Verifier-enhanced Hard problem Generation) 框架，采用“出题者-解题者-验证者”的三方自我博弈机制。验证者独立评估题目有效性，并结合解题者的反馈来约束出题者的奖励；实现了两种验证器：Hard 符号验证器（针对不定积分任务）和 Soft 基于 LLM 的验证器（针对通用数学推理任务）。

Result: 在不定积分和通用数学推理任务上的实验表明，VHG 在生成问题的有效性和难度方面均显著优于现有的基准方法。

Conclusion: VHG 通过引入独立验证器，有效解决了三方自我博弈中生成难题的无效性和单一激励问题，为大模型自动化科学研究和训练提供了更优的难题生成方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities for solving scientific and mathematical problems, yet they struggle to produce valid, challenging, and novel problems - an essential component for advancing LLM training and enabling autonomous scientific research. Existing problem generation approaches either depend on expensive human expert involvement or adopt naive self-play paradigms, which frequently yield invalid problems due to reward hacking. This work introduces VHG, a verifier-enhanced hard problem generation framework built upon three-party self-play. By integrating an independent verifier into the conventional setter-solver duality, our design constrains the setter's reward to be jointly determined by problem validity (evaluated by the verifier) and difficulty (assessed by the solver). We instantiate two verifier variants: a Hard symbolic verifier and a Soft LLM-based verifier, with evaluations conducted on indefinite integral tasks and general mathematical reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that VHG substantially outperforms all baseline methods by a clear margin.

</details>


### [195] [Towards Generation-Efficient Uncertainty Estimation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06053)
*Mingcheng Zhu,Yu Liu,Tingting Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种LLM不确定性早期估计框架，通过仅利用部分生成（Logit Magnitude）或仅基于输入（MetaUE），在降低推理成本的同时实现了高效的误导性响应识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性估计方法通常依赖多次完整生成，导致推理成本高昂且延迟严重，难以满足医疗和金融等高风险应用对实时可靠性评估的需求，因此需要探索低成本、早期的不确定性估计方案。

Method: 1. 建立统一框架，按观测信息量对不确定性估计方法进行分类；2. 提出“Logit Magnitude”方法，利用早期停止生成前缀的Top-M Logit证据进行估计；3. 提出“MetaUE”方法，将基于生成的估计蒸馏成轻量级的输入感知估计模型。

Result: 实验表明，“Logit Magnitude”在保持高性能的同时显著降低了生成需求；“MetaUE”在多种场景下提供了有效的输入感知近似；证明了仅使用部分生成即可实现高效的不确定性预测。

Conclusion: 有效的LLM不确定性估计并不总是需要完整的自回归生成过程，通过部分生成或仅基于输入的信息即可实现，从而显著降低推理成本，提高高风险应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation is important for deploying LLMs in high-stakes applications such as healthcare and finance, where hallucinations can appear fluent and plausible while being factually incorrect, making it difficult for users to judge whether an output should be trusted. Existing methods require one or more full autoregressive generations to estimate uncertainty, which introduces substantial inference cost and often delays uncertainty assessment. In this paper, we investigate whether effective uncertainty estimation can be achieved with partial generation or even input-only information. Specifically, we first develop a unified framework that formulates uncertainty estimation as an early estimation problem over the autoregressive generation process of LLMs. This framework organises existing and proposed estimators by the information they observe, ranging from multi-generation to input-only prediction, and clarifies the performance-cost trade-off underlying different uncertainty estimation methods. Building on this view, we study two largely underexplored low-cost settings: estimating uncertainty with part of the generation, and predicting uncertainty from the input prompt. We propose Logit Magnitude, which uses top-M logit evidence to estimate uncertainty from an early-stopped generation prefix, and MetaUE, which distils generation-based uncertainty into a lightweight input-only estimator trained with uncertainty scores. Extensive experiments on general and domain-specific benchmarks show that Logit Magnitude achieves strong performance, and partial generations of LLMs are often sufficient for effective uncertainty estimation. MetaUE further provides a competitive input-only approximation in several settings. These findings suggest that effective uncertainty estimation requires less generation than commonly assumed, enabling unreliable responses to be identified earlier.

</details>


### [196] [Towards Self-Explainable Document Visual Question Answering with Chain-of-Explanation Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06058)
*Kjetil Indrehus,Adrian Duric,Changkyu Choi,Ali Ramezani-Kebrya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 CoExVQA，一个通过显式“证据定位-区域锚定-答案解码”流程实现自我解释的 DocVQA 框架，在提高预测透明度的同时显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DocVQA 模型通常被视为“黑盒”，难以验证模型预测与视觉证据之间的依赖关系，且将相关证据检索与答案定位过程混为一谈。

Method: 提出了 CoExVQA 框架，包含三个步骤的解释链：(1) 识别与问题相关的证据；(2) 显式定位答案区域；(3) 仅基于定位区域解码生成答案。

Result: 在 PFL-DocVQA 数据集上实现了当前可解释 DocVQA 任务的最佳性能（SotA），ANLS 指标较现有可解释基准提升了 12%。

Conclusion: CoExVQA 通过引入“解释链”机制，在保持模型可解释性和推理过程透明度的同时，显著提升了文档问答任务的准确性，证明了显式定位对提升模型性能的关键作用。

Abstract: Document Visual Question Answering (DocVQA) requires vision-language models to reason not only about what information in a document is relevant to a question, but also where the answer is grounded on the page. Existing DocVQA models entangle question-relevant evidence and answer localization and operate largely as black boxes, offering limited means to verify how predictions depend on visual evidence. We propose CoExVQA, a self-explainable DocVQA framework with a grounded reasoning process through a chain-of-explanation design. CoExVQA first identifies question-relevant evidence, then explicitly localizes the answer region, and finally decodes the answer exclusively from the grounded region. Prediction via CoExVQA's chain-of-explanation enables direct inspection and verification of the reasoning process across modalities. Empirical results show that restricting decoding to grounded evidence achieves SotA explainable DocVQA performance on PFL-DocVQA, improving ANLS by 12% over the current explainable baselines while providing transparent and verifiable predictions.

</details>


### [197] [Normalized Architectures are Natively 4-Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06067)
*Maxim Fishman,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文提出了nGPT架构，通过将权重和激活值归一化至单位超球面，从底层解决了大型语言模型在4-bit低精度训练中的鲁棒性问题，无需复杂的补偿技术即可实现稳定的训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在4-bit精度下进行高效训练具有极高价值，但传统量化训练容易受到精度损失的影响，往往需要复杂的额外干预措施（如随机Hadamard变换或逐张量缩放）。针对此问题，研究者旨在寻找一种内在具备抗量化干扰能力的模型架构。

Method: 提出nGPT架构，将权重和隐藏表示限制在单位超球面上（Normalization）。通过分析点积过程，对比了标准架构与归一化架构在处理量化噪声与信号积累上的表现。并在1.2B稠密模型及高达30B参数的混合（Mamba-Transformer）MoE模型上进行NVFP4训练验证。

Result: nGPT成功实现了稳定的端到端NVFP4训练，无需预处理干扰。实验证明，在隐藏维度增加时，该架构能实现信号的建设性累积，有效提升信噪比，在多种模型规模下均表现出良好的训练稳定性和模型质量保存能力。

Conclusion: nGPT架构通过将权重和隐藏表示约束在单位超球面上，从本质上提高了对低精度（4-bit）算术的鲁棒性。这种架构不仅消除了复杂的量化校准干扰，还能在增加隐藏维度时提供更高的信噪比和更平坦的损失函数平面，显示出在大规模模型训练中的扩展潜力。

Abstract: Training large language models at 4-bit precision is critical for efficiency. We show that nGPT, an architecture that constrains weights and hidden representations to the unit hypersphere, is inherently more robust to low-precision arithmetic. This removes the need for interventions-such as applying random Hadamard transforms and performing per-tensor scaling calculations-to preserve model quality, and it enables stable end-to-end NVFP4 training. We validate this approach on both a 1.2B dense model and hybrid (Mamba-Transformer) MoE models of up to 3B/30B parameters. We trace this robustness to the dot product: while quantization noise remains largely uncorrelated in both standard and normalized architectures, the signal behaves differently. In nGPT, the hypersphere constraint enhances weak positive correlations among the element-wise products, leading to a constructive accumulation of the signal across the hidden dimension while the noise continues to average out. This yields a higher effective signal-to-noise ratio and a flatter loss landscape, with the effect strengthening as the hidden dimension grows, suggesting increasing advantages at scale. A reference implementation is available at https://github.com/anonymous452026/ngpt-nvfp4

</details>


### [198] [Understanding diffusion models requires rethinking (again) generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06077)
*Pierre Marion,Yu-Han Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扩散模型的泛化不同于传统监督学习，旨在呼吁学界从研究“为何模型泛化”转向探索“模型在记忆发生之前的学习过程”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统计学习理论及监督学习中的“良性过拟合”范式无法充分解释扩散模型的泛化现象。扩散模型中过度的记忆会导致生成副本而非新样本，因此需要建立新的理论框架。

Method: 通过对在 CIFAR-10 上训练的扩散模型进行实证研究，探讨其泛化机制，并识别关键的开放性科学问题。

Result: 指出了现有解释（如容量限制、隐式正则化、结构归纳偏置）的局限性，并提出了关于扩散模型泛化机制的若干具体开放性研究问题。

Conclusion: 扩散模型中，记忆训练数据与泛化到新样本在本质上是不兼容的。研究重点应从“模型为何不记忆”转向“模型在预记忆阶段学到了什么”。

Abstract: This position paper argues that understanding generalization in diffusion models requires fundamentally new theoretical frameworks that go beyond both classical statistical learning theory and the benign overfitting paradigm developed for supervised learning. In diffusion models, unlike in supervised learning, memorization of training data and generalization to novel samples are incompatible: a model that has fully memorized its training set generates copies rather than novel data. Several theoretical explanations for why practical diffusion models nevertheless generalize have been proposed, based on capacity limitations, implicit regularization from optimization, or architectural inductive biases, but their interactions remain unclear. We argue that the field should pivot from explaining why the diffusion models do not memorize to investigating what the model actually learns during pre-memorization phase. To highlight our stance, we conduct empirical study of diffusion models trained on CIFAR-10, and we distill the findings into concrete open questions that we believe are key to improve understanding of generalization in diffusion models.

</details>


### [199] [Fast Gauss-Newton for Multiclass Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06081)
*Mikalai Korbit,Mario Zanon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了 Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) 来简化多分类 Softmax 的 GGN 计算；通过将 GGN 分解并丢弃复杂的类间协方差项，实现了高效的矩阵自由优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分类 Softmax GGN 受限于类别数的增长，导致曲率矩阵计算和存储复杂，难以扩展。该论文旨在通过曲率近似降低计算量，同时保持收敛稳定性。

Method: 1. 将多分类 GGN 分解为“真 vs 其他”项和“竞争者内协方差”项；2. 提出了 Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) 算法，通过丢弃该协方差项实现曲率近似；3. 利用雅可比向量积及共轭梯度法在矩阵自由（matrix-free）前提下求解更新。

Result: 在固定特征的多分类头部任务与机制实验中表明，FGN 是精确 GGN 的半正定下界，在模型训练中表现出良好的伸缩性和理论特性的匹配度。

Conclusion: FGN 为多分类交叉熵损失提供了高效的 GGN 近似，通过在曲率层面通过丢弃竞争者协方差项，实现了计算效率的提升，且在特定条件下（如竞争类概率集中或高阻尼）表现优异。

Abstract: In multiclass softmax cross-entropy, the full generalized Gauss-Newton (GGN) curvature couples all output logits through the softmax covariance, making curvature-vector products harder to scale as the number of classes grows. We show that the standard multiclass GGN can be decomposed exactly into a true-vs-rest term and a positive semidefinite within-competitor covariance term. Fast Gauss-Newton (FGN) retains the first term and drops the second, yielding a positive semidefinite under-approximation of the multiclass GGN that is exact for binary classification. The derivation uses an exact true-vs-rest scalar-margin representation of softmax cross-entropy: the loss and gradient are unchanged, and the approximation enters only at the curvature level. Exploiting the FGN curvature structure, the damped update can be written as an equivalent whitened row-space system with one row per mini-batch example. We solve this system matrix-free by conjugate gradient using Jacobian-vector and vector-Jacobian products of the scalar margin map. Targeted mechanism experiments and an evaluation on a fixed-feature multiclass head support the predictions from the decomposition: FGN stays closest to the full softmax GGN when competitor mass is concentrated or damping is large, and deviates as the dropped within-competitor covariance grows.

</details>


### [200] [Beyond Autoregressive RTG: Conditioning via Injection Outside Sequential Modeling in Decision Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06104)
*Yongyi Wang,Hanyu Liu,Lingfeng Li,Bozhou Chen,Ang Li,Qirui Zheng,Xionghui Yang,Chucai Wang,Wenxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SlimDT 提出了一种瘦身版的 Decision Transformer，通过将 RTG 信息预先注入状态表示而非将其作为独立 Token，显著降低了计算代价并提升了离线 RL 的任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 Decision Transformer (DT) 将 RTG 作为独立 Token 会导致不必要的计算资源浪费（随序列长度平方增长的注意力开销），且相对稀疏的 RTG 标量信息与其占据的 Token 计算预算不匹配。

Method: 提出 SlimDT 架构：移除序列中的 RTG Token，转而将 RTG 信息注入状态表示（State Representations）中，从而将序列长度缩短了 1/3，并保留了动作预测的序列建模能力。

Result: 在 D4RL 基准测试中，SlimDT 的推理效率优于标准 DT，且性能超过标准 DT，表现与现有 SOTA 方法相当。

Conclusion: 通过将稀疏的条件信号（RTG）从序列建模中解耦并整合到状态表示中，SlimDT 在减少计算开销的同时提升了离线强化学习任务的性能。

Abstract: Decision Transformer (DT) formulates offline reinforcement learning as autoregressive sequence modeling, achieving promising results by predicting actions from a sequence of Return-to-Go (RTG), state, and action tokens. However, RTG is a scalar that summarizes future rewards, containing far less information than typical state or action vectors, yet it consumes the same computational budget per token. Worse, the self-attention cost of Transformers grows quadratically with sequence length, so including RTG as a separate token adds unnecessary overhead. We propose SlimDT, which removes RTG from the autoregressive sequence. Instead, we inject RTG information into the state representations before the sequential modeling step, allowing the Transformer to process only a compact (state, action) sequence. This reduces the sequence length by one-third, directly improving inference efficiency. On the D4RL benchmark, SlimDT surpasses standard DT across various tasks and achieves performance comparable to existing state-of-the-art methods. Decoupling a sparse conditioning signal from an information-rich sequence thus yields both computational gains and higher task performance.

</details>


### [201] [BoostLLM: Boosting-inspired LLM Fine-tuning for Few-shot Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06117)
*Yi-Siang Wang,Kuan-Yu Chen,Yu-Chen Den,Darby Tien-Hao Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BoostLLM 提出了一种将 Boosting 原理应用于 LLM 参数高效微调的框架，通过多轮残差优化和结合决策树归纳偏置，在表格任务的低数据场景中显著提升了模型性能，超越了传统 GBDT 和大参数模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型表格任务适配方法（序列化为自然语言）在低数据场景下表现不及梯度提升决策树（GBDT），亟需一种能有效处理表格数据特征且具备良好小样本学习能力的微调方案。

Method: 提出 BoostLLM 框架：1) 将参数高效微调（PEFT）转化为多轮残差优化过程，即通过级联训练多个微小的 PEFT 适配器作为弱学习器；2) 引入决策树路径（Decision-tree paths）作为辅助输入，利用树模型的归纳偏置指导模型学习，后期逐渐转向纯特征驱动。

Result: BoostLLM 在多个 LLM 后端和数据集上表现稳定，在广泛的数据量（shot counts）下达到或超过 XGBoost，并以 4B 参数量在性能上优于基于 GPT-4o 的基准方法。此外，通过增加 boosting 轮次和结合更强的决策树模型，性能可进一步扩展。

Conclusion: 将提升（Boosting）范式作为大模型微调的通用训练原则是有效的，特别是在结构化数据的低数据场景下具有显著优势。BoostLLM不仅能匹配或超过GBDT的表现，还能通过引入决策树路径引导模型学习，证明了弱学习器集成在LLM参数高效微调中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been adapted to tabular prediction by serializing structured features into natural language, but their performance in low-data regimes remains limited compared to gradient-boosted decision trees (GBDTs). In this work, we revisit the boosting paradigm, traditionally associated with tree ensembles, and ask whether it can be applied as a general training principle for LLM fine-tuning. We propose BoostLLM, a framework that transforms parameter-efficient fine-tuning into a multi-round residual optimization process by training sequential PEFT adapters as weak learners. To incorporate tabular inductive bias, BoostLLM integrates decision-tree paths as a second input view alongside raw features; analysis reveals that the path view acts as a structured teacher in early training steps before the model shifts toward feature-driven representations. Empirically, BoostLLM achieves consistent improvements over standard fine-tuning across multiple LLM backbones and datasets, matching or surpassing XGBoost across a wide range of shot counts and outperforming GPT-4o-based methods with a 4B model. We further show that the framework scales: pairing with stronger tree models and extended boosting horizons yields additional gains under appropriate stabilization. These results suggest that boosting can serve as a general training principle for LLM fine-tuning, particularly in low-data regimes for structured data.

</details>


### [202] [Listwise Policy Optimization: Group-based RLVR as Target-Projection on the LLM Response Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06139)
*Yun Qu,Qi Wang,Yixiu Mao,Heming Zou,Yuhang Jiang,Yingyue Li,Wutong Xu,Lizhou Cai,Weijie Liu,Clive Bai,Kai Yang,Yangkun Chen,Saiyong Yang,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 推理增强，本文揭示了主流 RLVR 算法本质上是隐式的单纯形投影，并提出了 LPO 框架，通过显式的精确投影实现了更稳定、更高效的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于群组策略梯度（Group-based Policy Gradient）的强化学习验真奖励（RLVR）方法缺乏明确的目标定义。作者旨在揭示这些方法背后共同的几何结构，即隐式目标投影，并在此基础上构建更具数学解释性和可控性的优化算法。

Method: 提出 Listwise Policy Optimization (LPO) 框架。该方法通过将近端强化学习目标限制在响应单纯形上，实现显式的目标投影，并通过解耦的投影步骤进行精确的散度最小化（divergence minimization）。

Result: 在多种推理任务和不同 LLM 基座上，LPO 在匹配目标下始终优于典型的策略梯度基线（baseline），同时保证了训练的稳定性和响应的多样性，并提供了关于单调提升和投影梯度的理论保证。

Conclusion: LPO 通过显式目标投影框架，在保持优化稳定性和响应多样性的同时，实现了比传统策略梯度算法更优的推理性能。它证明了将 RLVR 视为在响应单纯形上的分布投影问题是有效的，且具有更好的理论保证和灵活性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard approach for large language models (LLMs) post-training to incentivize reasoning capacity. Among existing recipes, group-based policy gradient is prevalent, which samples a group of responses per prompt and updates the policy via group-relative advantage signals. This work reveals that these optimization strategies share a common geometric structure: each implicitly defines a target distribution on the response simplex and projects toward it via first-order approximation. Building on this insight, we propose Listwise Policy Optimization (LPO) to explicitly conduct the target-projection, which demystifies the implicit target by restricting the proximal RL objective to the response simplex, and then projects the policy via exact divergence minimization. This framework provides (i) monotonic improvement on the listwise objective with bounded, zero-sum, and self-correcting projection gradients, and (ii) flexibility in divergence selection with distinct structural properties through the decoupled projection step. On diverse reasoning tasks and LLM backbones, LPO consistently improves training performance over typical policy gradient baselines under matched targets, while intrinsically preserving optimization stability and response diversity.

</details>


### [203] [SymDrift: One-Shot Generative Modeling under Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06140)
*Samir Darouich,Vinh Tong,Lluís Pastor-Pérez,Tanja Bien,Loay Mualem,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SymDrift 是一种新型高效的单步生成模型，通过引入对称感知漂移场，解决了物理模拟中的对称性难题，在保证精度的同时大幅提升了计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的漂移模型在生成具有全局对称性（如旋转不变性）的物理系统（如分子）时存在对称性挑战，而现有的解决方案（如对称化经验分布）计算成本昂贵，且多步采样方法效率低下。

Method: 提出了 SymDrift 框架，包含两种互补策略：（1）基于最优对齐的坐标空间对称漂移；（2）通过构造消除了对称歧义的 G-不变嵌入 (G-invariant embedding)。

Result: 在构象异构体 (conformer) 和过渡态生成任务上，SymDrift 优于现有的单步生成方法，且推理计算开销降低了 40 倍。

Conclusion: SymDrift 通过使漂移场本身具有对称感知能力，解决了漂移模型在生成物理系统时面临的对称性难题，在保持与多步采样方法竞争力的同时，实现了高效的单步生成，显著降低了计算成本。

Abstract: Generative modeling of physical systems, such as molecules, requires learning distributions that are invariant under global symmetries, such as rotations in three-dimensional space. Equivariant diffusion and flow matching models can incorporate such invariances effectively, even when trained on a non-invariant empirical distribution, but they typically rely on costly multi-step sampling. Recently, drifting models have emerged as an efficient alternative, enabling single-step generation and achieving state-of-the-art performance in generative modeling tasks. However, we show that drifting models face a symmetry-specific challenge, since an equivariant generator does not generally produce the same drifting field as the one obtained from the symmetrized target distribution. Addressing this issue would require expensive symmetrization of the empirical distribution. To avoid this cost, we propose SymDrift, a framework that makes the drifting field itself symmetry-aware. We introduce two complementary strategies: (i) a symmetrized drift in coordinate space based on optimal alignment, and (ii) a $G$-invariant embedding that removes symmetry ambiguity by construction. Empirically, SymDrift outperforms existing one-shot methods on standard benchmarks for conformer and transition state generation, while remaining competitive with significantly more expensive multi-step approaches. By enabling one-shot inference, SymDrift reduces computational overhead by up to 40$\times$ compared to existing baselines, making it promising for high-throughput applications such as virtual drug screening and large-scale reaction network exploration.

</details>


### [204] [Matrix-Valued Optimism is Matrix-Valued Augmentation: Additive Hybrid Designs for Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06141)
*Jiayi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了增强拉格朗日法和乐观原始-对偶法在矩阵纠偏层面上具有代数等价性，并通过利用这种灵活性提出了一个混合优化规则，在非线性约束优化中实现了比单一方法更好的数值表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增强拉格朗日法（增加原始曲率）与乐观原始-对偶法（增加对偶记忆）在解决等式约束优化时具备表面不同的稳定机制，但近期研究显示两者在标量参数下等价。作者旨在将这一等价性推广到矩阵值纠偏，并利用其潜在的设计自由度改善算法的收敛与可行性。

Method: 将增强拉格朗日法和乐观原始-对偶法统一在矩阵值纠偏框架下；证明了矩阵纠偏的“加法原理”，即原始轨迹仅取决于纠偏矩阵之和；建立了受限步长的设计问题模型，并推导了基于局部谱权重的闭式混合策略（Hybrid Rule）。

Result: 理论上确立了两种机制的代数等价分解，但在有限步内由于其对“原始曲率”与“对偶记忆尺度”的不同影响，表现出差异。实验验证了混合设计在处理非线性等式约束问题时优于纯增强或纯乐观方法，且在病态条件下有效。

Conclusion: 提出了一种针对有约束优化问题的混合设计规则。研究证明增强拉格朗日法和乐观原始-对偶法中的纠偏矩阵加法原理，利用该自由度通过混合策略在有限步可行性和收敛性之间取得更好的平衡。实验表明该方法在轻度到中度病态问题上性能优于单一方法。

Abstract: Augmented Lagrangian and optimistic primal--dual methods stabilize equality-constrained optimization through seemingly different mechanisms: the former adds constraint-dependent primal curvature, while the latter adds dual memory. Recent work has shown that these mechanisms are equivalent for scalar parameters. We extend this equivalence to matrix-valued correction. We prove an additivity principle: for symmetric matrix parameters, the ideal primal trajectory depends only on the summed correction matrix, not on how it is split between augmented and optimistic channels. This exposes a design freedom: algebraically equivalent decompositions can have different finite-step feasibility because augmented correction affects primal curvature, whereas optimistic correction affects the scale of the dual memory correction. We formulate the resulting step-size-limited design problem and derive a closed-form hybrid rule that selects a matrix correction, splits it between the two channels, and chooses primal and dual steps using local spectral weights. Experiments on nonlinear equality-constrained problems with controlled constraint-Jacobian conditioning show that the hybrid design improves over pure augmented and pure optimistic endpoints, closely tracks a grid-search hybrid oracle, and is competitive with first-order primal--dual baselines under mild-to-moderate ill-conditioning. The experiments also identify the expected limitation: exact cancellation requires increasingly large matrix corrections as the constraint Jacobian becomes ill-conditioned.

</details>


### [205] [Unifying Goal-Conditioned RL and Unsupervised Skill Learning via Control-Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06145)
*Alireza Modirshanechi,Benjamin Eysenbach,Peter Dayan,Eric Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了无监督技能学习（MISL）与目标条件强化学习（GCRL）之间的理论纽带，揭示了MISL如何通过优化不同形式的“敏感性”来支持下游目标达成，并为强化学习预训练提供了理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督预训练在目标条件强化学习（GCRL）中虽取得实证进展，但缺乏理论基础，尤其是基于互信息（MISL）的技能学习方法为何能有效支持下游目标达成这一现象尚不明确。

Method: 1. 将GCRL和MISL统一为“控制最大化”（control maximization）框架；2. 定义三种典型的GCRL范式并证明其不等价性；3. 通过引入“目标敏感性”（goal-sensitivity）和“技能敏感性”（skill-sensitivity）概念，建立MISL目标与GCRL下游任务之间的理论边界和映射关系。

Result: 证明了三种主流GCRL范式在同一环境下可能导致不兼容的最优策略；推导得出MISL目标可以被视为一种技能敏感性度量，且受限于特定GCRL范式的下游目标敏感性边界，从而证明了增加MISL下的行为多样性可直接提高相应GCRL任务的性能。

Conclusion: 通过将GCRL和MISL统一为控制最大化问题，本文为强化学习无监督预训练奠定了理论基础，证明了不同MISL目标与特定GCRL任务之间存在明确的对应关系，并指导用户根据下游任务需求选择合适的预训练目标。

Abstract: Unsupervised pretraining has driven empirical advances in goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL), but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In particular, an influential class of methods, mutual information skill learning (MISL), discovers behaviorally diverse skills that can later be used for downstream goal-reaching. However, it remains a theoretical mystery why skills learned through MISL should support goal-reaching. A subtle challenge is that both GCRL and MISL are umbrella terms: different GCRL tasks use distinct criteria for measuring goal-reaching performance, while different MISL methods optimize distinct notions of behavioral diversity. We address this challenge and unify GCRL and MISL as instances of control maximization. We identify three canonical GCRL formulations and prove that they are fundamentally inequivalent: they can induce incompatible optimal policies even in the same environment. Nevertheless, they all share a common interpretation: a well-performing goal-conditioned policy is one whose future trajectory is highly sensitive to the commanded goal, with the precise notion of sensitivity determined by the GCRL formulation. Noting that MISL objectives can be understood as measures of skill-sensitivity akin to goal-sensitivity, we show that MISL objectives are bounded by formulation-specific downstream goal-sensitivities. These bounds establish a precise correspondence between MISL methods and downstream GCRL tasks: for every GCRL formulation, there exists a matching MISL objective for which more diverse skills afford greater downstream goal sensitivity. Our results thus lay a theoretical foundation for RL pretraining and have important practical implications, such as suggesting which pretraining objectives to use when a user cares about a specific class of downstream tasks.

</details>


### [206] [AdaGamma: State-Dependent Discounting for Temporal Adaptation in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06149)
*Yaomin Wang,Jianting Pan,Ran Tian,Xiaoyang Li,Yu Zhang,Hengle Qin,Tianshu YU*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度强化学习中固定折扣因子的局限性，本文提出了 AdaGamma。通过引入回报一致性目标，AdaGamma 实现了稳定的状态相关折扣学习，并在基准测试及物流平台实际应用中取得了显著成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度强化学习方法通常使用单一固定的折扣因子（$\gamma$），但这无法兼顾不同状态下对规划视野和自举强度的不同需求；而简单的状态相关折扣实现容易导致不稳定性及 TD 误差崩溃。

Method: 提出 AdaGamma 方法，将状态相关的折扣函数与回报一致性（return-consistency）目标相结合，以正则化备份结构，防止 TD 误差崩溃。同时对状态相关折扣的贝尔曼算子进行了理论分析。

Result: AdaGamma 能够无缝集成到 SAC 和 PPO 算法中，在连续控制基准测试中表现出一致的改进，并在京东物流平台的在线 A/B 测试中取得了显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 状态相关折扣因子在深度强化学习中是可行且有效的，前提是必须配合回报一致性目标来防止退化行为。AdaGamma 成功解决了传统方法中出现的不稳定性问题，显著提升了算法性能。

Abstract: The discount factor in reinforcement learning controls both the effective planning horizon and the strength of bootstrapping, yet most deep RL methods use a single fixed value across all states. While state-dependent discounting is conceptually appealing, naive deep actor--critic implementations can become unstable and degenerate toward TD-error collapse. We propose AdaGamma, a practical deep actor--critic method for state-dependent discounting that learns a state-dependent discount function together with a return-consistency objective to regularize the induced backup structure. On the theory side, we analyze the Bellman operator induced by state-dependent discounting and establish its basic well-posedness properties under suitable conditions. Empirically, AdaGamma integrates into both SAC and PPO, yielding consistent improvements on continuous-control benchmarks, and achieves statistically significant gains in an online A/B test on the JD Logistics platform. These results suggest that state-dependent discounting can be made effective in deep RL when coupled with a return-consistency objective that prevents degenerate target manipulation.

</details>


### [207] [Entropy-Regularized Adjoint Matching for Offline RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06156)
*Abdelghani Ghanem,Mounir Ghogho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线强化学习中生成策略受固定行为分布制约的问题，本文提出了 ME-AM 框架，通过最大熵正则化和混合先验有效缓解了流行度偏差并提升了策略探索的几何支撑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流匹配的离线策略（如 QAM）受限于固定的行为分布，存在“流行度偏差”（抑制低密度高奖励动作）和“支撑绑定”（限制了离线流形之外的探索）问题，且现有的残差策略方案无法彻底解决单峰分布带来的表达瓶颈。

Method: 提出了最大熵伴随匹配（Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching, ME-AM）框架：(1) 使用镜像下降（Mirror Descent）熵最大化目标减轻流行度偏差；(2) 采用混合行为先验（Mixture Behavior Prior）扩大流场几何支撑，以支持分布外的高奖励行动探索。

Result: 在多项稀疏奖励的连续控制基准测试中，ME-AM 表现出与现有最先进方法（SOTA）相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: ME-AM 通过引入镜像下降熵最大化目标和混合行为先验，成功克服了 QAM 的局限性，在稀疏奖励的连续控制任务中实现了更优的策略提取和探索能力，证明了该方法在离线强化学习中的有效性。

Abstract: Integrating expressive generative policies, such as flow-matching models, into offline reinforcement learning (RL) allows agents to capture complex, multi-modal behaviors. While Q-learning with Adjoint Matching (QAM) stabilizes policy optimization via the continuous adjoint method, it remains inherently bound to the fixed behavior distribution. This dependence induces a \textit{popularity bias} that can suppress high-reward actions in low-density regions, and creates a \textit{support binding} that restricts off-manifold exploration. Existing workarounds, such as appending \textit{residual} Gaussian policies, often re-introduce the expressivity bottlenecks associated with unimodal distributions. In this work, we propose \textit{Maximum Entropy Adjoint Matching} (ME-AM), a unified framework that addresses these limitations within the continuous flow formulation. ME-AM incorporates two mechanisms: (1) a Mirror Descent entropy maximization objective that mitigates the popularity bias to facilitate the extraction of optimal policies from offline datasets, and (2) a \textit{Mixture Behavior Prior} that mathematically broadens the geometric support to encompass out-of-distribution high-reward regions. By exploring this extended geometry, ME-AM identifies robust actions while preserving the absolute continuity of the generative vector field. Empirically, ME-AM demonstrates competitive or superior performance compared to prior state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across a diverse suite of sparse-reward continuous control environments.

</details>


### [208] [One Algorithm, Two Goals: Dual Scoring for Parameter and Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06166)
*Xinrui Chen,Liu Yang,Ou Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DualSFT 算法，通过共享梯度交互矩阵，实现 LLM 微调中参数掩码与数据子集的协同选择，显著降低计算开销并提升微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 LLM 微调中，参数和数据选择通常使用分离的评分机制，导致冗余计算开销，且难以实现两者之间的协同优化。

Method: 提出了 DualSFT（双重选择微调）算法：将参数和数据选择定义为基于统一验证目标的双层选择问题，推导出共享的梯度交互矩阵，通过计算该矩阵的行和列聚合值（一阶/二阶验证改进近似），实现“一次性”完成参数掩码和数据子集的联合选择。

Result: 在 3B-9B 参数规模的 LLM 上，DualSFT 的单轴变体提升了任务性能和稳定性-可塑性权衡；全量 DualSFT 在匹配预算下，相比串行混合基准方法，提供了更优的联合约束性能表现。

Conclusion: DualSFT 通过共享梯度信息实现了参数与数据的高效协同选择，在同等计算预算下，相比传统串行选择方法，在 LLM 微调中表现出更优的性能权衡。

Abstract: In Large Language Model (LLM) fine-tuning, parameter and data selection are common strategies for reducing fine-tuning cost, yet they are typically driven by separate scoring mechanisms. When a parameter mask and data subset jointly determine restricted fine-tuning, this separation incurs redundant overhead and makes coordinated selection difficult. We cast parameter and data selection as two bilevel selection problems under a common validation objective and derive a shared local response-surrogate scoring rule. Under first- and second-order validation-improvement approximations, parameter importance and data utility emerge as column-wise and row-wise aggregations of a single gradient interaction matrix, yielding a closed-form row-column correspondence for co-extracting both signals. Building on this structure, we propose DualSFT (Dual-Selection Fine-Tuning), a one-shot dual-scoring algorithm that produces a parameter mask and data subset from shared gradient statistics. On 3B-9B LLMs, single-axis DualSFT variants strengthen target-task performance and stability-plasticity trade-offs within their comparison groups, while full DualSFT yields a more favorable joint-constrained trade-off than sequential hybrid baselines under matched budgets.

</details>


### [209] [In-Context Black-Box Optimization with Unreliable Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06187)
*Nicolas Samuel Blumer,Julien Martinelli,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FICBO是一种新型反馈感知上下文黑盒优化方法，通过Transformer建模反馈源可靠性，实现对辅助信息的有效利用与风险规避，提升了优化效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黑盒优化方法难以同时处理多种潜在的有偏或误导性反馈数据，且缺乏跨任务的通用性。FICBO旨在解决如何在存在多种反馈来源的情况下，通过模型学习识别反馈可靠性，从而加速优化过程。

Method: 提出“反馈感知上下文优化（FICBO）”框架，通过设计结构化反馈先验来建模反馈源的访问权限、相关性和失真度；利用基于Transformer的预训练模型在推理阶段动态评估反馈源的可靠性，并结合历史轨迹与辅助反馈进行查询选择。

Result: 在合成任务和真实世界任务中，FICBO均优于现有基准方法；实验表明模型能准确识别测试阶段反馈源的质量，证明了其在面对不可靠反馈时的鲁棒性及其决策的可解释性。

Conclusion: FICBO通过引入结构化反馈先验，成功实现了在黑盒优化中利用多种辅助反馈的能力。它不仅能有效利用高质量反馈，还能在面对误导性或弱反馈时具备鲁棒性，并通过上下文感知机制实现了决策过程的可解释性。

Abstract: Black-box optimization in science and engineering often comes with side information: experts, simulators, pretrained predictors, or heuristics can suggest which candidates look promising. This information can accelerate search, but it can also be biased, input-dependent, or misleading. Feedback-aware BO methods typically handle one task at a time, limiting their ability to generalize over multiple sources of feedback. In-context optimizers address cross-task adaptation, but usually assume that optimization history is the only available signal at test time. We study feedback-informed in-context black-box optimization (FICBO), where a pretrained optimizer conditions on both the observed history and cheap auxiliary feedback for the current candidate set. We introduce a structured feedback prior that models how feedback sources vary in their access, relevance, and distortion relative to the true objective, and use it to pretrain a feedback-aware transformer. At test time, the model estimates source reliability in context by comparing observed objective values with auxiliary signals, improving query selection. On synthetic and real-world tasks, FICBO effectively exploits informative feedback while remaining robust to weak or misleading sources, improving over other baselines. Empirical investigations further illustrate how the model perceives test-time sources, offering insights into its interpretability and decision-making process.

</details>


### [210] [Constrained Contextual Bandits with Adversarial Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06190)
*Dhruv Sarkar,Abhishek Sinha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对对抗性上下文下的带预算约束赌博机问题，提出了一种基于在线回归预言机的简化框架，实现了比现有方法更优的遗憾与预算控制效果，且分析过程更简洁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带预算约束的上下文赌博机问题（Contextual Bandits），现有研究多局限于随机上下文设置。本文旨在解决更具挑战性的对抗性上下文场景，并提出一种在持续运行（continuing setting）环境下，能同时控制遗憾（regret）和预算违规（budget violation）的简单且通用的算法框架。

Method: 基于 Foster 等人 (2018) 的 $\mathsf{SquareCB}$ 框架，利用在线回归预言机，将带约束的优化问题简化为标准的无约束上下文赌博机问题，并通过构造自适应的替代奖励函数（surrogate reward functions）来控制遗憾和预算违规。

Result: 在对抗性上下文环境下，成功实现了对遗憾和预算违规的有效控制，提供了比现有文献更优的性能保证，且算法运行高效，理论分析更加简洁透明。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个基于在线回归预言机的模块化框架，通过构建自适应替代奖励函数，有效解决了预算约束下的对抗性上下文赌博机问题，在通用性、效率和分析简洁性上均优于已有成果。

Abstract: We study budget-constrained contextual bandits with adversarial contexts, where each action yields a random reward and incurs a random cost. We adopt the standard realizability assumption: conditioned on the observed context, rewards and costs are drawn independently from fixed distributions whose expectations belong to known function classes. We focus on the continuing setting, in which the algorithm operates over the entire horizon even after the budget for cumulative cost is exhausted. In this setting, the objective is to simultaneously control regret and the violation of the budget constraint. Building on the seminal $\mathsf{SquareCB}$ framework of Foster et al. [2018], we propose a simple and modular framework that leverages online regression oracles to reduce the constrained problem to a standard unconstrained contextual bandit problem with adaptively defined surrogate reward functions. In contrast to prior works, which focus on stochastic contexts, our reduction yields improved guarantees for more general adversarial contexts, together with an efficient algorithm with a compact and transparent analysis.

</details>


### [211] [Memory Inception: Latent-Space KV Cache Manipulation for Steering LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06225)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Michael Zhang,Ilana Greenberg,Adam Alnasser,Lucas Baker,John Sous*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“内存植入（Memory Inception）”技术，通过在模型中间层注入结构化 KV 键值代替冗长的提示词，在实现强大模型控制的同时，显著降低了长上下文的缓存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型转向方法存在局限性：提示工程会占用大量缓存并干扰长文本交互；激活转向（CAA）虽然紧凑但控制力较弱且不支持复杂的结构化引导。

Method: 提出了一种无需训练（training-free）的“内存植入（Memory Inception）”方法，通过在特定的 Transformer 层插入由文本生成的键值（KV）存储库（KV banks），在潜在注意力空间中实现模型转向，而非传统的提示词注入。

Result: 1. 在个性化转向任务中，MI实现了控制力与偏移量之间的最佳权衡；2. 在不重写对话文本的情况下，支持对话中途的行为动态调整；3. 在结构化推理任务中，表现优于可见提示词，且 KV 存储空间开销减少了高达 118 倍。

Conclusion: Memory Inception (MI) 是一种高效的大模型引导方法，在需要持久性、结构化引导或受限上下文场景下具有显著优势。它弥补了传统提示工程和激活转向（CAA）在上下文占用与控制能力之间的差距。

Abstract: Steering large language models (LLMs) is usually done by either instruction prompting or activation steering. Prompting often gives strong control, but caches guidance tokens at every layer and can clutter long interactions; activation steering is compact but typically weaker and does not support large structured reminders. We introduce memory inception (MI), a training-free method that steers in latent attention space by inserting text-derived key-value (KV) banks only at selected layers. Rather than materializing reminder content throughout the prompt cache, MI treats steering as selective KV allocation, injecting latent slots only where the model routes to them. On matched personality-steering tasks, MI gives the best overall control--drift trade-off, remaining competitive with prompting while consistently outperforming CAA. On updateable guidance, MI supports mid-conversation behavior shifts without rewriting the visible transcript, achieving the highest post-shift alignment on Qwen3. On structured reasoning, MI outperforms visible prompting on HARDMath and PHYSICS (10/12 subject$\times$mode cells), serving as proxies for structured reasoning in verifiable domains, while cutting content-matched KV storage by up to 118$\times$. These results position MI as a powerful steering method when guidance is persistent, structured, or expensive to keep in the visible transcript.

</details>


### [212] [Soft Deterministic Policy Gradient with Gaussian Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06228)
*Hyunjun Na,Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 DPG 在离散/稀疏奖励下梯度不稳定的问题，论文提出了基于高斯平滑的 Soft-DPG 算法，消除了对 critic 动作梯度的依赖，提高了学习的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的确定性策略梯度（DPG）在动作与奖励函数微分的假设下工作，而在稀疏或离散奖励环境下，评价函数不够平滑，导致梯度定义不明确或学习不稳定。

Method: 提出了一种基于高斯平滑的平滑贝尔曼方程，构建了 Soft-DPG，在策略更新时消除了对动作-评价函数梯度（critic action-gradients）的直接依赖。

Result: 在标准连续控制基准测试和离散奖励变体中，Soft DDPG 表现出与 DDPG 相当的性能，并在离散奖励环境中展现出显著的稳定性和性能提升。

Conclusion: Soft-DPG 通过高斯平滑技术解决了传统 DPG 在面对稀疏或离散奖励时梯度不稳定的问题，提供了一种鲁棒且高效的连续控制算法。

Abstract: Deterministic policy gradient (DPG) is widely utilized for continuous control; however, it inherently relies on the differentiability of the critic with respect to the action during policy updates. This assumption is violated in practical control problems involving sparse or discrete rewards, leading to ill-defined policy gradients and unstable learning. To address these challenges, we propose a principled alternative based on a smoothed Bellman equation formulated via Gaussian smoothing. Specifically, we define a novel action-value function based on a smoothed Bellman equation and derive the soft deterministic policy gradient (Soft-DPG). Our formulation eliminates explicit dependence on critic action-gradients and ensures that the gradient remains well-defined even for non-smooth Q-functions. We instantiate this framework into a deep reinforcement learning algorithm, which we call soft deep deterministic policy gradient (Soft DDPG). Empirical evaluations on standard continuous control benchmarks and their discretized-reward variants show that Soft DDPG remains competitive in dense-reward settings and provides clear gains in most discretized-reward environments, where standard DDPG is more sensitive to irregular critic landscapes.

</details>


### [213] [Band Together: Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination against Evasion-based Promotion Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06238)
*Guanmeng Xian,Ning Yang,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态推荐系统在多用户推广攻击下的梯度匹配缺陷，提出了UAT-MC方法，通过梯度对齐机制优化对抗训练，实现了更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推荐系统易受规避式推广攻击，且在多用户场景下存在“跨模态梯度不匹配”现象（即视觉与文本模态因受不同用户群体影响而向相反方向优化），导致攻击效能受损及鲁棒训练对最差情况风险的低估。

Method: 提出了“多模态协调下的无目标对抗训练（UAT-MC）”框架。通过将所有物品视为潜在攻击目标以应对规避式攻击，并引入“梯度对齐机制（Gradient Alignment Mechanism）”来强制修正视觉与文本模态间的方向不一致，实现扰动的同步优化。

Result: 实验表明，UAT-MC 在防御推广攻击方面表现出色，显著优于现有基线方法，同时在保持推荐性能方面符合防御与精度权衡的需求。

Conclusion: UAT-MC 能够有效缓解多模态推荐系统在多用户推广场景下的梯度不匹配问题，显著提升系统对抗规避式攻击的鲁棒性，并在防御与推荐精度之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal recommender systems exploit visual and textual signals to alleviate data sparsity, but this also makes them more vulnerable to evasion-based promotion attacks. Existing defenses are largely limited to single-modal settings and mainly focus on poisoning-based threats, leaving evasion-based threats underexplored. In this work, we first identify a cross-modal gradient mismatch under the multi-user promotion setting, where visual and textual perturbations are optimized in inconsistent directions due to the dominance of distinct user groups. This phenomenon dilutes the attack effectiveness and leads robust training to underestimate worst-case risks. To address this issue, we propose Untargeted Adversarial Training with Multimodal Coordination (UAT-MC). UAT-MC tackles the challenge of unknown targeted items in evasion-based attacks (as opposed to poisoning-based attacks) by treating all items as potential targets, and introduces a gradient alignment mechanism to explicitly correct this mismatch. This design ensures synchronized perturbations across modalities, thereby maximizing adversarial strength for robust training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UAT-MC significantly improves robustness against promotion attacks while maintaining acceptable recommendation performance under the defense-accuracy trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/gmXian/UAT-MC.

</details>


### [214] [When Graph Language Models Go Beyond Memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06239)
*Masatsugu Yamada,Mahito Sugiyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过新的诊断协议揭示了图语言模型学习行为的核心：其结构化对齐能力随规模扩大而从“逐字记忆”转向“结构泛化”，但在处理低频罕见模式时表现依然受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前尚不清楚图语言模型是真的习得了图的结构性规律，还是仅仅记住了训练集图结构，且现有的聚合忠实度指标无法区分这两者。

Method: 开发了一套校准诊断协议，结合了频繁子图挖掘（frequent subgraph mining）、图级自助法基准（graph-level bootstrap baseline）以及三级频率分层（three-level frequency stratification）方法，以 disentangle（解析）记忆与结构对齐。

Result: 在5个TU基准测试中，大规模模型（3.75M图）的逐字记忆急剧下降，但子图秩相关性（rank correlation）保持在极高水平；固定子样本分析显示对新颖子图的对齐能力并非源于记忆；验证了该现象在不同序列化方式下的稳健性。

Conclusion: 图语言模型在规模化（scale）下能够习得超出单纯记忆的结构性规律，特别是在高频模式中。然而，在小规模训练下，模型的忠实度很大程度上等同于逐字记忆。稀有模式的学习仍然是一个挑战，即使增大模型容量也仅有微小改善。

Abstract: It remains unclear whether graph language models learn structural regularities or merely memorize training graphs; this cannot be resolved by current aggregate fidelity metrics alone. We develop a calibrated diagnostic protocol that combines frequent subgraph mining, a graph-level bootstrap baseline, and three-level frequency stratification to disentangle memorization from structural alignment. Using this framework, we show that graph language models can acquire structural regularities beyond memorization at scale, primarily in the high-frequency regime. This is supported by the following empirical evidence: On five TU benchmarks, LLaMA-style graph language models reach high subgraph-rank correlation, yet their alignment is matched or exceeded by the memorization bootstrap in most cases. At small scale, under our bootstrap diagnostic, fidelity is largely indistinguishable from verbatim recall. In contrast, at large scale with 3.75M graphs, verbatim memorization drops sharply while rank correlation remains near ceiling. Crucially, in a separate fixed-subsample analysis, frequent subgraph mining restricted to the novel-only subset closely tracks the corresponding all-generation Spearman correlation, providing evidence that the alignment is not driven solely by verbatim recall. Across all scales, high-frequency patterns are well reproduced, while rare patterns remain poorly covered, and this deficit narrows only marginally as capacity increases. We observe the same scale-dependent crossover under two distinct graph serializations (canonical DFS code and action sequences), providing evidence of robustness in our analysis.

</details>


### [215] [A Note on TurboQuant and the Earlier DRIVE/EDEN Line of Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18555)
*Ran Ben-Basat,Yaniv Ben-Itzhak,Gal Mendelson,Michael Mitzenmacher,Amit Portnoy,Shay Vargaftik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出TurboQuant是EDEN量化方案的次优特例，通过理论分析与复现实验证明其在缩放参数选择及残差处理上存在缺陷，性能全面弱于EDEN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了澄清TurboQuant与之前的DRIVE及EDEN量化方案之间的关系，指出TurboQuant在设计上的次优性及理论重叠。

Method: 通过数学推导（对比量化缩放参数S、残差量化策略）与对比实验，系统性剖析TurboQuant与EDEN/DRIVE的架构关系。

Result: 实验证明：优化后的有偏EDEN优于TurboQuant_mse，无偏EDEN显著优于TurboQuant_prod（效能提升超过1 bit），且EDEN在所有实验设置中均表现更优。

Conclusion: TurboQuant在理论上是EDEN方案的次优特例，其实验表现全面落后于EDEN方案，且在数学性质和优化策略上存在显著缺陷。

Abstract: This note clarifies the relationship between the recent TurboQuant work and the earlier DRIVE (NeurIPS 2021) and EDEN (ICML 2022) schemes. DRIVE is a 1-bit quantizer that EDEN extended to any $b>0$ bits per coordinate; we refer to them collectively as EDEN.
  First, TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ is a special case of EDEN obtained by fixing EDEN's scalar scale parameter to $S=1$. EDEN supports both biased and unbiased quantization, each optimized by a different $S$ (chosen via methods described in the EDEN works). The fixed choice $S=1$ used by TurboQuant is generally suboptimal, although the optimal $S$ for biased EDEN converges to $1$ as the dimension grows; accordingly TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$ approaches EDEN's behavior for large $d$.
  Second, TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$ combines a biased $(b-1)$-bit EDEN step with an unbiased 1-bit QJL quantization of the residual. It is suboptimal in three ways: (1) its $(b-1)$-bit step uses the suboptimal $S=1$; (2) its 1-bit unbiased residual quantization has worse MSE than (unbiased) 1-bit EDEN; (3) chaining a biased $(b-1)$-bit step with a 1-bit unbiased residual step is inferior to unbiasedly quantizing the input directly with $b$-bit EDEN.
  Third, some of the analysis in the TurboQuant work mirrors that of the EDEN works: both exploit the connection between random rotations and the shifted Beta distribution, use the Lloyd-Max algorithm, and note that Randomized Hadamard Transforms can replace uniform random rotations.
  Experiments support these claims: biased EDEN (with optimized $S$) is more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{mse}}$, and unbiased EDEN is markedly more accurate than TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$, often by more than a bit (e.g., 2-bit EDEN beats 3-bit TurboQuant$_{\text{prod}}$). We also repeat all accuracy experiments from the TurboQuant paper, showing that EDEN outperforms it in every setup we have tried.

</details>


### [216] [The Role of Node Features in Graph Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06250)
*Jan von Pichowski,Alžbeta Hrabošová,Ingo Scholtes,Christopher Blöcker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了图池化效果不佳的根本原因是节点特征与图拓扑结构的不对齐，并提出了评估特征质量的指标，证明了满足对齐要求的特征能显著提升池化方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图池化（Graph Pooling）在图分类问题中被广泛使用，但其相对于标准的 WL-1 表达能力 GNN 而言，实际性能提升往往较小且不稳定，作者希望分析这种“效果不佳”现象背后的根本原因。

Method: 1. 分析节点特征与图拓扑的交互作用对图池化目标的影响；2. 形式化定义了使图池化有效的节点特征要求；3. 提出了一种量化节点特征质量的度量指标；4. 基于上述要求在多个数据集上进行验证。

Result: 研究发现，现有的 pooling 操作通常忽视了节点特征对图拓扑的对齐要求；实验表明，当节点特征满足所提出的质量要求时，图池化确实能够显著提升模型在相应数据集上的表现。

Conclusion: 图池化（Graph Pooling）的有效性高度依赖于节点特征与图拓扑结构之间的对齐程度，即 pooling 不是通用的性能提升手段，而是需要特定特征质量支撑的特定优化步骤。

Abstract: Graph pooling is commonly applied in graph classification, yet its empirical gains over standard WL-1 expressive GNNs are often marginal or inconsistent. We study this gap by analysing the interaction between node features and graph topology and their effect on pooling objectives. Our analysis reveals that pooling operators require node features that are well-aligned with the graph's topology -- a condition often overlooked and not guaranteed in empirical networks. We formalise fundamental requirements for node features to enable effective pooling, and introduce a quantitative measure of feature quality. Our empirical evaluation shows that, when these requirements are satisfied, pooling can be beneficial and improve performance on appropriate datasets.

</details>


### [217] [Trade-off Functions for DP-SGD with Subsampling based on Random Shuffling: Tight Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06259)
*Marten van Dijk,Murat Bilgehan Ertan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推导了随机洗牌隐私SGD的精确、闭式f-DP权衡界，相比泊松采样更具透明度，并通过新型证明技术改善了多轮训练下的隐私边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的泊松子采样（Poisson subsampling）f-DP分析方法通常产生非闭式、难以解释的隐式公式。研究者旨在通过随机洗牌机制，在f-DP框架下推导出一套简洁、透明且紧致的隐私代价权衡分析方法。

Method: 利用Berry-Esseen定理推导了单轮训练权衡函数的闭式界限；引入了一种基于大数定律推广的新型证明技术，用于处理多轮迭代的合成（composition）问题，以分析渐近状态下的性能。

Result: 在噪声倍率 $\sigma \geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$ 的条件下，获得了随机洗牌DP-SGD的紧致闭式界限；在单轮实验中展示了具体的隐私参数设置；通过证明技术创新，实现了在多轮迭代（E-fold composition）下隐私泄露 $\delta$ 的线性依赖关系改进，并给出了渐近随机猜测界限。

Conclusion: 本文针对基于随机洗牌（random shuffling）的DP-SGD在f-DP框架下给出了紧致的权衡函数分析。证明了在特定噪声倍率范围内，随机洗牌相比泊松采样具有更清晰的闭式界限（closed-form bounds）；通过改进证明技术，实现了在多轮迭代中渐近达到最优隐私保真度，并提出了关于收敛速率的未来研究方向。

Abstract: We derive a tight analysis of the trade-off function for Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) with subsampling based on random shuffling within the $f$-DP framework. Our analysis covers the regime $σ\geq \sqrt{3/\ln M}$, where $σ$ is the noise multiplier and $M$ is the number of rounds within a single epoch. Unlike $f$-DP analyses for Poisson subsampling, which yield non-closed implicit formulas that can be machine computed but are non-transparent, random shuffling admits a tight analysis yielding transparent and interpretable closed-form bounds. Our concrete bounds, derived via the Berry-Esseen theorem, are tight up to constant factors within the proof framework. We demonstrate worked parameter settings for a single epoch ($E=1$) with a corresponding trade-off function $\geq 1-a-δ$, that is, only $δ$ below the ideal random guessing diagonal $1-a$: For $δ= 1/100$ and $σ= 1$, roughly $M \approx 1.14\times 10^6$ rounds and $N \approx 1.14\times 10^7$ training samples suffice to achieve meaningful differential privacy. This is in contrast to recent negative results for the regime $σ\leq 1/\sqrt{2 \ln M}$. Our concrete bounds can be composed over multiple epochs leading to $δ$ having a linear in $E$ dependency, which restricts $E=O(\sqrt{M})$. To go beyond Berry--Esseen, we introduce a new proof technique based on a generalization of the law of large numbers that yields an asymptotic random guessing diagonal-limit result: if $E=c_M^2M$ with $c_M\to 0$, then the $E$-fold composed trade-off function satisfies $f^{\otimes E}(a)\to 1-a$ uniformly in $a\in[0,1]$ with $δ$ having only an $O(\sqrt{E})$ dependency. We compare this asymptotic regime with the corresponding Poisson subsampling asymptotic, and highlight the characterization of explicit convergence rates as an open question.

</details>


### [218] [Beyond Rigid Alignment: Graph Federated Learning via Dual Manifold Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06260)
*Wentao Yu,Bo Han,Jie Yang,Chen Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 FedGMC，一种通过流形校准机制解决图联邦学习中语义与结构异质性的新方法，放弃了限制性的参数对齐，实现了更好的全局协同与本地个性化平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图联邦学习方法通过强制对齐模型参数或原型来处理异质性，往往隐含了“全局线性假设”，这限制了客户端个性化表示空间，无法有效处理分布式子图之间显著的语义和结构差异。

Method: 提出了一种名为 FedGMC（Federated Graph Manifold Calibration）的范式，通过双重流形校准机制（针对语义异质性的语义流形校准和针对结构异质性的结构流形校准）取代了传统的刚性模型参数/原型对齐。服务端构建语义锚点和结构模板，引导客户端进行本地流形校准，并动态聚合更新全局流形。

Result: 在11个同质和异质图数据集上的广泛实验表明，FedGMC 在平衡全局共性与本地个性化方面表现卓越，显著优于当前最先进的基准方法。

Conclusion: FedGMC 通过提出的双重流形校准机制，能够有效平衡图联邦学习中的全局共性与本地个性化需求，在处理语义和结构异质性方面显著优于现有主流方法。

Abstract: Graph Federated Learning (GFL) enables collaborative representation learning across distributed subgraphs while preserving privacy. However, heterogeneity remains a critical challenge, as subgraphs across clients typically differ significantly in both semantics and structures. Existing methods address heterogeneity by enforcing the rigid alignment of model parameters or prototypes between clients and the server. However, these alignments implicitly rely on a restrictive global linearity assumption that summarizes local data distributions using a single and globally consistent representation space. This severely compresses the personalized representation space of clients and fails to preserve diverse local graph distributions. To overcome these limitations, we propose Federated Graph Manifold Calibration (FedGMC), a novel paradigm that tackles semantic heterogeneity and structural heterogeneity from a unified manifold perspective. Instead of enforcing rigid alignment, FedGMC introduces a dual manifold calibration mechanism that preserves global commonalities while maximizing the personalized representation space of local clients. Specifically, for semantic heterogeneity, the server constructs a geometrically optimal semantic manifold via equidistant semantic anchors, so as to guide the calibration of local semantic manifolds. For structural heterogeneity, the server constructs a global structural manifold by building global structural templates, so as to guide the calibration of local structural manifolds. Finally, the server dynamically refines both global semantic manifolds and structural manifolds by aggregating local manifolds. Extensive experiments on eleven homophilic and heterophilic graphs demonstrate that FedGMC effectively balances global commonality and local personalization, thereby significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baseline methods.

</details>


### [219] [Inference-Time Refinement Closes the Synthetic-Real Gap in Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06261)
*Eugenio Lomurno,Filippo Balzarini,Francesco Benelle,Francesca Pia Panaccione,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TARDIS是一种针对表格数据扩散模型的新型推理时细化框架，通过BCR（双向倒角细化）策略，在无需重训模型的前提下，显著提升了合成数据的下游任务实用性，成功实现甚至超越了真实原始数据的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格数据生成主要关注训练时的架构优化或规模扩展，而利用预训练模型在推理阶段进行“后处理”以优化生成质量的方法在表格领域尚属空白。作者旨在通过推理时细化，弥合合成数据与真实数据之间的实用性差距。

Method: 提出TARDIS框架，在冻结的预训练扩散模型主干上，通过Tree-structured Parzen Estimator（TPE）搜索最优的推理时采样策略及打分引导。核心机制是“双向倒角细化”（BCR），通过连续的基于梯度的优化和离散的基于批处理排序的后处理，最小化真实数据与合成数据间的对称倒角距离。

Result: 在15个基准数据集上，TARDIS显著优于TabDiff基线（提升12.9%），并且在11个数据集上实现了比使用真实数据训练的模型平均高出8.6%的下游任务性能，同时保持了良好的多样性和隐私性。仅需单块消费级GPU即可在80分钟内完成优化。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在预训练的表格扩散模型上应用推理时细化（Inference-time refinement）是提升合成数据质量的高效途径，BCR（双向倒角细化）是该任务的主流模式，能够弥补甚至超越真实数据的实用性，且具备高计算效率。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generators set the current state of the art for synthetic tabular data. These methods approach but rarely exceed real-data utility, and closing this synthetic-real gap has so far been pursued exclusively at training time, via architectural advances, scaling, and retraining of monolithic generators. The inference-time alternative, i.e., refining the outputs of a pre-trained backbone with parameters left untouched, has remained largely unexplored for tabular synthesis. We introduce TARDIS (Tabular generation through Refinement, Distillation, and Inference-time Sampling), an inference-time refinement framework that operates on a frozen pre-trained backbone, configured per dataset by a Tree-structured Parzen Estimator search over score-level guidance during reverse diffusion, with each trial's objective set by an inner grid search over post-hoc sample selectors and an optional soft-label distillation step. The search space encodes a single mathematical pattern we name Bidirectional Chamfer Refinement (BCR): the symmetric Chamfer functional between synthetic and real samples is minimized both continuously, via a score-level gradient, and discretely, via batch-ranking post-generation. The per-dataset search recovers BCR-aligned configurations on most datasets, evidence for BCR as the dominant refinement pattern. Across 15 binary, multiclass, and regression benchmarks TARDIS achieves a median +8.6% downstream-task improvement over models trained on real data (95% CI [+3.3, +16.4], Wilcoxon p=0.016, 11/15 strict wins) and improves over the TabDiff backbone on all 15 datasets (mean +12.9%, p<10^-4), matching the backbone on manifold fidelity, diversity, and sample-level privacy. Inference-time refinement of a pre-trained tabular diffusion backbone reaches and exceeds real-data utility in 1 to 80 minutes on a single consumer-grade GPU.

</details>


### [220] [Can Attribution Predict Risk? From Multi-View Attribution to Planning Risk Signals in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06264)
*Le Yang,Ruoyu Chen,Haijun Liu,Jiawei Liang,ShangQuan Sun,Xiaochun Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于端到端自动驾驶的分层归因框架，旨在通过分析视觉输入对规划路径的具体贡献，量化并预测自动驾驶系统的潜在规划风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶模型存在“黑盒”问题，目前的解释方法与规划过程解耦且缺乏对复杂多视角输入协同作用的理解，导致难以定位风险。

Method: 提出了一个分层归因框架：首先通过基于L2一致性的由粗到细策略，定位关键视觉区域；随后从中提取三种归因统计特征（归因熵、相机内空间方差、跨相机基尼系数），用于量化规划风险。

Result: 该框架在BridgeAD、UniAD和GenAD等模型上表现优异，归因指标与规划误差的相关性（Spearman相关系数）达0.30，碰撞检测的AUROC达0.77，且具有良好的泛化性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的分层归因框架能有效揭示端到端自动驾驶规划的视觉证据，并通过提取的归因统计指标成功预测规划风险，为提升自动驾驶系统的可解释性与安全性提供了有效手段。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving models generate future trajectories from multi-view inputs, improving system integration but introducing opaque decisions and hard-to-localize risks. Existing methods either rely on auxiliary monitoring models or generate textual explanations, but are decoupled from the planning process and fail to reveal the visual evidence underlying trajectory generation. While attribution offers a direct alternative, planning differs from image classification by taking six-view camera images as input and predicting continuous multi-step trajectories, requiring attribution to capture both critical views and regions and their influence on outputs. Moreover, whether attribution maps can support risk identification remains underexplored. To address this, we propose a hierarchical attribution framework for end-to-end planning.
