Specifically, using L2 consistency with the original trajectory as the objective, we design a coarse-to-fine region attribution strategy that searches candidate regions across the full six-view input and refines attribution within them. We further extract three attribution statistics as predictive signals for planning risk, including attribution entropy to measure how concentrated the planner's reliance is over the joint visual space, within-camera spatial variance to characterize how spread out the attribution is within each view, and cross-camera Gini coefficient to quantify how unevenly attribution is distributed across the six cameras. Experiments on BridgeAD, UniAD, and GenAD show that these statistics correlate with planning risk, achieving Spearman correlations of $0.30 \pm 0.07$ with trajectory error and AUROC of $0.77 \pm 0.04$ for collision detection. The signal generalizes to held-out scenes with negligible degradation and remains stable under an alternative attribution baseline.

</details>


### [221] [A Flow Matching Algorithm for Many-Shot Adaptation to Unseen Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06272)
*Tyler Ingebrand,Ruihan Zhao,Kushagra Gupta,David Fridovich-Keil,Sandeep P. Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FP-FM 是一种创新的流匹配算法，通过将目标分布投影到预训练的基函数空间，实现了无需微调即可快速适配多样化分布的强大生成能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型在面对从示例数据点（example data points）进行模型适配时面临挑战，缺乏高效、无需额外推断训练的针对特定分布的适配方案。

Method: 提出“流匹配的功能投影”（Function Projection for Flow Matching, FP-FM）。通过学习一组基函数来覆盖训练分布的速度场，并利用最小二乘法将新分布投影到该基空间中；同时引入了多种时间相关系数变体以权衡计算效率与模型表现。

Result: FP-FM 在精确度（Precision）和召回率（Recall）指标上显著提升，特别是在处理未见过的目标分布时表现出强大的通用性和稳健性。

Conclusion: FP-FM 提出了一种基于最小二乘投影的高效通用适配框架，无需重新训练即可实现针对多样化目标分布的零样本（Zero-shot）生成，在合成数据和图像数据集上均优于基线方法。

Abstract: While generative modeling has achieved remarkable success on tasks like natural language-conditioned image generation, enabling model adaptation from example data points remains a relatively underexplored and challenging problem. To this end, we propose Function Projection for Flow Matching (FP-FM), an algorithm that directly conditions generation on samples from the target distribution. FP-FM learns basis functions to span the velocity fields corresponding to a set of training distributions, and adapts to new distributions by computing a simple least-squares projection onto this basis. This enables efficient generation of samples from diverse target distributions without additional training at inference time. We further introduce multiple variants of FP-FM that provide a trade-off in expressivity and compute by enriching the coefficient calculation, e.g., by making the coefficients dependent on time. FP-FM achieves greatly improved precision and recall relative to baselines across synthetic and image-based datasets, with especially strong gains on unseen distributions.

</details>


### [222] [When Labels Have Structure: Improving Image Classification with Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06274)
*April Chan,Davide D'Ascenzo,Sebastiano Cultrera di Montesano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用类层次结构的损失函数HACE，通过整合预测聚合和祖先标签平滑机制，显著提升了分类模型的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵忽视了类别之间存在的语义层级关系，对待所有错误分类一视同仁，未利用类层次结构提供的先验信息。

Method: 提出了Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE)，包含两个核心组件：1. 预测聚合（Prediction Aggregation）：将模型概率质量沿类层次结构向上传递；2. 祖先标签平滑（Ancestral Label Smoothing）：将真实标签信号沿从叶子节点到根节点的路径进行分发。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、FGVC Aircraft和NABirds数据集上进行验证：端到端训练中，18个组合中有15个性能提升，平均提高4.66%；在DINOv2-Large冻结特征的线性探查中，HACE在所有数据集上均优于基线方法，平均提升2.18%。

Conclusion: HACE是一种有效的分类损失函数改进方案，通过利用类层次结构信息，能够显著提升模型在多种架构和数据集上的分类性能，并表现出比标准交叉熵更强的泛化能力。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy is the default classification loss across virtually all of machine learning, yet it treats all misclassifications equally, ignoring the semantic distances that a class hierarchy encodes. We propose Hierarchy-Aware Cross-Entropy (HACE), a drop-in replacement for standard cross-entropy that incorporates a known class hierarchy directly into the loss. HACE combines two components: prediction aggregation, which propagates the model's probability mass upward through the class hierarchy to ensure that parent nodes accumulate the confidence of their children; and ancestral label smoothing, which distributes the ground-truth signal along the path from the true class to the root. We evaluate HACE on CIFAR-100, FGVC Aircraft, and NABirds in two regimes: end-to-end training across six architectures spanning convolutional and attention-based designs, and linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features. In end-to-end training, HACE improves accuracy over standard cross-entropy in 15 out of 18 architecture--dataset pairs, with a mean gain of 4.66\%. In linear probing on frozen DINOv2-Large features, HACE outperforms all competing methods on all three datasets, with a mean improvement of 2.18\% over the next best baseline.

</details>


### [223] [PACE: Prune-And-Compress Ensemble Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06278)
*Fabian Akkerman,Julien Ferry,Théo Guyard,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE是一个结合了“主动生成”与“智能剪枝”的集成模型压缩框架，旨在克服传统大集成模型难以部署和解释的问题，在保持高预测性能和忠实度的前提下实现模型精简。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型虽然预测性能优异，但因模型规模庞大，导致部署困难、可解释性差以及难以进行鲁棒性验证等问题；现有剪枝与压缩方法存在局限性。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段利用理论支撑的过程主动生成多样化的弱学习器以增强初始集成模型；第二阶段对增强后的集成模型进行剪枝以精简规模。全程保持对原始模型忠实度的可控性。

Result: 实验表明，PACE在性能上优于现有的剪枝和压缩方法，且能够提供基于原则的忠实度保证。

Conclusion: PACE框架通过结合主动生成与剪枝，在保证对原始集成模型忠实度的同时，显著提升了模型压缩与优化的性能。

Abstract: Ensemble models achieve state-of-the-art performance on prediction tasks, but usually require aggregating a large number of weak learners. This can hinder deployment, interpretability, and downstream tasks such as robustness verification. Remedies to this issue fall into two main camps: pruning, which discards redundant learners, and compression, which generates new ones from scratch. We introduce PACE, a framework that interleaves these paradigms in a two-phase strategy. First, new learners are actively generated via a theoretically grounded procedure to enhance the diversity of the initial ensemble. When no more relevant learners can be found, a second phase of pruning is performed on this enriched ensemble. During both operations, PACE allows fine control on the faithfulness to the original ensemble. Experiments show that our method outperforms prior pruning and compression methods while offering principled control of faithfulness guarantees.

</details>


### [224] [INEUS: Iterative Neural Solver for High-Dimensional PIDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06281)
*Jean-Loup Dupret,Davide Gallon,Patrick Cheridito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INEUS是一种新型无网格神经求解器，通过简化非局部积分计算和递归回归策略，有效提升了求解高维PIDEs的效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统针对PIDEs的PINNs方法在处理非局部积分项时存在计算效率瓶颈，且在高维空间下难以扩展。

Method: 提出INEUS框架，通过单跳采样替代显式积分评估，并将PIDE求解重构为递归回归问题序列，避免了全残差的代价高昂的微分计算。

Result: 理论上证明了线性PIDEs下的压缩映射收敛性；数值实验表明该方法在高维线性和非线性问题中均能提供精确且可扩展的数值解。

Conclusion: INEUS是一种高效、可扩展且具备收敛性保证的求解偏积分微分方程（PIDEs）的无网格神经求解器，特别适用于高维空间。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce INEUS, a meshfree iterative neural solver for partial integro-differential equations (PIDEs). The method replaces the explicit evaluation of nonlocal jump integrals with single-jump sampling and reformulates PIDE solving as a sequence of recursive regression problems. Like Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), INEUS learns global solutions over the entire space-time domain, yet it offers a more efficient treatment of nonlocal terms and avoids the computationally expensive differentiation of full PIDE residuals. These features make INEUS particularly well suited for high-dimensional PDEs and PIDEs. Supported by a contraction-based convergence proof for linear PIDEs, our numerical experiments show that INEUS delivers accurate and scalable solutions for various high-dimensional linear and nonlinear examples.

</details>


### [225] [Molecules Meet Language: Confound-Aware Representation Learning and Chemical Property Steering in Transformer-VAE Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06303)
*Zakaria Elabid,Jan Andrzejewski,Bartosz Brzoza,Attila Cangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入混杂感知评估框架，揭示了分子生成模型隐空间中的“化学可控性”常被序列级捷径所虚假推高，校准后证明模型确实能学习到有效的化学性质方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成模型中所谓的“潜在几何意义”经常受到质疑，因为模型可能仅仅学会了序列级别的规律（shortcuts）而非真正的化学规律。研究旨在分析并验证在解开（entangled）的分子隐空间中，潜在的分子性质可控性是否真实存在。

Method: 1. 使用基于SELFIES表示的无监督自回归Transformer-VAE模型；2. 使用线性探针（Linear Probes）基于RDKit描述符提取潜在全局转向方向；3. 引入混杂感知评估（Confound-aware evaluation），包括残差化处理、混杂方向对齐分析及解码分子遍历，以排除SELFIES序列特征对隐空间的干扰。

Result: 1. 发现SELFIES模型的隐空间中存在严重的序列级混杂信号；2. 在通过混杂感知评估校准后，cLogP, FractionCSP3等多种理化性质展示了稳健的单调转向能力；3. 部分性质展现为全局线性方向，而另一些更适合使用局部隐梯度描述。

Conclusion: 分子生成模型的隐空间中确实存在化学意义上的可控方向，但这种可控性必须通过分子解码和严格控制表征层面的混杂因素（如分子长度、Token分布等）进行验证，否则容易受到序列级捷径（shortcuts）的误导。

Abstract: Molecular generative models often assume meaningful latent geometry, but apparent property predictability can reflect sequence-level shortcuts rather than chemical organization. We study this issue in an unsupervised autoregressive Transformer-VAE trained on SELFIES. After training, we freeze the model, fit linear probes to RDKit descriptors, and use the probe weights as candidate global steering directions. To separate chemical signal from SELFIES artifacts, we introduce a confound-aware evaluation based on residualization, confound-direction alignment analysis, and decoded-molecule traversal. This is necessary because SELFIES length, branch tokens, ring tokens, and token entropy are strongly encoded in the latent space. Under this confound-aware evaluation, we find robust monotonic steering for cLogP, FractionCSP3, HeavyAtomCount, TPSA, BertzCT, and HBA. Nonlinear probes further show that some properties admit stable global directions, while others are better described by local latent gradients. Overall, our results show that chemically meaningful steering can emerge in entangled molecular latent spaces, but only when validated through decoded molecules and controlled for representation-level confounds.

</details>


### [226] [When Does $\ell_2$-Boosting Overfit Benignly? High-Dimensional Risk Asymptotics and the $\ell_1$ Implicit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06314)
*Ye Su,Jian Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了 $\ell_1$ 隐式偏差在贪婪集成中导致良性过拟合性能下降，过剩方差的收敛速度由 $\ell_2$ 下的线性速率降至对数速率，并提出了一种基于 Boosting 流次微分机制的早停策略以实现最优预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “良性过拟合”（Benign Overfitting）在 $\ell_2$ 几何空间中已被充分研究，但在通过贪婪集成（greedy ensembles）体现出的 $\ell_1$ 隐式偏差下，由于坐标选择阈值的非线性耦合，使得传统的谱解析工具失效，导致相关研究存在障碍。

Method: 通过耦合凸高斯极小极大定理（CGMT）与双侧截断高斯矩的渐近展开，研究连续时间 $\ell_2$-Boosting 的高维风险；分析 Boosting 流的非光滑次微分动力学。

Result: 在各向同性纯噪声模型下，证明了 $\ell_1$ 偏差下的良性过拟合失效，过剩方差仅以对数速率 $Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$ 衰减；对于尖峰各向同性模型，风险收敛速度也远慢于 $\ell_2$ 的线性速率，同样为对数速率。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需调参的早停规则，该规则在满足特定界限条件下，能够恢复 Lasso 基本不等式，并对 $\ell_1$ 有界信号达到极小极大最优经验预测率。

Abstract: Benign overfitting is well-characterized in $\ell_2$ geometries, but its behavior under the $\ell_1$ implicit bias of greedy ensembles remains challenging. The analytical barrier stems from the non-linear coupling of coordinate selection thresholds, which invalidates standard spectral resolvent tools. To isolate this algorithmic bias, we characterize the high-dimensional risk of continuous-time $\ell_2$-Boosting over $p$ features and $n$ samples. By coupling the Convex Gaussian Minimax Theorem with delicate asymptotic expansions of double-sided truncated Gaussian moments, we analytically resolve the non-smooth $\ell_1$ interpolant. Under an isotropic pure-noise model, we prove that benign overfitting fails at the linear rate: greedy selection localizes noise into sparse active sets, and the excess variance decays at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(p/n))$ for noise variance $σ^2$. We remark that while this localization mechanism should persist in the presence of signals, the exact signal-noise decomposition remains an open problem. For spiked-isotropic designs with $k^*$ head eigenvalues and $r_2 = p - k^*$ tail dimensions, the risk converges to zero when $r_{2} \gg n$, but only at a logarithmic rate $Θ(σ^2/\log(r_2/n))$, which is slower than the linear decay observed in $\ell_2$ geometries. To avoid this slow convergence, we analyze the non-smooth subdifferential dynamics of the boosting flow. This yields a tuning-free early stopping rule that, under a bounded $\ell_1$-path condition, recovers the Lasso basic inequality and attains the minimax-optimal empirical prediction rate for $\ell_1$-bounded signals.

</details>


### [227] [Pro-KLShampoo: Projected KL-Shampoo with Whitening Recovered by Orthogonalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06316)
*Ruotong Sun,Ermin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Pro-KLShampoo 优化器，通过将 KL-Shampoo 的克罗内克预处理与正交化技术结合，利用梯度矩阵的“尖峰-平坦”结构，实现了更优的收敛速度和资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大模型（LLM）预训练中，KL-Shampoo（基于KL散度最小化的预处理）和 Muon（基于梯度动量正交化）是两大代表性优化器，但现有方法缺乏跨技术的整合，未能充分利用梯度矩阵的内在谱结构。

Method: 1. 观察：KL-Shampoo的克罗内克预处理因子表现出“尖峰-平坦”（spike-and-flat）的特征谱；
2. 参数化：限制因子至特定结构（低秩子空间上的完全谱结构 + 剩余维度的共享特征值）；
3. 整合：在剩余维度应用正交化，并证明该方法在代数上能够恢复全量 KL-Shampoo 的预处理功能。

Result: 在 GPT-2 和 LLaMA 等多个预训练尺度（124M-450M 参数）的实验中，Pro-KLShampoo 在验证集损失、单 GPU 峰值内存占用以及达到目标损失的挂钟时间等方面，均一致性地优于原始的 KL-Shampoo。

Conclusion: Pro-KLShampoo 通过将显式克罗内克积预处理与正交化有机结合，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了预训练效率与资源利用率，证明了利用梯度矩阵“尖峰-平坦”谱结构的有效性。

Abstract: Optimizers that exploit the matrix structure of gradients are central to modern LLM pre-training, with two distinct frontiers: explicit Kronecker-factored preconditioning -- most recently KL-Shampoo, which estimates the preconditioner via KL divergence minimization -- and orthogonalization of the gradient momentum, exemplified by Muon and analyzed as steepest descent under the spectral norm. The two routes are typically developed in isolation. We make a structural observation about KL-Shampoo's Kronecker preconditioners: their eigenvalue spectra exhibit a \emph{spike-and-flat} shape -- a few dominant eigenvalues followed by an approximately uniform tail -- across layers and training stages, holding exactly under a rank-$ρ$ signal-plus-noise gradient model. We exploit this structure by restricting one of KL-Shampoo's Kronecker factors to a parametric family aligned with the spike-and-flat shape: full spectral structure on a tracked $r$-dimensional subspace, single shared eigenvalue across the remaining $n-r$ directions. On these directions, we apply orthogonalization. An identity shows that this orthogonalization recovers the algebraic form of full KL-Shampoo's preconditioner. On four pre-training scales (GPT-2 124M / 350M, LLaMA 134M / 450M), Pro-KLShampoo consistently outperforms KL-Shampoo at every subspace rank we test in validation loss, peak per-GPU memory, and wallclock time to reach each loss level.

</details>


### [228] [Eliciting associations between clinical variables from LLMs via comparison questions across populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06335)
*Fabian Kabus,Kian Kordtomeikel,Thomas Brox,Heinz Wiendl,Daiana Stolz,Harald Binder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过“患者比较三元组”和不变因果预测（ICP）从语言模型中挖掘临床因果关系的方法，无需访问模型内部参数即可实现对医学特征关联的稳健推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型涵盖广泛的生物医学文献，包含各种患者群体的特征。研究旨在探讨如何从这些模型中高效、稳健地提取患者特征间的相关性和因果联系，以支持医学决策，同时避免直接询问所带来的偏差。

Method: 1. 提出基于“患者比较三元组”的结构化提问方法，无需访问模型内部激活；2. 构建一个基于该语言模型表示的统计模型，用于估计变量间的相关性；3. 通过引入提示级（prompt-level）的环境偏移，为不同子群体获取相关性估计；4. 利用不变因果预测（ICP）方法，筛选出保守的因果父节点候选连接。

Result: 在慢阻肺（COPD）和多发性硬化症（MS）两个临床领域的实验表明，该方法引出的相关性具有平滑、稳定且临床可解释的特点，且在不同环境下具有统计学意义上的显著差异，实现了有效的因果不变性测试。

Conclusion: 通过三元组比较的间接引出方法，能够从大型语言模型中提取有意义的关联结构，并为从隐性相关性向符合模型回答模式的因果表述迈进提供了一条稳健的路径。

Abstract: The training data of large language models (LLMs) comprises a wide range of biomedical literature, reflecting data from many different patient populations. We investigate how it might be possible to recover information on correlation and causal links between patient characteristics, as a key building block for medical decision making. To avoid the pitfalls of direct elicitation, we propose an approach based on structured comparison questions, specifically patient comparison triplet questions. This is combined with a statistical model for the LLM representation that provides estimates of correlations without access to activations or model internals. Intuitively, we consider how similarity decisions of LLMs based on a first variable are affected by providing information on a second variable for one of the patients being assessed. We then induce prompt-level environment shifts to obtain correlation estimates for different subpopulations, which enables an invariant causal prediction (ICP) approach to obtain conservative candidate parent links. We demonstrate the method in two clinical domains, chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (COPD) and multiple sclerosis (MS). Across prompted environments, the elicited correlations are smooth, stable, and clinically interpretable, yet vary in a statistically significant way that supports downstream invariance testing, such that ICP provides a small set of candidate invariant parent links. These results show that indirect elicitation via triplet comparisons can recover meaningful association structure from LLMs and offer a cautious route from implicit correlations to causal statements that are congruent with LLM answering patterns.

</details>


### [229] [Memory Efficient Full-gradient Attacks (MEFA) Framework for Adversarial Defense Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06357)
*Yuan Du,Mitchel Hill,HanQin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于梯度检查点的、内存高效的全梯度评估框架，旨在克服迭代净化防御在白盒攻击中因近似梯度导致的评测不准确问题，从而提供更可靠的鲁棒性基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对扩散模型和朗之万采样等迭代净化防御的鲁棒性评估往往受限于内存，不得不使用近似梯度计算，这可能导致攻击效果不佳，从而错误地高估防御的鲁棒性。此外，随机特性在评估中常被忽视。

Method: 提出了一种结合梯度检查点（Gradient Checkpointing）的内存高效全梯度评估框架，通过以计算换内存的方式解决了长轨迹反向传播的内存瓶颈，并引入评估协议以控制随机性带来的偏差。

Result: 实验证明该框架能发现先前近似梯度方法忽略的防御漏洞，在 $\ell_{\infty}$ 和 $\ell_{2}$ 范数下实现了更强的白盒攻击，并验证了其在分布外（OOD）鲁棒性探测中的有效性。

Conclusion: 对于迭代式随机净化防御的可靠基准测试，使用精确梯度评估至关重要；近似梯度会导致对防御鲁棒性的高估。

Abstract: This work studies the robust evaluation of iterative stochastic purification defenses under white-box adversarial attacks. Our key technical insight is that gradient checkpointing makes exact end-to-end gradient computation through long purification trajectories practical by trading additional recomputation for substantially lower memory usage. This enables full-gradient adaptive attacks against diffusion- and Langevin-based purification defenses, where prior evaluations often resort to approximate backpropagation due to memory constraints. These approximations can weaken the attack signal and risk overestimating robustness. In parallel, stochasticity in iterative purification is frequently under-controlled, even though different purification trajectories can substantially change reported robustness metrics. Building on this insight, we introduce a memory-efficient full-gradient evaluation framework for stochastic purification defenses. The framework combines checkpointed backpropagation with evaluation protocols that control stochastic variability, thereby reducing memory bottlenecks while preserving exact gradients. We evaluate diffusion-based purification and Langevin sampling with Energy-Based Models (EBMs), demonstrating that full-gradient attacks uncover vulnerabilities missed by approximate-gradient evaluations. Our framework yields stronger state-of-the-art $\ell_{\infty}$ and $\ell_{2}$ white-box attacks and further supports probing out-of-distribution robustness. Overall, our results show that exact-gradient evaluation is essential for reliable benchmarking of iterative stochastic defenses.

</details>


### [230] [Preliminary Insights in Chronos Frequency Data Understanding and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06361)
*Alessandro Pagani,Marco Cominelli,Liying Han,Gaofeng Dong,Sergio Benini,Francesco Gringoli,Mattia Savardi,Mani B. Srivastava,Trevor Bihl,Erik P. Blasch,Daniel O. Brigham,Kara Combs,Lance M. Kaplan,Federico Cerutti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了Chronos时间序列基础模型对频域信息的处理与表征能力，通过MDL探针揭示了其在正弦信号上的频率编码特征及性能边界，为该模型的实际应用提供了科学评估依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管时间序列基础模型（如Chronos）在多任务中表现优秀，但其是否具备处理和内部表征基础信号属性（尤其是频域信息）的能力尚未得到充分验证。需要填补这一空白，以提升模型的解释性和应用评估能力。

Method: 采用受控实验方法，使用离散正弦信号作为输入，利用轻量级在线最小描述长度（MDL）探针分析Chronos解码器架构内部表示中频率信息的存在性和可分离性。

Result: 研究证实了Chronos能够捕获频域信息。结果指出不同频谱范围内捕获质量存在差异，识别出了模型表示质量可能发生退化的特定信号区域。

Conclusion: 通过对Chronos模型在正弦信号上的分析，证实了其内部能够表征频率信息。研究揭示了频谱捕获的特性及潜在的质量退化区域，为时间序列基础模型在信号处理中的应用提供了可解释性基准和优化方向。

Abstract: This paper presents a preliminary analysis of the ability of Chronos foundation model to process and internally represent frequency domain information. Foundation models that process time-series data offer practitioners a unified architecture capable of learning generic temporal representations across diverse tasks and domains, reducing the need for task-specific feature engineering and enabling transfer across signal modalities. Despite their growing adoption, the extent to which such models encode fundamental signal properties remains insufficiently characterised. We address this gap by analysing Chronos under controlled conditions, starting from the simplest class of signals: discrete sinusoids generated at fixed frequencies. Using lightweight online minimum description length probes applied to the decoder architecture, we test for the presence and separability of frequency information in the model's internal representations. The results provide insight into how frequential content is captured across the frequency spectrum and highlight regimes in which representation quality may degrade or require particular care. These findings offer practical guidance for users of Chronos in signal processing and information fusion contexts, and contribute to ongoing efforts to improve the interpretability and evaluation of foundation models for temporal data.

</details>


### [231] [Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06364)
*Xin Peng,Ang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuxPath-FM 引入了任意辅助变量来灵活定义概率路径，打破了传统流匹配对高斯路径的依赖，增强了生成模型在多样化数据分布和特定任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件流匹配方法主要局限于高斯噪声路径，限制了概率路径设计的灵活性和对特定任务（如标签引导生成）的适应性。

Method: 提出了 AuxPath-FM 框架，将轨迹形式定义为 $X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η$，其中 $η$ 可服从任意分布（如高斯、均匀、拉普拉斯或离散分布），并证明了该结构满足连续性方程及边缘一致性训练目标。

Result: 该方法支持多种非高斯先验分布的概率路径，具备独特几何特性，并能够有效支持结构化语义信息的编码，从而实现如标签引导生成等专门化任务。

Conclusion: AuxPath-FM 框架通过在概率路径中引入任意辅助分布，扩展了条件流匹配的范式，为概率路径设计提供了通用的理论基础和更强的应用灵活性。

Abstract: We introduce a new generative modeling framework, \textbf{Flow Matching with Arbitrary Auxiliary Paths (AuxPath-FM)}, which generalizes conditional flow matching by incorporating an auxiliary variable drawn from an arbitrary distribution into the probability path. Unlike prior methods that restrict auxiliary components to Gaussian noise, AuxPath-FM allows the variable $η$ to follow any distribution, producing trajectories of the form $X_t = a(t)X_1 + b(t)X_0 + c(t)η$. We theoretically demonstrate that this construction preserves the continuity equation and maintains a training objective consistent with the marginal formulation. This flexibility enables the design of diverse probability paths using various priors, including Gaussian, Uniform, Laplace, and discrete Rademacher distributions, each offering unique geometric properties for generative flows. Furthermore, our framework allows for specialized tasks such as label-guided generation by encoding structured semantic information into the auxiliary distribution. Overall, AuxPath-FM provides a principled and general foundation for probability path design, offering both theoretical generality and practical flexibility for diverse generative modeling tasks.

</details>


### [232] [A Unified Pair-GRPO Family: From Implicit to Explicit Preference Constraints for Stable and General RL Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06375)
*Hao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Pair-GRPO 框架，通过引入 Soft-Pair-GRPO 和 Hard-Pair-GRPO 两种算法，解决了传统偏好学习中的梯度不稳和高方差问题，并在理论和实验层面验证了其在 LLM 对齐和 RL 任务中的优越表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于成对偏好学习的大语言模型（LLM）对齐方法（如 RLHF）在训练中普遍存在策略更新不稳定、梯度方向模糊、可解释性差以及梯度方差过高等问题。

Method: 提出了 Pair-GRPO 框架，包含两个变体：1. Soft-Pair-GRPO：将 GRPO 中的组归一化标量奖励替换为二元成对偏好奖励，并证明其梯度与标准 GRPO 等价；2. Hard-Pair-GRPO：引入了显式的局部概率约束和受限 KL 拟合，以进一步抑制梯度噪声和策略偏移。

Result: Pair-GRPO 在多个 LLM 对齐基准（HH-RLHF, UltraFeedback）及通用强化学习任务（MuJoCo）中均超越了现有最优基线，展现出更高的对齐质量、偏好获胜率和训练稳定性。

Conclusion: Pair-GRPO 框架在理论上通过梯度等价性和更强的约束优化机制，解决了传统 RLHF 方法在面对成对偏好学习时存在的梯度不稳定和方差高的问题。实验证明其在 LLM 对齐和通用强化学习任务中具有优越的性能和稳定性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) alignment via reinforcement learning from human preferences (RLHF) suffers from unstable policy updates, ambiguous gradient directions, poor interpretability, and high gradient variance in mainstream pairwise preference learning paradigms. To systematically address these limitations, we establish a unified theoretical framework for preference-based RL optimization centered on the Pair-GRPO family, comprising two tightly coupled variants: Soft-Pair-GRPO and Hard-Pair-GRPO. Soft-Pair-GRPO is a minimal modification of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) that replaces group-normalized scalar rewards with binary pairwise preference rewards, retaining GRPO's clipped surrogate and KL-regularized structure. We prove a critical gradient equivalence theorem: under first-order Taylor expansion around the current policy, Soft-Pair-GRPO's gradient is a positive scalar multiple of standard GRPO's gradient, explaining its empirical stability despite discarding continuous reward magnitudes. Building on this foundation, we propose Hard-Pair-GRPO, an advanced variant introducing explicit local probability constraints and constrained KL-fitting optimization to further suppress gradient noise and global policy drift. We provide comprehensive theoretical guarantees for both variants--including monotonic policy improvement, deterministic gradient direction, gradient-variance reduction, and dynamic step-size convergence. Extensive experiments on standard LLM alignment benchmarks (HH-RLHF,UltraFeedback) and the MuJoCo continuous control task HalfCheetah-v4 demonstrate that our Pair-GRPO family consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in alignment quality, human preference win rate, training stability, and generalization to general reinforcement learning. Ablation studies validate the critical contributions of each core component.

</details>


### [233] [Data-Driven Covariate Selection for Nonparametric and Cycle-Agnostic Causal Effect Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06385)
*Ana Leticia Garcez Vicente,Gijs van Seeventer,Saber Salehkaleybar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通用的、与因果图是否有环无关的协变量选择方法，证明了基于条件独立性的方法在处理存在反馈循环的复杂因果模型时依然有效，无需修改算法即可实现跨模型应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断方法通常假设因果模型是无环的（DAG），或者依赖全局结构学习，难以应对存在潜变量混杂和反馈循环的现实场景，且计算效率有限。

Method: 基于条件独立性信息的局部、数据驱动型协变量选择方法，并利用 $\sigma$-环化（$\sigma$-acyclification）下的条件独立性断言不变性进行理论证明。

Result: 理论上证明了现有的条件独立性协变量选择方法在循环模型中同样具有完备性；并在大量合成数据实验中验证了该方法在循环模型中的可靠性能。

Conclusion: 本文通过证明条件独立性准则在循环因果模型中的有效性，提出了一种无需区分循环或非循环结构的统一协变量选择方法，简化了估计因果效应的复杂性。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects from observational data requires identifying valid adjustment sets. This task is especially challenging in realistic settings where latent confounding and feedback loops are present. Existing approaches typically assume acyclicity or rely on global causal structure learning, limiting applicability and computational efficiency. In this work, we study a local, data-driven method for covariate selection based on conditional independence information. While this method is known to be sound and complete in acyclic causal models, its validity in the presence of cycles has remained unclear. Our main contribution is to show that these guarantees extend to cyclic causal models. In particular, our result relies on the invariance of conditional independence assertions under $σ$-acyclification. These findings establish a unified, cycle-agnostic perspective on covariate selection and causal effect estimation, showing that the method applies across cyclic and acyclic settings without modification. Empirically, we validate this on extensive synthetic data, showing reliable performance in cyclic causal models.

</details>


### [234] [Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation: Bridging Exploitation and Imitation at the Token Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06387)
*Nan Jia,Haojin Yang,Xing Ma,Jiesong Lian,Shuailiang Zhang,Weipeng Zhang,Ke Zeng,Xunliang Cai,Zequn Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的缺陷，本文提出了非对称策略蒸馏（AOPD）框架，通过优化负面反馈区域的更新方式，显著提升了模型在数学推理及工具使用任务中的表现与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中，基于优势加权的策略梯度存在高方差、0优势区域梯度消失以及纠正信号不足导致的探索瓶颈等结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出非对称策略蒸馏（AOPD），在正优势区域保持策略强化，而在非正优势区域（即负面反馈区）采用局部散度最小化（Localized Divergence Minimization）代替无效的负强化更新。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试中表现优于标准OPD，在强/弱初始化下的平均增益分别为4.09和8.34；同时在训练期间保持了更高的策略熵，并具备更强的序贯工具使用适配能力。

Conclusion: AOPD通过改进策略梯度更新机制，有效克服了传统策略蒸馏的结构性缺陷，在数学推理任务中表现出显著的性能优势和更好的训练稳定性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own trajectories with token-level teacher feedback and often outperforms off-policy distillation and standard reinforcement learning. However, we find that its standard advantage weighted policy gradient suffers from three structural weaknesses, including high variance updates, vanishing gradients in zero-advantage regions, and exploration bottlenecks when corrective signals are insufficient.We therefore propose Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation (AOPD), which replaces ineffective negative reinforcement with localized divergence minimization in non-positive advantage regions while preserving positive reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that AOPD consistently outperforms standard OPD, with average gains of 4.09 / 8.34 under strong / weak initialization, respectively. AOPD also maintains higher policy entropy during training and better capability retention during sequential tool-use adaptation.

</details>


### [235] [SparseForge: Efficient Semi-Structured LLM Sparsification via Annealing of Hessian-Guided Soft-Mask](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06402)
*Liu Hanzuo,Chaofan Lin,Weixuan Sun,Yulong Wang,Key,Rayying,Mingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SparseForge 是一种高效的后训练半结构化修剪框架，通过 Hessian 微分优化和软掩码退火策略，仅需少量重训练数据即可实现极高的精度恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的半结构化修剪方法依赖大规模的稀疏重训练，计算成本过高，且容易因结构耦合导致显著的性能下降。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SparseForge 的训练后修剪框架，核心技术包括：1. 基于 Hessian 矩阵的初始重要性估计；2. 将软掩码（soft mask）逐步退火（progressive annealing）转化为硬件执行的结构化稀疏掩码。

Result: 在 LLaMA-2-7B 模型（2:4 稀疏度）上，仅使用 5B tokens 重训练即可达到 57.27% 的零样本精度，优于稠密模型（56.43%），并接近使用 40B tokens 的前沿方法（57.52%）。

Conclusion: SparseForge 通过直接优化稀疏掩码（mask），在大幅减少重训练数据量的前提下，实现了与现有最先进方法相当甚至更好的稀疏模型恢复效果，证明了其在平衡模型精度与恢复效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Semi-structured sparsity provides a practical path to accelerate large language models (LLMs) with native hardware support, but post-training semi-structured pruning often suffers from substantial quality degradation due to strong structural coupling. Existing methods rely on large-scale sparse retraining to recover accuracy, resulting in high computational cost.
  We propose SparseForge, a post-training framework that improves recovery efficiency by directly optimizing the sparsity mask rather than scaling up retraining tokens. SparseForge combines Hessian-aware importance estimation with progressive annealing of soft masks into hardware-executable structured sparsity, enabling stable and efficient sparse recovery. On LLaMA-2-7B under 2:4 sparsity, SparseForge achieves 57.27% average zero-shot accuracy with only $\textbf{5B}$ retraining tokens, surpassing the dense model's 56.43% accuracy and approaching the 57.52% result of a state-of-the-art method using $\textbf{40B}$ tokens. Such improvements on the accuracy-efficiency trade-off from SparseForge are shown to be consistent across model families.

</details>


### [236] [FRInGe: Distribution-Space Integrated Gradients with Fisher--Rao Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06404)
*Gabriele Martino,Sebastian Tschiatschek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对集成梯度法（IG）依赖启发式基线和路径导致脆弱性的问题，本文提出 FRInGe，通过在概率分布空间沿 Fisher-Rao 测地线进行归因，实现了更稳定且符合模型属性的特征归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的集成梯度（IG）方法高度依赖于启发式的基线选择和简单的直线路径，导致归因结果脆弱，且容易受到饱和和非线性效应的影响。

Method: 1. 提出 Fisher-Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe)； 2. 在预测分布空间而非输入空间定义参考点（最大熵）和插值路径（Fisher-Rao 测地线）； 3. 利用 Fisher 度量拉回（pullback）转换回输入空间，并引入 KL 和欧几里得信任域进行路径稳定。

Result: 在六种 ImageNet 架构上，FRInGe 显著提升了校准导向的归因指标（特别是 MAS 分数），同时在扰动 AUC 和不忠实度（infidelity）指标上保持了竞争力。

Conclusion: FRInGe 提供了一种几何上更严谨的归因方法，通过在预测分布空间定义路径，改善了传统集成梯度法在基线和路径选择上的启发式依赖，并在评估指标上优于基准方法。

Abstract: Gradient-based attribution methods are model-faithful and scalable, but Integrated Gradients (IG) can be brittle because explanations depend on heuristic baselines, straight-line paths, discretization, and saturation. We propose Fisher--Rao Integrated Gradients (FRInGe), which defines both the reference and interpolation schedule in predictive distribution space. FRInGe replaces input baselines with a maximum-entropy predictive reference and follows a Fisher-Rao geodesic on the probability simplex. The corresponding input-space trajectory is realized through the pullback Fisher metric and stabilized by KL and Euclidean trust regions; attributions are obtained by integrating input gradients along this trajectory. Across six ImageNet architectures, FRInGe most clearly improves calibration-oriented attribution metrics, especially MAS scores, while remaining competitive on perturbation AUC and infidelity.

</details>


### [237] [Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06440)
*Daniel Uyterlinde,Swasti Shreya Mishra,Pascal Mettes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HypCBM 通过将概念映射至双曲空间以捕捉其语义层级，实现了更具解释性、一致性及鲁棒性的概念瓶颈模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念瓶颈模型 (CBM) 在欧几里得空间中将概念视为独立且正交的维度，未能捕捉现实中概念之间存在的复杂语义层级关系。

Method: 提出双曲概念瓶颈模型 (HypCBM)，将概念嵌入双曲空间以建模其语义层级结构，利用蕴含锥 (entailment cones) 实现无需额外监督或训练的稀疏性激活，并引入自适应缩放法则实现层级干预。

Result: 在稀疏性要求极高的可解释性场景下，HypCBM 仅需少量数据即可达到甚至超越传统欧几里得模型，且表现出更好的层级一致性和对输入扰动的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HypCBM 通过利用双曲空间的几何特性，有效解决了传统 CBM 在处理概念层级结构上的不足，在保证解释性的同时提升了模型鲁棒性与层级一致性。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) have become a popular approach to enable interpretability in neural networks by constraining classifier inputs to a set of human-understandable concepts. While effective, current models embed concepts in flat Euclidean space, treating them as independent, orthogonal dimensions. Concepts, however, are highly structured and organized in semantic hierarchies. To resolve this mismatch, we propose Hyperbolic Concept Bottleneck Models (HypCBM), a post-hoc framework that grounds the bottleneck in this structure by reformulating concept activation as asymmetric geometric containment in hyperbolic space. Rather than treating entailment cones as a pre-training penalty, we show they encode a natural test-time activation signal: the margin of inclusion within a concept's entailment cone yields sparse, hierarchy-aware activations without any additional supervision or learned modules. We further introduce an adaptive scaling law for hierarchically faithful interventions, propagating user corrections coherently through the concept tree. Empirically, HypCBM rivals post-hoc Euclidean models trained on 20$\times$ more data in sparse regimes required for human interpretability, with stronger hierarchical consistency and improved robustness to input corruptions.

</details>


### [238] [FedFrozen: Two-Stage Federated Optimization via Attention Kernel Freezing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06446)
*Junye Du,Zhenghao Li,Yushi Feng,Long Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 FedFrozen 框架，通过在联邦学习中冻结 Transformer 的 Query/Key 模块并仅优化 Value 模块，有效缓解了异构数据带来的客户端漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决联邦学习中由客户端数据异构性导致的“客户端漂移”难题，且观察到 Transformer 架构在异构环境下的潜在鲁棒性，从而深入研究注意力机制中各组件对联邦优化的影响。

Method: 提出 FedFrozen 两阶段优化框架：（1）全模型预热阶段：优化整体模型；（2）冻结阶段：固定 Query/Key 模块（确定注意力核），仅优化 Value 模块（执行语义转换）。基于线性注意力机制，从理论上揭示了该过程与正则化核轮廓目标优化及受限值块优化的关系。

Result: 理论分析明确了预热时长与模型性能之间的权衡关系（bias-drift trade-off）；实验结果表明，该方法相比现有方法在异构联邦学习中表现出更高的稳定性和预测准确性。

Conclusion: FedFrozen 通过分离注意力机制中的 Query/Key 和 Value 模块，利用‘先预热、后冻结’的策略，有效缓解了异构联邦学习中的客户端漂移（client drift）问题，提升了 Transformer 模型的训练稳定性和效果。

Abstract: Federated learning with heterogeneous clients remains a significant challenge for deep learning, primarily due to client drift arising from inconsistent local updates. Existing federated optimization methods typically address this issue through objective-level regularization or update-correction mechanisms. Recent studies, however, suggest that Transformer-based architectures may be inherently more robust than conventional models under heterogeneous federated training. Motivated by this observation, we investigate how different parameter components within the attention mechanism influence federated optimization. Specifically, we decompose the attention module into a query/key block, which determines the attention kernel, and a value block, which performs semantic transformation under the induced kernel. Based on this perspective, we propose FedFrozen, a two-stage federated optimization framework that first performs full-model warm-up training and then freezes the query/key block while continuing to optimize the value block. Under a linear-attention formulation, we show that the warm-up stage can be interpreted as an inexact descent procedure on a regularized kernel-profile objective, while the frozen stage reduces to a restricted value-block optimization problem under a fixed attention kernel. Our analysis further reveals an explicit trade-off that governs the choice of warm-up length. Simulations validate the predicted bias-drift behavior, and real-data experiments demonstrate that FedFrozen improves both the stability and effectiveness of Transformer models in heterogeneous federated learning.

</details>


### [239] [Scene-Adaptive Continual Learning for CSI-based Human Activity Recognition with Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06447)
*Wenhan Zheng,Yuyi Mao,Ivan Wang-Hei Ho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 CSI 人体活动识别中的领域迁移难题，提出了 SAMoE-C 模型，通过混合专家架构与高效路由机制，在保证识别精度的前提下实现了低成本、可扩展的持续学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于 CSI 的人体活动识别 (HAR) 在不同物理环境下的域偏移问题。现有的持续学习 (CL) 方法存在扩展性差、依赖大型回放缓冲区或推理成本随领域增长而线性增加的局限性。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SAMoE-C (Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists) 的混合专家系统，利用基于注意力机制的语义路由根据输入动态激活特定专家，并配合一种仅需极小回放缓冲区即可稳定领域判别的训练协议。

Result: 在四场景 CSI 数据集上的实验表明，SAMoE-C 在达到最先进识别精度的同时，显著降低了推理成本。

Conclusion: SAMoE-C 通过模块化专家、选择性激活路由和轻量级训练协议的有机结合，实现了低训练开销、高计算效率且可扩展的跨场景 CSI 人体活动识别，非常适合现实场景部署。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI)-based human activity recognition (HAR) is vulnerable to performance degradation under domain shifts across varying physical environments. Continual learning (CL) offers a principled way to learn new domains sequentially while preserving past knowledge, but existing CL solutions for CSI-based HAR scale poorly with accumulating domains, rely on a large replay buffer, or incur linearly growing inference cost. In this letter, we propose Scene-Adaptive Mixture of Experts with Clustered Specialists (SAMoE-C), which formulates cross-domain CSI-based HAR as a mixture-of-experts system that enables scene-specific adaptation, via an attention-based semantic router that activates only selected experts for each input. Moreover, we develop a novel training protocol, which requires only a tiny replay buffer for stabilizing domain discrimination of the router. Experimental results on a four-scene CSI dataset demonstrate that SAMoE-C approaches the state-of-the-art accuracy, while maintaining a significantly lower inference cost. By jointly combining modular experts, selective activation with router and a lightweight training protocol, SAMoE-C enables scalable cross-domain CSI-based HAR deployment with low training overhead and high computational efficiency in real-world settings.

</details>


### [240] [MINER: Mining Multimodal Internal Representation for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06460)
*Weien Li,Rui Song,Zeyu Li,Haochen Liu,Gonghao Zhang,Difan Jiao,Zhenwei Tang,Bowei He,Haolun Wu,Xue Liu,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MINER 是一种高效的插件模块，它通过提取并融合视觉模型各层的内部特征来增强单向量检索器的性能，实现了性能与检索效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉文档检索方法存在权衡：后期交互模型（Late-interaction）质量高但索引空间和延迟代价大；稠密单向量检索器（Dense single-vector）效率高但性能较差。作者发现模型内层表示中包含未被充分利用的检索相关信号，旨在挖掘这些信号以提升性能。

Method: 提出了 MINER 模块，包含两个阶段：1. 检索对齐层探测（Retrieval-Aligned Layer Probing），通过轻量级探针识别并提取各层中与检索相关的维度；2. 自适应稀疏多层融合（Adaptive Sparse Multi-Layer Fusion），通过性能自适应的神经元级掩码对各层信息进行筛选并融合，最终生成高质量的单向量表示。

Result: 在 ViDoRe V1/V2/V3 基准测试中，MINER 在大多数指标上超越了现有的稠密检索器，nDCG@5 提升最高可达 4.5%，且在保持低存储和高性能的同时，显著缩小了与后期交互模型的性能差距（最小差距仅为 0.2）。

Conclusion: MINER 证明了通过挖掘视觉文档模型中的内部表示，可以在不增加索引存储和推理延迟（保持单向量检索的优势）的前提下，显著提升稠密检索的性能，弥补了与后期交互模型之间的大部分质量差距。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval has become essential for accessing information in visually rich documents. Existing approaches fall into two camps. Late-interaction retrievers achieve strong quality through fine-grained token-level matching but store hundreds of vectors per page, incurring large index footprints and high serving costs. By contrast, dense single-vector retrievers retain storage and latency advantages but consistently lag in quality because they compress all information into a single final-layer embedding. In this work, we first conduct a layerwise diagnostic on single-vector retrievers, revealing that retrieval-relevant signal resides in internal representations. Motivated by these findings, we propose MINER (Mining Multimodal Internal RepreseNtation for Efficient Retrieval), a lightweight plug-in module that probes and fuses internal signals across transformer layers into a single compact embedding without modifying the backbone or sacrificing single-vector efficiency. The first Retrieval-Aligned Layer Probing stage attaches a lightweight probe at each layer, surfacing which dimensions carry retrieval-relevant information. The subsequent Adaptive Sparse Multi-Layer Fusion stage applies performance-adaptive neuron-level masking to the selected layers and fuses the surviving signals into the final dense vector. Across ViDoRe V1/V2/V3, MINER outperforms existing dense single-vector retrievers on the majority of benchmarks, with up to 4.5% nDCG@5 improvement over its corresponding backbone. Compared to strong late-interaction baselines, in some settings MINER substantially narrows the nDCG@$5$ gap to $0.2$ while preserving the storage and serving advantages of dense retrieval.

</details>


### [241] [Diversity Curves for Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06466)
*Katharina Limbeck,Nadja Häusermann,Martin Carrasco,Guy Wolf,Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“多样性曲线”的方法，通过跟踪边收缩过程中图的结构多样性改进，实现了对不同规模图的解释性强、可比较的无监督图表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图级表示方法在处理不同尺度（Cardinality）的图时存在困难，且在无监督背景下难以兼顾可解释性、可扩展性和结构信息捕捉的稳健性。作者旨在通过多尺度结构分析解决这些挑战。

Method: 1. 引入了“图的扩展（spread of graphs）”作为一种新型等距不变量，用于度量几何和结构多样性；
2. 利用边收缩（edge contraction）技术对图进行多级粗化；
3. 将图在不同粗化级别下的结构多样性演变轨迹转化为“多样性曲线（diversity curves）”作为图嵌入表示。

Result: 实验证明，多样性曲线在以下任务中显著优于现有基准方法：1. 不同尺寸模拟图的聚类与可视化；2. 单细胞图的几何特征区分；3. 分子图数据集的结构比较；4. 几何形状的表征。理论分析亦证明边收缩提升了表示的表达能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的“多样性曲线（diversity curves）”提供了一种可扩展、可解释且对图尺寸不敏感的图级表示方法。通过跟踪粗化过程中的结构演变，该方法能够有效地在不同尺度和数据集上捕捉图的几何特性和结构多样性，为无监督图表征任务提供了一个稳健的框架。

Abstract: Graph-level representations are crucial tools for characterising structural differences between graphs. However, comparing graphs with different cardinalities, even when sampled from the same underlying distribution, remains challenging. Unsupervised tasks in particular require interpretable, scalable, and reliable size-aware graph representations. Our work addresses these issues by tracking the structural diversity of a graph across coarsening levels. The resulting graph embeddings, which we denote diversity curves, are interpretable by construction, efficient, and directly comparable across coarsening hierarchies. Specifically, we track the spread of graphs, a novel isometry invariant that is inherently well-suited for encoding the metric diversity and geometry of graphs. We utilise edge contraction coarsening and prove that this improves expressivity, thus leading to more powerful graph-level representations than structural descriptors alone. Demonstrating their utility over a range of baseline methods in practice, we use diversity curves to (i) cluster and visualise simulated graphs across varying sizes, (ii) distinguish the geometry of single-cell graphs, (iii) compare the structure of molecular graph datasets, and (iv) characterise geometric shapes.

</details>


### [242] [Hitting Time Isomorphism for Multi-Stage Planning with Foundation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06470)
*Magnus Victor Boock,Abdullah Akgül,Mustafa Mert Çelikok,Melih Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于算子论的离线强化学习框架 IEL，通过在希尔伯特空间中建模定向到达时间几何，实现了更稳健的长时序路径规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线强化学习方法通常无法捕捉受控马尔可夫过程的定向时间几何结构，导致学到的距离度量缺乏非对称性或不满足三角不等式，限制了在长时序规划中的表现。

Method: 提出了一种基于算子论的表示学习框架，通过希尔伯特空间中的位移几何来建模到达时间（Hitting time）；开发了同构嵌入学习（IEL）算法，结合 HILP 一致性目标与显式到达时间回归，确保学习到的几何结构反映决策时间过程。

Result: 理论上证明了该表示的唯一性及误差界限；实验表明，IEL 在离线迷宫导航任务中优于现有最先进的基准方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 IEL 框架通过显式回归到达时间，成功构建了一种非对称、可组合的希尔伯特空间表示，有效解决了离线强化学习中时间几何结构缺失的问题，并在长时序导航任务中提升了基线性能。

Abstract: We present a new operator-theoretic representation learning framework for offline reinforcement learning that recovers the directed temporal geometry of a controlled Markov process from hitting time observations. While prior art often produces symmetric distances or fails to satisfy the triangle inequality, our framework learns a Hilbert-space displacement geometry where expected hitting times are realized as linear functionals of latent displacements. We prove that this representation exists under latent linear closure and is uniquely identifiable up to a bounded linear isomorphism. For finite-dimensional implementations, we show that global hitting-time error is bounded by one-step transition error amplified by the environment's transient spectral radius. Furthermore, we provide finite-sample guarantees accounting for approximation, statistical complexity, and trajectory-label mismatch. Derived from this theory, we curate Isomorphic Embedding Learning (IEL) as a new goal-agnostic foundation policy learning algorithm that anchors a HILP-style consistency objective with explicit hitting-time regression to ensure that the learned geometry reflects actual decision-time progress. This asymmetric and compositional structure enables robust graph-based multi-stage planning for long-horizon navigation. Our experiments demonstrate that IEL improves the state of the art of learning foundation policy policies from offline maze locomotion data. Our code can be found on https://github.com/MagnusBoock/IEL

</details>


### [243] [Operator-Guided Invariance Learning for Continuous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06500)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Fei Xu Yu,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种名为 VPSD-RL 的框架，通过李群机制自动发现连续强化学习中的非线性价值保持结构，提升了模型对扰动的鲁棒性和训练的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 RL 方法在处理具有复杂潜在结构的连续系统时，往往依赖于预设的对称性，无法自动发现非线性的价值保持结构，导致在数据效率和面对扰动时的鲁棒性不足。

Method: 1. 将连续 RL 建模为受控扩散过程；2. 利用李群（Lie-group）作用和回拉算子（pullback operators）定义价值保持映射；3. 通过确定方程残差最小化（determining-equation residual minimization）学习无穷小生成元；4. 使用常微分方程（ODE）流实现有限变换；5. 在 RL 中引入转换一致性正则化和转换增强。

Result: 在连续控制基准测试中，VPSD-RL 表现出比现有方法更优的数据效率和鲁棒性，并通过理论证明了生成元/奖励错配与最优价值函数稳定性之间的量化关系。

Conclusion: VPSD-RL 提供了一个在连续时间/状态/动作空间中发现价值保持结构的通用框架，通过理论保证了近似对称性的价值稳定性，并在连续控制任务中实现了更高的数据效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with continuous time and state/action spaces is often data-intensive and brittle under nuisance variability and shift, motivating methods that exploit value-preserving structures to stabilize and improve learning. Most existing approaches focus on special cases, such as prescribed symmetries and exact equivariance, without addressing how to discover more general structures that require nonlinear operators to transform and map between continuous state/action systems with isomorphic value functions. We propose \textbf{VPSD-RL} (Value-Preserving Structure Discovery for Reinforcement Learning). It models continuous RL as a controlled diffusion with value-preserving mappings defined through Lie-group actions and associated pullback operators. We show that a value-preserving structure exists exactly when pulling back the value function and pushing forward actions commute with the controlled generator and reward functional. Further, approximate value-preserving structures with rigorous guarantees can be found when the Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman mismatch is small. This framework discovers exact and approximate value-preserving structures by searching for the associated Lie group operators. VPSD-RL fits differentiable drift, diffusion, and reward models; learns infinitesimal generators via determining-equation residual minimization; exponentiates them with ODE flows to obtain finite transformations; and integrates them into continuous RL through transition augmentation and transformation-consistency regularization. We show that bounded generator/reward mismatch implies quantitative stability of the optimal value function along approximate orbits, with sensitivity governed by the effective horizon, and observe improved data efficiency and robustness on continuous-control benchmarks.

</details>


### [244] [PACZero: PAC-Private Fine-Tuning of Language Models via Sign Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06505)
*Murat Bilgehan Ertan,Xiaochen Zhu,Phuong Ha Nguyen,Marten van Dijk,Srinivas Devadas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACZero 是一种新的零阶微调机制，它通过利用梯度符号的一致性，在确保极高隐私保护（互信息 $I=0$）的同时，实现了与非隐私基线相接近的模型微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的差分隐私（DP）框架在极高隐私需求（如 $\varepsilon < 1$）下会导致模型性能急剧下降，无法提供有用的 utility；而 PAC-隐私框架可以通过限制“释放内容对秘密子集的依赖”来实现更好的隐私-可用性权衡。

Method: 提出了 PACZero，一种 PAC-隐私零阶优化机制。通过对子集聚合的零阶梯度进行符号量化，当所有候选子集在更新方向上达成一致（unanimity）时，释放的符号不消耗条件互信息。由此衍生出 PACZero-MI（预算互信息）和 PACZero-ZPL（互信息为 0）两种变体。

Result: 在 SST-2 和 SQuAD 任务的 OPT-1.3B/6.7B 模型上验证，PACZero-ZPL 在互信息 $I=0$ 的极端隐私设定下，其 SST-2 准确率仅比非隐私基线 (MeZO) 差 2.1 个百分点，显著优于现有 DP 方法在此设定下的表现。

Conclusion: PACZero 提供了一种全新的 PAC-隐私框架，允许在大语言模型微调中实现高隐私（甚至在互信息为 0 的极端情况下）的同时保持极高的可用性，填补了差分隐私（DP）在极高隐私设定下实用性不足的空白。

Abstract: We introduce PACZero, a family of PAC-private zeroth-order mechanisms for fine-tuning large language models that delivers usable utility at $I(S^*; Y_{1:T})=0$. This privacy regime bounds the membership-inference attack (MIA) posterior success rate at the prior, an MIA-resistance level the DP framework matches only at $\varepsilon=0$ and infinite noise. All DP-ZO comparisons below are matched at the MIA posterior level. The key insight is that PAC Privacy charges mutual information only when the release depends on which candidate subset is the secret. Sign-quantizing subset-aggregated zeroth-order gradients creates frequent unanimity, steps at which every candidate subset agrees on the update direction; at these steps the released sign costs zero conditional mutual information. We propose two variants that span the privacy-utility trade-off: PACZero-MI (budgeted MI via exact calibration on the binary release) and PACZero-ZPL ($I=0$ via a uniform coin flip on disagreement steps). We evaluate on SST-2 and SQuAD with OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B in both LoRA and full-parameter tracks. On SST-2 OPT-1.3B full fine-tuning at $I=0$, PACZero-ZPL reaches ${88.99\pm0.91}$, within $2.1$pp of the non-private MeZO baseline ($91.1$ FT). No prior method produces usable utility in the high-privacy regime $\varepsilon<1$, and PACZero-ZPL obtains competitive SST-2 accuracy and nontrivial SQuAD F1 across OPT-1.3B and OPT-6.7B at $I=0$.

</details>


### [245] [Is One Layer Enough? Understanding Inference Dynamics in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06510)
*Amir Rezaei Balef,Mykhailo Koshil,Katharina Eggensperger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对表格基础模型进行了深入的机械可解释性分析，发现模型内部普遍存在深度冗余，并据此提出一种参数量仅为20%的循环单层推理架构，实现了模型压缩的高效性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于Transformer的表格模型在性能上表现优异，但其推理机制（特别是内部层级如何协同工作以及信息如何演化）尚不明确，制约了模型的理解与轻量化应用。

Method: 针对6种前沿表格上下文学习（ICL）模型，开展大规模机械可解释性研究，分析层级深度动态、推理阶段划分及潜在空间特征，并据此设计了循环单层模型进行对比实验。

Result: 发现TFM存在明显的深度冗余（Substantial depthwise redundancy），推理过程表现为迭代细化而非深度处理；证明了仅使用 20% 参数的循环单层模型即可达到与原模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了表格基础模型（TFM）推理过程中的深度冗余现象，并验证了通过重用单层权重进行迭代推理（Looped model）的可行性，证明了在保留大部分性能的同时可显著降低参数量。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models (TFMs) dominate small to medium tabular predictive benchmark tasks, yet their inference mechanisms remain largely unexplored. We present the first large-scale mechanistic study of layerwise dynamics in 6 state-of-the-art tabular in-context learning models. We explore how predictions emerge across depth, identify distinct stages of inference and reveal latent-space dynamics that differ from those of language models. Our findings indicate substantial depthwise redundancy across multiple models, suggesting iterative refinement with overlapping computations during inference stages. Guided by these insights, we design a proof-of-concept, looped single-layer model that uses only 20% of the original model's parameters while achieving comparable performance. The code is available at https://github.com/amirbalef/is_one_layer_enough.

</details>


### [246] [On the Implicit Reward Overfitting and the Low-rank Dynamics in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06523)
*Hao Ye,Jisheng Dang,Junfeng Fang,Bimei Wang,Yizhou Zhang,Ning Lv,Wencan Zhang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示RLVR是通过“秩-1组件”专门优化奇异谱来提升数学推理能力的，不仅发现了隐式奖励过拟合现象，还证明了RLVR通过优化采样效率起作用，而非提升模型整体通用知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明RLVR增强的推理能力集中在秩-1组件中，作者旨在深入探究RLVR如何具体塑造模型参数以及RLVR训练的深层机制。

Method: 采用“周期性秩-1替换”（Periodic Rank-1 Substitution）技术，并对模型参数进行奇异值分解（SVD）分析，对RL训练过程中的奇异值分布和奇异向量对齐趋势进行表征。

Result: 1. RLVR训练的模型中秩-1组件仅保留数学推理能力，不含有其他通用知识；2. RLVR通过优化特定的奇异谱起作用，且线性层的奇异值呈现重尾分布；3. 秩-1对应的左奇异向量在训练中趋于更强对齐，表明RLVR本质上在优化采样效率。

Conclusion: RLVR通过专门优化特定的奇异谱来增强数学推理能力，而非全局性强化。该研究揭示了RLVR存在隐式奖励过拟合现象，并为改进RL范式及实现持续学习提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Recent extensive research has demonstrated that the enhanced reasoning capabilities acquired by models through Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) are primarily concentrated within the rank-1 components. Predicated on this observation, we employed Periodic Rank-1 Substitution and identified a counterintuitive phenomenon: RLVR may exhibit implicit reward overfitting to the training dataset. Specifically, the model can achieve satisfactory performance on the test set even when its rewards remain relatively low during the training process. Furthermore, we characterize three distinct properties of RL training: (1) The effective rank-1 component in RLVR don't maintain other model knowledge except mathematical reasoning capability. (2) RLVR fundamentally functions by optimizing a specific singular spectrum. The distribution of singular values of almost all linear layers in RLVR-trained model behaves like heavy-tailed distribution. (3) the left singular vectors associated with rank-1 components demonstrate a stronger alignment tendency during training, which echoes the discovery that RLVR is optimizing sampling efficiency in essence. Taken together, our findings and analysis further reveal how RLVR shapes model parameters and offer potential insights for improving existing RL paradigms or other training paradigms to implement continual learning.

</details>


### [247] [Sequential Design of Genetic Circuits Under Uncertainty With Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06552)
*Michal Kobiela,Diego A. Oyarzún,Michael U. Gutmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的摊销式建模框架，解决了生物电路设计中因分子随机性和环境差异导致的参数不确定性问题，消除了传统贝叶斯设计方法中繁琐的实时计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统设计受限于生物分子反应的内在随机性和不同实验室间的环境变异，而传统的贝叶斯循环优化方法计算代价高昂，导致设计过程存在滞后。

Method: 采用基于微分方程或马尔可夫跳跃过程的模拟器，结合强化学习（RL）策略；引入摊销优化（Amortized Optimization）方法，在参数分布上预先训练模型，从而在无需实验间实时参数推断的情况下实现即时观察反馈。

Result: 在异源基因表达模型和抑制子电路（repressilator）中验证了该框架，结果表明其能高效处理分子噪声和实验条件差异，且显著减少了设计生命周期中的计算延迟。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于强化学习的摊销式优化框架，能够有效地实时适应生物系统的内在随机性和实验条件差异，通过预训练规避了设计周期中昂贵的参数推断，实现了对基因电路的鲁棒设计。

Abstract: The design of biological systems is hindered by uncertainty arising from both intrinsic stochasticity of biomolecular reactions and variability across laboratory or experimental conditions. In this work, we present a sequential framework to optimize genetic circuits under both forms of uncertainty. By employing simulator models based on differential equations or Markov jump processes alongside a reinforcement learning (RL) policy-based approach, our method suggests experiments that adapt to unknown laboratory conditions while accounting for inherent stochasticity. While previous Bayesian methods address uncertainty through iterative experiment-inference-optimization cycles, they typically require computationally expensive inference and optimization steps after each experimental round, leading to delays. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose an amortized approach trained up-front across a distribution of possible uncertain parameters. This strategy sidesteps the need for explicit parameter inference during the design cycle, enabling immediate, observation-based adaptation. We demonstrate our framework on models for heterologous gene expression and a repressilator circuit, showing that it efficiently handles both molecular noise and cross-laboratory variability.

</details>


### [248] [Diverse Sampling in Diffusion Models with Marginal Preserving Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06553)
*Gal Vinograd,Idan Achituve,Ethan Fetaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EDDY 是一种无需额外训练的模型引导机制，通过应用无散度动力学扰动粒子轨迹，在保持扩散模型生成质量的同时显著增强了样本多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在扩散模型和流匹配模型中，如何在保持生成样本高质量的前提下，有效提升样本之间的多样性。

Method: 利用 Fokker-Planck 方程的对称性，构造基于核函数的反对称成对矩阵场（无散度动力学场），对粒子轨迹进行扰动，并针对大规模生成任务（如文生图）提出了计算近似方案。

Result: 在合成分布和文生图任务中，EDDY 显著提升了生成样本的多样性，同时维持了极高的分布保真度（fidelity）。

Conclusion: EDDY 是一种高效且无需额外训练的引导机制，通过维持边缘分布不变的同时通过无散度动力学扰动粒子轨迹，实现了扩散模型生成样本的多样性提升，并在保持样本质量的同时优于主流 baseline。

Abstract: We present EDDY (Exact-marginal Diversification via Divergence-free dYnamics), a guidance mechanism for diffusion and flow matching models that promotes diversity among samples generated while maintaining quality. EDDY exploits symmetries of the Fokker-Planck equation, using drift perturbations that change particle trajectories while preserving the evolving marginal distribution. We instantiate this principle through kernel-based anti-symmetric pairwise matrix fields, constructed from the repulsive directions. The resulting divergence-free dynamics promote diversity at the joint particle level while preserving each particle's marginal distribution without any additional training. As computing the guidance can be computationally expensive in cases such as text-to-image generation with perceptual embeddings, we propose practical approximations as an effective and efficient solution. Experiments on synthetic distributions and text-to-image generation show that EDDY improves diversity while maintaining strong distributional fidelity compared to common baselines.

</details>


### [249] [Optimal Counterfactual Search in Tree Ensembles: A Study Across Modeling and Solution Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06561)
*Awa Khouna,Youssouf Emine,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于约束规划（CPCF）的方法来计算树模型的最优反事实解释，确保建议的更改最小化，并在性能上优于现有的MILP和MaxSAT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的反事实解释可能并非最优（即改变程度不是最小的），导致用户获得不必要且昂贵的补救建议；通过研究在合理性和可操作性约束下为树模型计算最优反事实解释，解决该组合优化问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为CPCF的约束规划（CP）公式，通过将数值特征编码为由划分阈值诱导的区间域，将树模型反事实解释问题转化为有限域组合优化问题；同时对比了MaxSAT和MILP方法。

Result: 在十个数据集和三种树模型上进行的实验表明，CP方法具有最好的伸缩性和性能；MaxSAT在硬投票集合上表现出色；MILP在特定摊销推断场景下仍具有竞争力。

Conclusion: CPCF（基于约束规划的方法）在寻找最优反事实解释方面表现出最佳的综合性能，但不同数学规划范式（CP、MaxSAT、MILP）在不同场景（如硬投票与软投票集合、推断设置）下各有优势。

Abstract: Trust in counterfactual explanations depends critically on whether their recommended changes are truly minimal: suboptimal explanations may vastly overshoot the actual changes needed to alter a decision, and heuristic errors can affect individuals unevenly, giving some users relevant recourse while assigning others unnecessarily costly recommendations. Consequently, we study the problem of computing optimal counterfactual explanations for tree ensembles under plausibility and actionability constraints. This is a combinatorial problem: for a fixed model, counterfactual search boils down to selecting consistent branching decisions and threshold-defined regions under a distance objective. We exploit this structure through CPCF, a constraint programming (CP) formulation in which numerical features are encoded as interval domains induced by split thresholds, while discrete features retain native finite-domain representations. This yields a compact finite-domain formulation that supports multiple distance objectives without continuous split-boundary search. We then place CPCF in a broader comparison across mathematical programming paradigms: we extend a maximum Boolean satisfiability (MaxSAT) formulation, originally designed for hard-voting random forests, to soft-voting ensembles, and compare against the current state-of-the-art mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) optimal approach. Across ten datasets and three types of tree ensembles, we analyze scalability, anytime performance, and sensitivity to distance metrics. We observe that CP achieves the best overall performance. More importantly, our results identify regimes in which the specific strengths of each paradigm make it best suited: CP is most versatile overall, MaxSAT handles hard-voting ensembles particularly well, and MILP remains competitive in amortized inference settings with a moderate number of split levels.

</details>


### [250] [Feature Dimensionality Outweighs Model Complexity in Breast Cancer Subtype Classification Using TCGA-BRCA Gene Expression Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06562)
*Meena Al Hasani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对比了逻辑回归、随机森林和SVM在高维基因表达数据分类中的表现，发现逻辑回归在处理不平衡的癌症亚型数据时性能最稳健，强调了模型简洁性和评估指标对生物分类任务的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因表达数据通常具有高维数和小样本的特点，这给癌症亚型分类任务带来了挑战，需要探索不同模型复杂度和特征选择策略对分类性能的影响。

Method: 基于TCGA-BRCA基因表达数据，对比了逻辑回归、随机森林和支持向量机三种模型；通过改变高变基因数量（50至20,518个）进行特征选择；采用分层5折交叉验证，并使用准确率和宏平均F1分数进行评估。

Result: 所有模型虽整体准确率较高，但在宏平均F1分数上差异明显；逻辑回归在各亚型间表现最稳定且平衡；随机森林在少数类亚型上表现不佳；支持向量机对特征维度较为敏感。

Conclusion: 在处理高维小样本生物医学数据时，模型的简洁性、合理的评估指标以及特征选择至关重要。逻辑回归在平衡分类性能及罕见类别检测方面表现最优，优于随机森林和支持向量机。

Abstract: Accurate classification of breast cancer subtypes from gene expression data is critical for diagnosis and treatment selection. However, such datasets are characterized by high dimensionality and limited sample size, posing challenges for machine learning models.
  In this study, we evaluate the impact of model complexity and feature selection on subtype classification performance using TCGA-BRCA gene expression data. Logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machine (SVM) models were trained using varying numbers of highly variable genes (50 to 20,518). Performance was evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation and assessed with accuracy and macro F1 score. While all models achieved high accuracy, macro F1 analysis revealed substantial differences in subtype-level performance. Logistic regression demonstrated the most stable and balanced performance across subtypes, including improved detection of rare classes. Random forest underperformed on minority subtypes despite strong overall accuracy, while SVM showed sensitivity to feature dimensionality. These findings highlight the importance of model simplicity, evaluation metrics, and feature selection in high-dimensional biological classification tasks.

</details>


### [251] [Directional Consistency as a Complementary Optimization Signal: The GONO Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06575)
*Victor Daniel Gera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有优化器无法识别梯度方向一致性与失真的问题，提出了根据梯度方向一致性动态调整动量的 GONO 算法，提升了优化过程对震荡的敏感度及性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者观察到深层学习中“方向对齐”与“损失收敛”存在解耦现象，即当前的优化器（如 Adam、SGD）仅依赖幅度信号，难以区分和处理平台期、鞍点及真实收敛情况。

Method: 提出 GONO（Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer），通过引入基于连续梯度余弦相似度（cc_t）的机制，动态调整 Adam 的 beta_1 动量系数，从而在方向一致时放大动量，在发生震荡时抑制动量。

Result: cc_t 在震荡检测中达到 F1=1.00，远优于梯度范数；GONO 在 MNIST、CIFAR-10 及 ResNet-18 等基准测试中展现出与 AdamW 相当的性能。

Conclusion: 方向一致性（directional alignment）是深层学习优化中一个被低估的信号；GONO 验证了利用该信号动态调整动量是优化算法设计的一种有效路径。

Abstract: We identify and formalize an underexplored phenomenon in deep learning optimization: directional alignment and loss convergence can be decoupled. An optimizer can exhibit near-perfect directional consistency (cc_t -> 1, measured via consecutive gradient cosine similarity) while the loss remains high or decreases slowly. This observation reveals that existing optimizers such as Adam, SGD, and RMSprop lack explicit mechanisms to exploit temporal consistency in gradient directions, relying instead on magnitude-based signals that fail to distinguish plateaus, saddle points, and genuine convergence. Motivated by this, we introduce GONO (Gradient-Oriented Norm-Adaptive Optimizer), which adapts Adam's momentum coefficient beta_1 based on cc_t: amplifying momentum under directional consistency and suppressing it during oscillation. We prove GONO matches Adam's O(1/sqrt(T)) convergence rate and reduces exactly to Adam when the signal is uninformative. Empirically, cc_t achieves oscillation detection with F1=1.00 (vs. 0.45 for gradient norm), and GONO remains competitive with AdamW on MNIST (98.15%), CIFAR-10 (43.14%), and ResNet-18 (75.44%), establishing directional alignment as a theoretically grounded, practically actionable optimization signal. Code: https://github.com/victordaniel/gono-optimizer

</details>


### [252] [On the Safety of Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06576)
*Xiaoguang Guo,Zehong Wang,Ziming Li,Shawn Spitzel,Soonwoo Kwon,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRL-Safety基准，旨在评估图表征学习算法在腐蚀、OOD、不平衡、公平性和解释性等五个维度上的可靠性。研究发现当前模型（包括基础模型）在应对复杂部署压力时存在明显短板，且安全性表现与模型设计和具体压力因素的耦合密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GRL评估主要关注清洁数据下的传输、适配及任务覆盖，缺乏对图信号、上下文、标签支持、结构组及预测证据受到压力打击时的可靠性研究。因此，作者旨在建立一套全面且标准化的图表征学习安全性评估体系。

Method: 开发了GRL-Safety多轴安全性评估基准。该基准在25个图数据集上评估了12种代表性方法，涵盖拓扑嵌入、监督GNN、自监督模型和图基础模型。评估维度包括：腐蚀鲁棒性、分布外（OOD）泛化、类别不平衡、公平性和可解释性，并采用分维度和分条件的细粒度分析标准。

Result: 通过细粒度分析得出了三项跨轴洞察：1. 安全性取决于表征设计与外界干扰因素的交互影响；2. 图基础模型（GFM）具有轴向特异性优势，而非全能；3. 存在即使是最优模型也难以应对的部署困境，明确了现有技术的局限性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，图表征学习（GRL）的安全性受表征设计与受压图因素相互作用的影响，而非单纯由模型家族决定。GFM（图基础模型）在特定轴向上表现出优势，但并未展现出全面的安全性统治力。研究揭示了当前模型在若干部署场景下仍存在显著的能力缺口，亟需研发新的鲁棒性、适应性及训练目标。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) has evolved from topology-only graph embeddings to task-specific supervised GNNs, and more recently to reusable representations and graph foundation models (GFMs). However, existing evaluations mainly measure clean transfer, adaptation, and task coverage. It remains unclear whether GRL methods stay reliable when deployment stresses affect graph signals, graph contexts, label support, structural groups, or predictive evidence. We introduce GRL-Safety, a multi-axis safety evaluation benchmark for GRL. GRL-Safety evaluates twelve representative methods, spanning topology-only embedding methods, supervised GNNs, self-supervised graph models, and GFMs, on twenty-five graph datasets under standardized evaluation conditions while preserving method-native adaptation. The evaluation covers five safety axes: corruption robustness, OOD generalization, class imbalance, fairness, and interpretation, with per-axis and sub-condition reporting rather than a single aggregate score. Our analysis yields three cross-axis insights that can inspire future research. First, safety behavior is shaped by the interaction between representation design and the stressed graph factor, rather than by method family alone. Second, foundation-era methods show axis-specific strengths rather than broad safety dominance. Third, several deployment regimes remain difficult even for the best evaluated method, revealing capability gaps that require new robustness, adaptation, or training objectives beyond model selection. The benchmark, evaluation protocols, and code are available at: https://github.com/GXG-CS/GRL-Safety.

</details>


### [253] [Distributionally-Robust Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06585)
*Vinit Ranjan,Jisun Park,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Wasserstein分布鲁棒性的算法参数学习框架，统一了L2O与PEP方法，在保证算法稳健性的同时提升了其在多类优化问题上的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统“学会优化”（L2O）方法容易过拟合特定实例，以及“最差情况设计”（PEP）方法可能过于趋于保守的问题，寻求性能与鲁棒性之间的最佳权衡。

Method: 利用Wasserstein分布鲁棒优化理论改进性能估计问题（PEP），通过在内层求解半正定规划（SDP）并结合随机梯度下降（SGD）进行参数更新，实现对一阶优化算法超参数的学习。

Result: 提出了学习算法真实风险的高概率收敛界；在二次规划、LASSO及线性规划等基准测试中，所提方法在样本外测试中显著优于传统的L2O baseline和最差情况PEP设计。

Conclusion: 通过分布鲁棒的PEP框架学得的算法在未知样本上表现优异，且具备可证明的鲁棒性，有效平衡了传统L2O（性能好但可能过拟合）与最差情况PEP（鲁棒但可能保守）之间的权衡。

Abstract: We propose a distributionally robust approach to learning hyperparameters for first-order methods in convex optimization. Given a dataset of problem instances, we minimize a Wasserstein distributionally robust version of the performance estimation problem (PEP) over algorithm parameters such as step sizes. Our framework unifies two extremes: as the robustness radius vanishes, we recover classical learning to optimize (L2O); as it grows, we recover worst-case optimal algorithm design via PEP. We solve the resulting problem with stochastic gradient descent, differentiating through the solution of an inner semidefinite program at each step. We prove high-probability bounds showing that the true risk of the learned algorithm is at most the in-sample L2O optimum plus a slack that shrinks with the sample size, and is no worse than the worst-case PEP bound. On unconstrained quadratic minimization, LASSO, and linear programming benchmarks, our learned algorithms achieve strong out-of-sample performance with certifiable robustness, outperforming both worst-case optimal and vanilla L2O baselines.

</details>


### [254] [Weight-Decay Turns Transformer Loss Landscapes Villani: Functional-Analytic Foundations for Optimization and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06599)
*Abhijit Das,Sayantan Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从泛函分析的角度证明了权重衰减（L2正则化）使 Transformer 的损失函数满足 Villani 强制性准则，并利用 log-Sobolev 不等式在理论上量化了正则化强度对模型收敛速度和泛化性能的影响，并通过在大规模模型上的实验提供了实证支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管权重衰减在大型语言模型中被广泛应用，但其在重塑 Transformer 损失函数地形方面的理论机理尚不明确，缺乏严谨的函数分析特征描述。

Method: 1. 理论推导：利用泛函分析证明 Transformer 目标函数（交叉熵损失+L2正则化）满足 Villani 的强制性能量函数准则；通过 log-Sobolev 和 Poincaré 不等式建立正则化强度与模型收敛速度及泛化界限的联系。2. 实验验证：提出 Villani 诊断指标 $\Psi_s(\theta)$，利用 Hutchinson 迹估计法在超 1 亿参数的模型（GPT-Neo-125M）上进行验证。

Result: 证明了正则化损失函数满足无限可微、二次增长、高斯积分尾等特性；推导出了依赖于正则化强度 $\lambda$ 和模型维度 $d$ 的收敛性常数；实验证实了理论预测的二次增长特性及指数收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了权重衰减（Weight decay）不仅能提升泛化能力，还在数学上为深度学习提供了实现快速 Langevin 混合和基于曲率优化的理论基础，即通过正则化确保了损失函数的良好几何特性。

Abstract: Weight decay is widely used as a regularizer in large language models, yet its precise role in shaping Transformer loss landscapes remains theoretically underexplored. This paper provides the first rigorous functional-analytic characterization of the standard Transformer objective--cross-entropy loss with $L^2$ regularization--by proving it satisfies Villani's criteria for coercive energy functions. Specifically, we show that the regularized loss $\mathcal{F}$ is infinitely differentiable, grows at least quadratically, has Gaussian-integrable tails, and satisfies the differential growth condition $-Δ\mathcal{F} + \tfrac{1}{s}\|\nabla\mathcal{F}\|^{2} \to \infty$ as $\|θ\| \to \infty$ for all $s>0$. From this structure, we derive explicit log-Sobolev and Poincaré constants $C_{\mathrm{LS}} \leq λ^{-1} + d/λ^{2}$, linking the regularization strength $λ$ and model dimension $d$ to finite-time convergence guarantees for noisy stochastic gradient descent and PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds that tighten with increasing $λ$. To validate our theory, we introduce a scalable Villani diagnostic $Ψ_s(θ) = -Δ\mathcal{F} + s^{-1}\|\nabla \mathcal{F}\|^2$ and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson trace probes in models with over 100M parameters. Experiments on GPT-Neo-125M across Penn Treebank and WikiText-103 confirm the predicted quadratic growth of $Ψ_s$, spectral inflation of the Hessian, and exponential convergence behavior consistent with our log-Sobolev analysis. These results demonstrate that weight decay not only improves generalization empirically but also establishes the mathematical conditions required for fast Langevin mixing and theoretically grounded curvature-aware optimization in deep learning.

</details>


### [255] [SoftSAE: Dynamic Top-K Selection for Adaptive Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06610)
*Jakub Stępień,Marcin Mazur,Jacek Tabor,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftSAE通过可微分的Soft Top-K机制，实现了稀疏自编码器活跃特征数的动态调整，解决了固定稀疏度架构无法匹配数据复杂性波动的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏自编码器（如Top-K SAEs）采用固定的稀疏度（即所有输入激活的特征数相同），这忽略了真实世界数据流形复杂度的差异，导致简单输入被注入噪声、复杂输入丢失重要结构。

Method: 提出了一种名为SoftSAE的稀疏自编码器架构，核心是引入了可微分的“Soft Top-K”算子，使模型能够根据每个输入样本的复杂性动态学习并确定活跃特征的数量（即输入依赖的稀疏度k）。

Result: 实验结果表明，SoftSAE能够有效识别具有语义意义的特征，并能根据输入的实际复杂程度自适应选择最合适的特征数量，从而实现更精准的特征解耦与解释。

Conclusion: SoftSAE通过引入动态稀疏度机制，证明了自适应选择活跃特征数量对于提高稀疏自编码器解释模型内部分布的有效性，解决了传统固定Top-K方法缺乏灵活性、难以适应数据复杂性变化的问题。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have become an important tool in mechanistic interpretability, helping to analyze internal representations in both Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Transformers (ViTs). By decomposing polysemantic activations into sparse sets of monosemantic features, SAEs aim to translate neural network computations into human-understandable concepts. However, common architectures such as TopK SAEs rely on a fixed sparsity level. They enforce the same number of active features (K) across all inputs, ignoring the varying complexity of real-world data. Natural data often lies on manifolds with varying local intrinsic dimensionality, meaning the number of relevant factors can change significantly across samples. This suggests that a fixed sparsity level is not optimal. Simple inputs may require only a few features, while more complex ones need more expressive representations. Using a constant K can therefore introduce noise in simple cases or miss important structure in more complex ones. To address this issue, we propose SoftSAE, a sparse autoencoder with a Dynamic Top-K selection mechanism. Our method uses a differentiable Soft Top-K operator to learn an input-dependent sparsity level k. This allows the model to adjust the number of active features based on the complexity of each input. As a result, the representation better matches the structure of the data, and the explanation length reflects the amount of information in the input. Experimental results confirm that SoftSAE not only finds meaningful features, but also selects the right number of features for each concept. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SoftSAE-8F71/.

</details>


### [256] [Crafting Reversible SFT Behaviors in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06632)
*Yuping Lin,Pengfei He,Yue Xing,Yingqian Cui,Jiayuan Ding,Subhabrata Mukherjee,Hui Liu,Zhen Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种通过LCDD将SFT行为压缩至稀疏“载体”的技术，配合SFT-Eraser软提示，实现了在不修改权重的前提下对模型特定行为的因果性控制与选择性抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SFT行为研究多依赖事后分析，缺乏因果性，导致难以在推理阶段精确控制或抑制SFT带来的特定行为。作者旨在探索能否将SFT行为显式地压缩至具有“因果必要性”的稀疏子网中，并实现无需修改权重即可对模型行为进行控制。

Method: 1. 提出“损失约束对偶下降”（LCDD）：联合优化路由掩码（routing masks）和模型权重，将SFT行为压缩至稀疏的“载体”（carrier）中。 2. 提出“SFT-Eraser”：一种通过激活匹配优化得到的软提示（soft prompt），用于抑制或反转载体中的目标行为。

Result: LCDD成功构建了稀疏载体，在保留目标行为的同时，配合SFT-Eraser能显著抑制该行为。消融实验证实，对于未经LCDD结构化的标准SFT模型，同样的激活匹配方法失效，证明了稀疏结构是实现行为可控性的核心。

Conclusion: 通过LCDD构建稀疏载体不仅能保留SFT诱导的行为，还为其反转提供了因果必要的基础。研究表明，结构稀疏性是实现模型行为选择性抑制的关键前提，为控制大模型行为提供了新路径。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) induces new behaviors in large language models, yet imposes no structural constraint on how these behaviors are distributed within the model. Existing behavior interpretation methods, such as circuit attribution approaches, identify sparse subnetworks correlated with SFT-induced behaviors post-hoc. However, such correlations do not imply *causal necessity*, limiting the ability to selectively control SFT-induced behaviors at inference time. We pursue an alternative by asking: can an SFT-induced behavior be deliberately compressed into a sparse, mechanistically necessary subnetwork, termed a *carrier*, while remaining controllable at inference time without weight modification? We propose (a) **Loss-Constrained Dual Descent (LCDD)**, which constructs such carriers by jointly optimizing routing masks and model weights under an explicit utility budget, and (b) **SFT-Eraser**, a soft prompt optimized via activation matching on extracted carrier channels, to reverse the SFT-induced behavior. Across safety, fixed-response, and style behaviors on multiple model families, LCDD yields sparse carriers that preserve target behaviors while enabling strong reversion when triggered by SFT-Eraser. Ablations further establish that the sparse structure is the key precondition for reversal: the same trigger optimization fails on standard SFT models, confirming that structure rather than trigger design is the operative factor. These results provide direct evidence that the learned carriers are causally necessary for the behaviors, pointing to a new direction for systematically localizing and selectively suppressing SFT-induced behaviors in deployed models.

</details>


### [257] [Inductive Venn-Abers and related regressors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06646)
*Ivan Petej,Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Venn-Abers预测器扩展至无界回归领域，结合共形预测技术，并证明其在大型数据集下优于传统回归方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Venn-Abers预测器虽具备优良的预测有效性，但其应用范围局限于二分类及有界回归问题，无法处理更广泛的无界回归场景。

Method: 通过引入共形预测（Conformal Prediction）技术，将仅适用于二分类和有界回归的Venn-Abers预测器扩展至无界回归框架下。

Result: 通过模拟和实证研究表明，该通用化方法在处理无界回归任务时表现良好，能够提升大型数据集下的预测效率。

Conclusion: 将Venn-Abers预测器推广至无界回归是可行的，且在较大训练集上，由此导出的点回归器在预测效率上优于标准回归器。

Abstract: Venn-Abers predictors are probabilistic predictors that enjoy appealing properties of validity, but their major limitation is that they are applicable only to the case of binary classification, with a recent extension to bounded regression. We generalize them to the case of unbounded regression, which requires adding an element of conformal prediction. In our simulation and empirical studies we investigate the predictive efficiency of point regressors derived from Venn-Abers regressors and argue that they somewhat improve the predictive efficiency of standard regressors for larger training sets.

</details>


### [258] [Optimizer-Model Consistency: Full Finetuning with the Same Optimizer as Pretraining Forgets Less](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06654)
