*Yuxing Liu,Jianyu Wang,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“优化器-模型一致性”原则，指出在SFT阶段使用与预训练相同的优化器能获得更好的学习-遗忘平衡，并揭示了优化器通过规则化权重空间对模型性能产生的深层影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究大型语言模型（LLM）训练中优化器选择对预训练与微调阶段的影响，特别是探索如何提升微调效果并缓解模型在微调过程中的“灾难性遗忘”现象。

Method: 通过受控实验与理论分析，探讨优化器对模型权重的规则化效应；对比Muon与AdamW在预训练与SFT全阶段的表现；利用合成语言模型实验分析特定优化器对模式习得的影响。

Result: 提出了“优化器-模型一致性”（Optimizer-Model Consistency）现象；发现优化器通过规则化激活值改变预训练检查点周围的损失景观，使用同款优化器能通过特定的权重更新结构有效降低遗忘。

Conclusion: 在SFT阶段使用与预训练阶段相同的优化器能够实现更优的“学习-遗忘权衡”（即在保持高性能的同时减少对预训练知识的遗忘）；此外，研究强调了Muon优化器相较于AdamW在数据受限的SFT任务中可能表现不佳，因为它更倾向于死记硬背而非泛化模式学习。

Abstract: Optimizers play an important role in both pretraining and finetuning stages when training large language models (LLMs). In this paper, we present an observation that full finetuning with the same optimizer as in pretraining achieves a better learning-forgetting tradeoff, i.e., forgetting less while achieving the same or better performance on the new task, than other optimizers and, possibly surprisingly, LoRA, during the supervised finetuning (SFT) stage. We term this phenomenon optimizer-model consistency. To better understand it, through controlled experiments and theoretical analysis, we show that: 1) optimizers can shape the models by having regularization effects on the activations, leading to different landscapes around the pretrained checkpoints; 2) in response to this regularization effect, the weight update in SFT should follow some specific structures to lower forgetting of the knowledge learned in pretraining, which can be obtained by using the same optimizer. Moreover, we specifically compare Muon and AdamW when they are employed throughout the pretraining and SFT stages and find that Muon performs worse when finetuned for reasoning tasks. With a synthetic language modeling experiment, we demonstrate that this can come from Muon's strong tendency towards rote memorization, which may hurt pattern acquisition with a small amount of data, as for SFT.

</details>


### [259] [UniPool: A Globally Shared Expert Pool for Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06665)
*Minbin Huang,Han Shi,Chuanyang Zheng,Yimeng Wu,Guoxuan Chen,Xintong Yu,Yichun Yin,Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniPool打破了MoE“每层独立专家”的传统范式，通过引入全局共享专家池和特定路由机制，实现了参数的亚线性增长与更高效率的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE架构强制每层分配独立专家导致参数随深度线性增长，研究发现深层模型对特定层的专家路由存在冗余，即深层专家容量利用效率低下。

Method: 提出了UniPool架构，将按层分配的专家机制转变为共享专家池，并引入了池级别的辅助损失（pool-level auxiliary loss）和NormRouter以确保训练的稳定性与负载均衡。

Result: 在五个不同量级的模型实验中，UniPool不仅在整体验证损失和困惑度上优于基线（最大降幅0.0386），且仅需约42%-67%的专家参数预算即可达到或超越基线性能。

Conclusion: UniPool证明了专家容量可以作为全局架构预算处理，允许专家参数实现亚线性增长，同时保持比传统MoE更高的训练效率和任务性能。

Abstract: Modern Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures allocate expert capacity through a rigid per-layer rule: each transformer layer owns a separate expert set. This convention couples depth scaling with linear expert-parameter growth and assumes that every layer needs isolated expert capacity. However, recent analyses and our routing probe challenge this allocation rule: replacing a deeper layer's learned top-k router with uniform random routing drops downstream accuracy by only 1.0-1.6 points across multiple production MoE models. Motivated by this redundancy, we propose UniPool, an MoE architecture that treats expert capacity as a global architectural budget by replacing per-layer expert ownership with a single shared pool accessed by independent per-layer routers. To enable stable and balanced training under sharing, we introduce a pool-level auxiliary loss that balances expert utilization across the entire pool, and adopt NormRouter to provide sparse and scale-stable routing into the shared expert pool. Across five LLaMA-architecture model scales (182M, 469M, 650M, 830M, and 978M parameters) trained on 30B tokens from the Pile, UniPool consistently improves validation loss and perplexity over the matched vanilla MoE baselines. Across these scales, UniPool reduces validation loss by up to 0.0386 relative to vanilla MoE. Beyond raw loss improvement, our results identify pool size as an explicit depth-scaling hyperparameter: reduced-pool UniPool variants using only 41.6%-66.7% of the vanilla expert-parameter budget match or outperform layer-wise MoE at the tested scales. This shows that, under a shared-pool design, expert parameters need not grow linearly with depth; they can grow sublinearly while remaining more efficient and effective than vanilla MoE. Further analysis shows that UniPool's benefits compose with finer-grained expert decomposition.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [260] [Maximizing Rollout Informativeness under a Fixed Budget: A Submodular View of Tree Search for Tool-Use Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05262)
*Yuelin Hu,Zhenbo Yu,Zhengxue Cheng,Wei Liu,Li Song*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: InfoTree 通过将中间状态选择建模为子模最大化问题，设计了 UUCB 指导的训练时树搜索框架，显著提升了在有限计算预算下大模型的复杂推理与工具使用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对固定预算下的 Rollout 信息量 (RIFB) 问题，指出传统的预算无关采样方式在处理硬提示 (hard prompts) 时存在性能崩塌，旨在通过更优的计算资源选材策略提升强化学习 (GRPO) 的训练效率。

Method: 1. 将中间状态选择重构为单调子模最大化问题，利用贪心算法实现 1-1/e 近似保证；2. 提出不确定性感知置信上界 (UUCB) 作为边缘增益；3. 设计 InfoTree 框架，结合了 UUCB、自适应预算分配器 (ABA) 和异步推测扩展 (Speculative Expansion) 技术。

Result: 1. ABA 将混合结果比例从 58.1% 提升至 76.3%（预算开销 <5%）；2. 异步推测扩展将墙上时钟过载从 14.3% 降至 4.8%；3. 在九个基准测试中超越了 GRPO、DeepSearch、Tree-GRPO 等现有主流方法，且表现出极强的超参数稳健性。

Conclusion: InfoTree 引入了一种基于子模最大化的状态选择策略，并通过 UUCB 和 ABA 实现了高效的训练时树搜索优化。验证表明，该方法在数学推理、智能体任务等领域显著优于现有的 GRPO 变体，且具有极高的鲁棒性。

Abstract: We formalize Rollout Informativeness under a Fixed Budget (RIFB) as the expected non-vanishing policy-gradient mass that a tool-use rollout set injects into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). We prove that any budget-agnostic independent sampler suffers a collapse rate bounded away from zero for hard prompts regardless of the budget. Motivated by this, we recast intermediate state selection as a monotone submodular maximization problem, where a greedy one-step selector enjoys a 1 minus 1/e approximation guarantee.
  Our Uncertainty-aware Upper Confidence Bound (UUCB) terms arise as closed-form marginal gains of this objective. This turns the token-level entropy bonus from an empirical trick into an analytic consequence of the formulation. We present InfoTree, a training-time tree-search framework coupling UUCB with a learned Adaptive Budget Allocator (ABA) and an asynchronous Speculative Expansion scheme.
  ABA rescues prompts whose initial tree is wasted on uniform outcomes, lifting the mixed-outcome ratio from 58.1 percent to 76.3 percent with less than 5 percent budget overhead. Speculative Expansion reduces wall-clock overhead from 14.3 percent to 4.8 percent by tolerating bounded staleness in UUCB scores.
  Across nine benchmarks spanning math reasoning (AIME 2024 and 2025, MATH-500, OlympiadBench, USAMO), web-search agents (GAIA, HLE-100, BrowseComp-lite), and tool-rich coding and OS agents (APPS-verified, AgentBench-OS), InfoTree outperforms flat GRPO, DeepSearch, Tree-GRPO, AT2PO, CW-GRPO, and RC-GRPO. Head-to-head compositions with Tree-GRPO prefix sharing and CW-GRPO contribution weights deliver further gains, confirming that our selector operates orthogonally to rollout reuse and trajectory re-weighting. A 5 by 5 by 5 robustness grid reveals that over three quarters of the hyperparameter space lies on a performance plateau, confirming UUCB robustness.

</details>


### [261] [Forecasting Oncology Demand Trends with Boosting-Based Bayesian Conjugate Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05270)
*Ademir Batista dos Santos Neto,Tiago Alessandro Espinola Ferreira,Paulo Renato Alves Firmino*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对肿瘤诊疗需求预测的贝叶斯增强模型，通过结合泊松过程与残差增强机制，在保持模型分析可处理性的同时，显著提高了趋势预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗时间序列需求预测对于资源规划至关重要，但现有的线性和深度学习方法在应对趋势变动及预测准确性方面存在局限，需要一种既能保持数学可解释性又能灵活适应趋势变化的模型。

Method: 提出一种基于贝叶斯框架的预测方法，将每周预约量建模为泊松过程，并为需求率设定Gamma先验；引入基于Gamma-对数正态共轭结构的残差增强机制（Residual-based boosting），用以跟踪短期和长期趋势偏移。

Result: 在巴西卡里里（Cariri）的真实肿瘤服务数据测试中，该模型表现超越了线性回归、ARIMA、LSTM和XGBoost等基准；在某些案例中，其趋势方向预测的准确率较第二优模型提升了38.25%。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯增强模型在肿瘤诊疗需求趋势预测中表现卓越，通过结合共轭先验的数学易处理性和残差增强机制，显著提升了对复杂时间序列趋势变化的捕捉能力，优于传统统计方法及深度学习模型。

Abstract: Accurate trend forecasting in healthcare time series is essential for planning and resource allocation. This paper proposes a Bayesian framework for predicting oncology demand trends, modeling weekly appointments as a Poisson process with a Gamma prior to the demand rate. To enhance adaptability and capture persistent directional patterns, we incorporate a residual-based boosting mechanism grounded in a Gamma-Log-Normal conjugate structure. This boosting approach allows the model to track both short- and long-term trend shifts while maintaining the analytical tractability of conjugate Bayesian updating. The methodology was evaluated on real oncology service data from Cariri, Ceara, Brazil, and compared against established baselines, including linear regression, ARIMA, naive forecasting, LSTM neural networks, and XGBoost. Results showed that the proposed model outperforms competing methods in trend detection accuracy, with gains in terms of percentage of correct direction of 38.25% in relation to the second best approach in some cases.

</details>


### [262] [Convexity in Disguise: A Theoretical Framework for Nonconvex Low-Rank Matrix Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05446)
*Chengyu Cui,Gongjun Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种新的理论框架，通过引入“良性正则化器”揭示了非凸低秩矩阵估计方法中隐含的局部强凸性，从而无需额外正则化即可为广泛的此类算法提供统一的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非凸低秩矩阵估计分析往往依赖于不必要的额外正则化，且缺乏通用性，多局限于特定模型，导致理论分析难以推广到复杂系统。

Method: 提出了一种“良性正则化器（benign regularizer）”分析框架；该方法不改变原始算法的更新规则，而是通过该工具将非凸问题转化为等价的局部强凸（locally strongly convex）形式。

Result: 通过良性正则化框架，成功解释了非凸过程在低秩矩阵估计中表现良好的根本机制，并为该类算法提供了更广泛、更具普适性的理论收敛保证。

Conclusion: 该文揭示了非凸低秩矩阵估计过程中隐含的“伪装凸性（disguised convexity）”，通过良性正则化工具，为分析非凸算法提供了一个统一且通用的理论框架，证明了无需额外显式正则化也能获得收敛保证。

Abstract: Nonconvex methods have emerged as a dominant approach for low-rank matrix estimation, a problem that arises widely in machine learning and AI for learning and representing high-dimensional data. Existing analyses for these methods often require additional regularization to mitigate nonconvexity, even though such regularization is often unnecessary in practice. Moreover, most analyses rely on problem-specific arguments that are difficult to generalize to more complex settings. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework for studying nonconvex procedures across a broad class of low-rank matrix estimation problems. Rather than focusing on a specific model, we reveal a fundamental mechanism that explains why nonconvex procedures can behave well in low-rank estimation. Our key device is a {\it benign regularizer} that does not alter the original update rule, but yields an equivalent locally strongly convex formulation of the algorithm. This perspective uncovers a disguised convexity inherent in the nonconvex procedure and provides a new route to theoretical guarantees for nonconvex low-rank matrix estimation.

</details>


### [263] [Relaxed Sparsest-Permutation Formulation for Causal Discovery at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05568)
*Sunmin Oh,Sang-Yun Oh,Gunwoong Park*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对大规模因果结构学习的计算复杂度问题，本文提出了一种基于稀疏 Cholesky 分解的 SCOPE 方法，通过支持水平松弛技术实现了高效、可扩展的因果结构恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果结构学习方法在大规模数据集上计算成本过高，且精确的 Cholesky 分解对于结构恢复并非必要。

Method: 提出一种支持水平（support-level）松弛方法，通过在精度支持筛选图上搜索稀疏三角因子，利用掩码零填充不完全 Cholesky 分解（masked zero-fill incomplete Cholesky factorization）高效评估候选排序。

Result: 在人口统计水平上建立了马尔可夫等价类（MEC）恢复的稳健性，实验证明 SCOPE 在保持准确性的前提下，运行速度显著快于基准方法并可扩展至超大规模变量集。

Conclusion: SCOPE 框架通过支持水平松弛有效地解决了因果结构学习的规模瓶颈问题，在保持与现有基准相当的准确性的同时，实现了卓越的计算效率，可扩展至 1 万个变量。

Abstract: Despite the growing availability of large datasets, causal structure learning remains computationally prohibitive at scale. We revisit sparsest-permutation learning for linear structural equation models and show that exact Cholesky factorization is unnecessary for structure recovery. This observation motivates a support-level relaxation that searches for sparse triangular factors over a precision-support screening graph. The relaxed formulation can be efficiently evaluated via masked zero-fill incomplete Cholesky factorization, enabling scalable comparison of candidate orderings. At the population level, we establish soundness for Markov equivalence class (MEC) recovery under no-cancellation and sparsest Markov representation assumptions, as well as robustness to ordering misspecification. Motivated by these guarantees, we introduce SCOPE, a sparse-Cholesky pipeline that provides a scalable implementation of the relaxed formulation. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets demonstrate that SCOPE matches the MEC recovery accuracy of substantially slower baselines, while achieving significantly reduced runtime and scaling to 10k variables.

</details>


### [264] [In-Context Positive-Unlabeled Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05591)
*Siyan Liu,Yi Chang,Manli Cheng,Qinglong Tian,Pengfei Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 为了解决现有 PU 学习方法计算成本高、难以快速部署的问题，作者提出了 PUICL——一种基于 Transformer 的预训练模型，通过上下文学习实现了一次前向传递即可完成从正样本和未标注样本中进行分类，并在多个基准测试中展现了卓越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PU 学习方法通常需要针对特定数据集进行训练或迭代优化，难以满足快速解决多任务或资源受限场景下的需求。

Method: 提出 PUICL 模型：一种基于 Transformer 的架构，通过在随机生成的结构因果模型（SCMs）产生的合成 PU 数据集上进行预训练，使其在推理阶段能够通过上下文学习直接处理带有正样本和未标注样本的输入，无需梯度更新。

Result: 在 20 个半监督 PU 基准测试中，PUICL 在平均 AUC 和准确率方面优于四种标准 PU 学习基线，且 F1 分数具有竞争力。

Conclusion: PUICL 证明了上下文学习（In-context learning）范式可以有效扩展至半监督 PU 学习领域，通过一次前向传递即可实现高效且通用的 PU 分类，无需额外的微调或迭代优化。

Abstract: Positive-unlabeled (PU) learning addresses binary classification when only a set of labeled positives is available alongside a pool of unlabeled samples drawn from a mixture of positives and negatives. Existing PU methods typically require dataset-specific training or iterative optimization, which limits their applicability when many tasks must be solved quickly or with little tuning. We introduce PUICL, a pretrained transformer that solves PU classification entirely through in-context learning. PUICL is pretrained on synthetic PU datasets generated from randomly instantiated structural causal models, exposing it to a wide range of feature-label relationships and class-prior configurations. At inference time, PUICL receives the labeled positives and the unlabeled samples as a single input and returns class probabilities for the unlabeled rows in one forward pass, with no gradient updates or per-task fitting. On 20 semi-synthetic PU benchmarks derived from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, OpenML, and scikit-learn, PUICL outperforms four standard PU learning baselines in average AUC and accuracy, and is competitive on F1-score. These results show that the in-context learning paradigm extends naturally beyond fully supervised tabular prediction to the semi-supervised PU setting.

</details>


### [265] [Spherical Flows for Sampling Categorical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05629)
*Jannis Chemseddine,Gregor Kornhardt,Gabriele Steidl*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在 $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ 超球面上进行离散序列建模的生成模型，利用 vMF 分布的径向对称性简化了得分计算，并在实验中验证了其在复杂离散序列生成任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的离散序列生成模型通常在欧几里得空间或概率单纯形上操作，难以处理离散分布的特殊约束，作者提出利用超球面空间及其对应的 vMF 分布来改善生成模型的建模能力和采样效果。

Method: 1. 将离散序列映射到超球面 $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ 空间；2. 利用 vMF 分布构建噪声过程；3. 利用径向对称性将 $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ 上的连续性方程简化为余弦相似度上的标量 ODE 以求解速度场；4. 采用后验加权切向量和的方式实现边际速度和得分计算；5. 支持 ODE 和 PC (Predictor-Corrector) 两种采样方式。

Result: 与测地线（geodesic）和欧几里得空间方法相比，vMF 指导下的扩散路径在 Sudoku 和语言模型任务中表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在超球面空间上使用 vMF 分布进行离散序列生成建模是有效且优越的，能够通过解决标量常微分方程获得闭式条件得分，并显著提升了在 Sudoku 和语言建模任务上的性能。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning generative models for discrete sequences in a continuous embedding space. Whereas prior approaches typically operate in Euclidean space or on the probability simplex, we instead work on the sphere $\mathbb S^{d-1}$. There the von Mises-Fisher (vMF) distribution induces a natural noise process and admits a closed-form conditional score. The conditional velocity is in general intractable. Exploiting the radial symmetry of the vMF density we reduce the continuity equation on $\mathbb S^{d-1}$ to a scalar ODE in the cosine similarity, whose unique bounded solution determines the velocity. The marginal velocity and marginal score on $(\mathbb S^{d-1})^L$ both decompose into posterior-weighted tangent sums that differ only by per-token scalar weights. This gives access to both ODE and predictor-corrector (PC) sampling. The posterior is the only learned object, trained by a cross-entropy loss. Experiments compare the vMF path against geodesic and Euclidean alternatives. The combination of vMF and PC sampling significantly improves results on Sudoku and language modeling.

</details>


### [266] [Spectral Lens: Activation and Gradient Spectra as Diagnostics of LLM Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05683)
*Andy Zeyi Liu,Elliot Paquette,John Sous*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文通过激活协方差和梯度SVD谱分析，揭示了损失值之外的“隐藏”学习动态，证明了批大小会显著影响模型表征几何，并提出了一套能够预测模型下游性能及区分学习逻辑改进的有效诊断协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的损失函数（Loss）和吞吐量（Throughput）指标无法揭示语言模型训练内部表征的差异；作者旨在通过谱测量手段开发一种实用诊断协议，以探究隐藏的学习机制差异。

Method: 基于modded-NanoGPT框架构建受控解码器模型，引入激活协方差（Activation Covariance）和单样本梯度SVD（Per-sample Gradient SVD）进行双视角谱分析，并通过构建机理模型验证实验观察。

Result: 1. 批大小作为表征几何的潜决定因素：相同损失的模型可能拥有不同的激活谱；2. 训练初期的激活协方差长尾分布可预测下游Token效率；3. 激活谱头部（领先模式）与梯度谱的演变能有效区分架构改进带来的学习增益与执行优化。上述规律在不同深度（12-48层）的模型中保持一致。

Conclusion: 谱分析（激活协方差与梯度SVD）是诊断大模型内部学习过程的有效工具。研究证明了批大小（Batch Size）对表征几何具有决定性影响，且谱特征能够预测模型下游性能及区分学习机制与执行效率增益，为通过观测内部状态理解模型训练行为提供了理论与实验支撑。

Abstract: Training loss and throughput can hide distinct internal representation in language-model training. To examine these hidden mechanics, we use spectral measurements as practical and operational diagnostics. Using a controlled family of decoder-only models adapted from the modded NanoGPT codebase, we introduce an empirical protocol based on activation covariance and per-sample gradient SVD spectra. This dual-view reveals three empirical findings and one mechanistic explanation. First, batch size acts as a latent determinant of representation geometry: runs that reach equal loss settle into systematically distinct activation spectra. Second, the activation covariance tail measured early in training reliably forecasts downstream token efficiency. Third, movement of the activation spectrum head (leading modes), together with gradient spectra, characterizes underlying learning-dynamics changes, separating learning-side architectural improvements from primarily execution-side gains. These predictive and diagnostic signals persist across the 12-, 36-, and 48-layer model tiers. Finally, a mechanistic model proves the main observations and explains how activation covariance spectra correlate with task-aligned feature learning.

</details>


### [267] [Fourier Feature Methods for Nonlinear Causal Discovery: FFML Scoring and FFCI Testing in Mixed Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05743)
*Joseph D. Ramsey*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对非线性因果发现中 GP 方法计算昂贵的问题，提出了 FFML 和 FFCI 两种基于随机傅里叶特征（RFF）的高效方法，显著降低了计算复杂度，并在处理混合数据时表现出优异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于高斯过程（GP）边际似然和核条件独立性（CI）检验的非线性因果发现方法在理论上具有优势，但在处理大规模数据时计算成本过高，无法直接应用。

Method: 1. FFML（傅里叶特征边际似然）：利用有限维特征表示近似 GP 边际似然，通过内积计算降低核矩阵运算复杂度；支持混合数据，使用 Kronecker 或 Hadamard 积处理离散变量。
2. FFCI（傅里叶特征条件独立性测试）：对变量分别进行特征化（持续变量用 RFF/ORF，离散变量用 Cholesky 分解），通过特征空间内的岭回归残差化实现条件独立性检验，测试统计量为残差化互协方差的 Frobenius 范数。

Result: 1. FFML 在非线性数据上优于线性及核岭回归基准方法。
2. FFCI 与主流 RCIT 方法相比，在保持具有竞争力的精度的同时，实现了更好的召回率和更低的结构汉明距离（SHD），且运行速度提升至原来的三倍。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套基于随机傅里叶特征（RFF）的工具包，实现了非线性因果发现的高效化。FFML提供了一种可扩展的评分机制，而FFCI提供了一种高效的非线性条件独立性检验，两者在基准测试中均展现出优于现有方法的性能和计算效率。

Abstract: Gaussian process marginal likelihood scores and kernel conditional independence tests are theoretically appealing for nonlinear causal discovery but computationally prohibitive at scale. We present two complementary RFF-based methods forming a practical toolkit for score-based, constraint-based, and hybrid causal discovery.
  The Fourier Feature Marginal Likelihood (FFML) score approximates the exact GP marginal likelihood by replacing the n x n kernel Gram matrix with a finite-dimensional feature representation, reducing cost to O(nm^2 + m^3) while retaining the probabilistic interpretation and automatic complexity penalty of the exact score. FFML extends to mixed (continuous + discrete) parent sets via a product-kernel construction, with a Kronecker path for small discrete parent sets and a Hadamard-product path otherwise.
  The Fourier Feature Conditional Independence (FFCI) test is a fast nonparametric CI test for mixed data. Each variable is featurized individually: continuous variables via RFF or Orthogonal Random Features (ORF), discrete variables via a Cholesky-factored categorical feature map, with blocks concatenated. Conditioning uses ridge residualization in feature space; the test statistic is a Frobenius norm of the residualized cross-covariance, approximated as a weighted sum of chi-squared variables.
  Although FFML and FFCI share the same RFF/ORF machinery, they differ architecturally: FFML builds a joint kernel over a parent set for scoring, while FFCI featurizes variables individually for testing. We compare FFML to TRFF, a penalized Student-t regression alternative. Empirically, BOSS+FFML outperforms linear and kernel-ridge baselines on nonlinear data. When run through the same PC-Max implementation, FFCI and RCIT exhibit complementary precision-recall profiles: RCIT is more precise while FFCI achieves better recall and lower SHD, and runs in one third the time.

</details>


### [268] [Transformers Provably Implement In-Context Reinforcement Learning with Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05755)
*Haodong Liang,Lifeng Lai*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究证明了Transformer可以通过其自身架构特性在上下文中执行强化学习算法（如SARSA和Actor-Critic），并首次提供了梯度流收敛性的理论证明与实验验证，阐明了Transformer从训练到零样本执行强化学习的动力学机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究Transformer模型如何通过上下文学习（In-Context Learning）在不更新参数的情况下执行强化学习算法（ICRL），并从机制和训练动力学角度阐释其内在逻辑。

Method: 1. 理论构造：证明线性自注意力层可显式实现策略改进算法；2. 训练方法：设计教师模拟训练方案；3. 动力学分析：分析梯度流动力学；4. 实验验证：在随机生成的表格型MDP上进行训练与部署测试。

Result: 1. 证明了线性自注意力模块能实现策略改进算法；2. 建立了首个ICRL收敛性理论保证（梯度流在富足的MDP分布下指数级收敛于最优参数流形）；3. 实验表明模型不仅恢复了理论构造的参数结构，且在未见过的MDP上具备强大的控制表现。

Conclusion: 该研究从理论和实验两方面证实了Transformer能够通过内嵌的机制实现强化学习算法（如SARSA和Actor-Critic），并为ICRL模型提供了首个收敛性保证，揭示了Transformer在上下文中执行经典算法的能力与训练动力学之间的联系。

Abstract: We investigate the ability of transformers to perform in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL), where a model must infer and execute learning algorithms from trajectory data without parameter updates. We show that a linear self-attention transformer block can provably implement policy-improvement methods, including semi-gradient SARSA and actor-critic, via explicit parameter constructions. Beyond existence, we design a teacher-mimicking training procedure, analyze its gradient-flow dynamics, and establish the first convergence guarantee in the ICRL literature: under suitable richness conditions on the training MDP distribution, gradient flow converges locally and exponentially to an optimal parameter manifold corresponding to the desired RL update. Empirically, training transformers on randomly generated tabular MDPs confirms these predictions: the learned models recover the parameter structure of our explicit constructions and, when deployed on unseen MDPs, deliver strong in-context control performance. Together, these results illuminate how transformer architectures internalize and execute classical reinforcement learning algorithms in context, bridging mechanistic understanding and training dynamics in ICRL.

</details>


### [269] [Ratio-based Loss Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05808)
*Lena Helgerth,Andreas Christmann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文综述了机器学习中基于比率（ratio-based）的回归损失函数，重点分析其数学性质（如连续性、凸性等），旨在为处理乘法误差结构的回归问题提供理论支持与新的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督学习损失函数主要分为基于间隔（margin-based，分类）和基于距离（distance-based，回归）两类，但针对具有乘法误差结构、关注相对误差（即 $y_i/f(x_i)$）的回归任务，缺乏系统的理论概括和研究。

Method: 综述研究法。作者系统地调研了现有的基于比率的损失函数，并提出了几种新的损失函数，同时重点分析了这些函数在连续性、Lipschitz连续性、凸性和可微性等方面的通用数学性质。

Result: 论文系统性地整理了基于比率的损失函数，提出了一些新型损失函数，并总结了此类函数在优化和泛化过程中至关重要的数学属性，为后续算法的性能分析（如一致性、收敛速度、稳定性）提供了研究框架。

Conclusion: 该论文通过系统梳理和分析基于比率（ratio-based）的损失函数，填补了现有文献关于此类函数研究的空白，并为未来在机器学习算法中应用这些函数提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Algorithms in machine learning and AI do critically depend on at least three key components: (i) the risk function, which is the expectation of the loss function, (ii) the function space, which is often called the hypothesis space, and (iii) the set of probability measures, which are allowed for the specified algorithm. This paper gives a survey of a certain class of loss functions, which we call ratio-based. In supervised learning, margin-based loss functions for classification tasks depending on the product of the output values $y_i$ and the predictions $f(x_i)$ as well as distance-based loss functions depending on the difference of $y_i$ and $f(x_i)$ for regression are common. Distance-based loss functions are in particular useful, if an additive model assumption seems plausible, i.e. the common signal plus noise assumption. However, in the literature, several loss functions proposed for regression purposes have a multiplicative error structure in mind and pay attention to relative errors, i.e. to the ratio of $y_i$ and $f(x_i)$. In this survey article, we systematically investigate such ratio-based loss functions and propose a few new losses, which may be interesting for future research. We concentrate on investigating general properties of ratio-based loss functions like continuity, Lipschitz-continuity, convexity, and differentiability, because these properties play a central role in most machine learning algorithms. Therefore, we do not focus on some specific machine learning algorithm to derive universal consistency, learning rates, or stability results. Instead, we want to enable future research in this direction.

</details>


### [270] [CITE: Anytime-Valid Statistical Inference in LLM Self-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05873)
*Hirofumi Ota,Naoto Iwase,Yuki Ichihara,Junpei Komiyama,Masaaki Imaizumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对大模型推理答案抽样难以控制伪阳性认证的问题，提出了 CITE 算法，实现了在未知答案空间和动态停止策略下的统计学严谨认证及高效采样控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型通过多次采样聚合答案以提升推理能力，但在无法预知答案空间且停止策略依赖数据的情况下，缺乏一种高效且严格的方法来控制“错误认证”的概率。

Method: 提出了 CITE（Certification by Intersection-union Testing with E-processes）算法。该方法利用 E-processes 进行交集-并集检验，实现对目标答案分布模式的“随时有效”（anytime-valid）认证，同时支持带置信度权重的投票机制。

Result: 理论上证明了 CITE 具有错误控制能力，且具备独立于答案集规模的停止时间速率，并推导出最小化极大下界；实验表明 CITE 在长尾分布设置下具有良好的鲁棒性和实证表现。

Conclusion: CITE 算法提供了一种在任意数据驱动停止策略下，依然能严格控制错误认证率的方法，且在不需要先验知识的情况下表现出优异的样本效率，为大模型的推理可靠性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Large language models often improve reasoning by sampling multiple outputs and aggregating their final answers, but precise and efficient control of error levels remains a challenging task. In particular, deciding when to stop sampling remains difficult when the stopping rule is data-dependent and the set of possible answers is not known in advance. We study anytime-valid certification of a prespecified target answer as the unique mode of the model's response distribution, a guarantee distinct from answer correctness. We propose the Certification by Intersection-union Testing with E-processes (CITE) algorithm, which provably controls false certification at any prescribed level under arbitrary data-driven stopping, without requiring prior knowledge of the answer category set. We also prove an category-set-size-free stopping-time rate, establish matching minimax lower bounds up to constants in the main regime, and extend the construction to confidence-weighted voting. Simulations and LLM self-consistency experiments show empirical error control and improved certification in diffuse-tail settings.

</details>


### [271] [Tuning Derivatives for Causal Fairness in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05882)
*Filip Edström,Guilherme W. F. Barros,Tetiana Gorbach,Xavier de Luna*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对连续敏感属性的因果公平性新框架，通过路径特异性偏导数处理因果路径，并设计了相应的微调算法，以平衡统计平价与预测平价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果公平性定义多局限于分类属性，无法有效处理连续敏感属性，且传统的统计平价（SP）过于严格，未能区分业务必需的因果路径与不公平的影响路径。

Method: 引入基于结构因果模型（SCM）的框架，利用路径特异性偏导数（path-specific partial derivatives）对连续敏感属性进行建模，并提出了一种公平微调算法以在SP与PP之间进行权衡。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在需要兼顾业务必要性（PP）的场景下，表现优于现有的公平性算法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的针对连续敏感属性的因果公平性框架能够有效地在统计平价（SP）与预测平价（PP）之间寻求平衡，通过路径特异性偏导数的方法提供了一种比传统方法更灵活且更具可操作性的公平性解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial-intelligence systems are becoming ubiquitous in society, yet their predictions typically inherit biases with respect to protected attributes such as race, gender, or age. Classical fairness notions, most notably Statistical Parity (SP), demand that predictions be independent of the protected attributes, but are overly restrictive when these attributes influence mediating variables that are considered business necessities. Recent causal formulations relax SP by distinguishing allowed from not-allowed causal paths and by complementing SP with Predictive Parity (PP), requiring the predictor to replicate the legitimate influence of business-necessities. Existing path-based definitions are mainly practical when applied to categorical attributes. This paper introduces a new framework for fairness in structural causal models that is tailored to continuous protected attributes. We formalize SP and PP through path-specific partial derivatives, establish conditions under which these criteria coincide with prior causal definitions, and characterize when a fair predictor, one that satisfies SP along not-allowed paths while achieving PP along allowed paths, exists. Building on this theory, we propose a fair tuning algorithm that either constructs such a predictor or, when not possible, allows for a trade-off between SP and PP. We present experiments on simulated and real data to evaluate our proposal, compare it with previously proposed methods, and show that it performs better when PP is considered.

</details>


### [272] [Towards Reliable LLM Evaluation: Correcting the Winner's Curse in Adaptive Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05973)
*Yang Xu,Jiefu Zhang,Haixiang Sun,Zihan Zhou,Tianyu Cao,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文针对大模型微调中评估数据被反复利用导致的“选择偏差”问题，提出了SIREN评估协议，通过严格区分筛选与测试流程及使用自举法进行推断，有效解决了性能估算偏差过高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM微调中，由于训练和评估过程重复使用Benchmark数据，基于搜索出的“最优模型”报告得分会导致评估结果呈现明显的选择偏差（selection-sensitive），即过高估计了模型在实际部署环境下的性能。

Method: 提出SIREN协议：1. 冻结搜索后的候选模型列表；2. 将数据拆分为“筛选”与“留出测试”两部分；3. 采用基于项目层级的高斯乘数自举法（Gaussian multiplier bootstrap）进行不确定性量化，从而实现有效的联合推断。

Result: 在受控模拟及MMLU-Pro微调实验中证明，相较于传统的“胜者报告法”（winner-based reporting）表现出的乐观偏差，SIREN协议能更准确地逼近真实有限样本的性能目标，并有效避免错误的部署决策。

Conclusion: 在提示词和程序搜索普及的背景下，直接报告最优模型得分会导致严重的样本选择偏差。SIREN协议通过控制筛选过程与评估过程的独立性，实现了对大模型微调部署性能的有效推断，显著提升了评估结果的鲁棒性和可靠性。

Abstract: Adaptive prompt and program search makes LLM evaluation selection-sensitive. Once benchmark items are reused inside tuning, the observed winner's score need not estimate the fresh-data performance of the full tune-then-deploy procedure. We study inference for this procedure-level target under explicit tuning budgets. We propose SIREN, a selection-aware repeated-split reporting protocol that freezes the post-search shortlist, separates splitwise selection from held-out evaluation, and uses an item-level Gaussian multiplier bootstrap for uncertainty quantification. In a fixed-shortlist regime with smooth stabilized selection, the estimator admits a first-order item-level representation, and the bootstrap yields valid simultaneous inference on a finite budget grid. This supports confidence intervals for procedure-performance curves and pre-specified equal-budget and cross-budget comparisons. Controlled simulations and MMLU-Pro tuning experiments show that winner-based reporting can be optimistic and can change deployment conclusions, while SIREN remains close to the finite-sample reporting target.

</details>


### [273] [TabCF: Distributional Control Function Estimation with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05993)
*Geping Chen,Chunlin Li,Tianzhong Yang,Zhengyuan Zhu,Jing Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TabCF 是一种利用表格基础模型进行控制函数回归的新方法，旨在高效、简单地实现因果分布（如均值和分位数）的准确估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 IV 和 CF 方法多局限于均值效应估计，且存在拟合与调参困难的问题。为解决这些局限性，提出一种简单、高效、无需精细调参且透明度高的分布因果推断方法。

Method: 利用表格基础模型（Tabular Foundation Models）进行控制函数回归；引入基于 Copula 的近似方法处理多变量结果。

Result: 在各类中小型合成及真实数据集上，TabCF 的性能优于现有代表性方法，能够实现对干预均值和分位数等分布量的准确估计。

Conclusion: TabCF 是一种有效的分布因果推断工具，可作为未来方法开发的强基准模型。

Abstract: Instrumental variable (IV) and control function (CF) methods are powerful tools for causal effect estimation in the presence of unmeasured confounding, yet most existing approaches target only mean effects and/or demand substantial fitting and tuning effort. In this paper, we introduce a simple method, TabCF, for control function regression using tabular foundation models, which enables accurate, fast, identification-transparent, and tuning-light causal estimation of distributional quantities, such as interventional means and quantiles; we also propose a copula-based approximation for multivariate outcomes. TabCF performs favorably against representative methods across a broad range of small- to medium-sized synthetic and real data scenarios. The central message is two-fold: for practitioners, it highlights that TabCF is an effective tool for distributional causal inference; for researchers, it suggests that the proposed approach could be considered a strong baseline for future method development. Code is available at https://github.com/GepingChen/TabCF.

</details>


### [274] [Gaussian mixture models in Hilbert spaces via kernel methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05996)
*Daniel López-Montero,Antonio Álvarez-López,Marcos Matabuena*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对希尔伯特空间中的复杂动态数据，本文提出了一种基于核均值嵌入的高斯混合聚类框架，解决了高维数据密度定义困难的问题，并验证了其在理论与实际应用中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据（如动态函数数据、随机图）通常呈现出时间演化和高维特性，在希尔伯特空间建模时，传统的概率密度定义方式存在应用困难，需要开发新的聚类与建模方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于核均值嵌入（Kernel Mean Embeddings）的希尔伯特空间值数据高斯混合模型框架，并开发了相应的有效优化算法进行参数估计。

Result: 理论上证明了该算法的定义良好性，并验证了模型在无限维空间中具有稠密近似能力；通过在L²函数数据和拉普拉斯空间随机图等实际数据集上的实验，证明了该方法的有效性和多功能性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于核均值嵌入的高斯混合模型能够有效地处理希尔伯特空间中的复杂、动态数据，并提供了坚实的理论支撑和良好的实际应用表现。

Abstract: Modern datasets across many disciplines increasingly consist of time-evolving, potentially infinite-dimensional random objects, such as dynamic functional data, which are naturally modeled in Hilbert spaces. In these settings, characterizing probability measures, for example, through densities, can be ill-defined or technically challenging. Motivated by clustering applications, we propose a Gaussian mixture framework for Hilbert-space-valued data based on kernel mean embeddings and develop efficient optimization algorithms for estimation. We establish theoretical guarantees showing that the proposed algorithm is well defined and that the model yields a dense class of approximations in infinite-dimensional spaces. We evaluate the framework through extensive experiments on diverse structures and data geometries, including $L^2$-functional data and random graphs in Laplacian spaces arising in modern medical applications.

</details>


### [275] [Expressivity of Bi-Lipschitz Normalizing Flows: A Score-Based Diffusion Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06172)
*Meira Iske,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究通过概率流ODE揭示了双Lipschitz归一化流的强大表达能力，证明了其在L1距离下能逼近任意概率密度，并为扩散模型的收敛性提供了理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管许多归一化流架构施加了正则性约束，但其分布逼近特性尚未被充分刻画。研究旨在通过基于分数的扩散模型视角，量化双Lipschitz归一化流的表达能力。

Method: 1. 利用概率流ODE（Probability Flow ODE）建立了评分函数（score function）正则性与诱导传输映射（transport maps）正则性之间的联系；2. 验证了包括紧支集密度、高斯卷积和有限高斯混合在内的多种目标密度的评分函数正则性；3. 通过高斯回拉（Gaussian pullbacks）分析双Lipschitz方差保持（VP）传输映射。

Result: 1. 证明了双Lipschitz方差保持传输映射诱导的高斯回拉在所有概率密度空间中是L1稠密的；2. 对于高斯卷积目标，实现了无早停（early stopping）的KL散度收敛。

Conclusion: 证明了双Lipschitz归一化流具有通用分布逼近能力（在L1范数下），并为基于扩散的传输提供了确定性收敛保证。

Abstract: Many normalizing flow architectures impose regularity constraints, yet their distributional approximation properties are not fully characterized. We study the expressivity of bi-Lipschitz normalizing flows through the lens of score-based diffusion models. For the probability flow ODE of a variance-preserving diffusion, Lipschitz regularity of the score induces a flow of bi-Lipschitz diffeomorphic transport maps. This ODE bridge allows us to analyze the distributional approximation power of bi-Lipschitz normalizing flows and, conversely, derive deterministic convergence guarantees for diffusion-based transport. Our key idea is to use the probability flow ODE to link regularity of the score to regularity of the induced transport maps. We verify score regularity for broad target densities, including compactly supported densities, Gaussian convolutions of compactly supported measures and finite Gaussian mixtures. We obtain a universal distributional approximation result: Gaussian pullbacks induced by bi-Lipschitz variance-preserving transport maps are $L^1$-dense among all probability densities. For Gaussian convolution targets, we further obtain convergence in Kullback-Leibler divergence without early stopping.

</details>


### [276] [When Does Trimming Help Conformal Prediction? A Retained-Law Diagnostic under Calibration Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06204)
*Congye Wang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文分析了共形预测中修剪校准点处理污染的数学机制，指出其本质是分布的条件化，并证明了只有满足特定得分中性条件的修剪才能有效提升覆盖率，同时提供了量化的诊断工具与审计保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在共形预测中，修剪（Trimming）作为应对校准集污染的常用手段，其对清洁目标覆盖率影响背后的数学本质和限制条件。

Method: 将固定阈值修剪建模为一种条件化（conditioning）过程，利用有限样本等式将清洁目标的覆盖率简化为一维得分CDF转换问题，并推导出基于“保留/清理比例”的成分式界和总体诊断方法。

Result: 证明了修剪效果的上限由清洁侧的协方差成本和保留污染成本决定；对于无法在清洁人群中保持得分分布中性的修剪策略，其降低污染的能力极其有限；并提出了可用于独立审计的有限样本保证模板。

Conclusion: 通过固定阈值修剪（trimming）来处理共形预测中的污染问题，其有效性取决于修剪后所诱导的“保留分布”，而非单纯依赖污染程度。研究表明，只有当异常得分能在保持纯净人群得分分布不变的前提下区分出高保留概率样本时，修剪才有效。

Abstract: Trimming suspicious calibration points is a common response to contamination in conformal prediction. Its effect on clean-target coverage, however, is governed by the retained law induced by trimming, not by the contamination level alone. We analyse fixed-threshold trimming as conditioning rather than purification. It replaces the contaminated calibration law with a retained law, reducing clean-target coverage to a one-dimensional score-CDF transfer problem with an exact finite-sample identity. A componentwise bound on the transfer gap gives a population-level diagnostic. This separates a clean-side covariance cost from a retained-contamination cost, governed by the dirty-to-clean retention ratio. Trimming helps when the anomaly score separates retention probabilities while remaining score-neutral on the clean population. Otherwise, it cannot substantially reduce contamination through the retained mixture coefficient. We also give finite-sample certificate templates that provide numerical guarantees under independent audit.

</details>


### [277] [Super-Level-Set Regression: Conditional Quantiles via Volume Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06210)
*Sacha Braun,Michael I. Jordan,Francis Bach*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对多变量回归中预测区域构建难的问题，提出了一种名为SLS的框架，通过直接优化几何边界而非估计条件密度，实现了更高效、准确的条件覆盖预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量回归中构建满足条件覆盖率的最小体积预测区域存在挑战，传统的两阶段法（先估计密度再阈值化）存在计算代价高、对估计误差敏感及估计难度大等问题。

Method: 提出了超水平集回归（Super-level-set regression, SLS）框架，引入体积保持的前沿函数（volume-preserving frontier functions），直接对预测区域的几何边界进行参数化和端到端优化，无需显式估计条件密度。

Result: 该方法解决了预测区域体积目标与模型估计误差耦合的难题，能够有效处理复杂、多模态且不连续的条件结构。

Conclusion: SLS 提供了一种多变量条件分位数回归的新范式，通过直接的几何优化策略，成功替代了传统的密度优先估算方法，并在处理复杂分布时展现出更强的灵活性。

Abstract: Constructing minimum-volume prediction regions that satisfy conditional coverage is a fundamental challenge in multivariate regression. Standard approaches rely on explicitly estimating the full conditional density and subsequently thresholding it. This two-step plug-in process is notoriously difficult, sensitive to estimation errors, and computationally expensive. One would like to instead optimize the region directly. Formulating a direct solution is challenging, however, because it requires minimizing a volume objective that is coupled with the conditional quantiles of the model's own estimation error. In this work, we address this challenge. We introduce super-level-set regression (SLS), a novel mathematical framework that successfully resolves this implicit coupling, allowing us to directly parameterize and optimize the geometric boundaries of the target conditional level sets. By bypassing full distribution estimation and leveraging flexible volume-preserving frontier functions, our approach natively captures complex, multimodal, and disjoint conditional structures end-to-end. Ultimately, SLS offers a new perspective on multivariate conditional quantile regression, replacing the restrictive assumptions of density-first methods with a direct geometric optimization strategy.

</details>


### [278] [Multimodal Deep Generative Model for Semi-Supervised Learning under Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06289)
*Heegeon Yoon,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 文章提出了一种针对类不平衡半监督学习的多模态深度生成模型，通过引入Student's t-分布和$\gamma$-幂散度优化目标，有效缓解了标签不平衡导致的偏见传播，提升了多模态数据的分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决在部分标注条件下，多模态数据面临的类别不平衡问题。现有的半监督学习方法主要针对单模态，且在处理不平衡数据时容易因生成的伪标签产生偏差传播，无法充分利用多模态数据的互补性。

Method: 1. 设计了一种融合多模态数据的深度生成模型，采用分层编码器及专家乘积（Product-of-Experts）方法简化联合后验计算；2. 使用Student's t-分布替代传统的高斯分布，以更好地建模不平衡数据中重尾的潜在分布；3. 推导了一种基于$\gamma$-幂散度（$\gamma$-power divergence）的优化目标函数，以同时处理有标签和无标签数据。

Result: 在基准数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，该模型在泛化能力和分类准确率上均优于现有的基准方法。

Conclusion: 该多模态深度生成模型有效解决了半监督学习中类别不平衡引起的偏差传播问题，通过引入Student's t-分布和基于$\gamma$-幂散度的目标函数，在处理带有不平衡标签的多模态数据时表现出优越的泛化能力和分类性能。

Abstract: When modeling class-imbalanced data, it is crucial to address the imbalance, as models trained on such data tend to be biased towards the majority classes. This problem is amplified under partial supervision, where pseudo-labels for unlabeled data are predicted based on imbalanced labeled data, propagating the bias. While recent semi-supervised models address class imbalance, they typically assume single-modal input data. However, with the growing availability of multimodal data, it is essential to leverage complementary modalities. In this article, we propose a multimodal deep generative model for semi-supervised learning under class imbalance. Our approach uses separate encoders for each modality, sharing latent variables across modalities, and simplifies joint posterior computation with a product-of-experts method. To further address class imbalance, we replace typical Gaussian distributions with Student's t-distributions for the prior, encoder, and decoder, better capturing the heavy-tailed latent distributions in imbalanced data. We derive a new objective function for training the proposed model on both labeled and unlabeled data using $γ$-power divergence. Empirical results on benchmark and real-world datasets demonstrate that our model outperforms baseline methods in generalization, achieving superior classification performance for partially labeled multimodal data with imbalanced class distributions.

</details>


### [279] [End-to-End Identifiable and Consistent Recurrent Switching Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06315)
*Carles Balsells-Rodas,Zhengrui Xiang,Xavier Sumba,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为带有状态切换的非线性动力系统建立了更广泛的可辨识性理论，并引入了一种基于流的精确似然优化算法 ΩSDS，有效解决了序列数据表示学习中的可辨识性难题与模型近似误差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生成模型在处理具状态切换特征的序列数据时，面临可辨识性理论假设过于严苛（如平稳性假设）以及 VAE 近似带来的性能损失等挑战。

Method: 提出了一种流式（flow-based）估计器 ΩSDS，核心在于利用期望最大化（EM）算法实现精确的似然优化，从而避免了 VAE 中存在的近似间隙。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上的实验表明，相比基于 VAE 的方法，ΩSDS 显著提升了潜在表示的解耦（disentanglement）水平，并具备更准确的动态预测能力。

Conclusion: 该研究通过理论证明和新型流式估计器 ΩSDS，成功解决了具状态切换动力学序列数据中潜在表示的可辨识性问题，实现了比 VAE 更优的解耦效果和预测精度。

Abstract: Learning identifiable representations in deep generative models remains a fundamental challenge, particularly for sequential data with regime-switching dynamics. Existing approaches establish identifiability under restrictive assumptions, such as stationarity or limited emission models, and typically rely on variational autoencoder (VAE) estimators, which introduce approximation gaps that limit the recovery of the latent structure. In this work, we address both the theoretical and practical limitations of this setting. First, we establish identifiability of a broad class of recurrent nonlinear switching dynamical systems under flexible assumptions, significantly extending prior results. Second, we introduce $Ω$SDS, a flow-based estimator that enables exact likelihood optimization using expectation-maximisation. Through empirical validation on both synthetic and real-world data, our results demonstrate that $Ω$SDS achieves improved disentanglement compared to VAE-based estimators and more accurate forecasting of underlying dynamics.

</details>


### [280] [The Interplay of Data Structure and Imbalance in the Learning Dynamics of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06367)
*Flavio Nicoletti,Chenxiao Ma,Enrico Ventura,Luca Saglietti,Stefano Sarao Mannelli*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和实验揭示了数据异质性和采样不平衡如何导致扩散模型在不同类别间产生学习差异，发现模型倾向于优先学习高方差类别，而少数类往往面临学习滞后和记忆不充分的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论大多假设数据是同质的，而现实世界数据的异质性（类间结构差异和采样不平衡）如何影响扩散模型的训练动态及潜在的不公平性尚不明确，需要建立理论框架进行定量研究。

Method: 提出了一个基于高维数据的分析框架，利用训练在高斯混合分布上的随机特征模型，推导了特征协方差谱，以表征不同类别的泛化和记忆时间（speciation times），并结合Fashion MNIST上的U-Net模型进行了实证验证。

Result: 揭示了类别方差是决定学习顺序的主要因素（优先学习高方差类），质心几何结构起次要作用；采样不平衡可以反转这一顺序，少数类在强不平衡下会表现出明显的学习延迟。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在学习过程中存在内在的学习层级：高方差类别优先被学习，而采样不平衡会调制这一过程；强不平衡可能导致少数类在扩散模型中出现滞后的、独立的记忆（speciation）阶段，从而导致模型对不同类别的学习程度不均。

Abstract: Real-world datasets are inherently heterogeneous, yet how per-class structural differences and sampling imbalance shape the training dynamics of diffusion models-and potentially exacerbate disparities-remains poorly understood. While models typically transition from an initial phase of generalization to memorizing the training set, existing theory assumes homogeneous data, leaving open how class imbalance and heterogeneity reshape these dynamics. In this work, we develop a high-dimensional analytical framework to study class-dependent learning in score-based diffusion models. Analyzing a random-features model trained on Gaussian mixtures, we derive the feature-covariance spectrum to characterize per-class generalization and memorization times. We reveal the explicit hierarchy governing these dynamics: class variance is the primary determinant of learning order-consistently favoring higher-variance classes-while centroid geometry plays a secondary role. Sampling imbalance acts as a modulator that can reverse this ordering and, under strong imbalance, forces minority classes to acquire distinct, delayed speciation times during backward diffusion. Together, these results suggest that diffusion models can memorize some classes while others remain insufficiently learned. We validate our theoretical predictions empirically using U-Net models trained on Fashion MNIST.

</details>


### [281] [Beyond the Independence Assumption: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Deep Q-Learning under $τ$-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06373)
*Leon Halgryn,Sophie Langer,Janusz M. Meylahn,E. Moritz Hahn*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文突破了 DQN 分析中“数据独立”的惯用假设，利用 $\tau$-mixing 理论定量分析了时间相关性对 DQN 样本复杂度和收敛速率的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DQN 的有限样本分析通常错误地假设经验回放（Experience Replay）中的数据是独立同分布的（i.i.d.），忽略了轨迹数据内在的时间相关性。

Method: 将 DQN 的更新过程建模为带有 $\tau$-mixing（时间相关）观测值的非参数回归问题；在 ReLU 全连接架构下推导其有限样本风险界（finite-sample risk bounds）；并以此推导相应的样本复杂度。

Result: 理论上证明了在 $\tau$-mixing 条件下，由于时间依赖性的存在，DQN 的收敛速率会降低；通过 Gymnasium 环境的实证分析，揭示了经验回放采样中存在呈指数衰减的相关性，证实了理论框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 时间相关性会通过引入额外的维数惩罚项来降低 DQN 的统计收敛速率，该惩罚项反映了 $\tau$-mixing 数据有效样本量的减少。实验验证了独立性假设在实践中通常不成立。

Abstract: Finite-sample analyses of deep Q-learning typically treat replayed data as independent, even though it is sampled from temporally dependent state-action trajectories. We study the Deep Q-networks (DQN) algorithm under explicit dependence by modelling the minibatches used for updating the network as $τ$-mixing. We show that this assumption holds under certain dependence conditions on the underlying trajectories and the mechanism used to sample minibatches. Building on this observation, we extend statistical analyses of DQN with fully connected ReLU architectures to dependent data. We formulate each update as a nonparametric regression problem with $τ$-mixing observations and derive finite-sample risk bounds under this dependence structure. Our results show that temporal dependence leads to a degradation in the statistical rate by inducing an additional dimensionality penalty in the rate exponent, reflecting the reduced effective sample size of $τ$-mixing data. Moreover, we derive the sample complexity of DQN under $tau$-mixing from these risk bounds. Finally, we empirically demonstrate on standard Gymnasium environments that the independence assumption is systematically violated and that replay sampling yields approximately exponentially decaying correlations, supporting our theoretical framework.

</details>


### [282] [Risk-Controlled Post-Processing of Decision Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06479)
*Sunay Joshi,Tao Wang,Hamed Hassani,Edgar Dobriban*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在风险受控前提下对现有决策策略进行最小化修改的后处理方法，通过阈值策略平衡了基准一致性与风险控制，并具备严谨的理论证明与实证支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际场景中，利益相关者不愿改变现有的决策策略，除非存在明确的风险约束需要干预。因此，如何在一个已有的基准策略基础上，在满足特定风险约束的同时最大化与基准的一致性，成为了一个研究重点。

Method: 通过引入风险约束下的机会约束优化问题，利用阈值结构选择策略；在有限样本下，通过校准数据选择最优阈值；并利用算法稳定性和随机过程工具，从理论上证明了该方法在独立同分布样本下的收敛速度及在可交换假设下的风险控制保证。

Result: 理论分析证明了该方法的预期超额风险为 O(log n/n)，在存在安全备用策略的情况下可实现精确的风险控制；实验结果表明该方法在COVID-19诊断、LLM路由等任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: 本文提出的风险受控后处理方法在满足风险约束的同时，能比传统的盲目随机混合策略更好地保留与基准策略的一致性，证明了其在实际应用场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Predictive models are often deployed through existing decision policies that stakeholders are reluctant to change unless a risk constraint requires intervention. We study risk-controlled post-processing: given a deterministic baseline policy, choose a new policy that maximizes agreement with the baseline subject to a chance constraint on a user-specified loss. At the population level, we show that the optimal policy has a threshold structure: it follows the baseline except on contexts where switching to the oracle fallback policy yields a large reduction in conditional violation risk. At the finite-sample level, given a fitted fallback policy and score, we develop a post-processing algorithm that uses calibration data to select a threshold. Leveraging tools from algorithmic stability and stochastic processes, we show that under regularity conditions, in the i.i.d. setting, the expected excess risk of the post-processed policy is $O(\log n/n)$. In the special case when an exact-safe fallback policy is available, the algorithm achieves precise expected risk control under exchangeability. In this setting, we also give high-probability near-optimality guarantees on the post-processed policy. Experiments on a COVID-19 radiograph diagnosis task, an LLM routing problem, and a synthetic multiclass decision task show that targeted post-processing can meet or nearly meet risk budgets while preserving substantially more agreement with the baseline than score-blind random mixing.

</details>


### [283] [DARTS: Targeting Prognostic Covariates in Budget-Constrained Sequential Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06608)
*Kateryna Husar,Alexander Volfovsky*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: DARTS 是一种面向随机对照试验的高效实验设计框架，通过基于汤普森采样的自适应协变量选择机制，在有限的数据获取预算下最小化处理效应估计的方差，同时确保统计推断的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机对照试验中，获取高维预处理数据成本高昂，现有的方法往往忽视了获取成本与协变量自适应精度之间的权衡问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为“基于汤普森采样的动态自适应重随机化”（DARTS）的方法，将协变量获取建模为序贯优化问题；使用预算约束的组合汤普森采样器学习协变量的预测能力，驱动重随机化与回归调整；并利用解耦结果确保了批次间的随机化有效性。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法保留了批次级别的随机化有效性，估计量达到了理论上的渐近覆盖率，且获取层的贝叶斯风险界接近极小极大下界；实证结果显示该方法能高效分配预算，提升平均处理效应（ATE）的估计精度。

Conclusion: DARTS 框架在严格保持推断有效性的同时，通过自适应选择最具预测性的协变量，系统地优化了预算分配，显著缩小了受限设计与理想预言（Oracle）设计之间的效率差距。

Abstract: Randomized controlled trials typically assume that prognostic covariates are known and available at no cost. In practice, obtaining high-dimensional pretreatment data is costly, forcing a trade-off between covariate-adaptive precision and a measurement budget. We introduce Dynamic Adaptive Rerandomization via Thompson Sampling (DARTS), which treats covariate acquisition as a sequential optimization problem embedded within a design-based causal inference task. A budgeted combinatorial Thompson sampler learns which covariates are most prognostic across successive batches; selected covariates then drive rerandomization and regression adjustment to reduce batch-level average treatment effect variance. Our primary theoretical contribution is a decoupling result: adaptive covariate selection based on past batches preserves batch-level randomization validity, and the cumulative inverse-variance weighted estimator achieves at least nominal asymptotic coverage. We further derive a Bayes risk bound for the acquisition layer that matches the minimax lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Empirically, DARTS systematically concentrates the budget on informative features, significantly closing the efficiency gap to oracle designs while maintaining strict inferential validity.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [284] [ADELIA: Automatic Differentiation for Efficient Laplace Inference Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06392)
*Afif Boudaoud,Lisa Gaedke-Merzhäuser,Alexandros Nikolaos Ziogas,Vincent Maillou,Alexandru Calotoiu,Marcin Copik,Håvard Rue,Mathieu Luisier,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ADELIA 是一种利用自动微分优化 INLA 的新方法，通过利用模型稀疏性进行高效的多 GPU 计算，解决了大规模高斯模型中梯度计算开销巨大和能耗高的问题，实现了性能与能效的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高性能计算（HPC）环境下，现有的 INLA 实现主要依赖有限差分（FD）进行超参数优化，但 FD 的计算开销随参数维度 d 线性增长，导致在大规模模型中计算效率低下且耗能巨大，因此迫切需要一种更高效的梯度计算方法。

Method: 提出 ADELIA 系统：首个支持自动微分的 INLA 实现。该方法的核心在于利用结构化稀疏内核，实现了高效的多 GPU 反向传播，通过反向模式自动微分直接计算精确梯度，摆脱了对超参数维度 d 的依赖。

Result: 在十个基准模型（涵盖高达 190 万潜在变量）上，ADELIA 实现了 4.2 到 7.9 倍的梯度计算加速，并且在 FD 难以收敛的超大规模模型上表现出可靠的收敛性；在同等算力规模下，ADELIA 能耗比 FD 降低了 5 到 8 倍。

Conclusion: ADELIA 通过基于自动微分（AD）的梯度计算，证明了在大规模潜在高斯模型中，相比传统的有限差分方法（FD），在计算效率和能效方面具有显著优势，且更具扩展性。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal Bayesian inference drives environmental and health sciences using latent Gaussian models. Integrated Nested Laplace Approximations (INLA) enable inference for these models at HPC scale but rely on derivative-based optimization over $d$ hyperparameters. State-of-the-art INLA implementations approximate derivatives via central finite differences (FD), requiring $2d{+}1$ evaluations. These evaluations are embarrassingly parallel, but total work and energy grow with $d$, limiting time-to-solution under fixed budgets. Reverse-mode automatic differentiation (AD) computes exact gradients independently of $d$, but its efficient application to INLA's structured-sparse kernels is an open challenge. We present ADELIA, the first AD-enabled INLA implementation with a structure-exploiting multi-GPU backward pass leveraging model sparsity. We evaluate ADELIA on ten benchmark models, including real-world air-pollution monitoring. We achieve $4.2$--$7.9\times$ per-gradient speedups and reliable convergence on production-scale models with up to 1.9M latent variables, where FD struggles. Even when scaled to 16--32 GPUs to match ADELIA's wall-clock time, FD consumes $5$--$8\times$ more energy.

</details>


### [285] [Nitsum: Serving Tiered LLM Requests with Adaptive Tensor Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05467)
*Vikranth Srivatsa,Zijian He,Pu Guo,Dongming Li,Yiying Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Nitsum通过将张量并行度（TP）作为可动态调整的执行参数，并结合高效的权重/KV缓存处理机制，极大优化了多租户LLM环境下的SLO达成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限的GPU预算下，多租户LLM服务面临延迟敏感与后台任务混合的挑战，现有的静态执行配置无法应对随时间变化的负载特性，导致满足SLO的总体吞吐量（Goodput）受限。

Method: 提出一种支持动态TP调整的分布式LLM服务系统Nitsum，联合优化TP等级、预填充/解码GPU资源分配及请求调度，并引入TP感知权重重用与快速KV迁移技术以降低切换开销。

Result: 在真实负载和微基准测试中，Nitsum将满足SLO的Goodput提升了最高5.3倍，证明了动态TP调整在处理多租户动态负载时的优越性。

Conclusion: Nitsum通过将张量并行（TP）作为动态运行时控制手段，配合高效的权重重用和KV迁移机制，实现了多租户场景下LLM服务性能的显著提升，有效解决了多层级SLO下的资源争夺问题。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly multi-tenant: the same deployment must handle latency-critical interactive requests and more relaxed background workloads under a fixed GPU budget. This creates a tiered-SLO setting where maximizing overall goodput (requests that satisfy both TTFT and TPOT targets) is challenging because workload mix, request lengths, and load intensity vary over time. Existing systems mainly optimize request-level controls (e.g., queuing and batching) while keeping execution configuration largely static, which limits adaptation under multi-tier contention.
  We present Nitsum, a distributed LLM serving system that treats tensor parallelism (TP) as a first-class runtime control surface rather than a static deployment choice. Nitsum jointly optimizes TP level, prefill/decode GPU split, and request scheduling. To make frequent TP adaptation practical, Nitsum introduces TP-aware weight reuse and fast KV migration. Experiments on real traces and targeted microbenchmarks show that Nitsum improves SLO-compliant goodput over SoTA by up to 5.3 times.

</details>


### [286] [Irminsul: MLA-Native Position-Independent Caching for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05696)
*Bole Ma,Jan Eitzinger,Harald Köstler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对 Agentic LLM 导致的前缀缓存频繁失效问题，Irminsul 通过利用 Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA) 的架构特性，引入了内容寻址的缓存机制，显著提升了 KV 缓存命中率并降低了首字延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM 工作负载会导致生成的 token 不断发生微小偏移，使得传统的前缀缓存（Prefix Caching）在第一个分歧点就失效，导致严重的 TTFT（首字延迟）抖动和计算浪费。

Method: 提出了 Irminsul 系统，该系统结合了基于内容哈希（Content-hash）的键控、CDC（基于内容的切片）以及针对 MLA 架构中旋转位置编码（RoPE）的 δ-旋转校正规则。

Result: 在 MLA-MoE 模型（DeepSeek-V2-Lite 等）上，Irminsul 能够恢复高达 83% 的提示词 tokens，并实现每个缓存命中减少 63% 的预填充能量消耗。

Conclusion: Irminsul 证明了基于内容寻址的缓存（而非传统的基于前缀匹配的缓存）应当成为大模型推理部署中的一等公民，能有效解决 Agentic 工作负载中 KV 缓存失效的问题。

Abstract: Agentic LLM workloads put bit-identical tokens at shifted positions every turn, voiding prefix caches at the first byte of divergence. Operators report cache-hit regressions ranging from moderate slowdowns to severe TTFT spikes of 10-16s on unchanged content. Prior position-independent caching systems correct RoPE on the full $d_K$-dimensional key, an architectural cost imposed by GQA, not by caching itself. Multi-Head Latent Attention, deployed at scale in DeepSeek-V2/V3/R1, Kimi-K2/Moonlight, GLM-5, and Mistral Large 3, factors each KV row into a position-free $c_{KV}$ and a 64-dim $k_r$ correctable in closed form; this structure motivates content-addressed caching as a natural fit rather than a GQA workaround. We present Irminsul, which extends SGLang's radix cache with content-hash keying over CDC-chunked segments and a $δ$-rotation rule for $k_r$. We evaluate three native MLA-MoE deployments - DeepSeek-V2-Lite (16B/2.4B), Kimi Moonlight-16B-A3B, and JoyAI-Flash (48B/3B) - with output-consistency on all three and recovery measured on the two endpoints; Irminsul recovers up to ~83% of prompt tokens above exact-prefix on agentic traffic while delivering 63% prefill energy savings per cache hit. We argue that content-addressed caching belongs in the serving stack as a first-class primitive, not a retrofit over prefix matching.

</details>


### [287] [Relay Buffer Independent Communication over Pooled HBM for Efficient MoE Inference on Ascend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06055)
*Tianlun Hu,Tiancheng Hu,Shengsheng Litang,Sheng Wang,Xiaoming Bao,Yuxing Li,Wei Wang,Zhongzhe Hu,Lijun Li,Hongwei Sun,Jingbin Zhou\\*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对 MoE 推理中通信瓶颈，本文提出一种无中继缓存通信架构，通过直接读写远程设备内存，消除了冗余的中转开销，在 Ascend 平台上实现了推理性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE（混合专家模型）推理过程中，大规模的 Token 跨设备交换带来了巨大的通信开销，特别是路由驱动的布局转换、临时中继和输出恢复，成为了推理性能的瓶颈，现有方案过于依赖缓存中继。

Method: 提出了一种针对 Ascend 系统的无中继缓存（relay-buffer-free）通信设计。该方案通过全局内存池和对称内存分配，实现向目标专家窗口的直接写入和从远程专家窗口的直接读取。针对预填充（prefill）和解码（decode）阶段，分别开发了兼顾吞吐量和延迟的调度策略，仅保留轻量级的控制状态（指针与同步元数据）。

Result: 在 Ascend 系统上的实验证明，该方案有效降低了调度（dispatch）和合并（combine）的延迟；在服务层面，显著改善了首字延迟（TTFT），维持了有竞争力的每个输出字延迟（TPOT），并扩大了实际延迟约束下的调度可行空间。

Conclusion: 在具有全局可寻址设备内存的平台上，通过消除 MoE 推理中冗余的中间缓存和输出恢复操作，是加速推理的有效途径。该设计显著降低了通信延迟，提升了服务性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) inference requires large-scale token exchange across devices, making dispatch and combine major bottlenecks in both prefill and decode. Beyond network transfer, routing-driven layout transformation, temporary relay, and output restoration can add substantial overhead. Existing MoE communication paths are often buffer-centric, using explicit inter-process relay and reordering buffers around collective transfer. This report presents a relay-buffer-free communication design for MoE inference acceleration on Ascend systems. The design reorganizes dispatch and combine around direct placement into destination expert windows and direct reading from remote expert windows. Built on globally pooled high-bandwidth memory and symmetric-memory allocation, it removes most intermediate relay and reordering buffers while retaining only lightweight control state, including counts, offsets, and synchronization metadata. We instantiate the design as two schedules for the main phases of MoE inference: a prefill schedule with richer planning state for throughput-oriented execution, and a compact decode schedule for latency-sensitive execution. Experiments on Ascend-based MoE workloads show reduced dispatch and combine latency in both settings. At the serving level, the implementation improves time to first token (TTFT), preserves competitive time per output token (TPOT), and enlarges the feasible scheduling space under practical latency constraints. These results indicate that, on platforms with globally addressable device memory, reducing intermediate buffering and output restoration around expert execution is an effective direction for accelerating MoE inference.

</details>


### [288] [FalconGEMM: Surpassing Hardware Peaks with Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06057)
*Honglin Zhu,Jiaping Cao,Jiang Shao,Siyuan Feng,Qian Qiu,Peng Chen,Xu Zhang,Yixian Zhou,Man Lung Yiu,Guang Ji,Minwen Deng,Wenxi Zhu,Jintao Meng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FalconGEMM 是一个跨平台的矩阵乘法优化框架，通过自动化部署、组并行处理和性能预测模型，实现了 LCMA 算法在主流软硬件环境中的高性能部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习（尤其是大型语言模型）的发展对高效矩阵乘法提出了更高需求，而现有的低复杂度矩阵乘法算法（LCMA）在异构硬件上的落地部署和算子优化存在巨大挑战。

Method: 1. 部署模块：通过代码生成技术实现多硬件和配置的可移植性；2. 执行模块：引入组并行优化（Group-Parallel Optimizations），提升片上数据复用并降低带宽开销；3. 决策模块：利用轻量级分析性能模型，根据矩阵形状与硬件架构动态选择最优算法策略。

Result: 在 H20/A100 GPU 及 ARM/x86 CPU 等多种架构上，FalconGEMM 性能超越了传统成熟库（如 cuBLAS、MKL 等）7.59%-17.85%，显著优于现有 LCMA 竞品（如 AlphaTensor）12.41%-55.61%。

Conclusion: FalconGEMM 通过自动化部署、细粒度的并行优化和智能策略调度，成功将低复杂度矩阵乘法算法（LCMA）从理论研究转化为生产环境中的高性能计算解决方案，打破了传统 GEMM 库的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Peak breaking Matrix Multiplication is a promising technique to improve the performance of DL, especially in LLM training and inference. We present FalconGEMM, a cross-platform framework that automates the deployment, optimization, and selection of Lower-Complexity Matrix Multiplication Algorithms (LCMAs) across diverse hardware. There are three key innovations: (1) a Deployment Module that enables portable execution across various hardware and input configurations through code generation; (2) an Execution Module with Group-Parallel Optimizations that maximizes on-chip data reuse, utilizes parallel resources, and reduces bandwidth overhead; and (3) a Decision Module featuring a lightweight analytical performance model to select the optimal strategy based on matrix shapes and hardware profiles. Extensive evaluation is conducted on LLM workloads across GPU (H20, A100) and CPU (ARM, x86) architectures with multiple data types. FalconGEMM succeeds in delivering peak breaking performance and outperforms GEMM libraries (e.g., cuBLAS, CUTLASS, Intel MKL, etc) by 7.59%-17.85% and LCMA competitors like AlphaTensor by 12.41%-55.61%. Our framework makes the theoretical promise of LCMAs practical for production deployment across the heterogeneous landscape of modern hardware.

</details>


### [289] [Tackling the Data-Parallel Load Balancing Bottleneck in LLM Serving: Practical Online Routing at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06113)
*Tianci Bu,Yuan Lyu,Zixi Chen,Chendong Song,Hong Liang,Tsepten Gurung,Yuwei Fan,Yinyu Ye,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对大模型推理中数据并行（DP）负载不均衡导致的计算瓶颈，BalanceRoute 提出了一种轻量级、低延迟的实时路由算法家族，有效提高了大规模 LLM 服务系统吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模 LLM 推理中，数据并行（DP）负载不均衡是导致推理瓶颈的主要原因。由于请求分配具有高粘性（KV 缓存迁移成本高）、负载随时间动态变化以及路由调度极度时间敏感（需在百毫秒内完成），现有方法难以有效优化。

Method: 引入 BalanceRoute 算法家族：BR-0（基于分段线性 F-score，无需预测架构，计算开销极低）和 BR-H（在 BR-0 基础上引入常数级的前瞻窗口 H 和终止分类器，优化长周期调度）。

Result: 在 144 个 NPU 的集群上，通过对比 Azure-2024 等生产 trace，BalanceRoute 证明了其在减少 DP 节点间不均衡程度和提升整体 Serving 吞吐量方面的有效性。

Conclusion: BalanceRoute 是一套实用的在线路由算法，能够显著降低大规模 LLM 推理场景下数据并行（DP）节点间的负载不均衡，从而提升系统的端到端吞吐量。

Abstract: Data-parallel (DP) load balancing has emerged as a first-order bottleneck in large-scale LLM serving. When a model is sharded across devices via tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP) and replicated across many DP workers, every decode step ends in a synchronization barrier whose latency is set by the most heavily loaded worker; even modest persistent imbalance across DP workers compounds, step after step, into a substantial fraction of wasted compute. The problem is hard for reasons specific to LLM decoding: assignments are sticky (migrating KV caches has a high cost), per-request loads grow over time, arrivals are non-stationary, and the router must decide within a sub-100\,ms decode budget over hundreds of waiting requests and tens of workers. We present \textbf{BalanceRoute}, a family of practical online routing algorithms that target this bottleneck. The first, \textbf{BR-0}, requires no prediction infrastructure and uses a piecewise-linear F-score that captures the sharp asymmetry between admissions that fill safe margin and those that overflow into the envelope; a two-stage decomposition keeps per-step cost compatible with millisecond-scale scheduling. The second, \textbf{BR-H}, generalizes BR-0 with a short, constant lookahead $H$ and a lightweight termination-classifier interface, extending the F-score to a horizon-discounted form. We deploy BalanceRoute on a 144-NPU cluster and evaluate against vLLM baselines on both a proprietary production trace and the public Azure-2024 trace. Across both workloads, BalanceRoute substantially reduces average DP imbalance and improves end-to-end serving throughput.

</details>


### [290] [TACO: A Toolsuite for the Verification of Threshold Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06118)
*Paul Eichler,Tom Baumeister,Mouhammad Sakr,Mahboubeh Kalateh Dowlati,Marcus Völp,Swen Jacobs*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TACO 是一个用于开发和自动验证容错/基于阈值分布式算法的模块化工具套件，集成了多种模型检测算法，涵盖了从可判定片段到半决策程序的广泛验证需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式算法（特别是容错和基于阈值的算法）的正确性验证极具挑战性，现有工具在处理不同算法复杂度和判定性需求方面存在局限，需要一个统一的框架来支持。

Method: TACO 集成了三种针对不同可判定片段的模型检测方法，以及两种处理不可判定片段的半决策程序；该工具具备模块化架构，支持算法开发与验证。

Result: 实验结果表明 TACO 能够高效处理各类阈值自动机，并在验证性能上表现良好，具备实际应用价值。

Conclusion: TACO 是一个功能强大、模块化且可扩展的工具套件，为研究和验证阈值自动机提供了坚实的基础，能够有效处理不同复杂度的分布式算法验证问题。

Abstract: We present TACO, a toolsuite for the development and automatic verification of fault-tolerant and threshold-based distributed algorithms. Our toolsuite implements three approaches for model checking threshold automata in different decidable fragments known from the literature and two semi-decision procedures going beyond these decidable fragments. Moreover, TACO is a modular, extensible, and well-documented framework for developing algorithms and tools for threshold automata. We present important features, give an overview of the implemented algorithms, and evaluate their performance experimentally.

</details>


### [291] [ResiHP: Taming LLM Training Failures with Dynamic Hybrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06374)
*Tenghui Ma,Jihu Guo,Wei Gao,Sitian Lu,Zhisheng Ye,Hanjing Wang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ResiHP 是一款专为大规模混合并行 LLM 训练设计的弹性系统，通过区分序列长度波动与硬件故障，并动态优化并行策略，显著提升了故障环境下的训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大规模LLM混合并行训练系统未能有效处理数据集序列长度变异性（导致误报性能下降）和设备性能偏差（导致资源利用效率低下），从而影响训练整体效率。

Method: 1. 开发了一个工作负载感知（workload-aware）的执行时间预测器，用于区分真实的硬件故障与序列长度变化引起的迭代时间波动（检测器）；2. 设计了一个动态调度器，能够根据故障情况调整并行组大小、模型分区策略及工作负载分配（调度器）。

Result: 在256个GPU的集群上，针对多种故障场景的实验表明，ResiHP相比现有的最先进（SOTA）弹性训练系统，训练吞吐量提升了1.04-4.39倍。

Conclusion: ResiHP通过在混合并行训练中实现鲁棒的故障检测和细粒度资源调度，有效解决了大规模训练中因性能偏差和硬件故障导致的效率低下问题，显著提升了训练吞吐量。

Abstract: Hybrid parallelism underpins large-scale LLM training across tens of thousands of GPUs. At such scale, hardware failures on individual devices lead to performance skew across devices, diminishing overall training efficiency. Existing resilient systems overlook sequence length variability in datasets and device performance skew under hybrid parallelism. As a result, (1) iteration time fluctuations induced by sequence length variability can trigger spurious fail-slow detections, and (2) failures are mitigated through individual adaptations in hybrid parallelism, leading to unnecessary detection overhead and inefficient resilient training. To respond, this paper presents ResiHP, a resilient system that enables robust failure detection and fine-grained adaptation for hybrid parallel training. First, we develop a Detector to accurately identify failures. In particular, it employs a workload-aware execution time predictor that disentangles failures from iteration time fluctuations while remaining lightweight for online detection. Second, we design a Scheduler that dynamically adapts parallelism group sizes, model partitioning, and workload scheduling policies to improve training efficiency under failures. Experiments show that ResiHP improves training throughput by 1.04-4.39$\times$ compared with state-of-the-art resilient training systems under diverse failure scenarios in a 256-GPU cluster.

</details>


### [292] [CCL-Bench 1.0: A Trace-Based Benchmark for LLM Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06544)
*Eric Ding,Byungsoo Oh,Bhaskar Kataria,Kaiwen Guo,Jelena Gvero,Abhishek Vijaya Kumar,Arjun Devraj,Lindsey Bowen,Atharv Sonwane,Emaad Manzoor,Rachee Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对现有 LLM 基准测试无法解释性能差异的问题，本文提出了 CCL-Bench，通过细粒度的执行追踪和自动化分析工具，揭示了硬件配置与软件框架选择对训练性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 基础设施基准测试仅提供缺乏解释性的端到端性能数字，无法理清硬件、框架、并行计划与通信库等复杂配置空间对性能的具体影响。

Method: 开发了 CCL-Bench（基于追踪的基准测试集），通过记录包含执行追踪（trace）、YAML 工作负载说明和启动脚本的完整数据包，并配套一套社区可扩展的工具箱来计算计算、内存和通信效率指标。

Result: 揭示了三个关键发现：1. 高计算-通信重叠度并不总是等同于最优性能；2. 不同硬件（TPU vs GPU）对带宽提升的响应存在显著差异；3. 同一硬件上不同框架的性能差距可达 3 倍。

Conclusion: CCL-Bench 能够提供细粒度的性能证据和度量指标，弥补了现有端到端基准测试在评估 LLM 基础设施方面的不足，有助于深入挖掘不同硬件、软件和并行策略组合下的性能差异。

Abstract: Evaluative claims about LLM infrastructure -- ``workload X is fastest on hardware Y with software Z'' -- depend on a complex configuration space spanning hardware accelerators, interconnect bandwidth, software frameworks, parallelism plans, and communication libraries. Current infrastructure evaluation benchmarks publish a small set of end-to-end numbers that do not explain why one configuration outperforms another. We present CCL-Bench, a trace-based benchmark that addresses the limitations of existing benchmarks by recording reusable evidence for every ML workload. Each contributed data point in CCL-Bench packages an execution trace, a YAML workload card, and the launch scripts. We have developed a community-extensible toolkit to compute fine-grained compute, memory, and communication efficiency metrics from this evidence. Using CCL-Bench, we surface three claims that summary-statistic benchmarks cannot support: (i) higher compute-communication overlap can coincide with longer training step time and reveal inefficient parallelization choices, (ii) doubling TPU interconnect bandwidth yields a much higher end-to-end improvement in step time than doubling GPU interconnect bandwidth on small and medium workloads, and (iii) the best-tuned configuration on one training framework can run up to 3$\times$ slower than the best-tuned configuration on a peer framework on identical hardware.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [293] [Anatomy of a Query: W5H Dimensions and FAR Patterns for Text-to-SQL Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05525)
*Vicki Stover Hertzberg,Eduardo Valverde,Joyce C. Ho*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了QUEST评估框架，通过FAR（结构化）和W5H（语义维度）分析发现，当前自然语言数据库接口在高级因果逻辑与程序化推理方面存在严重的性能上限，亟需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自然语言数据库接口在评估和设计上的理论基础薄弱，缺乏对查询理解的统一范式。

Method: 提出了QUEST框架，包含两个核心组件：1. FAR结构不变性（将查询归纳为过滤、聚合、返回）；2. W5H维度框架（将过滤标准映射至谁、什么、哪里、何时、为何、如何六个维度）。并在五个数据集（n=120,464）上进行了验证。

Result: FAR模型具有全域通用性；不同领域的W5H维度特征差异巨大（如医疗领域偏向时间及人物维度），且发现各领域在因果（WHY）和机制（HOW）推理上几乎为空白。

Conclusion: 当前的文本到SQL（Text-to-SQL）系统在因果推理（WHY）和程序化推理（HOW）方面显著缺失，这是迈向真正机器智能所必须突破的技术瓶颈。

Abstract: Natural language interfaces to databases have gained popularity, yet the theoretical foundations for evaluating and designing these systems remain underdeveloped. We present QUEST (Query Understanding Evaluation through Semantic Translation), a framework resting on two independently motivated components: the FAR structural invariant, which holds that every well-formed query reduces to Filter, Aggregate, and Return operations; and the W5H dimensional framework, which holds that all filtering criteria map to six semantic dimensions (Who, What, Where, When, Why, and How). Validated across five text-to-SQL datasets (n = 120,464), FAR conformance is universal across all domains and schema types, while W5H dimensional profiles vary substantially. Healthcare queries are strongly concentrated in temporal (WHEN: 80.4%) and person-centric (WHO: 73.0%) dimensions far exceeding general-domain benchmarks, and causal (WHY) and mechanistic (HOW) reasoning are near-zero everywhere, with apparent HOW exceptions reflecting quantitative aggregation rather than genuine procedural reasoning. These results identify a frontier that must be crossed for genuine machine reasoning over structured data.

</details>


### [294] [An Extensible and Verifiable Language for Query Rewrite Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05536)
*Sicheng Pan,Shuxian Wang,Wesley Zheng,Zirong Zeng,Vijay Sharma,Alvin Cheung*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Rulescript是一种无需依赖特定数据库引擎的DSL，旨在简化查询重写规则的开发、移植与形式化验证，实现跨多后端引擎的通用部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库查询重写规则多为手动实现、与特定引擎强耦合，缺乏正确性保证，导致开发与移植成本高昂且易引入错误。

Method: 提出了Rulescript，一种与引擎无关的领域特定语言（DSL）。通过基于关系代数的核心语言，将重写规则解构为“匹配”和“转换”两个阶段。同时，引入了形式化验证机制以确保正确性，并通过轻量级适配器与目标数据库引擎对接。

Result: 通过重实现Apache Calcite的33条重写规则，并成功部署到CockroachDB和Apache Data Fusion中，验证了其跨引擎的可移植性与低开发成本。

Conclusion: Rulescript实现了一种“一次编写，处处部署”的逻辑查询重写范式，通过解耦规则定义与执行引擎，大幅降低了重写规则的开发成本与移植难度，并保证了形式化的正确性。

Abstract: Logical query plan rewriting transforms a relational database query into an equivalent but more efficient form and is crucial to the performance of database-backed applications. In existing systems, rewrite rules are typically implemented manually, tightly coupled to specific execution engines, and often lack formal correctness guarantees. Consequently, developing a new engine requires reimplementing both legacy and new rules, incurring significant engineering cost, limiting portability, and every new implementation is an opportunity for introducing new bugs.
  We introduce Rulescript, an engine-agnostic domain-specific language (DSL) for developing query rewrite rules. Rulescript separates rule definition from execution infrastructure via a relational algebra-inspired core language and an explicit decomposition of rules into matching and transformation phases. Developers express rewrites by pattern-matching query plans using Rulescript's core operators and constructing semantically equivalent transformed plans, with all rewrites automatically verified formally to ensure correctness. Rulescript is extensible: users can define custom operators in terms of the core language to capture engine-specific semantics. To integrate with an existing system, developers need only implement a lightweight adapter that maps Rulescript's core and custom operators to the operators implemented in the target engine.
  We evaluate Rulescript by reimplementing 33 rewrite rules from Apache Calcite and extending the language with several custom operators. To demonstrate portability, we automatically deploy these rules to CockroachDB and Apache Data Fusion, two engines with substantially different backends. Our results show that Rulescript enables "write once, deploy everywhere" paradigm for query plan rewriting, with minimal effort required to deploy previously written rules on a new data engine.

</details>


### [295] [Low-Latency Out-of-Core ANN Search in High-Dimensional Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05787)
*Ziwen Song,Bin Wang,Xiaochun Yang,Junhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SkipDisk 是一种高性能的磁盘-内存混合 ANN 搜索方案，通过优化的下界过滤、高效的内存剪枝和异步 I/O，在显著降低内存消耗的同时实现了比 HNSW 更低的搜索延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图索引 ANN 搜索方法在内存存储（高性能但内存消耗大）与磁盘存储（省内存但延迟高）之间存在权衡，现有磁盘索引的过滤机制不够紧凑，导致磁盘 I/O 成为性能瓶颈。

Method: 1. 针对下界估计：为每个点设计专用枢纽点（Pivot），结合基于估计的过滤方法，通过三角不等式实现更紧凑的数据过滤；2. 内存优化：采用三级数据剪枝策略，在满足低内存占用的同时保留核心信息；3. 延迟优化：通过在内存中预存邻居节点，实现内存内搜索与磁盘 I/O 的解耦，并配合异步 I/O 策略加速查询。

Result: 在内存占用仅为 HNSW 的 10% 时，延迟可达 HNSW 的 85%；在内存占用为 20% 时，延迟可进一步降低至 HNSW 的 63%。

Conclusion: SkipDisk 通过创新的枢纽点过滤机制、三级数据剪枝策略和异步 I/O 设计，成功突破了磁盘检索的延迟瓶颈，实现了在极低内存占用下优于内存索引 HNSW 的检索性能。

Abstract: In-memory graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search has superior search performance but incurs significant memory footprint. Disk-based methods reduce memory usage but suffer from high disk access latency. A common challenge is how to achieve low-latency search while significantly reducing memory footprint. In this paper, we propose SkipDisk, a disk-memory hybrid ANN search that significantly reduces memory footprint while achieving search latency comparable to or lower than in-memory method HNSW. By analyzing existing disk-based methods, we observed that disk access remains the primary bottleneck, and existing lower bound based filtering methods are two loose to effectively reduce disk access. Therefore, we design SkipDisk to achieve tight lower bound with low memory footprint to reduce the search latency. First, we design a dedicated pivot for each point to improve the lower bound of the triangle inequality for effective filtering.
