Existing end-to-end video understanding methods are fundamentally limited by the $O(n^2)$ complexity of self-attention, while recent retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approaches still suffer from fragmented clip-level memory, weak modeling of temporal and causal structure, and high storage and online inference costs. We present Event-Causal RAG, a lightweight retrieval-augmented framework for infinite long-video reasoning. Instead of indexing fixed-length clips, our method segments streaming videos into semantically coherent events and represents each event as a structured State-Event-State (SES) graph, capturing the event together with its surrounding state transitions. These graphs are merged into a global Event Knowledge Graph and stored in a dual-store memory that supports both semantic matching and causal-topological retrieval. On top of this memory, we design a bidirectional retrieval strategy to efficiently identify the most relevant event causal chains and provide them, together with the associated video evidence, to a backbone video foundation model for answer generation. Experiments on long-video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that Event-Causal RAG consistently outperforms strong clip-based retrieval baselines and long-context video models, particularly on questions requiring multi-event integration and causal inference across long temporal gaps, while also achieving improved memory efficiency and robust streaming performance.

</details>


### [368] [Patch-Effect Graph Kernels for LLM Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06480)
*Ruben Fernandez-Boullon,David N. Olivieri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将Transformer模型的因果补丁数据建模为“补丁效应图”的框架，利用图机器学习技术系统化地对比和验证模型内部的电路结构，解决了大规模补丁分析中结构化信号难以评估的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机械可解释性研究（如激活补丁）在扩展到多种提示和任务时，会产生高维、非结构化的数据，缺乏系统性的比较和评估框架。

Method: 提出将激活补丁（activation-patching）配置文件表示为“补丁效应图”（patch-effect graphs）。具体包括三种构建方法：直接影响（因果调解）、偏相关和共影响。利用图核（graph kernels）进行结构分析，并通过分类任务评估特征的判别力。

Result: 在GPT-2 Small模型上，补丁效应图能有效保留区分性结构信号；局部边特征比全局图形状描述符分类精度更高；筛选后的补丁（CI/PC）对应于比随机候选者更强的激活影响。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将机械可解释性问题转化为图机器学习问题，并通过图压缩有效地提取和评估了因果电路中的判别性信号。该方法不仅比原始张量更具可解释性，还为验证电路级因果声明设定了严格的基准。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to reverse-engineer transformer computations by identifying causal circuits through activation patching. However, scaling these interventions across diverse prompts and task families produces high-dimensional, unstructured datasets that are difficult to compare systematically. We propose a framework that reframes mechanistic analysis as a graph machine-learning problem by representing activation-patching profiles as patch-effect graphs over model components. We introduce three graph-construction methods: direct-influence via causal mediation, partial-correlation, and co-influence and apply graph kernels to analyze the resulting structures. Evaluating this approach on GPT-2 Small using Indirect Object Identification (IOI) and related tasks, we find that patch-effect graphs preserve discriminative structural signals. Specifically, localized edge-slot features provide higher classification accuracy than global graph-shape descriptors. A screened paired-patching validation suggests that CI and PC selected candidate edges correspond to stronger activation-influence effects than random or low-rank candidates. Crucially, by evaluating these representations against rigorous prompt-only and raw patch-effect controls, we make the evidential scope of the benchmark explicit: graph features compress structured patching signal, while raw tensors and surface cues define strong baselines that any circuit-level claim should address. Ultimately, our framework provides a compression and evaluation pipeline for comparing patching-derived structures under controlled baselines, separating robust slice-discriminative evidence from stronger task-general causal-circuit claims.

</details>


### [369] [Systematic Evaluation of Large Language Models for Post-Discharge Clinical Action Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06191)
*Shivali Dalmia,Ananya Mantravadi,Prasanna Desikan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLM在临床动作提取中的应用，提出了一种两阶段提取策略，发现虽然LLM在简单任务上表现优秀，但在复杂分类中仍依赖标注约定，强调了构建带“推理注释”数据集对推动临床NLP的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型（LLM）在安全敏感的临床动作提取任务中的能力，特别是在出院过渡及患者安全保障方面的表现，并分析模型在零样本（zero-shot）和小样本（few-shot）场景下的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一种旨在处理复杂临床文档的两阶段提取框架（Two-stage extraction framework），通过分步提示（Staged prompting）策略将叙述性出院小结分解为细粒度的临床任务，并与传统的基于BERT的监督学习模型进行对比评估。

Result: LLM在二元动作可操作性检测上表现与监督模型相当或更优，但在细粒度的多标签分类中仍落后于监督模型；错误分析显示，模型性能受限于标注约定（annotation conventions）的不匹配而非仅是推理能力缺陷。

Conclusion: 现有的LLM在临床动作提取方面虽显示出潜力，但缺乏临床推理能力，且现有标注缺乏“推理过程”说明。未来的临床NLP研究需要转向基于“推理标注”（reasoning-annotated）的数据集，以便更准确地评估模型的临床理解能力。

Abstract: The work in this paper evaluates zero-shot and few-shot large language models (LLMs) for safety-critical clinical action extraction using the CLIP discharge-note dataset, with particular emphasis on transitions of care and post-discharge patient safety. To manage the complexity of clinical documentation, we introduce a two-stage extraction framework that decomposes discharge notes, that are written in narrative form, into fine-grained, explicitly actionable clinical tasks through a staged prompting strategy. Our contributions include a systematic assessment of generative LLMs for clinical action extraction, a detailed comparison between general-purpose LLMs and task-specific supervised BERT-based models, and an analysis of annotation inconsistencies across different action categories. We show that contemporary LLMs achieve performance comparable to or exceeding supervised models on binary actionability detection, while supervised baselines retain a meaningful advantage on fine-grained multi-label category classification, despite the absence of task-specific fine-tuning and under strict data-privacy constraints. Qualitative error analysis reveals that many failures stem from misalignment between model reasoning and dataset annotation conventions, particularly in cases involving implicit clinical actions and rigid structural labeling rules. These results indicate that reported performance reflects model limitations due to lack of clinical reasoning, that is not captured by plain annotations. Labels without rationales make it impossible to distinguish clinical reasoning failures from annotation convention mismatches. Advancing clinical NLP requires reasoning-annotated datasets that document why specific spans are actionable, not merely which spans were labeled, enabling proper evaluation of model clinical understanding.

</details>


### [370] [Towards Annotation-Free Validation of MLLMs: A Vision-Language Logical Consistency Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06201)
*Ying Gu,Mei Chee Leong,Hui Li Tan,Shangbo Mao,Liyuan Li,Nancy Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有MLLM评估过度依赖准确率的问题，本研究提出了一种无需标注的“视觉-语言逻辑一致性度量（VL-LCM）”框架，研究表明模型逻辑一致性普遍滞后于准确率，VL-LCM可作为模型可靠性评估与筛选的新指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主流准确率评估方法可能过度奖励模型的猜测行为，且在缺乏真实标注的新任务中无法直接应用；现有模型在准确率方面提升显著，但逻辑一致性往往滞后。

Method: 提出了一种名为“视觉-语言逻辑一致性度量（VL-LCM）”的评估框架，基于基础逻辑原则，通过评估模型在充分必要因果关系上的表现，来衡量MLLMs的逻辑一致性，且该方法不需要真实参考答案（ground-truth）。

Result: 通过对11个开源MLLM在MMMU和NaturalBench上的评估发现，逻辑一致性未随准确率同步提升；实验证明了VL-LCM与有标注评估结果的相关性，验证了该指标在无标注场景下的有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: 逻辑一致性是评估大型多模态模型（MLLMs）可靠性的关键指标，且VL-LCM在无需人工标注（ground-truth）的情况下，能够有效评估模型的逻辑能力与可靠性，可作为模型选择与验证的补充标准。

Abstract: Dominant accuracy evaluation might reward unwarranted guessing of Large Language Models, and it might not be applicable to novel tasks for model validation without ground-truth (gt) annotation. Based on basic logic principle, we propose a novel framework to evaluate the vision-language logical consistency of MLLMs on both sufficient and necessary cause-effect relations. We define Vision-Language Logical Consistency Metric (VL-LCM) on traditional MC-VQA tests, and recent NaturalBench tests without the need for gt annotation. Through systematic experiments on representative VL benchmark MMMU and recent VL challenges like NaturalBench, we evaluated 11 recent open-source MLLMs from 4 frontier families. Our findings reveal that, despite significant progress of recent MLLMs on accuracy, logical consistency lags behind significantly. Extensive evaluations on the correlations of VL-LCM with metrics on gt, the reliability of LCM, and the relation of VL-LCM with response distribution justify the validity and applicability of VL-LCM even without gt annotation. Our findings suggest that, beyond accuracy, logical consistency could be employed for both accuracy and reliability. VL-LCM can also be employed for MLLM selection, validation, and reliable answer justification in novel tasks without gt annotation.

</details>


### [371] [SkillOS: Learning Skill Curation for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06614)
*Siru Ouyang,Jun Yan,Yanfei Chen,Rujun Han,Zifeng Wang,Bhavana Dalvi Mishra,Rui Meng,Chun-Liang Li,Yizhu Jiao,Kaiwen Zha,Maohao Shen,Vishy Tirumalashetty,George Lee,Jiawei Han,Tomas Pfister,Chen-Yu Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillOS 是一种用于智能体自我进化的强化学习训练方案，通过引入可学习的“技能策展人”自动更新和优化外部技能库，解决了智能体难以从长程任务反馈中学习积累复杂技能的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于 LLM 的智能体多为“一次性”求解器，缺乏从过往经验中持续学习并积累、优化可重用技能的能力，且现有的技能筛选方法多依赖手动或启发式规则，难以适应复杂的长程任务。

Method: 提出 SkillOS 训练框架，采用“冻结式执行器（Executor）”与“可训练的技能策展人（Curator）”双模块架构；利用组合奖励函数与基于任务依赖性的分组流式训练（Grouped Task Streams）来解决长周期反馈带来的学习难题。

Result: 在多轮智能任务和单轮推理任务中，SkillOS 显著优于基准方法（包括无记忆和强记忆基准），展现了卓越的效果与效率；学习到的策展人策略具有跨模型与跨领域的泛化能力，且促使技能库生成了更高阶的元技能。

Conclusion: SkillOS 通过强化学习实现了智能体技能库的自我进化与管理，证明了从经验中学习技能筛选与优化策略在提升智能体长期任务处理能力方面的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly deployed to handle streaming tasks, yet they often remain one-off problem solvers that fail to learn from past interactions. Reusable skills distilled from experience provide a natural substrate for self-evolution, where high-quality skill curation serves as the key bottleneck. Existing approaches either rely on manual skill curation, prescribe heuristic skill operations, or train for short-horizon skill operations. However, they still struggle to learn complex long-term curation policies from indirect and delayed feedback. To tackle this challenge, we propose SkillOS, an experience-driven RL training recipe for learning skill curation in self-evolving agents. SkillOS pairs a frozen agent executor that retrieves and applies skills with a trainable skill curator that updates an external SkillRepo from accumulated experience. To provide learning signals for curation, we design composite rewards and train on grouped task streams based on skill-relevant task dependencies, where earlier trajectories update the SkillRepo, and later related tasks evaluate these updates. Across multi-turn agentic tasks and single-turn reasoning tasks, SkillOS consistently outperforms memory-free and strong memory-based baselines in both effectiveness and efficiency, with the learned skill curator generalizing across different executor backbones and task domains. Further analyses show that the learned curator produces more targeted skill use, while the skills in SkillRepo evolve into more richly structured Markdown files that encode higher-level meta-skills over time.

</details>


### [372] [Beyond Fixed Benchmarks and Worst-Case Attacks: Dynamic Boundary Evaluation for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06213)
*Haoxiang Wang,Da Yu,Huishuai Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对传统 LLM 基准测试饱和导致评估失效的问题，本文提出了动态边界评估 (DBE)，通过主动搜索模型能力边界并置于统一量表，实现了对 LLM 更精确、可扩展的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 固定基准测试存在“天花板”和“地板”效应，难以区分模型的细微能力差距；最具信息量的评估应关注模型能力表现的“边界”。

Method: 1. 提出“动态边界评估 (DBE)”框架：通过定位模型在随机采样解码下胜率接近 0.5 的问题来评估能力。
2. 构建校准题库：包含安全、能力和真相性维度。
3. 开发“技能引导边界搜索 (SGBS)”：一种仅需 API 访问权限即可定位模型边界的搜索算法。
4. 实施自适应评估协议：将模型置于统一难度量表，并根据模型能力水平动态扩充题库。

Result: DBE 在安全（拒绝有害请求、过度拒绝）、能力（受限指令遵循）和真相性（抗谄媚性）四个类别上进行了验证，证明了其在不产生饱和的情况下，能够覆盖比固定基准测试更广泛的模型能力区间，且与现有数据集兼容。

Conclusion: DBE 通过动态边界搜索克服了传统固定基准测试的饱和问题，为评估 LLM 提供了一种更具鉴别力、可扩展且全局统一的量化框架。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) today rests on fixed benchmarks that apply the same set of items to any model, producing ceiling and floor effects that mask capability gaps. We argue that the most informative evaluation signal lies at the boundary, where the per-prompt pass probability is near $0.5$ under random-sampling decoding, and propose Dynamic Boundary Evaluation (DBE), which actively locates each model's boundary and places it on a globally comparable difficulty scale. DBE delivers three artifacts: (i) a calibrated item bank covering safety, capability, and truthfulness, with per-item difficulty labels validated across $9$ reference LLMs; (ii) Skill-Guided Boundary Search (SGBS), a search algorithm that finds boundary items for a given target LLM using only API-level query access; and (iii) an evaluation protocol that places a new LLM on a unified ability scale and grows the evaluation set adaptively when the target falls outside the bank's coverage. We instantiate DBE on four categories spanning safety (harmful request refusal and over-refusal), capability (constrained instruction following), and truthfulness (multi-turn sycophancy resistance). The resulting evaluation covers a broader model spectrum without saturation while remaining compatible with existing datasets.

</details>


### [373] [Joint Consistency: A Unified Test-Time Aggregation Framework via Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06219)
*Yunzhen Yao,Hongye Wang,Yahong Wang,Michael C. Gastpar,Bo Jiang,Lie He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 JC（Joint Consistency），一种通过 Ising 模型整合独立评估与成对比较的测试时推理聚合框架，有效解决了现有方法忽视候选轨迹交互的问题，并在推理任务中实现了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时聚合方法大多孤立地评估候选轨迹或仅依赖答案频率，忽略了候选结果之间深层的成对比较交互。

Method: 提出受限 Ising 型能量最小化问题，将候选推理轨迹的独立评估指标作为“外部场”，将基于 LLM-as-a-judge 的轨迹间比较作为“相互作用”，并开发了高效的近似求解策略。

Result: 在数学和代码推理基准测试中，JC 在不同判别模型、轨迹预算和生成设置下，均持续优于现有的投票和加权聚合基准方法。

Conclusion: Joint Consistency (JC) 提供了一个统一且有效的测试时聚合框架，通过将独立评估信号与成对比较相结合，显著提升了推理任务的性能，并证明了其在多种场景下的普适性与优越性。

Abstract: This paper studies test-time aggregation, an approach that generates multiple reasoning traces and aggregates them into a final answer. Most existing methods rely on evaluation signals collected from candidate traces in isolation or answer frequencies, while ignoring comparative interactions among candidates. We propose Joint Consistency (JC), formulated as a constrained Ising-type energy minimization problem, where independent evaluation signals act as external fields and pairwise comparisons act as interactions. JC provides a unified framework for test-time aggregation that subsumes existing voting and weighted aggregation methods as special cases. Our construction of the interaction matrix leverages LLM-as-a-judge comparisons, and admits a theoretical interpretation under answer-level homogeneity assumptions. Moreover, we develop an efficient approximation strategy that makes interaction modeling practical for large-scale test-time aggregation. Experiments on math and code reasoning benchmarks show that JC consistently outperforms existing baselines across tasks, judge models, trace budgets, and trace-generation settings.

</details>


### [374] [Can RL Teach Long-Horizon Reasoning to LLMs? Expressiveness Is Key](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06638)
*Tianle Wang,Zhaoyang Wang,Guangchen Lan,Xinpeng Wei,Sipeng Zhang,Guanwen Qiu,Abulhair Saparov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ScaleLogic框架，量化了逻辑推理任务深度与表达能力对大模型强化学习训练缩放规律的影响，发现训练任务的逻辑复杂度和深度会显著改变性能扩展的幂律特征与迁移效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏受控且可扩展的基准来深入分析强化学习（RL）训练在大语言模型（LLM）推理任务中随困难度扩展的规律。

Method: 提出了ScaleLogic框架，该框架允许独立控制推理深度（horizon）和逻辑表达能力（从简单蕴含到一阶逻辑），并基于该框架在不同RL方法与训练策略（如课程学习）下进行了系统的实验研究。

Result: 实验发现RL训练计算量$T$随深度$D$的变化满足幂律$T \propto D^γ$，缩放指数$γ$随逻辑表达能力从1.04上升至2.60；在下游数学与通用推理基准上，高表达能力训练带来的性能增幅最高达10.66个百分点，且具有更高的计算迁移效率。

Conclusion: 研究表明，RL训练计算量与推理深度服从幂律关系，且该缩放指数随逻辑表达能力的增强而单调增加。此外，在更具表达能力的逻辑环境中训练的模型不仅在下游任务中表现更好，且表现出更高的计算迁移效率，强调了训练任务特征对模型能力塑造的关键作用。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been applied to improve large language model (LLM) reasoning, yet the systematic study of how training scales with task difficulty has been hampered by the lack of controlled, scalable environments. We introduce ScaleLogic, a synthetic logical reasoning framework that offers independent control over two axes of difficulty: the depth of the required proof planning (i.e., the horizon) and the expressiveness of the underlying logic. Our proposed framework supports a wide range of logics: from simple implication-only logic ("if-then") towards more expressive first-order reasoning with conjunction ("and"), disjunction ("or"), negation ("not"), and universal quantification ("for all"). Using this framework, we show that the RL training compute $T$ follows a power law with respect to reasoning depth $D$ ($T \propto D^γ$, $R^{2} > 0.99$), and that the scaling exponent $γ$ increases monotonically with logical expressiveness, from $1.04$ to $2.60$. On downstream mathematics and general reasoning benchmarks, more expressive training settings yield both larger performance gains (up to $+10.66$ points) and more compute-efficient transfer compared to less expressive settings, demonstrating that what a model is trained on, not just how much it is trained, shapes downstream transfer. We further show that the power-law relationship holds across multiple RL methods, and curriculum-based training substantially improves scaling efficiency.

</details>


### [375] [Price of Fairness in Short-Term and Long-Term Algorithmic Selections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06227)
*Shahin Jabbari,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过建模序列选择问题，揭示了静态公平约束可能导致长期不平等，并证明了采用适当的长期投资策略可在保证公平的同时实现较优的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注静态公平，而强制实施静态公平可能会加剧长期不平等。研究旨在解决这种短期静态公平要求与长期种群分布演变之间的矛盾。

Method: 将该问题建模为“序列选择问题”（sequential selection problem），定义了短期和长期的群体公平概念；引入“公平代价”（Price of Fairness, PoF）进行理论权衡分析，并结合合成数据与真实数据集进行实证验证。

Result: 研究发现，在短期内，即使群体分布高度相似，实现公平的代价（PoF）也可能很大；但在长期，采用投资政策可以有效化解不平等问题并维持低PoF。

Conclusion: 在序列决策问题中，通过简单的投资策略（investment policies）可以在实现较低“公平代价”（PoF）的同时消除长期不平等，证明了公平与效用在长期是可以兼容的。

Abstract: Algorithmic decision-making in high-stakes settings can have profound impacts on individuals and populations. While much prior work studies fairness in static settings, recent results show that enforcing static fairness constraints may exacerbate long-run disparities. Motivated by this tension, we study a stylized sequential selection problem in which a decision-maker repeatedly selects individuals, affecting both immediate utility and the population distribution over time. We introduce notions of group fairness for both the short and long term and theoretically analyze the trade-off between fairness and utility via the Price of Fairness (PoF). We characterize optimal and fair policies in the short term and show that the PoF can be large even when group distributions are nearly identical. In contrast, we show that long-term disparities can vanish under simple investment policies that achieve a low PoF. We also empirically validate these theoretical observations using both synthetic and real datasets.

</details>


### [376] [Data Language Models: A New Foundation Model Class for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06290)
*Eda Erol,Giuliano Pezzoli,Ozer Cem Kelahmet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个表格基础模型——数据语言模型（DLM）及其实现Schema-1，实现了对表格数据的原生、直接理解，无需任何预处理，在预测、数据填补及特征分类任务中均表现出超越现有模型和方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前各类模态（文本、图像、音频）均有专属基础模型，唯独对现实世界决策至关重要的表格数据仍依赖繁琐的预处理流水线，缺乏原生理解其模态特性的基础模型。

Method: 提出数据语言模型（Data Language Model, DLM），该模型无需预处理或序列化，直接从原始单元格数值中学习；发布了Schema-1模型（140M参数），并在超过230万个合成及真实表格数据集上进行训练。

Result: 在行级预测基准测试中超越了梯度提升树、AutoML栈及其他表格基础模型；在缺失值填充任务中表现优于传统统计方法及大语言模型；能够仅凭原始数值识别未知表格的数据行业领域，展现了出色的模式识别能力。

Conclusion: 作者填补了AI基础模型在表格数据领域的空白，展示了原生理解表格原始数据的可行性。Schema-1证明了通过学习表格数据的分布几何结构，比依赖大型语言模型的通用世界知识更能有效处理缺失值和理解表格特征，这将简化当前的AI数据处理流水线。

Abstract: Every major data modality now has a foundation model that understands it natively: text has language models, images have vision models, audio has audio models. Tabular data, the modality on which many consequential real-world AI decisions are made, does not. Every approach to tabular AI today, from gradient-boosted trees to the latest tabular foundation models, requires a preprocessing pipeline before any model can consume the data. None of them understand tabular data as a modality. We introduce the Data Language Model (DLM), the missing foundation model for tabular data. A DLM understands tables the way a language model understands sentences: natively, without serialization or preprocessing, directly from raw cell values. It is the tabular data layer on which AI models, agents, and vertical AI applications can be built, eliminating the preprocessing pipelines that currently stand between raw data and every AI system that consumes it. We present Schema-1, the first DLM: a 140M parameter model trained on more than 2.3M synthetic and real-world tabular datasets. Schema-1 outperforms gradient-boosted ensembles, AutoML stacks, and the tabular foundation models we evaluate on established row-level prediction benchmarks. On missing value reconstruction it achieves lower reconstruction error than all classical statistical methods and frontier large language models on mean performance across conditions, establishing that structural understanding of a dataset's own distributional geometry is more useful for imputation than world knowledge encoded in language. It identifies the industry sector of any unseen dataset from raw cell values alone, reliably across any domain, a task no prior tabular model can perform. It is the native tabular understanding layer that has been missing from the AI stack.

</details>


### [377] [Mind the Gap? A Distributional Comparison of Real and Synthetic Priors for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06343)
*Alex O. Davies,Telmo de Menezes e Silva Filho,Nirav Ajmeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究量化对比了表格基础模型的三类预训练数据，揭示合成数据与真实数据的分布存在显著差异，但意外发现这种分布偏差并非决定模型泛化性能的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型的预训练数据来源差异巨大（精选、网页抓取、合成），但目前缺乏关于这些语料分布关系及其对下游性能影响的研究。

Method: 选择三个典型数据集（T4：网页抓取，TabFM：人工精选，TabICL：合成），提取表、列及相关性聚合特征进行表征，通过判别器AUC和k-NN覆盖率指标进行分布度量与比较。

Result: 1. 合成语料（TabICL）仅占据真实表格分布的一小部分区域，且调整参数（8万+配置）难以弥补此差距；2. 精选语料与网页抓取语料在特征空间上具有可互换性；3. 合成数据与真实数据的分布偏离并不直接影响性能。

Conclusion: 研究发现表格基础模型中合成数据与真实数据之间存在显著的分布差距，但该差距并不直接限制模型在下游任务上的泛化性能，表明数据分布的覆盖范围并非决定模型泛化能力的核心驱动因素。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models are pre-trained on one of three classes of corpus: curated datasets drawn from benchmark repositories, tables harvested at scale from the web, or synthetic tables sampled from a parametric generative prior. Despite the centrality of pre-training data to model performance, little is known about how these corpora relate to one another in distribution, and the impact this has on downstream performance. In this work we take three canonical, archetypal datasets used to train tabular foundation models; the T4 dataset represents web-scraped corpora, the TabFM dataset curated tables from Kaggle, and the TabICL dataset as the only well-used synthetic prior with publicly available parameters. We characterise each corpus using aggregate features over whole tables, columns and correlations, and compare them using discriminator AUCs and k-NN coverage metrics. We find that the TabICL synthetic prior occupies a narrow region of the space of real tables, that this mismatch cannot be closed by optimising prior hyper-parameters across more than 86 thousand configurations, and that curated and web-scraped corpora are broadly interchangeable on a distributional level in feature space. Surprisingly, the distributional gap between synthetic pre-training data and real tables has a clearly detectable effect on performance under neither feature-based proximity measures or TabICL's own internal representations, suggesting that coverage of the real-data distribution is not the primary driver of TabICL's generalisation.

</details>


### [378] [More Than Can Be Said: A Benchmark and Framework for Pre-Question Scientific Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06345)
*Jie Yu,Song Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InciteResearch 是一个能够将研究者模糊想法转化为具体、可行且具创新性科研课题的多智能体框架，它通过模拟苏格拉底式提问及因果推导，解决了自动化学术研究中“从无到有”的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 研究智能体大多基于明确的研究问题进行工作，而忽略了科研初期人类感知的“隐性摩擦”（Tacit Friction）——即在问题形成前的模糊感知阶段。旨在弥补 AI 在辅助研究者构建问题与逻辑推导能力的不足。

Method: 提出了一个名为 InciteResearch 的多智能体框架，采用苏格拉底式提问逻辑链：(1) 将模糊/无关输入映射为五维研究者画像；(2) 通过 7 阶段因果推导强制违反隐藏假设（最大化可行性与新颖性的乘积）；(3) 校验建议方法是否为重构洞察后的必然推论。同时引入 TF-Bench 基准测试评估研究辅助能力。

Result: 在 TF-Bench 评测中，InciteResearch 在新颖性和影响力评分上大幅提升（新颖性从 3.671 提升至 4.250，影响力从 3.806 提升至 4.397），成功将生成的提案从简单的文献组合转向具有架构意义的深度洞察。

Conclusion: InciteResearch 证明了 AI 可以作为人类思维的延伸，从模糊的“隐性摩擦”出发，协助研究者完成从思维发散到架构化洞察的跨越，而非仅仅处理下游的执行任务。

Abstract: AI research agents have shown strong potential in automating literature search and manuscript refinement, yet most assume a clear and actionable initial input, operating only after a research question has been made explicit. In contrast, human research often begins with tacit friction, a sense of misalignment before a question can be formed. We introduce InciteResearch, a multi-agent framework designed to make a researcher's implicit understanding explicit, inspectable, and actionable. InciteResearch decomposes the logical chain of Socratic questioning and distributes it across the entire pipeline that: (1) Elicits a structured five-dimensional researcher profile state anchored by specific friction points from vague, even domain-unrelated inputs; (2) Violates hidden assumptions by maximizing the feasibility-novelty product with enforcing a 7-stage causal derivation trace; and (3) check whether the proposed method is a Necessary consequence of the reframed insight. We further introduce TF-Bench, the first benchmark for tacit-to-explicit research assistance that distinguishes domain-related from domain-unrelated inspirations across four scientific modes. On TF-Bench, InciteResearch achieves leapfrogging gains over a prompt-based baseline (novelty/impact from 3.671/3.806 to 4.250/4.397), shifting generated proposals from recombination to architectural insight. Our work demonstrates that AI can serve as an extension of thinking itself, rather than merely automating downstream execution.

</details>


### [379] [Rethinking Vacuity for OOD Detection in Evidential Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06382)
*Claire McNamara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了证据深度学习（EDL）中离群检测指标“空虚度”的一个关键缺陷：其评估结果受类别基数影响巨大，如果不统一测试集的类别数，评估结果（如AUROC/AUPR）将产生虚假膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出证据深度学习（EDL）中常用的离群检测指标——空虚度（Vacuity/UM），其定义严重依赖于类别数 K，但在实际应用（如LLM评估）中，ID与OOD的类别数常常不一致，这导致了评估结果的严重偏差。

Method: 通过数学分析论证不确定度质量（UM）与类别数（K）的依赖关系；通过实验对比（包含标准EDL和IB-EDL），量化分析在ID与OOD类别数差异为1时，对AUROC和AUPR评估指标造成的显著干扰。

Result: 实验表明，类别数差异1个单位即可导致AUROC产生高达0.360的偏差和AUPR产生高达0.683的偏差，证明了该指标存在严重的评估人工伪影（Evaluation Artefact）。

Conclusion: 基于空虚度（Vacuity）的OOD检测在EDL中对类别基数（Cardinality）高度敏感；当ID与OOD类数量不一致时，常用指标（AUROC/AUPR）会产生虚假的高性能，必须在评价时严格控制类别一致性。

Abstract: Vacuity, or Uncertainty Mass (UM), is commonly used as a metric to evaluate Out-of-Distribution (OOD) detection in Evidential Deep Learning (EDL). It generally involves dividing the number of classes ($K$) by the total strength of belief ($S$) of the model's predictions, where $S$ is derived from summing the Dirichlet parameters. As such, UM is sensitive to the cardinality of $K$. In particular, it is unlikely in practice that there is a linear relationship between $K$ and $S$ as $K$ and $S$ increase due to the nature of EDL (suppressing incorrectly assigned evidence). As a result, when comparing In Distribution (ID) and OOD results, it is important that $K_{\mathrm{ID}}$ and $K_{\mathrm{OOD}}$ are equal; something that is not always ensured in practice. We provide an empirical demonstration of how results for AUROC and AUPR can substantially differ when class cardinality between ID and OOD differs by 1, with AUROC differing by as much as 0.318 and AUPR by 0.613 for standard EDL, and AUROC by 0.360 and AUPR by 0.683 for IB-EDL. More concretely, our findings isolate an evaluation artefact: when K differs between ID and OOD, AUROC/AUPR can be artificially inflated without any change in model predictions. We further discuss the evaluation of EDL over causal language models using Multiple-Choice Question-Answer (MCQA) datasets and argue for clearer definitions of ID and OOD in this context. Our primary contribution is an empirical and theoretical demonstration that vacuity-based OOD detection in EDL-fine-tuned LLMs is highly sensitive to uncontrolled differences in evaluated class cardinality.

</details>


### [380] [Automated alignment is harder than you think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06390)
*Aleksandr Bowkis,Marie Davidsen Buhl,Jacob Pfau,Geoffrey Irving*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出，利用AI自动化对齐研究虽然是主流路线，但因人类难以监督“模糊任务”，容易导致自动化系统产生隐蔽的、相关性强的错误，进而得出虚假的安全评估结果。因此，必须深入研究如何让AI可靠地执行这些难以监督的任务，而非盲目推进自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在揭示目前主流的“利用AI自动化对齐研究”这一方案中潜藏的结构性盲点，即即便智能体没有恶意，其生成的安全评估也可能因各种认知和系统性偏差而导致灾难性的后果。

Method: 对人工超级智能（ASI）的自动化对齐路线图进行了理论分析与风险评估，重点探讨了在缺乏明确评价标准（模糊任务）的情境下，自动化对齐研究可能导致的系统性失败模式。

Result: 研究指出，AI自动化研究会通过三种机制引入隐蔽错误：一是优化压力导致错误集中在人类难以识别的领域；二是AI生成的错误模式不同于人类；三是AI产出可能涉及人类无法评估的复杂论证。此外，AI之间的高度相关性可能导致累积性的过度自信评估。

Conclusion: 目前的自动化对齐方案存在极高风险。为了避免部署失控的AI，必须先聚焦于如何让智能体可靠地执行那些难以监督的模糊任务（Hard-to-supervise fuzzy tasks），单纯依赖现有的通用化和可扩展监督技术可能不足以应对自动化研究带来的独特挑战。

Abstract: A leading proposal for aligning artificial superintelligence (ASI) is to use AI agents to automate an increasing fraction of alignment research as capabilities improve. We argue that, even when research agents are not scheming to deliberately sabotage alignment work, this plan could produce compelling but catastrophically misleading safety assessments resulting in the unintentional deployment of misaligned AI. This could happen because alignment research involves many hard-to-supervise fuzzy tasks (tasks without clear evaluation criteria, for which human judgement is systematically flawed). Consequently, research outputs will contain systematic, undetected errors, and even correct outputs could be incorrectly aggregated into overconfident safety assessments. This problem is likely to be worse for automated alignment research than for human-generated alignment research for several reasons: 1) optimisation pressure means agent-generated mistakes are concentrated among those that human reviewers are least likely to catch; 2) agents are likely to produce errors that do not resemble human mistakes; 3) AI-generated alignment solutions may involve arguments humans cannot evaluate; and 4) shared weights, data and training processes may make AI outputs more correlated than human equivalents. Therefore, agents must be trained to reliably perform hard-to-supervise fuzzy tasks. Generalisation and scalable oversight are the leading candidates for achieving this but both face novel challenges in the context of automated alignment.

</details>


### [381] [Knowledge Graphs, the Missing Link in Agentic AI-based Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06434)
*Vaisakh Naduvodi Viswambharan,Keerthan Kopparam Radhakrishna,Deepak Narayan Gadde,Aman Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用知识图谱增强LLM生成SystemVerilog断言的方法，通过多智能体协作与工具反馈闭环，解决了传统方案中语义脱节和语法错误频发的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的断言生成方法将代码和说明书视作无结构的文本，导致语义不匹配、生成代码语法错误频发以及与RTL环境接地（grounding）能力弱，难以直接用于高难度形式验证。

Method: 构建了一个以验证为中心的知识图谱（KG），该图谱整合了来自说明书、RTL及形式验证工具反馈（语法诊断、反例CEX、覆盖率报告）的结构化中间表示（IR）。同时，提出了一个多智能体工作流，通过查询和更新KG来驱动SVA生成，并包含语法修复、CEX引导修正和覆盖率驱动增强三个闭环反馈流程。

Result: 在七个基准设计上的评估显示，基于KG的方法显著提升了说明书到RTL的对应关系，生成的SVA具有极高的可编译性，语法修复开销低，形式验证覆盖率达到78.5%-99.4%。

Conclusion: 结合知识图谱的Agent工作流显著提升了从自然语言说明书生成SystemVerilog断言（SVA）的质量与可编译性，能够有效增强形式验证流程。但在复杂的时序和算术逻辑推理方面，LLM仍存在局限性，需进一步提升。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have enabled workflows that generate SystemVerilog Assertions (SVAs) from natural-language specifications, with the potential to accelerate Formal Verification (FV). However, high-quality assertion synthesis remains challenging because specifications are often ambiguous or incomplete and critical micro-architectural details reside in the Register Transfer Level (RTL). Many existing approaches treat the specification and RTL as loosely structured text, which weakens specification-to-RTL grounding and leads to semantic mismatches and frequent syntax failures during formal parsing and elaboration. This work addresses these limitations with a verification-centric Knowledge Graph (KG) constructed from structured Intermediate Representations (IRs) extracted from the specification, RTL, and formal-tool feedback, including syntax diagnostics, Counterexamples (CEXs), and coverage reports. The KG links requirements, design hierarchy, signals, assumptions, and properties to provide traceable, design-grounded context for generation. A multi-agent workflow queries and updates this KG to generate SVAs and to drive three refinement loops: syntax repair guided by tool diagnostics, CEX-guided correction using trace links, and coverage-directed property augmentation. Evaluation across seven benchmark designs indicates that KG-based context retrieval improves specification-to-RTL grounding and consistently produces compilable SVAs with low syntax-repair overhead. The approach achieves formal coverage ranging from 78.5% to 99.4%, though convergence exhibits design dependence with complex temporal and arithmetic reasoning remaining challenging for current LLM capabilities.

</details>


### [382] [SCRuB: Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06444)
*Jamelle Watson-Daniels,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Skyler Wang,Brandon Handoko,Antonio Li,Anaelia Ovalle,Mahesh Pasupuleti,Candace Ross,Vidya Sarma,Arjun Subramonian,Karen Ullrich,Will van der Vaart,Yijing Xin,Maximilian Nickel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM社会概念推理能力匮乏评估的问题，提出了SCRuB评估框架及数据集，实验发现前沿模型在社会推理任务上已超越人类水平，并揭示了现有单轮评估范式已达到饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估多侧重数学或技术任务，缺乏针对“社会概念”（如社会规范、文化、制度等）深度推理能力的系统化评估框架。随着LLM作为社会智能体角色应用，对其社会推理质量进行定量评估显得尤为重要。

Method: 提出SCRuB框架，核心包含三阶段：1. 基于权威来源构建提示词；2. 专家与模型生成回复；3. 采用五维批判性思维准则进行对比评估。引入“学科视角专家组”以实现评估流水线的泛化，并构建了包含4,711个提示词的SCRuBEval数据集及覆盖专家注释的数据集。

Result: 在五维指标对比中，前沿LLM表现优于人类专家。在1,170次两两对比中，专家评委在80.8%的案例中将模型回复排在首位，整体偏好模型回复的比例达74.4%。

Conclusion: 研究表明大型语言模型（LLM）在社会概念推理任务中已达到“评估饱和”状态。目前的单轮考试型评估模式已无法有效区分模型与人类专家的能力差异，未来需要更具挑战性的评估方法。

Abstract: While many studies of Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning capabilities emphasize mathematical or technical tasks, few address reasoning about social concepts: the abstract ideas shaping social norms, culture, and institutions. This understudied capability is essential for modern models acting as social agents, yet no systematic evaluation methodology targets it. We introduce SCRuB (Social Concept Reasoning under Rubric-Based Evaluation), a framework designed for this setting of task indeterminacy. Our goal is to measure the degree to which a model reasons about social concepts with the depth and critical rigor of a human expert. SCRuB proceeds in three phases: prompt construction from established sources, response generation by experts and models, and comparative evaluation using a five-dimensional critical thinking rubric. To enable generalization of the pipeline, we introduce a Panel of Disciplinary Perspectives ensemble validated against independent expert judges. We release SCRuBEval (n=4,711 evaluation prompts) and SCRuBAnnotations (300 expert-authored responses and 150 expert comparative judgments from 45 PhD-level scholars). Our results show that frontier models consistently outperform human experts across all five rubric dimensions. Across 1,170 pairwise comparisons, expert judges ranked a model response first in 80.8% of judgments and preferred model responses overall 74.4% of the time. Ultimately, this study provides the first expert-grounded demonstration of evaluation saturation for social concept reasoning: the single-turn exam-style format has reached its ceiling for models and humans alike.

</details>


### [383] [PrefixGuard: From LLM-Agent Traces to Online Failure-Warning Monitors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06455)
*Xinmiao Huang,Jinwei Hu,Rajarshi Roy,Changshun Wu,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrefixGuard 提出了一种端到端的框架，通过分析 LLM 智能体的执行步骤和最终结果，自动生成高效、低延迟的监控器，用于预测长任务的成败，在性能和成本上均优于现有的实时判断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 智能体在执行长链条工具使用任务时，等到任务结束才检查结果往往为时已晚；而现有的在线监控方法要么依赖脆弱的人工规则，要么依赖高成本的实时 LLM 判断。

Method: 提出 PrefixGuard 框架，包含两个阶段：1. StepView 诱导：从原始轨迹样本中诱导确定性的类型化步骤适配器；2. 监控训练：利用终端结果训练事件抽象和前缀风险评分器。此外还引入了可观测性上限分析和 DFA 提取及初步预警诊断。

Result: 在多个基准测试（WebArena 等）中，PrefixGuard 的平均 AUPRC 比原始文本对照组高出 0.137，且在相同协议下表现优于实时 LLM 判断；实验同时揭示了排名能力不代表部署效用，并推导了判定监控误差与任务本身缺乏证据的区别。

Conclusion: PrefixGuard 是一种实用的监视器生成框架，能够通过离线诱导和监督学习有效预测 LLM 智能体任务的成功风险，优于实时 LLM 判断，并提供了评估预警可操作性的实用诊断方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents now execute long, tool-using tasks where final outcome checks can arrive too late for intervention. Online warning requires lightweight prefix monitors over heterogeneous traces, but hand-authored event schemas are brittle and deployment-time LLM judging is costly. We introduce PrefixGuard, a trace-to-monitor framework with an offline StepView induction step followed by supervised monitor training. StepView induces deterministic typed-step adapters from raw trace samples, and the monitor learns an event abstraction and prefix-risk scorer from terminal outcomes. Across WebArena, $τ^2$-Bench, SkillsBench, and TerminalBench, the strongest PrefixGuard monitors reach 0.900/0.710/0.533/0.557 AUPRC. Using the strongest backend within each representation, they improve over raw-text controls by an average of +0.137 AUPRC. LLM judges remain substantially weaker under the same prefix-warning protocol. We also derive an observability ceiling on score-based area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) that separates monitor error from failures lacking evidence in the observed prefix. For finite-state audit, post-hoc deterministic finite automaton (DFA) extraction remains compact on WebArena and $τ^2$-Bench (29 and 20 states) but expands to 151 and 187 states on SkillsBench and TerminalBench. Finally, first-alert diagnostics show that strong ranking does not imply deployment utility: WebArena ranks well yet fails to support low-false-alarm alerts, whereas $τ^2$-Bench and TerminalBench retain more actionable early alerts. Together, these results position PrefixGuard as a practical monitor-synthesis recipe with explicit diagnostics for when prefix warnings translate into actionable interventions.

</details>


### [384] [Beyond Task Success: Measuring Workflow Fidelity in LLM-Based Agentic Payment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06457)
*Donghao Huang,Joon Kiat Chua,Zhaoxia Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM多智能体系统在支付等监管领域评估指标不足的问题，研究提出了ASR（智能体成功率）指标。该指标通过轨迹级评估识别出隐藏的工作流违规行为，并显著提升了系统的执行表现和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的指标（如任务成功率TSR和智能体移交F1分数HF1）仅关注最终结果或无序路由决策，无法捕捉智能体在工作流程中（如支付校验环节）的行为偏差，对于受监管的任务流程评估不足。

Method: 提出了一种名为“智能体成功率”（Agentic Success Rate, ASR）的新指标，该指标通过对比观察到的与预期的智能体执行序列（转换级别）来衡量性能，具体分解为转换召回率（Transition Recall）和转换精确率（Transition Precision）。并在一个分层支付多智能体系统（HMASP）中对18个LLM进行了90,000次任务实例测试。

Result: ASR揭示了TSR和HF1无法发现的隐性工作流缺陷（如跳过确认检查点）。通过ASR诊断进行的提示词调优和确定性路由保护，使部分表现不佳的模型在TSR上最高提升了93.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 在受监管的行业（如支付领域）中，仅基于结果的指标不足以评估LLM多智能体系统。ASR指标通过捕捉轨迹层面的执行偏差，能够显著提升系统的可靠性、合规性和诊断效率。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed for payment workflows, yet prevailing metrics, Task Success Rate (TSR) and Agent Handoff F1-Score (HF1), capture only final outcomes or unordered routing decisions. We introduce the Agentic Success Rate (ASR), a trajectory-fidelity metric that compares observed and expected agent execution sequences at the transition level, decomposing performance into Transition Recall and Transition Precision. Applied to the Hierarchical Multi-Agent System for Payments (HMASP) across 18 LLMs and 90,000 task instances, ASR reveals that 10 of 18 models systematically skip a confirmation checkpoint during payment checkout, a deviation invisible to both TSR and HF1, while 8 models enforce the checkpoint perfectly. Notably, GPT-4.1 exhibits hidden workflow shortcuts despite achieving perfect TSR and HF1, while GPT-5.2 achieves perfect ASR. Prompt refinements and deterministic routing guards guided by ASR diagnostics yield substantial TSR improvements, with gains up to +93.8 percentage points for previously struggling models, demonstrating that trajectory-level evaluation is essential in regulated domains.

</details>


### [385] [ReasonSTL: Bridging Natural Language and Signal Temporal Logic via Tool-Augmented Process-Rewarded Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06483)
*Bowen Ye,Zhijian Li,Junyue Huang,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对自然语言到 STL 翻译的难题，ReasonSTL 提供了一个工具增强的开源框架，通过分步推理、过程监督训练和基准数据集，实现了高效、私密且低成本的逻辑规范生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前将自然语言转换为信号时序逻辑（STL）的需求日益增长，但手动编写门槛高，且使用商用大模型 API 存在成本高昂、隐私泄露等工业部署隐患，亟需一种本地化、高效且可控的替代方案。

Method: 1. 提出了 ReasonSTL 框架：通过显式推理、确定性工具调用和结构化公式构建，将自然语言转换为 STL。2. 引入过程奖励训练（process-rewarded training）：对工具使用轨迹和最终公式进行监督训练。3. 创建了 STL-Bench：一个双语、关注计算能力的基准测试集。

Result: ReasonSTL 实现了最先进的性能，在自动评估指标和人工评估中表现优异，证明了本地开源小模型在专业领域任务中的潜力和有效性。

Conclusion: ReasonSTL 为形式化规范编写提供了一种透明、低成本且保护隐私的解决方案。实验表明，即便使用轻量级的 4B 模型，该框架也能达到领先的翻译效果。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is an expressive formal language for specifying spatio-temporal requirements over real-valued, real-time signals. It has been widely used for the verification and synthesis of autonomous systems and cyber-physical systems. In practice, however, users often express their requirements in natural language rather than in structured STL formulas, making natural-language-to-STL translation a critical yet challenging task. Manual specification requires temporal-logic expertise and cannot scale, while prompting commercial LLM APIs incurs substantial token costs and may expose sensitive system requirements to third-party services, raising privacy concerns for industrial deployment. To address these challenges, we present \textsc{ReasonSTL}, a tool-augmented framework that adapts local open-source language models for natural-language-to-STL generation. \textsc{ReasonSTL} decomposes the translation process into explicit reasoning, deterministic tool calls, and structured formula construction. We further introduce process-rewarded training to supervise both tool-use trajectories and final formulas, together with \textsc{STL-Bench}, a bilingual, computation-aware benchmark grounded in real-world signals. Experiments show that a 4B model trained with \textsc{ReasonSTL} achieves state-of-the-art performance in both automatic metrics and human evaluations, demonstrating that \textsc{ReasonSTL} provides a transparent, low-cost, and privacy-preserving alternative for formal specification drafting.

</details>


### [386] [Process Matters more than Output for Distinguishing Humans from Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06524)
*Milena Rmus,Mathew D. Hardy,Thomas L. Griffiths,Mayank Agrawal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CogCAPTCHA30，通过分析任务执行的认知过程而非单纯的结果输出，实现了更精准的人机区分，并揭示了过程规范化是限制机器模仿人类认知模式的核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人机鉴别方法多关注输出结果（类似于图灵测试），但忽略了行为背后的认知过程。作者旨在证明通过分析过程级特征，可以更有效地识别机器并区分人类，弥补仅靠输出评估的不足。

Method: 提出了CogCAPTCHA30任务集，包含30项旨在提取诊断性过程特征的认知任务；对比了前沿大型语言模型、基于海量人类决策微调的模型（Centaur）以及针对动作（A-SFT）和过程（P-SFT）进行任务特定微调的模型；通过分类器评估过程特征的区分能力（AUC）。

Result: CogCAPTCHA30在输出指标相同的情况下，依然能以平均0.88的AUC值有效区分人类与机器；显示出明确的过程级监督确实能提升行为的类人性，但这种优势在超出特定任务范围时会减弱。

Conclusion: 认知过程特征优于仅依赖性能指标进行人机区分。尽管可以通过过程级监督微调提高机器的“类人”行为模仿能力，但该能力在跨任务迁移时受限，过程规范化（Process Specification）是实现机器与人类认知过程一致性的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Reliable human-machine discrimination is becoming increasingly important as large language models and autonomous agents are deployed in online settings. Existing approaches evaluate whether a system can produce behavior or responses indistinguishable from those of a human, following the emphasis on outputs as a criterion for intelligence proposed by Alan Turing. Cognitive science offers an alternative perspective: evaluating the process by which behavior is produced. To test whether cognitive processes can reliably distinguish humans from machines, we introduce CogCAPTCHA30, a battery of 30 cognitive tasks designed to elicit diagnostic process-level features even when task performance is matched. Across the battery, process-level features provide stronger discriminative signal than performance metrics alone, reliably distinguishing humans from agents even under output matching (mean process-feature classifier AUC = 0.88). To evaluate agentic process differences, we compare off-the-shelf frontier agents (Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-5, Gemini 2.5 Pro), Centaur (a language model fine-tuned on 10.7M human decisions), and two task-specific fine-tuning approaches applied to Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct: action-level supervised fine-tuning (A-SFT) and process-level fine-tuning (P-SFT), which directly optimizes process features. Broad fine-tuning on human decisions improves human-like task processes relative to off-the-shelf agents, while task-specific process-level supervision further improves behavioral mimicry. However, this advantage diminishes under cross-task transfer when supervised process targets do not naturally generalize across tasks. Explicit process-level supervision can improve human behavioral mimicry, but only if appropriate task-specific process representations are available, highlighting process specification as a bottleneck for achieving human-like cognitive processes in machines.

</details>


### [387] [SpatialEpiBench: Benchmarking Spatial Information and Epidemic Priors in Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06530)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出了流行病时空预测基准测试框架SpatialEpiBench，发现现有邻接感知模型在真实场景中普遍弱于简单基线，并总结了制约模型性能的三大核心失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流行病预测在公共卫生领域至关重要，但面临非平稳、稀疏且嘈杂数据的挑战；现有相关研究缺乏统一的标准化基准，且现有评价方式（简单的按时间划分）难以模拟真实公共卫生决策场景。

Method: 提出了SpatialEpiBench基准测试集，涵盖11个流行病数据集，引入标准化滚动评估机制和疾病爆发特定的评价指标，对比分析了多种集成流行病学先验的邻接感知预测模型。

Result: 大多数现有的时空预测模型在1天至1个月的跨度内，其性能未能超越简单的“上一值”基线（Last-value baseline），即便是在利用了流行病学先验信息的情况下也表现不佳。

Conclusion: 现有的以邻接关系为核心的时空预测模型在实际流行病场景中表现欠佳，难以击败简单的基线模型。研究通过识别三种核心失效模式（爆发预判能力差、难以处理稀疏/噪声数据、地理邻接信息效用有限），为流行病预测模型的改进提供了明确的攻关目标。

Abstract: Accurate epidemic forecasting is crucial for public health response, resource allocation, and outbreak intervention, but remains difficult with sparse, noisy, and highly non-stationary data. Because epidemics unfold across interacting regions, spatiotemporal methods are natural candidates for improving forecasts. Despite growing interest in spatial information, no standardized benchmark exists, and current evaluations often use simple chronological train-test splits that do not reflect real-time forecasting practice. We address this gap with SpatialEpiBench, a challenging benchmark for spatiotemporal epidemic forecasting in realistic public-health settings. SpatialEpiBench includes 11 epidemic datasets with standardized rolling evaluations and outbreak-specific metrics. We evaluate adjacency-informed forecasting models with widely used epidemic priors that adapt general models to epidemiology, but find that most methods underperform a simple last-value baseline from 1 day to 1 month ahead, even during outbreaks and with these priors. We identify three major failure modes: (1) poor outbreak anticipation, (2) difficulty handling sparsity and noise, and (3) limited utility of common geographic adjacency for epidemiological spatial information. We release benchmark data, code, and instructions at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/SpatialEpiBench to support development of operationally useful epidemic forecasting models.

</details>


### [388] [Ex Ante Evaluation of AI-Induced Idea Diversity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06540)
*Nafis Saami Azad,Raiyan Abdul Baten*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了AI创作中被忽视的“拥挤效应”，通过提出一种新的评估框架量化了AI带来的多样性崩塌风险，并论证了通过模型设计优化可以缓解这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的评价体系仅关注单个输出的性能，忽视了创意在人口范围内的“拥挤效应”（即当生成内容高度同质时，其价值会稀释），导致AI可能在提升单体质量的同时引发群体层面的“多样性崩塌”。

Method: 提出了一个“人类相对于评估框架”，将创意构思建模为“可拥挤资源”；引入了过剩拥挤系数($Δ$)和人类相对多样性比率($ρ$)；在不需要人类-AI交互数据的情况下，仅通过对比AI自主生成的样本与人类基准集，识别源级拥挤情况。

Result: 在短篇故事、营销口号及替代用途任务中，三种前沿大模型在拥挤测试中均低于多样性平价标准($ρ<1$)；同时证明了通过调整生成协议，可以有效降低拥挤现象。

Conclusion: 当前的生成式AI模型在创作多样性上普遍存在“拥挤效应”，这削弱了输出内容的价值；作者提出的框架证明了可以通过模型设计层面的优化来缓解多样性崩塌，使该问题成为可观测、可衡量且可控的开发目标。

Abstract: Creative AI systems are typically evaluated at the level of individual utility, yet creative outputs are consumed in populations: an idea loses value when many others produce similar ones. This creates an evaluation blind spot, as AI can improve individual outputs while increasing population-level crowding. We introduce a human-relative framework for benchmarking AI-induced human diversity collapse without requiring human-AI interaction data, providing an ex ante protocol to estimate crowding risk from model-only generations and matched unaided human baselines. By modeling ideas as congestible resources, we show that source-level crowding is identifiable from within-distribution comparisons, yielding an excess-crowding coefficient $Δ$ and a human-relative diversity ratio $ρ$. We show that $ρ\ge1$ is the no-excess-crowding parity condition and connect $Δ$ to an adoption game with exposure-dependent redundancy costs. Across short stories, marketing slogans, and alternative-uses tasks, three frontier LLMs fall below parity across crowding kernels. Estimates stabilize with feasible model-only sample sizes. Importantly, generation-protocol variants show that crowding can be reduced through targeted design, making diversity collapse an actionable, development-time evaluation target for population-aware creative AI.

</details>


### [389] [Improved techniques for fine-tuning flow models via adjoint matching: a deterministic control pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06583)
*Zhengyi Guo,Jiayuan Sheng,David D. Yao,Wenpin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对流匹配模型的确定性能量对齐框架，通过将对齐转化为速度场的最优控制问题，引入轨迹末端截断计算策略，不仅简化了训练，还实现了比传统KL正则化更灵活、更高效的偏好对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配模型在人类偏好对齐过程中存在计算代价高、稳定性不佳以及难以在样本多样性与奖励最大化之间取得理想平衡的问题。

Method: 1. 将人类偏好对齐建模为基于速度场的最优控制问题；2. 设计了一种确定性伴随匹配（Deterministic Adjoint Matching）框架，通过回归至值梯度引导的目标来实现偏好对齐；3. 引入“截断伴随方案”（Truncated Adjoint Scheme），仅计算轨迹末端的梯度以节省显存与算力；4. 支持广义正则化目标，不再局限于传统的KL散度。

Result: 在SiT-XL/2和FLUX.2-Klein-4B等大规模模型上的实验表明，该方法在多个对齐指标上表现优异，并在提升奖励得分的同时，显著改善了生成结果的多样性和模态保持能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的确定性伴随匹配框架为流匹配生成模型提供了一种高效、稳定的对齐方案，通过专注于轨迹末端的计算能够显著降低资源消耗，同时在保持样本多样性和分布一致性的前提下实现了优越的偏好对齐效果。

Abstract: We propose a deterministic adjoint matching framework that formulates human preference alignment for flow-based generative models as an optimal control problem over velocity fields. One can directly regress the control toward a value-gradient-induced target under the current policy, leading to a simple and stable training objective. Building on this perspective, we introduce a truncated adjoint scheme that focuses computation on the terminal portion of the trajectory, where reward-relevant signals concentrate, which yields substantial computational savings while preserving alignment quality. We further generalize the framework beyond standard KL-based regularization, allowing more flexible trade-offs between alignment strength and distributional preservation. Experiments on SiT-XL/2 and FLUX.2-Klein-4B demonstrate consistent gains across multiple alignment metrics, along with substantially improved diversity and mode preservation.

</details>


### [390] [GlazyBench: A Benchmark for Ceramic Glaze Property Prediction and Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06641)
*Ziyu Zhai,Siyou Li,Juexi Shao,Juntao Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对陶瓷釉料研发耗时昂贵的痛点，本文提出了首个大规模釉料数据集GlazyBench，涵盖2.3万余条配方，并为釉料属性预测和视觉生成任务建立了基准测试，推动了AI在材料设计领域的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 陶瓷釉料的配方研发依赖昂贵且耗时的“试错法”，而现有AI模型缺乏大规模高质量数据集支撑，限制了该领域自动化设计的发展。

Method: 构建了包含23,148个真实釉料配方的数据集，并定义了两个核心任务：基于配方的属性预测（如颜色、透明度）和基于属性的视觉图像生成。实验评估了传统机器学习、大语言模型、深度生成模型和大型多模态模型在上述任务中的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，尽管AI在釉料属性预测和图像生成方面展示了巨大的潜力，但由于釉料化学的复杂性，当前模型在准确性和生成质量上面临显著挑战。

Conclusion: GlazyBench 通过提供首个大规模釉料数据集和标准化基准，为AI辅助材料设计开辟了新路径，证明了该领域的研究潜力和挑战。

Abstract: Developing ceramic glazes is a costly, time-consuming process of trial and error due to complex chemistry, placing a significant burden on independent artists. While recent advances in multimodal AI offer a modern solution, the field lacks the large-scale datasets required to train these models. We propose GlazyBench, the first dataset for AI-assisted glaze design. Comprising 23,148 real glaze formulations, GlazyBench supports two primary tasks: predicting post-firing surface properties, such as color and transparency, from raw materials, and generating accurate visual representations of the glaze based on these properties. We establish comprehensive baselines for property prediction using traditional machine learning and large language models, alongside image generation benchmarks using deep generative and large multimodal models. Our experiments demonstrate promising yet challenging results. GlazyBench pioneers a new research direction in AI-assisted material design, providing a standardized benchmark for systematic evaluation.

</details>


### [391] [AI Co-Mathematician: Accelerating Mathematicians with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06651)
*Daniel Zheng,Ingrid von Glehn,Yori Zwols,Iuliya Beloshapka,Lars Buesing,Daniel M. Roy,Martin Wattenberg,Bogdan Georgiev,Tatiana Schmidt,Andrew Cowie,Fernanda Viegas,Dimitri Kanevsky,Vineet Kahlon,Hartmut Maennel,Sophia Alj,George Holland,Alex Davies,Pushmeet Kohli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI co-mathematician 是一个交互式 AI 辅助数学研究工作台，通过模拟人类协作流程管理科研探索的迭代性，在处理复杂数学问题和基准测试中展现出领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学研究通常是开放式、迭代的，现有的 AI 工具难以满足数学家在探索性工作流程中的全方位需求，缺乏针对管理科研假设、处理失败路径和构建系统性知识的集成化支持。

Method: 开发了一个具备异步、有状态特性的交互式工作台，支持创意生成、文献综述、计算探索、定理证明及理论构建，旨在模拟人类数学家的科研协作流程并管理科研过程中的不确定性。

Result: AI co-mathematician 在辅助研究人员解决开放性问题、发现新方向及挖掘文献方面表现卓著；特别是在 FrontierMath Tier 4 基准测试中达到 48% 的准确率，创下了当前 AI 系统的最高记录。

Conclusion: AI co-mathematician 代表了一种人机协作进行复杂数学研究的新范式，通过提供具备状态管理和迭代探索能力的工作台，显著提升了数学家解决开放性问题和处理复杂挑战的效率。

Abstract: We introduce the AI co-mathematician, a workbench for mathematicians to interactively leverage AI agents to pursue open-ended research. The AI co-mathematician is optimized to provide holistic support for the exploratory and iterative reality of mathematical workflows, including ideation, literature search, computational exploration, theorem proving and theory building. By providing an asynchronous, stateful workspace that manages uncertainty, refines user intent, tracks failed hypotheses, and outputs native mathematical artifacts, the system mirrors human collaborative workflows. In early tests, the AI co-mathematician helped researchers solve open problems, identify new research directions, and uncover overlooked literature references. Besides demonstrating a highly interactive paradigm for AI-assisted mathematical discovery, the AI co-mathematician also achieves state of the art results on hard problem-solving benchmarks, including scoring 48% on FrontierMath Tier 4, a new high score among all AI systems evaluated.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [392] [Near-Tight Approximation Algorithms for Bottleneck Multiple Knapsack Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05233)
*Lin Chen,Tingwei Hu,Yuchen Mao,Yong Chen,Lili Mei,An Zhang,Guangting Chen,Guochuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了瓶颈多重背包问题(BMKP)，针对相同容量和任意容量两种情形，分别提供了近似算法，并确立了相应的近似性能界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 瓶颈多重背包问题（BMKP）旨在通过合理分配物品到多个背包，使得装载最少的背包其利润最大化（最大化最小值问题），旨在解决资源分配中的公平性与效率优化问题。

Method: 通过算法设计，分别针对容量相同和容量任意两种约束条件提出了近似算法；利用计算复杂性理论证明了相应的不可近似性界限。

Result: 在相同容量下获得了(2/3 - ε)的近似比；在任意容量下获得了(1/2 - ε)的近似比，且证明了(1/2 + ε)的不可近似性。

Conclusion: 对于相同容量的瓶颈多重背包问题，给出了一个趋近于2/3的近似算法；对于任意容量的情况，给出了一个趋近于1/2的近似算法，并证明了其为最优的上界（硬度界）。

Abstract: In the bottleneck multiple knapsack problem, we are given a set of items and a set of knapsacks, where each item has a profit and a weight, and each knapsack has a capacity. Our goal is to assign items to knapsacks so as to maximize the minimum profit received by any knapsack subject to the capacity constraint.
  When all knapsacks have identical capacity, we give a $(\frac{2}{3} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. This result almost matches the $(\frac{2}{3} + \varepsilon)$ inapproximability bound for the bottleneck multiple subset sum problem (Caprara et al., 2000).
  When the knapsacks can have arbitrary capacities, we propose a $(\frac{1}{2} - \varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$. We also prove a hardness bound of $(\frac{1}{2} + \varepsilon)$ for any constant $\varepsilon > 0$.

</details>


### [393] [A Separator for Minor-Free Graphs Beyond the Flow Barrier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05494)
*Hung Le*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入低直径分解优化了经典的迭代分割框架，成功突破了图论中长期存在的平衡分割大小的“流门槛”（flow barrier），实现了$K_h$-无子图最优路径上的重大进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alon-Seymour-Thomas猜想提出$K_h$-无子图存在大小为$O(h\sqrt{n})$的平衡分割器，但长期以来受限于$O(h \log h \sqrt{n})$的流-割对偶门槛，且现有算法要么存在增长过快的函数项，要么难以进一步提升。

Method: 1. 采用Alon-Seymour-Thomas的迭代框架。2. 引入低直径分解（low-diameter decomposition）技术来优化该框架中的核心——邻域界（neighborhood bound），将其由原来的$O(\sqrt{h})$优化为$O(\sqrt{\log h})$。

Result: 提出了一种构造$O(h \sqrt{\log h} \sqrt{n})$大小平衡分割器的算法，优于当前最好的已知的基于流的构造，且显著缩小了与阿尔隆-西摩-托马斯（Alon-Seymour-Thomas）猜想的距离。

Conclusion: 该论文成功打破了基于流与割对偶（flow-cut duality）的$O(h \log h \sqrt{n})$界限，通过结合迭代框架与低直径分解技术，将$K_h$-无子图的平衡分割器大小改进至$O(h \sqrt{\log h} \sqrt{n})$，向Alon-Seymour-Thomas猜想迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: In 1990, Alon, Seymour, and Thomas gave the first balanced separator of size $O(h^{3/2}\sqrt{n})$ for any $K_h$-minor-free graph, which has had numerous algorithmic applications. They conjectured that the size of the balanced separator can be reduced to $O(h\sqrt{n})$, which is asymptotically tight. Two decades later, Kawarabayashi and Reed constructed a separator of size $O(h\sqrt{n} + f(h))$ based on the graph minor structure theorem, where $f(h)$ is an extremely fast-growing function; their separator's size is only better for a very small value of $h$. Recently, Spalding-Jamieson constructed a separator of size $O(h\log h \log\log h \sqrt{n})$; the technique is rooted in concurrent flow-sparsest cut duality. Spalding-Jamieson's separator comes very close to $O(h\log h \sqrt{n})$, which is the barrier for techniques based on the flow-cut duality.
  In this work, we first present a simple adaptation of a flow-based algorithm of Korhonen and Lokshtanov to construct a balanced separator of size $O(h\log h \sqrt{n})$, matching the flow barrier. This result motivates the question of whether the flow barrier can be broken, which would be a stepping stone toward resolving the conjecture of Alon, Seymour, and Thomas. The main result of our work is a positive answer to this question: we construct a balanced separator of size $O(h \sqrt{\log h} \sqrt{n})$. Surprisingly, perhaps, our algorithm is still based on the iterative framework of Alon, Seymour, and Thomas, although a key component of their algorithm within this framework, called the neighborhood bound, was shown to be tight. Our new idea is to incorporate a low-diameter decomposition into the framework, allowing us to reduce the neighborhood bound by a factor of $h$, at the cost of a factor $\log h$, which translates to a reduction from $\sqrt{h}$ to $\sqrt{\log h}$ in the final separator's size.

</details>


### [394] [Nearly Optimal Attention Coresets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05602)
*Edo Liberty,Alexandr Andoni,Eldar Kleiner*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了Attention机制的核集（coreset）构造问题，通过理论推导证明了该核集在满足特定查询范数约束下，其大小可达到近乎最优的 $O(\sqrt{d} e^ρ/ε)$，并在理论上改进了该问题的上界和下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Attention机制在大数据集下计算和存储成本高昂，本文旨在寻找一种在小空间内估计Attention机制的方法，以减少计算复杂性。

Method: 采用理论证明方法，通过构建并量化Attention查询的近似核集，推导出了新的上界，并结合信息论或几何方法建立了相应的误差下界。

Result: 证明了Attention机制核集的大小上界为 $O({\sqrt{d} e^{ρ+o(ρ)}/\varepsilon})$，并提出了下界 $Ω(\sqrt{d} e^ρ/ε)$，优于当前已知的最佳结果。

Conclusion: 作者证明了Attention机制存在近乎最优大小的核集（coreset），即对于给定的查询范数，存在一个大小为 $O({\sqrt{d} e^{ρ+o(ρ)}/\varepsilon})$ 的子集，使其近似误差在 $\varepsilon$ 以内，同时给出了该问题的下界 $Ω({\sqrt{d} e^ρ/ε})$，证明了该结果接近最优。

Abstract: We consider the problem of estimating the Attention mechanism in small space, and prove the existence of coresets for it of nearly optimal size. Specifically, we show that for any set of unit-norm keys and values $(K,V)$ in $\mathbb{R}^d$, there exists a subset $(K',V')$ of size at most $O({\sqrt{d} e^{ρ+o(ρ)}/\varepsilon})$ such that \[ \left\| \operatorname{Attn}(q,K,V)- \operatorname{Attn}(q,K',V') \right\| \le \varepsilon \] simultaneously for all queries whose norm is bounded by $ρ$. This outperforms the best known results for this problem. We also offer an improved lower bound showing that $\varepsilon$-coresets must have size $Ω({\sqrt{d} e^ρ/ε})$.

</details>


### [395] [Algorithmic Phase Transition for Large Independent Sets in Dense Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05618)
*Abhishek Dhawan,Nhi U. Dinh,Eren C. Kızıldağ,Neeladri Maitra,Bayram A. Şahin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了密集随机超图中寻找最大独立集的算法可解性，针对两种模型设计了最优的在线算法，并给出了匹配的计算下界，确立了在线算法在这些问题上的性能极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对稀疏图的现有框架（如局部算法或低度多项式 LDP）在密集随机超图中表现不佳或完全失效，亟需针对密集设定下在线算法这一不同范式进行算法可理解性（tractability）的研究。

Method: 提出了针对密集 $r$-一致 Erdős-Rényi 超图和 $r$-部分超图的在线算法；利用分析技术确定了算法的乘法近似比，并建立了匹配的算法下界以证明其最优性。

Result: 对于密集 $r$-一致 Erdős-Rényi 超图，实现了 $r^{1/(r-1)}$ 的近似比；对于密集 $r$-部分超图，实现了 $(\max_i \gamma_i)^{-1/(r-1)}$ 的近似比，并证明了这些近似比无法通过在线算法进一步突破。

Conclusion: 研究确定了密集超图寻找最大独立集问题的算法复杂度边界，证明了所提出的在线算法在各自模型中所能达到的乘法近似比是该类算法的最优上限，即存在算法计算鸿沟。

Abstract: We study the algorithmic tractability of finding large independent sets in dense random hypergraphs. In the sparse regime, much of the natural algorithms can be formulated within either the local or the low-degree polynomial (LDP) framework, and a rich literature has subsequently identified nearly sharp algorithmic thresholds within these classes by exploiting their stability. In the dense setting, however, the algorithmic paradigms are fundamentally different: they are online and thus need not be stable. Perhaps more crucially, even for the classical Erdős-Rényi random graph $G(n,p)$, LDPs are conjectured to fail in the 'easy' regime accessible to online algorithms, thereby challenging their viability for dense models.
  Our focus is on two models: (i) finding large independent sets in dense $r$-uniform Erdős-Rényi hypergraphs, and (ii) the more challenging problem of finding large $γ$-balanced independent sets in dense $r$-uniform $r$-partite hypergraphs, where the $i$-th coordinate of $γ\in\mathbb{Q}^r$ specifies the proportion of vertices from $V_i$ in the independent set. For both models, we pinpoint the size of the largest independent set and design online algorithms that achieve a multiplicative approximation factor of $r^{1/(r-1)}$ in the uniform and $(\max_i γ_i)^{-1/(r-1)}$ in the $r$-partite model. Furthermore, we establish matching algorithmic lower bounds, showing that these computational gaps are sharp: no online algorithms can breach these gaps.

</details>


### [396] [Discrete Optimal Transport: Rapid Convergence of Simulated Annealing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05877)
*Yuchen He,Tianhui Jiang,Sihan Wang,Chihao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于离散Wasserstein度量的框架来分析有限状态空间下的模拟退火算法，通过引入“离散作用量”概念，为均值场Ising和Potts模型在特定条件下提供了多项式收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将连续空间退火Langevin动力学的作用量分析法推广至有限状态空间，以建立模拟退火算法在离散环境下的非渐近收敛性理论框架。

Method: 利用Maas提出的离散Wasserstein度量，定义了图上概率测度路径的广义离散Wasserstein-2距离及相应的“离散作用量”；通过利用模型对称性将高维问题投影至低维链，对离散作用量进行多项式上界估计。

Result: 证明了KL散度与离散作用量之间的控制关系；为均值场Ising模型在任意逆温度下实现了多项式时间求解，并证明了均值场q态Potts模型在失稳相变后的多项式时间收敛性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了离散最优传输框架在分析模拟退火算法稳定性与收敛性方面的有效性，证明了通过控制离散作用量（discrete action）可以获得关于模拟退火算法的非渐近收敛保证，并为特定物理模型提供了多项式时间收敛的理论支撑。

Abstract: We develop a discrete optimal transport framework for analyzing simulated annealing algorithms on finite state spaces. Building on the discrete Wasserstein metric introduced by Maas (J. Funct. Anal., 2011), we define a generalized discrete Wasserstein-2 distance and the associated notion of \emph{discrete action} for paths of probability measures on graphs. Using these tools, we establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees for simulated annealing: the KL divergence between the algorithm's output and the target distribution is controlled by the discrete action of the annealing path. This can be viewed as the discrete counterpart of the action-based analysis of annealed Langevin dynamics in continuous spaces by Guo, Tao, and Chen (ICLR 2025).
  As applications, we analyze simulated annealing for two fundamental models in statistical physics. For the \emph{mean-field Ising model}, we show that annealed single-site Glauber dynamics achieves $\varepsilon$ error in KL divergence in $O(n^5β^2/\varepsilon)$ steps at \emph{any} inverse temperature $β\ge 0$. For the \emph{mean-field $q$-state Potts model}, we show that annealed $(q-1)$-block Glauber dynamics achieves $\varepsilon$ error in $\mathrm{poly}(n, β, 1/\varepsilon)$ steps for all $β\ge β_{\mathsf{s}}=q/2$, the regime where the disordered phase has completely lost stability. In both cases, the key technical contribution is a polynomial upper bound on the discrete action, obtained by exploiting the symmetry of the model to reduce the analysis to a low-dimensional projected chain.

</details>


### [397] [Label Correcting Algorithms for the Multiobjective Temporal Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05954)
*Edina Marica,Clemens Thielen,Alina Wittmann*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对一般化的多目标时间最短路径问题，本文探讨了在缺失单调性和保序性假设下的求解方法，提出了基于路径长度限制的标号修正算法，并给出了无需该限制的收敛充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单源多目标时间最短路径问题算法主要依赖于目标函数的单调性（Monotonicity）和保序性（Isotonicity）。但在去除这些假设后，零时延（zero-duration）时间环路可能导致路径搜索陷入无限循环，无法找到所有非支配解。因此，需要研究在缺乏这些性质时的求解策略。

Method: 针对路径长度限制在 K 以内的受限问题，开发了通用的标号修正算法（Label Correcting Algorithms）；并从理论上推导了在何种条件下即使缺失单调性或保序性，依然无需长度限制即可求出所有非支配像（nondominated images）。

Result: 提出了一套通用的标号修正算法框架，该框架能够处理缺乏单调性和保序性的情况；证明了即使在没有这些性质的情况下，通过限制路径长度 K，依然能够有效探索搜索空间；并明确了在特定条件下，算法无需长度限制即可收敛到最优解集。

Conclusion: 当目标函数缺失单调性或保序性时，传统的标号设置算法失效。本文针对有向离散时间动态图，提出限制路径长度 K 的标号修正算法以解决包含零时延环路的问题，并给出了无需该限制即可求解的充分条件。

Abstract: Given a directed, discrete-time temporal graph $G=(V,R)$, a start node $s\in V$, and $p\geq1$ objectives, the single-source multiobjective temporal shortest path problem asks, for each $v\in V$, for the set of nondominated images of temporal $s$-$v$-paths together with a corresponding efficient path for each image. A recent general label setting algorithm for this problem relies on two properties of the objectives - monotonicity and isotonicity. Monotonicity generalizes the nonnegativity assumption required by label setting methods for the classical additive single-objective shortest path problem on static graphs, while isotonicity ensures that the order of the objective values of two paths is preserved when both are extended by the same arc.
  In this paper, we study the problem without assuming monotonicity and/or isotonicity. A key difficulty in this setting is that zero-duration temporal cycles may need to be traversed an arbitrary finite number of times to generate all nondominated images. This motivates the study of a restricted problem variant in which a maximum admissible path length $K$ is imposed, and only paths containing at most $K$ arcs are considered. We develop general label correcting algorithms for this setting and establish several sufficient conditions under which such a bound is not required, implying that the algorithms compute all nondominated images.

</details>


### [398] [The Pareto Frontier of Randomized Learning-Augmented Online Bidding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06106)
*Mathis Degryse,Imrane Saakour,Christoph Dürr,Spyros Angelopoulos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线竞价的随机学习增强问题，引入“竞价函数”框架统一了策略分析，并精确界定了鲁棒性与一致性之间的最优权衡曲线，在 R ≥ 2.885 时实现了闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线竞价是资源分配和近似算法分析等领域的经典问题，现有研究在随机学习增强变体中，关于一致性（Consistency）与鲁棒性（Robustness）之间的最优权衡关系仍存在理论缺口，需要更统一的分析框架来完善这一领域。

Method: 1. 引入“竞价函数（bidding function）”作为分析随机竞价策略的统一框架与抽象工具；
2. 构建并分析了在线算法在利用预言机（oracle）预测时的性能，推导出一致性 C 与鲁棒性 R 的理论界限；
3. 将理论模型应用于增量中位数问题（incremental median problem）进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 给出了最优一致性 C 作为鲁棒性 R 函数的解析上下界；
2. 当 R ≥ 2.885 时，上下界完美重合，彻底解决了该问题遗留的分析缺口；
3. 通过实验证明了该算法在增量聚类场景下的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入“竞价函数”这一抽象概念，统一并优化了随机竞价策略的分析框架。研究精确界定了最优一致性 C 与鲁棒性 R 之间的权衡关系，并在 R ≥ 2.885 时实现了上下界闭合，为在线决策问题的理论分析提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Online bidding is a classical problem in online decision-making, with applications in resource allocation, hierarchical clustering, and the analysis of approximation algorithms. We study its randomized learning-augmented variant, where an online algorithm generates a sequence of random bids while leveraging predictions from an oracle. We provide analytical upper and lower bounds on the optimal consistency $C$ as a function of the robustness $R$, which match when $R \geq 2.885$, effectively closing the gap left by previous work. The key technical ingredient is the notion of a bidding function, a novel abstraction that provides a unified framework for the design and analysis of randomized bidding strategies. We complement our theoretical results with an experimental application of randomized bidding to the incremental median problem, demonstrating the applicability of our algorithm in practical clustering settings.

</details>


### [399] [Bilateral Treewidth for QBF: Where Strategies and Resolution Meet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06262)
*Robert Ganian,Marlene Gründel*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对QBF求解，本文提出了“双侧树宽”，通过集成策略分支和Q-消解两种互补技术，成功实现了比现有方法更强大的固定参数可处理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的树宽变体在处理QBF时，要么仅依赖策略技术，要么仅依赖消解技术，两者互不兼容且限制了适用范围。作者希望通过结合这两种技术，定义一个更强大且能统一处理能力的参数。

Method: 提出双侧树宽（bilateral treewidth）这一新概念；设计一种结合了策略分支与Q-消解的复合算法，以处理该参数下的QBF问题。

Result: 证明了在双侧树宽下，QBF的判定问题是固定参数可处理（FPT）的，显著扩展了可高效求解的QBF实例范围。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的分解参数“双侧树宽”（bilateral treewidth），该参数通过结合策略分支（strategy branching）和Q-消解（Q-resolution）技术，在参数化复杂度层面上统一并超越了现有的QBF处理方法，实现了对QBF问题的固定参数可处理性（FPT）。

Abstract: Treewidth is a well-studied decompositional parameter to measure the tree-likeness of a graph. While the propositional satisfiability problem (SAT) is known to be tractable when parameterized by the treewidth of the underlying primal graph, the evaluation of quantified Boolean formulas (QBFs) remains PSPACE-complete even on formulas of constant treewidth. Intuitively, this is because ordinary treewidth does not take into account the prefix of the QBF: it neither distinguishes between existential and universal variables, nor accounts for the order in which they are quantified. In the past, several weaker variants of treewidth have been devised to incorporate prefix-sensitive information. To establish tractability for QBFs under these notions, prior work has employed either strategy- or resolution-based techniques, thereby dividing the parameterized complexity landscape of QBF into two regimes that are incomparable in strength. We establish fixed-parameter tractability with respect to bilateral treewidth, a novel and strictly more powerful decompositional parameter that combines these rivaling approaches by simultaneously allowing for branching on strategies and performing Q-resolution. As in previous works in this direction, our algorithm assumes that a suitable tree decomposition is provided on the input.

</details>


### [400] [On the Parameterized Approximability of (Mergeable) Sum of Radii Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06398)
*Ameet Gadekar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文解决了 $k$-MSR 聚类问题的参数化复杂度难题，证明了其 $W[2]$-hard 等级及其不存在高效参数化近似方案（EPAS）的结论；同时，通过可合并约束框架，为公平和私有聚类问题提供了性能更优的 FPT 近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $k$-MSR (最小化半径之和聚类) 问题在近似算法和参数化算法中已得到广泛研究，但其精确的参数化复杂度以及是否存在高效参数化近似方案 (EPAS) 仍未解决。该研究旨在填补这一理论空白，并探索其在受限聚类场景下的计算潜力。

Method: 1. 硬度理论分析（归约法）：证明 $k$-MSR 问题关于参数 $k$ 是 $W[2]$-hard 的，并排除存在 EPAS 的可能性。
2. 算法设计：利用“可合并约束（mergeable constraints）”框架来处理公平性、多样性等聚类约束。
3. 参数化近似算法：开发了改进的 FPT 近似算法，通过优化赋值过程提升边界。

Result: 1. 复杂度上界：证明 $k$-MSR 关于参数 $k$ 为 $W[2]$-hard。
2. 不可能性结果：在假设 $W[2] \neq FPT$ 及 ETH 的条件下，证明该问题不存在 EPAS。
3. 算法性能：在可合并约束框架下，将 FPT 近似比从 (4+ε) 改善至 (8/3+ε)；在特定条件下可实现 (2+ε) 近似，并推广至多种公平和私有聚类场景。

Conclusion: 该研究通过建立 $k$-MSR 的 $W[2]$-hard 性质，确定了其在 $W$-层级中的位置，并在此基础上证明了在 $W[2]=FPT$ 假设下不存在 EPAS。同时，通过引入可合并约束框架，显著提升了该问题的 FPT 近似比，将其从之前的 4 改进至 8/3，并在特定分配子程序下达到最优的 2 近似。

Abstract: The sum of radii problem ($k$-MSR) asks, given a metric space on $n$ points, to place $k$ balls covering all points so as to minimize the sum of their radii. Despite extensive study from the perspectives of approximation and parameterized algorithms, the exact parameterized complexity of the problem and the existence of efficient parameterized approximation schemes remained open. We advance this understanding on both the hardness and algorithmic fronts.
  We begin by showing that $k$-MSR is $W[2]$-hard parameterized by $k$, thereby pinpointing its location in the $W$-hierarchy. Moreover, via our reduction, we rule out efficient parameterized approximation schemes (EPAS)--that is, $(1+ε)$-approximations running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot \mathrm{poly}(n)$--unless $W[2] = FPT$. Assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis, we further rule out such algorithms running in time $f(k,ε)\cdot n^{o(k)}$, strengthening recent lower bounds for the problem.
  On the algorithmic side, we study $k$-MSR under the framework of mergeable constraints, which captures a broad class of clustering constraints, including fairness, diversity, and lower bounds. We obtain an FPT $(\frac{8}{3}+ε)$-approximation, improving upon the previous best guarantee of $(4+ε)$. Moreover, given access to a suitable assignment subroutine, we achieve a $(2+ε)$-approximation, matching the best known bound for the unconstrained problem. This, in turn, yields $(2+ε)$ FPT-approximations for several important settings, including $(t,k)$-fair, $(α,β)$-fair, $\ell$-diversity, and private clustering.

</details>


### [401] [Fast decremental tree sums in forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06555)
*Benjamin Aram Berendsohn,Marek Sokołowski*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对动态森林删除场景，通过微观-宏观树分解技术，将 tree-sum 查询的操作时间从传统的 $O(\log n)$ 降低至 $O(\log^* n)$，并提供了该问题的渐进通用最优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态森林数据结构（如 Top Trees）在处理边删除等动态更新时，单次操作时间复杂度为 $O(\log n)$，旨在探索删除专用场景下是否能实现更快的操作效率。

Method: 采用微观-宏观（micro-macro）树分解技术、小规模实例的预计算方法，以及针对 group 模型下的通用最优性算法设计。

Result: 对于 tree-sum 查询，在 $O(n)$ 预处理下实现了 $O(\log^* n)$ 的操作时间；对于无权森林，实现了 $O(1)$ 操作时间；此外，对于 tree-sum 问题设计了一种在大 $O$ 定义下渐进通用最优（asymptotically universally optimal）的算法。

Conclusion: 研究了树状森林中的两类增量动态查询（tree-sum 和 subtree-sum），在删除操作方面取得了优于传统对数时间复杂度的数据结构，并实现了一种渐进式的通用最优算法。

Abstract: We study two fundamental decremental dynamic graph problems. In both problems, we need to maintain a vertex-weighted forest of size $n$ under edge deletions, weight updates, and a certain information-retrieval query. Both problems can be solved in $O(\log n)$ time per update/query using standard dynamic forest data structures like top trees, even if additionally edge insertions are allowed. We investigate whether the deletion-only problem can be solved faster.
  First, we consider $\texttt{tree-sum}$ queries, where we ask for the sum of vertex weights in one of the connected components (i.e., trees) in the forest. We give a data structure with $O(n)$ preprocessing time and $O(\log^* n)$ time per operation, based on a micro-macro tree decomposition (Alstrup et al., 1997). If the forest is unweighted (i.e., all weights are 1 and cannot be changed), then the operation time can be improved to $O(1)$.
  Additionally, we give an asymptotically universally optimal algorithm. More specifically, our algorithm works in the group model, and processes $m$ operations on an initial forest $F$ in running time $O( \mathrm{OPT}(F, m) )$. Here $\mathrm{OPT}(F, m)$ is the number of weight additions and subtractions that a best possible algorithm performs to handle a worst-case instance for a fixed initial forest $F$ and a fixed number $m$ of operations. We achieve this with a combination of the aforementioned decomposition technique, precomputation of optimal data structures for very small instances, and some insights into the behavior of $\mathrm{OPT}$. Note that even the worst-case complexity of this algorithm remains unknown to us.
  Second, we consider $\texttt{subtree-sum}$ queries. Here, the forest is rooted, and a query $\texttt{subtree-sum}(v)$ returns the sum of weights in the subtree rooted at $v$. We show tight bounds for several variants of this problem. [...]

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [402] [Dynamic Graph with Similarity-Aware Attention Graph Neural Network for Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05238)
*Aadarsh Senapati,Neha Kujur,Vivek Yelleti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-SA-GNN模型，通过动态构建多相似度用户关系图并结合注意力机制与交叉注意力模块，解决了传统推荐系统无法适应用户动态偏好及缺乏显式关系建模的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统协同过滤依赖静态交互图，无法捕捉用户兴趣的动态变化；现有GNN方法缺乏显式的用户间关系建模及动态图演化能力。

Method: 提出DG-SA-GNN框架：1. 使用四种相似度函数（余弦、Jaccard、折扣皮尔逊系数、IPIJ）构建并行用户相似度图；2. 采用专门的UserGNN处理各图，通过Graph Transformer进行融合；3. 引入Cross-Attention模块优化嵌入；4. 在训练过程中周期性重建图以实现动态适应；5. 使用小批量训练和难负采样提升效率。

Result: 在MovieLens100K数据集上，Recall@20达到0.162，NDCG@20达到0.065，在召回率指标上表现优于LightGCN基线。

Conclusion: 通过动态多相似度图构建与注意力机制融合，DG-SA-GNN能有效捕捉用户偏好的动态变化，提升推荐系统的准确性，并优于LightGCN等经典基线模型。

Abstract: Recommender systems are essential components of modern online platforms which presents personalized content in various domain. The traditional collaborative filtering methods depends on static user-item interaction graphs and a limited subset of similarity measures which fail to capture the changing nature of preferences of an individual. Recent graph neural network (GNN) based approaches focus on user-item bipartite graphs which do not use explicit user-user relational modelling and dynamic graph evolution during training. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a Dynamic Graph SimilarityAware Attention Graph Neural Network (DG-SA-GNN) framework that integrates dynamic user similarity graph construction with multi-similarity propagation and attention-based aggregation. The proposed architecture constructs four parallel user similarity graphs using Cosine, Jaccard, Discounted Pearson Correlation Coefficient (Discount PCC), and IPIJ similarity functions, each processed by a dedicated UserGNN module. A Graph Transformer fuses the four graph views, and a CrossAttention module refines user embeddings through interaction with item embeddings. Crucially, the graphs are reconstructed at scheduled epochs during training, enabling the model to adapt to the learned embedding space constituting the dynamic graph component. Mini-batch training with hard negative sampling improves scalability and convergence.
